Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.16

Chapter 1501
Right now, Luo Zheng's footsteps were unprecedentedly light and fast.
When he first stepped into the Mountain Crushing Clan's territory with Han Liusu, he was extremely shocked when he saw the Mountain Crushing Clan members moving freely on the silver boulder.
After more than half a year of experience and absorbing the source of power here, the gravity was no longer worth mentioning to him!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh!"
These Great Demon Badgers lived in the Sacred Spring Mountain all year round and had absorbed a lot of energy.
Three bull-sized Demon Badgers pounced towards Luo Zheng. The power contained within them was not small. Even Lie Yu couldn't help but retreat when he saw this.
He had placed his hopes on Luo Zheng. Although he was not clear about Luo Zheng's strength, and this fellow had only been in the Mountain Crushing Clan for a short period of time, the problem was that he had absorbed such a large amount of energy …
Luo Zheng took a stance and gently took a step forward.
Before entering the Sacred Spring Mountain, his every move was extremely clumsy. Even a distance of two to three feet was difficult for him to cross. It was as if his entire body had sunk into an invisible swamp.
At this moment, that little bit of gravitational force was no obstacle to Luo Zheng at all!
Three incomparably swift punches were thrown!
"Pop pop pop!"
The three bull-sized Great Demon Badgers froze in the air, then suddenly exploded. Their skin and flesh shattered, and their bones and tendons also shattered inch by inch. In the end, they turned into three streaks of blood, smearing the ground in front of Luo Zheng …
These Great Demon Badgers were considered to be at the top of the food chain at the bottom of the cave. They had once surrounded and captured members of the Mountain Crushing Clan, including foreign Martial Warriors, but they had never thought that this human's strength would be so terrifying.
After a series of wails, the remaining Great Demon Badgers dispersed in a hubbub. How could they be willing to find trouble with Luo Zheng?
"Powerful, powerful …"
Lie Yu took a deep breath.
Lie Yu was extremely dissatisfied with Luo Zheng's treatment in the Mountain Crushing Clan. Not only him, but many of the Mountain Crushing Clan members and foreign Martial Warriors were also dissatisfied.
In their opinion, Luo Zheng was only relying on his innate talent of not being afraid of fire to swindle resources from the Mountain Crushing Clan. Whether or not he could really help the Mountain Crushing Clan was still a big question mark …
Now, Lie Yu was truly convinced. This fellow could not be compared to those foreign martial artists!
Luo Zheng rubbed his fists and curled his lips. "Let's go!"
Lie Yu nodded and immediately followed him.
This huge cave was a long slope that slowly led upwards. Along the way, Luo Zheng encountered a few strange beasts.
However, according to Lie Yu, these exotic beasts weren't particularly powerful. They were lucky …
The terrain gradually rose, and when it reached the middle section, many caves began to appear around it. These caves were connected to the many caves in Sacred Spring Mountain. No matter which path one chose, they would eventually come out from one of the caves and leave through the caves below.
Many warriors had been tossing and turning inside the Sacred Spring Mountain for a while. In the end, they had absorbed a considerable amount of Origin Energy. At this moment, they left one after another, gathering with Luo Zheng and the others.
After walking for about the time it takes for an incense stick to burn, the road ahead became brighter and brighter. Then, a huge mountain pass appeared in front of them.
Lei Toad crossed his arms and stood at the mountain pass. Behind him were many members of the Collapsing Mountain Tribe. They had been waiting here for a long time.
Warriors who could safely leave the Sacred Spring Mountain had passed the Collapsing Mountain Tribe's rite of passage. This was a sign that the Collapsing Mountain Tribe had matured.
"How was your harvest?"
Every year, there would be some warriors with great harvests. However, most of them were from the Collapsing Mountain Tribe. Unless the foreign martial artists encountered a particularly great opportunity, their harvest was usually ranked very low.
This time, however, Lei Toad was very concerned about a foreign martial artist.
The place he was bringing Luo Zheng to was too dangerous. Naturally, he hoped that Luo Zheng could grow as much as possible.
"Not bad," Luo Zheng nodded lightly.
Lie Yu originally wanted to speak, but along the way, Luo Zheng had quietly reminded him that there was some kind of mysterious power in that cave. It should be controlled by a living creature, and that living creature was not willing to reveal this secret. If possible, Lie Yu had better not report it …
Lie Yu's heart itched, but he thought that Luo Zheng's words made sense. The eight trigrams ball had been confiscated in the end. If he casually revealed this secret, wouldn't it bring trouble to the Collapsing Mountain Tribe? Therefore, Lie Yu held back.
Before Luo Zheng could answer Lei Toad, a Collapsing Mountain Tribe martial artist suddenly laughed loudly. "Haha, Uncle Lei! I soaked in a pool of energy source! "
"Oh?" Lei Toad's eyes flashed.
"That pool of energy source suddenly rained down on me. At that time, I was stunned! However, in the end, I only absorbed a small portion. Sigh … "The Collapsing Mountain Tribe member sighed.
"A pool of energy source rained down on you? That's the gravitational pull of the energy source being reversed! This … "Lei Toad's face revealed an excited expression. This kind of situation rarely happened in the Sacred Spring Mountain.
"What's so great about that? I just had a small stream here. There was so much energy source that I couldn't even digest it …" A foreign martial artist sneered.
Actually, along the way, they had also communicated with each other.
At the beginning, everyone thought that they had encountered a fortuitous encounter. However, after communicating, they realized that everyone's experiences were more or less the same. Only a few martial artists' caves did not have any energy source. That was why they had missed such a heaven-sent opportunity.
After listening to these people talking, Lei Toad finally understood what had happened in the Sacred Spring Mountain.
It seemed that after everyone had entered the Sacred Spring Mountain, the gravitational pull of the entire Sacred Spring Mountain had become chaotic. Therefore, many martial artists had been tossed up and down. It was also because of this reason that the energy source that was so far out of reach had poured down in torrents!
In the past, only a few martial artists had been able to obtain such a fortuitous encounter. However, this time, it was so common …
Many Collapsing Mountain Tribe members who had gone through the Coming of Age Ceremony and those who had not had the chance to do so, revealed looks of envy when they heard these words.
The energy source was too important to a Body Refinement Cultivator. It would often determine the limits of their future growth. The martial artists who had entered the Sacred Spring Mountain this year could be considered to have struck the jackpot. However, none of them knew that all of this was bestowed upon them by Luo Zheng.
"Luo Zheng, everyone's gains are not bad. How about you?" Lei Toad was still more concerned about Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly, "My luck is not bad. I absorbed some energy source."
"How much is some?" Lei Toad asked.
"I absorbed to the limit of my physical body's capacity. There is still a lot that I did not finish absorbing. It is a waste," Luo Zheng replied faintly. His face revealed a look of regret.
His answer was to prevent Lei Toad from asking any further questions.
Luo Zheng had indeed absorbed to his limit. However, his limit was to break open the world in his body. He had directly transformed the energy source into nine stars and placed it within. It was naturally a fact that there was still a lot that he did not finish absorbing. However, Lei Toad did not know that Luo Zheng's regret was the entire lake of energy source! Even if a True God were to soak in it, he might not be able to absorb it completely …
"Very good! Everyone has received the favor of the Sacred Spring Mountain. Follow me! "Lei Toad nodded and said with a smile.
After passing through the Sacred Spring Mountain, many warriors had to undergo the test of the Collapsing Mountain Tribe. After that, if the warriors from other tribes wanted to continue cultivating here, they could choose to join the Collapsing Mountain Tribe's team to hunt outside. Of course, they could also choose to leave the Collapsing Mountain Tribe.
According to the results of the test, the Collapsing Mountain Tribe would allocate teams. After all, when they went out to hunt, they would face powerful exotic beasts. When dividing the work, they would take into account everyone's strength.
Under Lei Toad's lead, many warriors moved and returned to the Collapsing Mountain Tribe.
On the huge square of the Mountain Crushing Tribe, there was now a huge golden rock. This golden rock was about ten feet tall and square in shape. Under the light, it emitted a dazzling golden glow, as if it was a piece of pure gold.
This golden rock was much harder and heavier than the huge silver rock on the ground of the Collapsing Mountain Tribe.
"They are back, Princess Royal," He Zuowei looked at the team in the distance and reminded her.
This time, Han Liusu did not have the qualifications to participate in the trial of the Sacred Spring Mountain. Although she could stand up, she could only stagger. Therefore, Han Liusu was very depressed …
"Luo Zheng is back?" Han Liusu blinked her eyes. She knew that there was a certain mortality rate in the Sacred Spring Mountain. After all, there were some unlucky people who could not return. Naturally, she did not want Luo Zheng to be such an unlucky person.
"Luo Zheng is walking at the front," He Zuowei replied. "This kid safely passed the trial of the Sacred Spring Mountain. I wonder how far he can push this rock in the next test?"
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1502
The culture of the Collapsing Mountain Clan was very simple. Compared to the other brilliant civilizations in the universe, it could even be considered very primitive.
Even the test of strength was very crude and simple.
The golden square stone was placed on the square and the martial artist had to exert all his strength to push the stone. The distance that the martial artist could push the stone would determine the winner …
Actually, the test for body cultivators should be very complicated, and the standard of the test was not only limited to pure strength.
For example, the physical body's resistance to attacks, toughness, flexibility and so on.
However, the Collapsing Mountain Clan only tested this, which was actually unfair. It also did not represent the martial artist's full strength.
It was just that the Collapsing Mountain Clan had always been proud of their strength, and this was the only thing that they valued the most.
According to the standard set by the Collapsing Mountain Clan, the strength of this test was divided into four levels.
To push the golden stone two inches was extraordinary.
To push it one inch was martial bravery.
To push it five inches was divine power.
To push it ten inches was Barbarian King!
With the strength of the Collapsing Mountain Clan's martial artists, if they were to push mountains, they could probably push tens of thousands of ordinary mountains!
But to push the golden square stone in front of them was not an easy task.
The younger generation of the Collapsing Mountain Clan viewed it as a great honor to follow their elders out to hunt. However, not all of the Collapsing Mountain Clan's martial artists could push the stone after undergoing the trial in the Sacred Spring Mountain. Less than a third of them could obtain this qualification every year.
Because the minimum requirement to participate in the hunt was to push it two inches, which was at least extraordinary!
But under the test of the majority of martial artists, this golden square stone did not move at all — after all, the Collapsing Mountain Clan's gravity was very strong. Coupled with the terrifying weight of this golden square stone, it was probably equivalent to the weight of tens of thousands of mountains compressed together. Not to mention two inches, even moving it by an inch was extremely difficult.
Because of this difficulty, the Collapsing Mountain Clan only gave four standards.
To be able to obtain an extraordinary evaluation, to the younger generation of the Collapsing Mountain Clan, was already a great honor …
The atmosphere this year was also slightly different.
Whether it was the Collapsing Mountain Clan or the martial artists from other clans, they were all full of confidence at this moment.
Other than a few martial artists who could not find the source of their strength and were dejected, the majority of the people were rubbing their palms. This year, their goal was not only "extraordinary", but "martial bravery". There were even some who wanted to challenge the title of "divine power".
At the side of the plaza, He Zuowei and the others supported Han Liusu and placed her on a soft stool to watch the Mountain Crushing Clan's test.
The Thunder Toad took two steps forward and leaped up. It sat on the golden square stone with a golden blade, and a list of names appeared in its large hand. "Suo Tuo!"
The Mountain Crumbling Race was the priority to be recorded on this list.
That Mountain Shattering Mountain Clan member called Suo Tuo slowly walked out. His expression was quite ugly. He was one of those extremely unlucky few.
After entering the Sacred Spring Mountain, Suo Tuo had also been tormented by the gravitational pull. Everyone had been tormented, but the problem was that he had at least obtained a considerable amount of energy …
Not only did he fail to obtain the source of power, but the area he was in just so happened to have many stalagmites protruding from the top. If not for his vigilance, he would have died there!
Soto really wasn't in the mood to accept this test. After all, he hadn't gained anything in the Sacred Spring Mountain at all.
However, he didn't dare to disobey the leader's orders. He could only walk over unhappily and nod at the Thunder Toad on the golden square stone.
The Thunder Toad had presided over this kind of test many times. From his expression, he could tell that Suo Tuo didn't gain much. However, although there weren't many rules in the Mountain Crushing Clan, there were still rules. This test wasn't allowed to be abandoned. Even warriors from other clans could give up. They had to pass the test before they could leave the Mountain Crushing Clan.
Suo Tuo stood on the spot and got into a stance.
Even if he didn't obtain the source of power, Suo Tuo still had a glimmer of hope in his heart. As long as he could move two inches, he would be able to obtain an extraordinary title. Some outstanding Mountain Crushing Clan members could do it even if they didn't enter the Sacred Spring Mountain.
Stepping into a horse stance, he tightened his muscles and coordinated all the muscles in his body to the extreme.
The larger the body of a living being, the harder it was to coordinate all the strength in his body. However, the Mountain Crushing Clan members had an extraordinary talent in this aspect.
"Ha!"
Suo Tuo suddenly roared and punched out.
Even if there wasn't much hope, he still tried his best. They were a clan that believed in power. They would do everything they could to fight for their own glory.
"Bang!"
A muffled sound echoed.
The huge golden square stone didn't move an inch. The Thunder Toad, who was sitting on top of the golden square stone, looked indifferent. "Suo Tuo, out!"
Suo Tuo's face darkened. He lowered his head and walked to the side. His family members were comforting him. As a member of the Mountain Crushing Clan, he would have a chance to grow with time.
"Ka Lan!"
The Thunder Toad called out another name.
This Mountain Crushing Clan member called Ka Lan was very lucky. The place he was at was the place Luo Zheng had passed by earlier.
That pool of source of power had directly poured down. However, he had absorbed a lot of it.
The power in his body was also in a state of saturation. When he heard the Thunder Toad call out his name, he jumped out. "Ka Lan is here!"
The Thunder Toad pursed his lips, signaling Ka Lan to show his power.
Ka Lan strode to the front of the huge golden stone. After cupping his hands at the Thunder Toad, he revealed a confident expression. "I want to challenge the god power!"
As soon as he said that, the surrounding people started to discuss.
"Such big words. He hasn't used god power for thirteen years already right?"
"It's different this time. In the past, it would be good if he could get a few dozen drops or even a few hundred drops. This time, many people ate until they were full!"
"That's possible. However, the bodies of the younger generation have a limit. It won't be easy to get the title of god power."
Ka Lan clenched his fingers. A crisp sound like firecrackers could be heard. His entire aura suddenly changed.
Using his entire body's power, a formless energy spread out. His posture gave people an indescribable feeling. It was a contradictory beauty. Both heavy and light were displayed on Ka Lan's body at the same time!
"Retreat … for me!"
"Sou!"
This fist suddenly struck out. The moment it hit the golden square stone, it let out a rumbling sound!
"Peng!"
This shocking power exploded out, spreading out a powerful shockwave!
"Eldest Princess!" He Zuowei was secretly shocked. He quickly blocked in front of the Eldest Princess.
Even if He Zuowei and the rest were sector lords, they were still wary of this power. The Mountain Shattering race was originally a monster race!
If it wasn't for the mission, He Zuowei and the rest would be very willing to enter this training ground to train.
"Hua …"
Under this fist, the golden square stone finally started to move. It slowly slid backwards!
Such power could only make the golden square stone slowly slide. One could imagine how heavy it was …
Two inches …
Five inches …
One inch …
Two inches …
Three inches …
Three and a half inches!
This golden square stone slid for three and a half inches before finally stopping.
Two inches was extraordinary. One inch was valiant. Five inches was god power.
"Three and a half inches. Hehe, this Ka Lan is still powerful!"
"What a pity … if only it went another two inches."
"What a joke. Actually, at two inches, the power would be exhausted. Do you really think that the bottom of the stone is oiled and can slide over by itself?"
Many people felt that it was a pity.
The gap between valiant and god power was too big. To obtain this title was still rather difficult.
"Three and a half inches, valiant, tiger hunt!" Lei Toad said loudly. Someone immediately recorded Ka Lan's result.
If this result was in the past, it would be considered amazing. However, Ka Lan's face was still a little resentful. Although valiant and god power were only one level apart, the gap was still huge. His power was still lacking and he didn't obtain this honor.
The Thunder Toad flipped over and gently kicked the golden stone back to its original position.
He couldn't use any force in the air. He could only rely on his own strength to push the golden square stone back three and a half inches. This was what it meant to lift something heavy as though it was light!
Some of the other race warriors' eyes lit up. However, the Mountain Shattering race members weren't surprised by this scene. If one didn't have such power, how could one become the leader of the Mountain Shattering race?
After Lei Toad flipped and sat properly, he shouted again, "Third, Ma Xi!"
The third person pushed the golden square stone four inches away, only to receive an extraordinary title.

Chapter 1503
Just as everyone had predicted, the Mountain Crumbling Clan's performance was very eye-catching this time.
In the past, only one out of three people would reach the Extraordinary level. However, this year, except for a few people, almost everyone could reach the Extraordinary level.
Not only that, but there were also several "Valiant Warriors".
However, there was only one person who received the title of "Divine Strength". That was a Mountain Crumbling Clan member named "Lin Meng".
Compared to the other Mountain Crumbling Clan members, this Lin Meng's body was indeed one size bigger. The energy source he absorbed was also much more than the other Mountain Crumbling Clan members. Naturally, the energy he released was much stronger.
When he punched down, even the golden square stone shook and retreated five feet and three inches!
The Mountain Crumbling Clan had not produced a "Divine Strength" for many years. After Lei Toad announced it, the entire Mountain Crumbling Clan was in an uproar.
Generally speaking, those who had the title of "Divine Strength" were very likely to become the leader of the Mountain Crumbling Clan in the future. In this way, although this test was very simple, it was very valuable …
After the Mountain Crumbling Clan finished the test, it was the foreign warriors' turn next.
Although there was a "Divine Strength" this year, Lei Toad knew that the Mountain Crumbling Clan members would not be able to compete with the foreign warriors in this test.
In terms of pure strength, the Mountain Crumbling Clan did have a great advantage. Their physical bodies were naturally much stronger than the foreign warriors.
However, there was a very fatal problem. The foreign warriors' body refining techniques were very scientific. Their physical bodies might not be as strong as the Mountain Crumbling Clan members, but they could activate their Dao Pillars. Especially after the eight-level Dao Pillars, they could carry much more energy source than the foreign warriors!
In the past, there were too many monks and too little porridge. The amount of energy source they could absorb was limited. However, the Mountain Crumbling Clan members were one step ahead. Naturally, they could absorb much more energy source than the foreign warriors.
This year, there were internal problems in the Sacred Spring Mountain. The Mountain Crumbling Clan members were not the only ones who were full. The foreign warriors in front of them were not bad either.
And these foreign warriors could use their techniques to maximize their energy source. Therefore, it was not surprising that they surpassed the Mountain Crumbling Clan.
"Yao An!"
Lei Toad called out the name of the first foreign warrior.
The one who walked out was a skinny old man. This old man was less than four feet tall and short. Especially when he stood in front of the Mountain Crumbling Clan members, he looked like a dwarf.
The hunchbacked man's mustache curled up slightly as he walked over and nodded at the Thunder Toad.
After which, Yao An extended his chicken claw-like hand and gently rubbed the surface of the golden square stone. Then, he knocked his thumb on it.
It was as if the object in front of him was not an unimaginably heavy stone, but an exquisite antique.
"Hehe, Golden Fork Strange Stone. This stone is quite valuable. Even in the Divine Realm, it can be auctioned off for a good price. I wonder if Chief Lightning has any intention of selling it?" Yao An asked faintly.
"Cut the crap. Once the test is over, scram!" Lei Toad replied coldly.
The huge silver rocks that were laid out in the Mountain Crumbling Clan were known as Silver Gang Stones. This kind of stone material was already considered a rare item.
Once, after some foreign warriors entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, they thought of ways to steal the Silver Gang Stones from the Mountain Crumbling Clan. Although the Silver Gang Stones were extremely sturdy and hard to destroy, under the drive of greed, they took the Sky Boric Acid and melted it piece by piece before transporting it away piece by piece. During that period of time, quite a number of Silver Gang Stones were stolen.
This action angered the Mountain Crumbling Clan members. During that period of time, they killed quite a number of foreign warriors. Moreover, this action was condemned by the other foreign warriors. Later on, fewer and fewer people dared to have this idea.
Other than the Silver Gang Stones, the Mountain Crumbling Clan also had a small number of Golden Fork Strange Stones. The one under Lei Toad's feet was the biggest one.
This Golden Fork Strange Stone was many times heavier than the Silver Gang Stones. Hence, it was even more rare and precious.
However, this kind of treasure was not something an ordinary warrior could take away. Even if it was placed in the top grade Sumeru Ring, it would instantly crush the space.
When some foreign warriors saw this stone, they would eventually have ideas and would be willing to pay a high price to buy it. Lei Toad did not know how many times he had rejected them. Now that he heard the old man speak, why would he care?
Lei Toad's tone was unfriendly. However, Yao An did not mind. He only chuckled and gently took a step back.
With a light shake of his body, his clothes turned to dust and dissipated.
A part of his meridians began to turn dark. As they intertwined, they formed a dragon-shaped pattern. This dragon's shape was a little strange. The dragon's body went around the old man's back and spiraled up his chest. The dragon's head gathered on his face!
"Evil Dragon Art." Lei Toad's eyelids twitched.
Although the Mountain Crumbling Clan members never cultivated foreign martial arts, it did not mean that they were ignorant.
Han Liusu sat on a chair and noticed the cultivation method on the old man's body. She sneered, "It should be Feng Zhu's people."
He Zuowei nodded, "Yes, it's Feng Zhu's people. They have recently sent many people down."
"It's useless." Han Liusu shook her head.
He Zuowei agreed, "Wherever the Saint Emperor's vision reaches, it is destined that they will not be able to escape. This kind of struggle is indeed useless."
When the dragon was fully formed, a red dot suddenly appeared on his cheek. It was exactly where the dragon's eyes were. Looking at it, it was as if this person had an eye on his cheek!
At this moment, an extremely ferocious aura suddenly burst out from the old man's body!
Then, he used a finger to knock heavily on the golden square stone in front of him.
"Pa!"
This sound seemed to be the sound of the old man's finger bones breaking. It was quite crisp.
He only used a finger to knock, and he was about to move such a large golden square stone …
"Heh, this old man's harvest this time is not bad. Using seventy-two meridians to create the Evil Dragon Dao Protection, and using the power source to create the Evil Dragon Root. The improvement is too obvious!"
"Lower your voice. Feng Zhu's people are extremely eccentric. It's best not to provoke them!"
"But can he really move this stone with a single finger?"
Just as everyone was discussing, a violent vibration suddenly came from the golden square stone.
This golden square stone began to slide backward!
"Rumble …"
The speed of the slide was also extremely fast. In an instant, it had moved a foot away!
Next, it was three feet, then five feet!
"Rumble …"
In the end, it slowly came to a stop at eight feet and three inches.
"God Power!"
"The first foreign martial artist gave birth to God Power!"
"This time, the foreign martial artists have struck it rich!"
The faces of the Mountain Crushing Tribe's people twitched and their faces turned ugly.
A race ultimately hoped for their own people to be strong. Actually, the races of the Six Great Sacred Grounds naturally had an instinctive resistance against foreign martial artists. Who would be willing to let the fertile water flow into the fields of an outsider?
This was equivalent to a sect establishing a sect. They had painstakingly built the sect, but it was all for the martial artists outside the sect, giving a portion of their resources to the outsiders.
Such a thankless task was no different from being an idiot.
But this was the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. These rules were all set by the clan's prophet. Even Lei Toad was powerless to change them. But it still gave the clan the greatest advantage …
That old man stretched out his hand and rubbed his finger. His face was slightly proud. He nodded slightly to the surrounding martial artists before retreating.
Lei Toad's face was still indifferent. He called out the next foreign martial artist's name.
"Liu Fang …"
The foreign martial artists started to take the test one after another.
It was similar to what Lei Toad had expected. These foreign martial artists were able to unleash the full potential of their power source. They were directly one level higher than the Mountain Crushing Tribe's people.
Most of the martial artists were able to push the golden square stone one foot away and obtain the title of Valiant. A portion of them were able to push the golden square stone five feet away and obtain the title of God Power.
The title of "God Power" was relatively rare. Lei Toad only had God Power back then!
But this year, two or three foreign martial artists had appeared consecutively. After this test, there were already more than ten of them. And one of them had even pushed the golden square stone nine feet and three inches away.
Another seven inches and that would be the title of Barbarian King!

Chapter 1504
Han Cangyan had also obtained a considerable amount of energy source under a fortuitous encounter.
More importantly, the technique that Han Cangyan cultivated was extremely special. He could send out all the energy in his body in one punch!
After this punch, Han Cangyan's entire body was drained of energy. He fell to the ground, no longer having the slightest bit of strength. And that golden square stone was pushed forty feet away …
In the history of the Collapsing Mountain Clan, this distance could be ranked in the top ten!
After Han Cangyan obtained the title of Barbarian King, he did not choose to leave the Collapsing Mountain Clan. Instead, he chose to continue training in the clan.
During a hunt, the Collapsing Mountain Clan's hunting team met with danger. After the leader fell, Han Cangyan led the clan to escape, making a great contribution. In addition, he obtained the title of Barbarian King. Hence, it was only logical that he became the leader of the Collapsing Mountain Clan. Until he left the Collapsing Mountain Clan, Han Cangyan had always been leading the Collapsing Mountain Clan.
It was not such an easy thing for an outsider to become the leader.
Many Collapsing Mountain Clan members were not willing to be controlled by outsiders. Even if Han Cangyan had helped the Collapsing Mountain Clan, after he became the leader, there were still many clansmen who disliked him.
After all, in the eyes of the Collapsing Mountain Clan, these outsiders were ultimately inferior existences. Even though many outsiders came from the Divine Domain.
Hence, the Collapsing Mountain Clan did not wish for a Barbarian King to be born here. After all, the appearance of a Barbarian King was very likely to lead to another outsider leader.
Luckily, the title of "Divine Power" had appeared many times. But ultimately, there had never been a Barbarian King!
Lei Toad's large hand flipped open a page of the register. His eyes flashed slightly, and then he shouted, "Luo Zheng!"
Once this name popped out, everyone's gaze instantly fell on Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng had been waiting in the crowd for a long time.
Previously, when Han Liusu brought Luo Zheng into the Collapsing Mountain Clan, it had attracted many people's attention.
In their eyes, Luo Zheng was just a second-class living being without any reputation. Naturally, he was not worthy of their attention.
But that Han Liusu's reputation was not small.
Many outsiders had noticed Han Liusu. At the same time, they wondered why this Han Jia's daughter would come to the Collapsing Mountain Clan …
Later, it became clear that this woman had brought Luo Zheng in. As for what her real purpose was, no one knew.
However, a portion of the people knew about the Deviant Fire that had appeared on Han Jia's daughter's body. The Deviant Fire on Han Jia's daughter's body was difficult to detect even in the Divine Domain. It was an unknown Deviant Fire that could burn almost everything.
The Han Family's Saint Emperor also placed great importance on this Deviant Flame, which was why he valued his daughter so much.
The problem was that Luo Zheng wasn't afraid of this strange flame …
Compared to the members of the Collapsing Mountain Clan, the ones who were the most shocked were the foreign martial artists from the Realm of the Gods. As insiders, they were naturally shocked to see Luo Zheng lying on the ground perfectly fine despite being covered in Deviant Fire.
As for the Mountain Shattering clansmen throwing Luo Zheng into the bonfire and barbecuing him, those foreign martial artists were secretly laughing in their hearts.
Although the mysterious flame from the Wood of Crossing Misfortune couldn't be underestimated, in the eyes of those from the Realm of the Gods, it paled in comparison. He wasn't even afraid of Han Jia's daughter's Deviant Fire, so why would he be afraid of your bonfire?
After that, Luo Zheng received the Collapsing Mountain Clan's courteous reception. A portion of the foreign martial artists saw this, but those who were in the know knew that Luo Zheng must have some great magical ability. There were even some who used secret techniques to secretly pass this information back.
Luo Zheng didn't belong to the Realm of the Gods, so he knew nothing about the situation in the Realm of the Gods. Even if there were undercurrents within the Collapsing Mountain Clan, and even if the foreign martial artists were secretly competing with each other, he didn't feel anything …
As for Han Liusu, she definitely knew. However, with her status, the fellows within the Collapsing Mountain Clan weren't worthy of her attention.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's appearance naturally attracted a lot of attention!
Lei Toad was very much looking forward to Luo Zheng's performance. He hoped that Luo Zheng would also be able to obtain the title of 'Divine Strength'. If Luo Zheng's strength could really reach this stage, then he would be able to bring Luo Zheng out soon!
But Lei Toad was still a bit worried.
Luo Zheng was only at the Extreme Divine Realm. He was clear that cultivation didn't represent a martial artist's strength. But, the higher one's cultivation, the greater the amount of energy they could carry.
Almost all the martial artists that entered the Collapsing Mountain Clan were at the Divine Transformation Realm. There were even quite a few at the peak of the Divine Transformation Realm, with the strength of a half-step World Lord.
This Luo Zheng's Extreme Divine Realm cultivation was ultimately a restriction to him.
"Are you ready?" Lei Toad revealed a kind smile towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng nodded, "Mm, I'm ready!"
He had a good impression of Lei Toad. Although this person was a bit rough, he was sincere.
Since Lei Toad had a favor to ask of him, Lei Toad would give him special treatment. Luo Zheng couldn't not accept this kindness!
"Are you sure you can obtain the title of 'Divine Strength'?" Lei Toad asked again.
Luo Zheng blinked, but shook his head and smiled, "I'm not sure."
Not far away, Lie Yu's eyes suddenly widened …
Lie Yu was a bit unhappy.
This time, Lie Yu also played a part in discovering the upside-down black and white ball!
If it weren't for him and Luo Zheng, how could these people obtain so much energy? Dream on!
However, after jumping into the lake of energy, the amount he could absorb was limited. He was no different from a martial artist who obtained an entire lake of energy …
The result of the test was that he only obtained the title of 'Wu Yong'.
In fact, this result had long exceeded Lie Yu's expectations. Before entering the Sacred Spring Mountain, his ultimate goal was to become 'extraordinary'. But now, the premise was different. Even if he obtained the title of 'Wu Yong', he was still disappointed.
But the scene of Luo Zheng absorbing half of the lake's energy was still vivid in his mind.
Until now, he still couldn't figure out where Luo Zheng had stored the energy. It couldn't have been absorbed by himself.
"I'm not sure …" After glaring at Luo Zheng, Lie Yu's face revealed a look of disdain. This guy didn't even blush when he lied. After all, Luo Zheng had shown a power that far exceeded Lie Yu's when he pushed the stone door open.
Lei Toan's expression didn't change much when Luo Zheng answered. After half a year of contact, Lei Toan had a general understanding of Luo Zheng's character. Then, he waved his hand, "Please."
Because Lei Toan had asked so many questions, the other martial artists immediately began to discuss and speculate about Luo Zheng's strength.
"How many feet can this guy push?"
"He has supernatural powers. His body must be special. If he also absorbs enough energy, I think five feet shouldn't be a problem. It won't be a problem for him to obtain the title of 'Divine Power'!"
"Humph, he's just not afraid of fire. This doesn't mean he has other talents. Maybe he can't even reach the 'extraordinary' level."
"Can't even reach the 'extraordinary' level? Aren't you underestimating him too much? "
"What's the big deal? What if he really doesn't move? Don't forget that Luo Zheng is merely at the Ultimate God Stage. He's also a second-level living being! "
Everyone was talking at once. There were all kinds of opinions.
Seeing that the foreign martial artists were discussing fervently, the people of the Collapsing Mountain Clan also joined in …
Compared to the opinions of the foreign martial artists, the people of the Collapsing Mountain Clan were more simple-minded. Naturally, they didn't think highly of Luo Zheng.
"Look at this guy's body. It's not even as thick as my arm! It's not a big deal if he can push it one or two inches! "
"Most likely!"
"But this guy is still useful to our Collapsing Mountain Clan. I hope he performs well!"
Beside them, Lie Yu's lips couldn't help but tremble when he heard these words.
This guy might even obtain the title of 'Barbarian King'!
But because the opinions of his friends and fellow clansmen were too different from his own, it was better for him not to say anything.
"He Zuowei, what do you think?" Han Liusu stared at Luo Zheng and said.
"Princess Royal, it's hard to say." He Zuowei shook his head.
Han Liusu blinked her beautiful eyes. "What do you mean?"
Because of Han Liusu, He Zuowei and the others were also observing Luo Zheng.
Seeing Luo Zheng's rapid advancement, they couldn't figure out why. They were also confused as to why Luo Zheng could withstand the burning of the 'Strange Flame'!
The Princess Royal's flame was simply unstoppable. Only true experts could use some methods to extinguish it and temporarily suppress it. But that didn't mean they weren't afraid of the 'Strange Flame' …
But this kind of talent didn't determine the strength of Luo Zheng. He Zuowei naturally replied, "It's hard to say."
Han Liusu also didn't really want an answer, but her beautiful face was filled with anticipation. She didn't expect Luo Zheng to be able to surpass her elder brother, but at the very least, he should be able to obtain the title of Divine Force, right?
But at this moment, there was no point in discussing. Because Luo Zheng was already prepared to attack.
Luo Zheng was like an iron tower, standing in front of the golden square stone.

Chapter 1505
In the past, when Luo Zheng punched, there were two stages when he used his power.
The first stage was to use his own power, and the second stage was to use the power of the dragon scales.
Now, the situation was more complicated. Luo Zheng's physical body could contain much more power source than normal martial artists, and even more than members of the Mountain Shattering Tribe.
Moreover, he also had eight dao platforms.
Outside, why were human martial artists stronger than members of the Mountain Shattering Tribe? Because in their conjoined body techniques, they either opened their dao platforms or expanded their meridians. In short, they could contain extra power source!
So, Luo Zheng not only had the power of his physical body, but also the power of the dao platforms.
These two combined were far more powerful than normal martial artists, but Luo Zheng's true power was the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier.
Those nine stars were like suns, floating silently in the sky, emitting a dazzling light that illuminated Luo Zheng's inner world.
Compared to the Nine Stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier, the Source Power absorbed by Luo Zheng's physical body and Dao Pedestal Tier could be said to be negligible. They were not on the same level at all. The Source Power contained within those nine gigantic stars was probably several hundred times more than the other two!
At this moment, Luo Zheng first activated the power in his physical body.
In the past, Luo Zheng often borrowed the power of the dragon scales, and was very familiar with borrowing the power of dao platforms and inner world. The only difference was that this power did not come from external objects, but from himself!
"Hu …"
Just as Luo Zheng's muscles tensed up, the power in his physical body and eight dao platforms was completely activated!
He was like a large stone thrown into water. Because the stone's mass was too large, it could abruptly push away the water around the stone, forming a powerful flow of water that spread out.
Now, Luo Zheng was this large stone, and the air around him was equivalent to water. Because of the sudden increase in power, the air around him was abruptly pushed away, forming a spiral of wind that suddenly rushed towards him!
"Hualala …"
In this square, everyone's hair began to flutter.
"This power …"
Thunder Toad suddenly stood up, looking down at Luo Zheng.
He sat on that golden square stone, closest to Luo Zheng, and was the most sensitive to the power that Luo Zheng emitted.
Moreover, as the leader of the Mountain Shattering Tribe, he had an extraordinary perception of power, and he felt the surging power in Luo Zheng's body!
"This power is so strong, as if there are tens of thousands of powerful strange beasts living in this brat's body!"
Looking down at Luo Zheng, Thunder Toad actually felt a kind of fear, as if he should not stand on this strange golden stone, because it was very dangerous here.
However, the Thunder Toad quickly rejected this thought that came from its body's instinct. It was too absurd.
Ever since Luo Zheng entered Mountain Shattering Tribe, he had always been taken care of Thunder Toad.
Luo Zheng Luo Zheng Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng Luo's Luo, Luo Zheng, Luo, Luo, Luo Zheng, Luo, Luo Zheng Zheng...
Luo Zheng could be considered outstanding, but he was definitely not at the top. Of course, that was disregarding Luo Zheng's innate talent of not fearing fire.
After entering Sacred Spring Mountain, Luo Zheng had indeed grown a lot, but he had been given too much power source, and this little fellow was unable to absorb it all. Being able to absorb twenty to thirty thousand drops of power source was already quite shocking.
Thunder Toad would never have thought that the amount of power source that Luo Zheng had absorbed could not be measured with drops. He had absorbed almost half a lake …
"Eldest Princess, I can make a judgment now." He Zuowei stared at Luo Zheng in a daze.
Han Liusu's eyes shone with a strange light, "I don't need you to say it anymore. Hehe, do you believe me now?"
"You mean the Soul Suppressing Cliff?" He Zuowei's expression froze.
Han Liusu nodded, her face filled with gratification. Thinking about how she had to lie on the ground motionlessly for this period of time, it was worth it.
"It's still impossible." He Zuowei shook his head, "There are ways to enter the Soul Suppressing Cliff, but it cannot be broken through with strength. At least, the martial artists of the Forbidden Land of God cannot do it. Even your brother could not do it in the past."
"My brother indeed could not do it, but what if Luo Zheng surpasses my brother?" Han Liusu asked again.
"… …"
He Zuowei could not answer, because he could feel that Luo Zheng's power was growing stronger and stronger. It was so strong that it was outrageous.
The crowd that had been discussing incessantly also completely quieted down at this time.
Even though Luo Zheng had not punched out, everyone already had an answer in their hearts. This fellow's strength probably far surpassed all the martial artists they had met before!
Especially that little old man called Yao An. His pair of soybean-sized eyes shone brightly, as if he had discovered a new treasure.
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng's punch was extremely slow.
He could freely use the power of his body and dao platform, but it was the first time Luo Zheng had used the inner world to draw out the power contained in the nine stars.
In the past, Luo Zheng had only drawn out true essence from his inner world, and then it was chaotic energy. But this time, it was an extremely strong power. He naturally had to be careful. In any case, this test did not have a time limit!
Thin ribbons of light rolled out from the nine stars and gathered above.
"One twentieth … …"
After several breaths of time, Luo Zheng had only drawn out one twentieth of the power.
Although it was only one twentieth, this power was already so strong that Luo Zheng could not imagine it. He did not dare to continue!
What surprised Luo Zheng was that the way the power of the nine stars moved was very unique. This power did not move through Luo Zheng's meridians, muscles, and bones. This power seemed to come directly from a distant space, and was directly controlled by Luo Zheng.
"If I directly condensed this power into a fist, wouldn't I be able to make it appear anywhere in my body?"
What Luo Zheng did not know was that this was actually a method of the Saints!
In the Divine Realm, this method was known as a divine ability!
For martial artists below the Saint level, including many True Gods, their inner worlds truly existed within their own bodies … …
But the inner worlds of the Saints were actually placed in the periphery of the Divine Realm. It was like a large ship suddenly had many small boats hanging from it, and each small boat was an inner world.
But no matter how far away a Saint was from the universe, if a Saint wanted to draw out the power of the universe, he only needed a thought to draw it out.
This seemed like a type of spatial law, but it was an existence that surpassed spatial law. This was because there was always distance in space. And while there seemed to be a distance between a Saint and the universe they created, in reality, there was no distance at all. This was why some True Gods called it a divine ability.
Luo Zheng's inner world was different from that of other Saints. His inner world was born somewhere in the primal chaos, and there was still a considerable distance between it and the Divine Realm.
When he drew out the power now, the power of the inner world would appear wherever Luo Zheng wanted it to appear according to his will.
This was something Luo Zheng discovered by himself, which was why he felt pleasantly surprised.
One twentieth of the power of the Spiritual Pedestal Nine Stars was gathered by Luo Zheng half an inch in front of his fist, turning into an invisible "fist glove" that wrapped around his right arm.
Thus, at this moment, everyone felt that all the might came from the fist, and even thought that Luo Zheng had cultivated some kind of special fist art that gathered all the power at the front of his fist.
Even those foreign martial artists from the Divine Realm could not see the reason. How could they imagine that Luo Zheng could display a Saint's divine ability that transcended the world?
After a short moment, Luo Zheng's fist finally landed right in front of the strange gold fork stone!
When this unimaginably huge power came into contact with the strange gold fork stone, it produced a fierce vibration!
Previously, this strange gold fork stone had been struck by numerous fists, but no matter if it was extraordinary, martial, or the title of Divine Power, no matter what kind of Body Refining Art they used, the power that erupted from it did not leave half a fist mark on it. The surface of the strange gold fork stone was still as smooth as before.
However, Luo Zheng's fist did not land on it. When it was still half an inch away from the strange gold fork stone, the invisible "fist glove" had already squeezed the strange gold fork stone, forming a clearly visible fist mark.
And as Luo Zheng's fist continued to invade, this fist mark continued to sink in.

Chapter 1506
As the surface of this strange golden-forked stone continued to cave in, a huge kinetic energy began to spread over the entire stone!
"Pop!"
With a muffled sound, the gloves Luo Zheng formed with the power of nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier finally exploded!
And at this instant, Luo Zheng also abruptly pulled back his right hand …
Luo Zheng still wasn't able to control such a huge power. Power was mutual in the end, and when his fist smashed onto this strange golden-forked stone, there would be a huge rebounding force.
However, this rebounding force wasn't attached to Luo Zheng's body. After all, there was still half an inch of distance between that "Power Gloves" and Luo Zheng's fist!
If Luo Zheng directly gathered this power in his hand, under such a rebounding force, one of his arms would directly shatter!
Quickly withdrawing his hand, Luo Zheng's body only swayed slightly, and he couldn't help but exhale slightly. This power was terrifyingly powerful, and it was also not easy for him to control it. If he was slightly careless, he'd be playing with fire!
After Luo Zheng's power spread over the entire stone, Lei Toad's expression suddenly changed, and he instantly felt that he couldn't stand steadily!
His reaction was also extremely fast. At this moment, with a sudden burst of strength, he somersaulted in the air and jumped straight down from the strange golden-forked stone, standing firmly on the ground.
And the moment he stood on the ground, he saw this strange golden-forked stone shoot forward at an extremely fast speed!
"Hu …"
This strange golden-forked stone was extremely heavy, and under its high speed, it pushed away the surrounding air with all its might, setting off fist-sized cyclones that continuously spiraled in the surroundings.
Even the surface of the plaza paved with Silver Star Stone was deeply indented by this strange golden-forked stone!
One could imagine the power Luo Zheng displayed within!
One foot …
Ten feet …
Twenty feet …
Thirty feet …
Forty feet …
Fifty feet!
"Hong!"
Ten inches was one foot, and ten feet was one zhang.
Fifty feet away from this strange golden forked rock, there was a building of the Mountain Crumbling Race. That building was also constructed with Silver Astral Stones, and it was built to store all kinds of beast bones and beast blood. Almost all the blood troughs in the plaza were gathered here!
The golden-forked strange stone continued to push forward with a strong inertia, directly slamming into the building!
"Boom …"
The entire flying structure made of Silver Gang Stone collapsed with a loud rumble, completely covering the strange golden stone.
Luo Zheng stood in place. Seeing the destruction caused by his punch, he was also a little stunned.
He immediately cursed in his heart. He had used too much power …
It was his first time borrowing the power of nine Stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier, so Luo Zheng wasn't able to control the amount of power very well. Or rather, he wasn't clear on how great the power contained within these nine Stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier was.
One twentieth … in Luo Zheng's eyes, it wasn't much, but even he himself had never thought that it would have such a powerful explosive power.
More importantly, the power of Luo Zheng's punch was slightly inclined downwards, so the friction between the strange golden stone and the Silver Gang Stone on the ground was relatively great. If he had punched upwards, he might have been able to send this strange golden stone flying …
This scene shocked even Luo Zheng himself, and one could imagine the expressions of the bystanders.
His path was blocked by that building, and he only slid out fifty feet. If there wasn't this building, how much could he have slid out?
Three hundred feet? Two hundred feet?
It was no longer important how much he slid out.
The Mountain Crumbling Race used this strange golden stone to test their power as a tradition that had continued for many years.
Among them, the best record was thirty-one feet.
Of course, this record was for martial artists who stepped into the Sacred Spring Mountain for the first time.
Almost all the adult hunters of the Mountain Crumbling Race could push this strange golden stone a distance of thirty to forty feet. As for a top-notch existence like Thunder Toad, with full power, he could also push out Luo Zheng's result.
But Luo Zheng had only been here for half a year. How could he compare to Thunder Toad and the others?
When this guy came in, he was a foreign martial artist who couldn't even crawl up …
Looking at the collapsed building, everyone in the plaza fell silent.
Han Liusu smiled brightly, "I said, I believe that flower seller."
He Zuowei swallowed, "This power isn't enough to break through the Subduing Soul Cliff …"
"Of course I know, but is Luo Zheng really using all his power? Did you see him retreat? "Han Liusu stared at He Zuowei and asked.
This was a question of common sense. Many of the martial artists present had overlooked it, but people like Thunder Toad and Han Liusu had noticed it.
The destruction caused by the strange golden stone might be very attractive, but they were more concerned about Luo Zheng's actions! This guy didn't retreat!
The effect of power was mutual. When you punched someone, there would be a rebounding force in your body.
If a body refinement martial artist punched downwards, under the sudden explosion of power, he would fly into the sky!
According to common sense, when Luo Zheng punched out, his own mass was countless times smaller than the strange golden stone. Under the rebounding force, he would probably fly out.
But Luo Zheng actually stabilized his body and only swayed in place!
They subconsciously thought that Luo Zheng still had some power left, so he could stand firmly in place!
But thinking carefully, it was still wrong.
Even if Luo Zheng had enough power left to resist the rebounding force from the strange golden stone, his body shouldn't be able to withstand it.
Taking a step back, Luo Zheng's body was terrifyingly strong. Then he should have left two deep footprints on the ground paved with Silver Gang Stone, or directly crushed the ground.
The problem was that none of this existed.
The rebounding force seemed to have disappeared. Luo Zheng just stood there unreasonably. The others couldn't solve the doubts in their hearts. At the same time, they had to face the reality of the scene in front of them!
"This … this subordinate also doesn't know why Luo Zheng didn't retreat." He Zuowei's mind was also in a mess. He really couldn't understand this.
"Then don't say that Luo Zheng doesn't have the ability." Han Liusu's mouth twitched. Her small face revealed a proud expression.
He Zuowei was helpless. He turned around to look at the few people behind him. The few sector lords were also helpless. They were like He Zuowei. Other than being shocked, they were even more confused and didn't understand.
"How did you do it?" Lei Toad didn't hide anything and directly asked Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's performance had far exceeded Lei Toad's expectations. Lei Toad even felt threatened. He had hoped that Luo Zheng could grow as much as possible, especially to obtain as much power source as possible in Sacred Spring Mountain. This would allow his strength to increase rapidly.
But this wasn't the right way to improve. This speed was completely beyond his imagination. Even if Lei Toad's personality was relatively calm, he still couldn't accept it.
"You're asking if I pushed this rock out?" Luo Zheng was also vexed. He could only think of a new excuse to explain his strength.
He didn't expect that Lei Toad wasn't concerned about this. He shook his head. "No, I want to know how you didn't retreat …"
"I … didn't retreat?" Luo Zheng was startled. He cursed inwardly again.
To avoid the rebounding force from the power gauntlet, he withdrew his fist in time. Thus, Luo Zheng didn't come into direct contact with this rock, so the rebounding force was greatly reduced.
In this way, his performance was a little too calm.
If he had known, Luo Zheng would have frantically retreated. It would be best if he flew a few hundred meters before stopping. That would be considered normal …
"I cultivated a Mystical Power that can transfer power out of my body, so I didn't retreat," Luo Zheng answered.
He wasn't lying. He had indeed transferred the power of nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier and transformed it into a formless gauntlet.
Lei Toad didn't believe Luo Zheng's words at all. He only said, "There are new talents in every generation, I'm too surprised. However …" That originally serious expression suddenly became kind. He smiled at Luo Zheng and said, "Still, congratulations to you for breaking this record and obtaining the title of Barbarian King! You can join the Divine Hunters! "
The requirement for the title of "Barbarian King" was only a distance of thirty meters. Luo Zheng had pushed out more than forty meters. Unfortunately, the Collapsing Mountain Race only had these four titles. There wasn't a title of a higher level.
Right at this moment, two children of the Collapsing Mountain Race walked over quickly. After bowing to Lei Toad, one of the children said, "Come with me, the prophet wants to meet you."
"Prophet? Where is he? "
Luo Zheng only knew that the Collapsing Mountain Race seemed to have a prophet after entering the Sacred Spring Mountain. However, he'd never seen him in the half year he'd been here.
