Chapter 1507
After Luo Zheng left, the whole square began to boil.
Among them, the most enthusiastic was probably the Mountain Crushing Race.
Those foreign martial artists, on the other hand, remained silent …
The origins of the foreign martial artists were very mixed, coming from various large universes and from the divine realm. Although they had paid attention to Luo Zheng before, most of them were envious of the special treatment Luo Zheng received from the Mountain Crushing Race.
Of course, this kind of treatment was not worth mentioning in the eyes of the divine realm's martial artists.
The only goal of the martial artists from the divine realm was the source of power. As for the Mountain Crushing Race's method of body refinement through the resonance of battle songs and devouring the flesh and blood of various rare beasts, these things were not very attractive to them. No matter how good the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was, it was still built by a Saint, so how could it compare to the divine realm?
The divine realm was the real world! So, the living beings born in the divine realm had the right to call the people of the universe second-class living beings.
But now, the expressions of these people from the divine realm had all changed!
They could not think of the reason why Luo Zheng suddenly became strong.
Was it because of the Mountain Crushing Race's treatment?
Was it because of the strong wine that the Thunder Toad bestowed Luo Zheng? Was it because he devoured the internal organs of those strange beasts? Even with their toes, they could get the answer "impossible".
"Tsk tsk, the key problem is still the source of power," the little old man called Yao An said in a low voice.
Beside Yao An was a martial artist whose body was completely black. This martial artist had also received the title of "God Power". Hearing the little old man's words, he said coldly, "He's only in the God Tier, how much source of power can he devour?"
Yao An frowned. "What if he has opened nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier?"
"Heng, is that possible? Even if he has opened nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier, the source of power he can absorb is still limited, at most twice as much. This is already the limit. In addition to the eighth layer of the Dao Pedestal Tier, and the source of power his physical body can contain is still limited, "the dark martial artist said.
"I'm confused on this point." Yao An shook his head, a malicious look flashing in his eyes. If there was a chance, could he capture this brat and interrogate him alone?
However, this kind of opportunity was not easy to seize. This was the Mountain Crushing Race's territory, and the Mountain Crushing Race still needed Luo Zheng's help, so how could they let a foreign martial artist succeed so easily?
If the Mountain Collapsing Clan got rid of Luo Zheng, there would be a chance …
Yao An had originally planned to leave after taking the test. While the subsequent cultivation of the Collapsing Mountain Race might be important, as a member of Feng Zhu, he had a better choice.
Luo Zheng's action had undoubtedly created a new record.
The key point was that they couldn't see the end point of Luo Zheng's record. If it wasn't blocked by that building, how far could Luo Zheng push this strange golden fork stone?
Eight hundred feet?
A thousand feet?
Or even two thousand feet?
This was destined to be an unprecedented record that would never be broken in the future.
"Alright! Next! " Lei Toad waved his hand and announced amidst the clamor. The shocked expression on his face gradually disappeared.
Luo Zheng's performance had already made Lei Toad feel relieved. His previous worries had completely dissipated. Perhaps Luo Zheng was sent by the heavens to help their Mountain Crushing Tribe. Now that Luo Zheng had gone to meet the Prophet, everything would depend on the Prophet's instructions.
Under the guidance of the Mountain Crushing Tribe's child, Luo Zheng bypassed one rough building after another.
The Mountain Crushing Tribe's building didn't have any special features. It was just casually built with the Silver Gang Stone. The living area here was forbidden for foreign martial artists. However, with just a casual glance, Luo Zheng knew that the Mountain Crushing Tribe's cultural inheritance was relatively weak. They probably didn't have any entertainment activities. It was no wonder that the Mountain Crushing Tribe's child would be easily attracted by foreign martial artists.
Walking along the residential area, a very tall building appeared at the end of the road.
After entering this building, Luo Zheng saw that there were many tombstones erected inside. They were also made with the Silver Gang Stone.
"This building is where the Mountain Crushing Tribe's tomb is located?" Seeing those tombstones, Luo Zheng was secretly shocked.
After walking past many silver tombstones, Luo Zheng saw several hundred golden tombstones in front of him. These tombstones were made with the same material as the golden square stone he was pushing. They were all made with the Golden Fork Strange Stone.
Thinking about the meaning that the little old man had expressed, these Golden Fork Strange Stone were all rare treasures. However, they were all used to make tombstones. Luo Zheng guessed that the people buried under these tombstones were the leaders of the Mountain Crushing Tribe.
There weren't many of them. There were only about two to three hundred golden tombstones.
After passing by the golden tombstones, Luo Zheng finally came to the end of the building. At the end of the building, there was a circular tent.
The tent's curtain was open. A pure white cloth was spread on the ground of the tent. On the cloth, there was an extremely tall but skinny old man sitting on it.
This old man was obviously also a member of the Mountain Crushing Tribe. However, he should be quite old. He seemed to be a person half covered in yellow soil.
The Forbidden Land of God Refinement didn't seem to have the Five Decay of Heaven and Man. This old man could be considered a person who was about to die. However, he wasn't covered in filth. Although this person looked old, his wrinkled face still had a pair of eyes that were very spirited.
"Young man, please come in..."
After Luo Zheng came closer, the old man opened his mouth and called out to him. His voice was very hoarse.
Luo Zheng nodded. Then, he stepped into the tent.
The old man stretched out his bamboo-like slender arm. He pointed at the white cloth and asked Luo Zheng to sit down.
After Luo Zheng sat down, he sat cross-legged facing the old man.
After sitting down, the old man didn't say anything. Luo Zheng was a little suspicious. This person should be the prophet of the Mountain Crushing Tribe. Since he had something to tell him, why didn't he say anything?
Luo Zheng's composure wasn't bad. Since the old man didn't say anything, Luo Zheng just sat cross-legged. Then, his whole body began to relax.
After that punch, Luo Zheng had a new understanding of the control of power.
Especially the power that came from the world in his body.
Now, Luo Zheng could turn that enormous power into an irregular glove. Could he turn it into a sword in the future? Or even a thin awl?
Unfortunately, in front of the prophet, Luo Zheng couldn't test his conjecture. He could only suppress his eagerness to try.
After a while, the prophet said, "We are attached to this Forbidden Land of God Refinement. This Forbidden Land of God Refinement is attached to the God Domain. It can be said that we travel eight trillion miles in a day..."
"Huh?" Luo Zheng was slightly taken aback when he heard this.
The prophet of the Mountain Crushing Tribe slowly said, "The entire God Domain is in a state of falling at a high speed. It is falling towards the depths of the Primal Chaos. We are in it, but we can't feel this speed..."
Hearing the prophet's explanation, Luo Zheng was still a little confused.
He understood the meaning. No matter which universe they were in or which Forbidden Land of God Refinement, the world in the body of the Sages was revolving around the God Domain. The God Domain itself was also revolving at an extremely high speed.
However, they couldn't feel their own movements while they were inside it.
For example, when a star was revolving, the living beings in the star couldn't feel their own position constantly changing.
But what did the prophet mean by falling?
The prophet of the Mountain Crushing Tribe continued, "The Sages all say that the Primal Chaos is endless. No matter how one falls, it is impossible to fall to the bottom... But my Lord, as one of the three newly conferred Great Sages, knows that the Primal Chaos has a limit. The God Domain can't endlessly fall. When the God Domain touches that limit, the final result will be destruction. The Gods will also be annihilated."
Luo Zheng was stunned. Finally, he asked, "This... what does it have to do with me?"
The prophet of the Mountain Crushing Tribe smiled, "There aren't many people in the God Domain who agree with this view. My Lord is one of them. Therefore, he gave up on building a whole universe and turned his life's cultivation into a Forbidden Land of God Refinement."
At this point, Luo Zheng finally understood. The Lord mentioned by the prophet should be the Sage who built this Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
The energy source that Luo Zheng absorbed was the energy source of that "Lord"!
"Other than you, no one in the Mountain Crushing Tribe knows about this matter." The prophet said indifferently, "My Lord hopes to share his power with all living beings. He hopes that all living beings will produce heroes and miracles! Supporting the God Domain... "
"Why are you telling me this?" Luo Zheng blinked.

Chapter 1508
Luo Zheng was skeptical of the prophet's words.
To the current Luo Zheng, the Realm of the Gods was still a distant thought.
As for what the entire Realm of the Gods would face, it was probably even more distant to Luo Zheng.
Thus, regarding the prophet's information, Luo Zheng's heart did not waver at all. Even if the sky collapsed, the True Gods would hold it up. Above the True Gods were the Saints...
However, the prophet's last words still caused Luo Zheng's heart to skip a beat.
Did this fellow know how much energy source he had absorbed?
In the end, the energy source came from the Saints. For an ordinary martial artist to absorb tens of thousands of drops was already a considerable amount.
The energy source that any random martial artist could absorb this year was probably equivalent to the energy source that they could absorb in a year in the past...
As for Luo Zheng, he had absorbed even more energy source. He had absorbed a third of the lake's energy source.
If the prophet knew that he had absorbed so much energy, would he ask him to return it?
Although Luo Zheng was somewhat flustered, he still had a reason to be certain.
The energy source of such a large lake was placed there, and there was even an eight trigram ball that could reverse gravity and allow martial artists to leave. This should be the arrangement of the "Lord" that the prophet spoke of. Since the prophet had made such arrangements, it meant that the prophet did not care if someone entered to absorb the energy source.
The only problem was that Luo Zheng had absorbed too much energy.
If the energy source of this lake was the Saint's entire strength, then Luo Zheng taking a third of it was too much.
The prophet saw a trace of unease on Luo Zheng's face, so he said, "How much energy source you can take away is your own ability. My Lord will not mind."
Luo Zheng stared and asked, "You really won't mind?"
The prophet laughed, "My Lord divides the martial artists in the Forbidden Land of God into two types of people, whether they come from the Realm of the Gods or from the universe."
Luo Zheng asked, "Which two types?"
"Those with hope and those without hope," the prophet replied.
"..."
This classification was too general. Luo Zheng did not know how to respond. After thinking for a moment, he asked, "Which type do I belong to? Is there hope? "
The Prophet shook her head. "No."
"Then there is no hope," Luo Zheng said with a wry smile, although he didn't know what the so-called hope was.
Unexpectedly, the Prophet still shook his head, "You don't belong to either of these two categories. Ever since my Master set up the Six Great Trial Sacred Grounds, he arranged six Prophets within them. The six of us are here to help my Master filter out the foreign martial artists who enter the Forbidden Land of God Refinement and distinguish between these two categories of martial artists. However … you belong to the third category."
"What's the third category?" Luo Zheng asked.
"A person who can turn hope into reality." The wrinkles on the Prophet's old face completely opened up, revealing a gratified smile. "Seven people have already been discovered from the Six Great Trial Holy Lands. These seven people can all reach the third type of standard. It's a pity that they have never appeared in my Mountain Crushing Clan. This day has finally come."
Luo Zheng finally understood the meaning of this restriction. The Forbidden Land of God Refinement was created by that Saint. That Saint had created many large and small cultivation places. He had created the Six Great Trial Holy Lands and even created different clans to guard these cultivation Holy Lands. Then, he created six transcendental Prophets to quietly observe all the foreign martial artists who entered these cultivation Holy Lands.
These Prophets were secretly evaluating each martial artist.
A martial artist who could obtain a Purple Trial Talisman in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was already considered an outstanding person. At the same time, they would also be able to display their strength and talent. Under such a selection, they would be able to obtain someone they were satisfied with!
No wonder …
Luo Zheng was also curious. This Mountain Crushing Clan didn't seem to have any need for the Trial Talismans, so why did they have to collect them?
After all, those things couldn't be eaten and could only be used as currency to exchange for resources with other clans. However, the Mountain Crushing Clan was completely self-sufficient and didn't need any resources at all.
This Trial Talisman was actually a proof of qualification. It was to prevent cats and dogs from squeezing into the Six Great Trial Holy Lands and causing a certain degree of difficulty in the selection process.
"You are the only person in my Mountain Crushing Clan who fits the third category, so I can give this to you." After the Prophet finished speaking, a Black Trial Talisman appeared in his hand. "I believe you know the value of this Trial Talisman." After speaking, the Prophet's face revealed a trace of pride.
This Prophet didn't know how Luo Zheng had entered the Mountain Crushing Clan.
According to a normal example, after a martial artist appeared in a place of birth, they would begin to complete all kinds of missions and fight for all kinds of Trial Talismans.
These Trial Talismans were divided into black, purple, red, green, and white.
However, in the eyes of all the foreign martial artists, a Purple Trial Talisman was a top grade Trial Talisman!
The Black Trial Talisman was practically a legend in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. They had only heard of it a few times, but no one had ever seen it before!
Later on, everyone simply believed that there was no Black Trial Talisman in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Thus, the Purple Trial Talisman became the highest grade Trial Talisman.
However, they didn't know that the Black Trial Talisman actually existed. It was just that the number of people who had obtained it was too few.
Only the third category of martial artists that the Prophet spoke of had the qualifications to obtain the Black Trial Talisman.
Then before Luo Zheng, there were only seven martial artists who had obtained the Black Trial Talisman …
Luo Zheng had only entered the Body Refining Holy Lands because of Liusu. He didn't have a clear understanding of the Black Trial Talisman's classification at all.
The Prophet had stayed here for so many years, and he had also imagined the scene of him handing out the Black Trial Talisman countless times. He originally thought that Luo Zheng would be incomparably shocked and excited when he saw this Black Trial Talisman.
However, Luo Zheng didn't have any expression on his face. He just casually took the Black Trial Talisman.
"This is a Black Trial Talisman." The Prophet emphasized again. Although he didn't use any tone, he still wanted to remind Luo Zheng of the preciousness of this item.
"Is it better than the Purple Trial Talisman?" Luo Zheng asked. He had seen He Zuowei take out the Purple Trial Talisman and give it to the Mountain Collapsing Clan. In Liusu's eyes, this Purple Trial Talisman was ordinary and could be casually taken out. If this Black Trial Talisman was better than the Purple Trial Talisman, then it was a whole level higher.
"Of, of course …" The Mountain Collapsing Clan's Prophet didn't know what to say. Since Luo Zheng asked this question, it meant that he didn't understand the classification of the Black Trial Talisman. Then, just how had this boy obtained the Purple Trial Talisman and entered the Mountain Collapsing Clan?
But this boy would eventually understand the benefits of this thing! The Prophet bitterly thought to himself, trying to make up for the imbalance in his heart.
"As for what Thunder Toad and the others are pursuing, you can help them. That is also a type of experience. But, the life forms of the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement cannot escape. My master has not given us a path to surpass the Heavenly Dao." The Prophet bitterly smiled and shook his head.
The other Saints had set up the Heavenly Dao. In the end, they had only given a single wooden bridge that allowed the martial artists of the universe to cross and enter the Realm of the Gods.
But, the master of the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement had never constructed the Heavenly Dao. There were no Empyreans here, and the life forms here would never be able to leave …
What Thunder Toad and the others were pursuing, the other five Holy Lands were also pursuing. But, the Prophets of these six Holy Lands were well aware that what they were pursuing was nothing more than a dream!
From this point of view, the many races of the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement were inferior to even second-class life forms.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng could somewhat understand the mentality of Thunder Toad and the others.
In the struggle of martial arts, if one didn't have an opponent, then one would lose the meaning of 'struggle'. Then, one wouldn't be able to walk far on the martial dao.
No matter how strong Thunder Toad and the others were, they would only be able to occupy a small corner of the Realm of the Gods. They wouldn't be able to compete with the martial artists of the Realm of the Gods. They would lose the motivation to move forward!
Luo Zheng even speculated that the item that Thunder Toad had asked him to help them retrieve from the flames was a groundless existence. That item was just an impossible task set up by the Saints. It was a mysterious attraction to the races guarding the six Holy Lands, giving them a reason to strive. It was like a banana hanging in front of a donkey that could never be eaten.
Of course, this matter had nothing to do with Luo Zheng.
He had promised Thunder Toad. Naturally, he would help him retrieve the item from the flames.
Taking the black Trial Talisman, Luo Zheng left the Prophet's tent.
The test had already ended. Thunder Toad and Luo Zheng had decided on a date to depart. Thunder Toad didn't ask what the Prophet had said to Luo Zheng …
Even if he did ask, Luo Zheng wouldn't say. Moreover, the Prophet had warned him not to tell the Mountain Collapsing Clan about this matter, let alone Thunder Toad.
That night, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a strange feeling coming from his inner world. When he looked inside, he was slightly startled.

Chapter 1509
Previously, Luo Zheng hadn't paid enough attention to the inner world.
According to cultivation, the flow of time in the inner world was different.
At the Divine Sea Realm, the time in the Divine Sea Realm was almost stagnant.
Therefore, the Divine Sea Realm couldn't nurture life because the nurturing process was very long, and time was the most important factor.
But at the Ultimate God Realm, the flow of time in the inner world would increase dramatically. True essence would turn into continents, and the lowest level of life forms would be born gradually …
The higher the cultivation, the faster the flow of time in the inner world!
Therefore, the inner world of a Realm Lord was much more prosperous than that of a Divine Transformation Realm martial artist.
But Luo Zheng didn't know that the speed of his inner world was even more ridiculous. Everything in it changed in an instant.
When Luo Zheng discovered that there was a problem with his inner world and tried to pry into it, he was shocked.
On the central continent of the Chaos Sea, a primitive civilization had begun to nurture, and a large-scale war had broken out!
Luo Zheng's consciousness had already entered the Inner World. With a flash of thought, his thoughts turned into a shape and became an "Inner Body Incarnation".
In this world, Luo Zheng was the absolute master, and also the supreme god!
Although this inner world was still in its initial stage, once Luo Zheng entered it, it was still very vast.
But with a thought, his Inner Incarnation flashed and stepped onto this continent.
This wasn't greater teleportation, and he didn't use the Laws of Space. He could appear anywhere he wanted!
"Charge …"
"Kill the dogs of the Teng Clan …"
"For the glory of the Mighty Clan!"
"…"
In his inner world, Luo Zheng was not only omnipotent, but also omniscient.
Luo Zheng was omniscient about everything in this world.
It turned out that after the races were born on this continent, the large and small tribes merged with each other and eventually formed two great tribes.
The eastern tribe was the Mighty Clan, and the western tribe was the Teng Clan.
At first, the two great tribes were equal in strength. Although there had always been friction between them, there had never been a large-scale war …
But not long ago, the Teng Clan suddenly made great progress. The warriors in their clan obtained a very powerful weapon, a long spear made from a dark green plant!
When this kind of spear was used, countless vines would extend from the body of the spear and pierce into the arms of the warriors. With the help of this kind of spear, the warriors of the Teng Clan were almost invincible.
After their strength improved greatly, the Teng Clan launched a war against the wealthy clans!
Now that the wealthy tribes had suffered great losses, they gathered the last 50,000 warriors and launched a final counterattack. They wanted to protect their own tribes.
"Those weapons are very strange …" Standing at the edge of the battlefield, Luo Zheng also noticed the long spears in the hands of the Teng Clan warriors.
With a single thought, Luo Zheng understood the origin of these weapons …
He turned his head, and his gaze passed through the endless distance and landed on the Tree of World.
The World Tree that Luo Zheng had once planted had grown into a giant tree that was tens of thousands of feet tall. A race had been born on this giant World Tree. This race looked similar to humans, but their skin and flesh were dark green.
The Teng Clan's weapons were given to them by these green-skinned people.
After understanding the source, Luo Zheng suddenly smiled. Where there were people, there would be conflicts. He didn't expect that his own inner world would also be filled with conspiracies.
The ultimate goal of this race on the Tree of World was to kill all the living beings on this continent and then occupy this continent …
Luo Zheng wouldn't interfere with the operation of his inner world, but large-scale wars still had to be stopped. This would affect the expansion and development of his inner world.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng slowly walked toward the battlefield …
On the battlefield, the warriors of the wealthy clans were riding huge war elephants and charging toward the Teng Clan.
Relying on the huge impact of the war elephants, the warriors of the wealthy clans were usually invincible.
However, after the Teng Clan was equipped with the green long spears, each of them became extremely strong. With a single spear, they could even pierce through a person and a war elephant!
To the Teng Clan, this was a massacre. To the wealthy clans, this was a resistance akin to moths flying into a flame. They had to use their lives to defend their tribe and honor!
"Pu!"
"Roar!"
A war elephant's chest was pierced. It let out a painful cry and its huge body fell to the side.
The one riding this war elephant was a woman.
The wealthy clans were matriarchal, and this woman was the queen of the wealthy clan.
All of her soldiers were slaughtered. In the last battle, she personally went into battle …
After the war elephant fell, the noble queen also fell heavily onto the ground. Her exposed skin was bruised and mixed with the mud, giving her an abnormally strong pain. This woman who had exceptional beauty and also stood at the pinnacle of power was now in an extremely sorry state.
Now, this woman was completely disheartened.
She couldn't resist the iron hooves of the Teng Clan. Her people would become the slaves of the Teng Clan, and her territory would be invaded by the Teng Clan …
"Whoosh!"
A green long spear was already pressed against the queen's neck.
The person holding the long spear was the Great General of the Teng Clan. This Great General let out a heroic laugh. "Surrender. The wealthy clan has already lost. As the queen of the wealthy clan, you can be the slave of the Teng Clan. Our king will spare your life and let you serve him!"
"In, in your dreams …"
The queen struggled to get up from the ground and pulled out a long sword hanging on her back.
Just as she held the sword in her hand, before she could swing it, the green long spear shook gently and knocked her sword away.
"Struggling is useless," the Great General said indifferently.
"My clansmen … are still struggling. As the king, how can I give up?"
The queen twisted her flexible waist and went around the long spear to pounce towards the Great General. However, the Great General twisted his hand and grabbed the queen's long black hair, lifting her up into the air.
"You only have this body and appearance left. Apart from that, you have nothing else. There's nothing you can give up except your life!" the Great General said coldly. However, at this moment, he suddenly saw someone.
It was a young man dressed in other clothes.
This young man walked in the center of the battlefield like this. He ignored the soldiers fighting around him and walked towards the Great General.
"Who are you?" the Great General asked coldly. The green long spear in his hand was already pointed at the young man.
The young man didn't answer. Instead, he continued walking towards the Great General.
"If you come any closer, you'll die!" the Great General said coldly.
The queen also realized that this person wasn't ordinary. However, she was held in the Great General's hand and couldn't struggle …
As Luo Zheng came closer and closer, the Great General finally lost his patience. He waved the long spear in his hand and stabbed towards Luo Zheng's head.
Luo Zheng didn't dodge. Instead, he allowed the long spear to stab him!
"Hmph!"
The Great General sneered. This green long spear contained unimaginable power. This spear could completely pierce through the opponent's head!
However, the Great General's face quickly froze. He saw an inconceivable scene.
The moment the tip of the long spear stabbed into the opponent's face, it broke apart inch by inch and shattered …
That wasn't all. After the long spear broke apart, it floated in the air. Then, it continuously merged back together and returned to its original form. However, the long spear was already in the opponent's hand.
Luo Zheng played with the long spear. With a light shake, countless tiny vines grew from the body of the spear and stabbed towards his arm. However, these vines couldn't pierce Luo Zheng's skin.
"Interesting. That World Tree actually gave birth to such a wonderful weapon …"
Then, Luo Zheng flicked the long spear lightly. The long spear was directly annihilated in the air, turning into a brown gas that dissipated in the air. It completely disappeared, leaving no fragments or substance behind. Luo Zheng had directly turned the long spear back into chaotic gas.
"Who the hell are you?"
The Great General had never seen such a miraculous technique. His face revealed a panicked expression. He threw the queen aside and quickly retreated!
The queen fell to the ground. When she turned her head, she saw Luo Zheng standing there!
Luo Zheng's gaze swept over her. When he saw the queen's face clearly, he was slightly stunned …
No wonder he heard that some people would fall in love with the creatures in their inner world.
Everything in the inner world was in a random state. Under the background of countless creatures being born, it was only possible for such a beautiful woman to be born.
"You … Who are you?"
The queen asked the exact same question. After all, Luo Zheng's magical powers and actions were too different. It was completely beyond their imagination. That was an ability that only gods had.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1510
Luo Zheng smiled faintly, "I just want to put an end to this meaningless war."
Hearing this, the queen showed an astonished expression, "You can do that?"
Luo Zheng nodded and looked around. Many soldiers who were fighting fiercely in the surroundings also noticed Luo Zheng at this moment …
Luo Zheng raised his arm slightly. Everyone didn't understand his action.
At this moment, the great general behind Luo Zheng suddenly pulled out a dagger that he always carried with him and charged straight at Luo Zheng.
He was representing the king of the Teng Clan and was about to achieve the final victory. How could he let this strange guy in front of him stop him?
"Be careful!"
The queen reminded him.
However, Luo Zheng didn't move at all. He allowed the great general to twist his body and stab the dagger towards his heart.
In his own internal world, Luo Zheng was the absolute controller. Not only was he in the state of "omniscience", he was also in the state of "omnipotence". How could that dagger stab into Luo Zheng's heart?
The moment the tip of the dagger touched Luo Zheng, it also cracked inch by inch.
"Stupid," Luo Zheng spat out a few words indifferently.
With a slight shake of his body, the great general suddenly flew up. Under Luo Zheng's gaze, he kept rising higher and higher …
Ten zhang …
Three hundred zhang …
Three thousand zhang …
In this era, the Divine Reflection Realm hadn't been born yet. The strongest experts were only Xiantian lifeforms.
Flying was their legendary dream. However, this Great General had become the first person to do so.
Luo Zheng didn't kill him. He just threw him three thousand zhang into the air. As the creator of this world, Luo Zheng wouldn't casually kill the lifeforms he created because it was meaningless.
Then, Luo Zheng raised his arm again.
On the battlefield, the weapons in everyone's hands began to vibrate and flew out of their hands!
"Ding ding dang dang …"
Sabers, swords, spears, daggers, bows and arrows …
All kinds of weapons gathered above Luo Zheng's head, forming a round ball made of weapons.
Then Luo Zheng gently pointed.
Those weapons began to disintegrate at the same time, turning back into streaks of brown Chaos Qi that dissipated from this world!
The soil formed by the solidification of Chaos energy contained all sorts of iron ores, copper ores, and gold ores.
After these humans mastered metallurgy, they extracted the metals and forged them into various weapons. However, regardless of whether it was steel blades or iron swords, they were still made of Chaos Qi. Luo Zheng could turn them back into Chaos Qi at any time.
Even the green spears that came from the World Tree were still formed after the World Tree had absorbed Chaos Qi, so they were still Chaos Qi.
Both the warriors from the Teng Tribe and the Hao Tribe were stunned by the scene before them.
Everyone had stopped fighting, and they had also lost their weapons ….
"You … are a god!"
The queen was the first to react. She plopped down on her knees in front of Luo Zheng. Her exquisite body directly prostrated on the ground. Her expression was sincere, without any hesitation.
"It's God!"
"That is the God of Creation!"
"That's the legendary nine-star creator!"
"No, he is the one holding up our world. He is the World Bearing Venerable!"
Many people cried out in alarm while prostrating on the ground.
When Luo Zheng heard these words, he didn't know whether to laugh or cry.
He was indeed the God of Creation of this world, but what the hell were the nine-star creator and the World Bearing Venerable?
He got the answer as soon as this question arose. Luo Zheng was very used to this feeling of omniscience. After all, he was not only "omniscient" about the world within his body, but also about the millions of worlds in the universe.
During the evolution of these civilizations, many legends were born. These legends described the existence of "gods" in detail. For example, the nine-star creator was a god worshipped by the Teng Tribe, and the World Bearing Venerable was the legendary God of Creation of the Hao Tribe. They believed that there was a god below the world on his shoulders. This god held up the entire world, so he was called the World Bearing Venerable.
"You have too few people now. I … don't want you to kill each other," Luo Zheng said faintly as he looked down at the queen on the ground.
"Venerable! The Hao Tribe is waging war on us! "The queen said excitedly.
Luo Zheng nodded. "I know."
After saying that, he waved his hand …
All the scars on the queen's body disappeared, returning to her delicate skin.
The queen was slightly stunned, and her expression became even more excited. She prostrated on the ground again. "Thank you, World Bearing Venerable!"
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. With a light stroke of his hand, a piece of soil protruded from the ground. That piece of soil instantly turned into an exquisite scepter. This scepter was inserted in front of the queen. "I give you the ability to rule your people well …"
The queen stared at the exquisite scepter in a daze. Just as she was about to say something, Luo Zheng's figure quietly disappeared in front of her.
"This … this is the scepter given to me by the World Bearing Venerable." The queen trembled all over. After all, she was the queen of the Hao Tribe and understood the significance of this scepter.
Even if this scepter didn't have any ability, it represented God's favor and was also a symbol of status.
At this moment, Luo Zheng also didn't expect that the scepter he casually created would become the most precious treasure in the world in the future. It would become the scepter of God that symbolized supreme authority and would be pursued by countless ambitious people.
The war between the Teng Tribe and the Hao Tribe would also be called the War of the Gods by later generations …
It wasn't until a long time after Luo Zheng disappeared that the queen picked up the scepter on the ground and slowly got up. She still had a thoughtful expression on her face.
The moment Luo Zheng disappeared, he appeared in a tribe thousands of miles away.
This was the Teng Tribe's tribe. The whole tribe was singing and dancing. News from the frontlines said that the Teng Tribe had won a great victory. They would have countless slaves and endless wealth and land.
The Teng Tribe's king was in a very good mood. He believed that the general would bring that woman back. That was the most attractive woman in the world. During this period, he had been thinking about her day and night.
However, the Hao Tribe's queen wasn't brought back. The one who appeared in front of him was Luo Zheng.
"Who, who are you … Guards, there's an assassin!" The king shouted in horror. He didn't know how this ghost-like guy in front of him had entered his tent.
Luo Zheng waved his hand lightly. The general, who was still hanging in the air, appeared beside Luo Zheng. Then, Luo Zheng threw the general to the ground.
"General … General …" The king was even more surprised. "Quick, kill this man for me!"
Unexpectedly, the general, who was brave and good at fighting, knelt down in front of Luo Zheng the moment he landed and prostrated himself on the ground.
"My God, please forgive the Teng Tribe. We shouldn't have started this war!" the general said respectfully.
The creatures of this era were still in a state of ignorance. No matter how brave the general was, he wouldn't go against Luo Zheng after confirming Luo Zheng's magical power!
"You, you are a God …" When the king heard the general's words, his fat face was full of sluggishness.
"Stop the war with the Hao Tribe." Luo Zheng didn't answer the king's words. After leaving behind this warning, his figure turned into a faint shadow and finally disappeared.
After mediating this war, Luo Zheng once again entered the World Tree.
Now, Luo Zheng's inner world was too small and not prosperous enough. He wouldn't allow the creatures on the World Tree to start a war between the human race and the human race.
On the World Tree, Luo Zheng also encountered some resistance. These green-skinned races were called the Heavenly Spirit Race.
They were very weak, but with the power of the World Tree, they had some magical powers that the human race couldn't match. Therefore, they had the urge to conquer the continent.
Luo Zheng directly appeared at the top of the World Tree. He found the king of the Heavenly Spirit Race and issued his warning.
He divided the World Tree and the continent into two worlds and two races. At least for tens of thousands of years, the two races were not allowed to fight each other!
When this inner world was truly prosperous, Luo Zheng wouldn't interfere too much even if there was a war. After all, the development of the world and the birth of civilization had its own rules. Too much interference might not be a good thing.
Luo Zheng's warning was very effective. Perhaps the Heavenly Spirit Race had different beliefs, but they also believed in the existence of gods. Luo Zheng's magical powers were exactly the same as the gods in the legends. They had no room for resistance.

Chapter 1511
After dealing with the internal world, Luo Zheng's internal incarnation dissipated with the wind.
Normally, martial artists wouldn't interfere too much with the internal world's development and let it evolve on its own.
However, the internal world's war this time involved the World Tree, and the World Tree was something Luo Zheng had implanted into it. It would inevitably have a bad influence. If Luo Zheng didn't interfere, the human race inside might not be able to escape the fate of total annihilation.
Lei Toad didn't ask what the Prophet and Luo Zheng talked about.
These Prophets were all extraordinary existences in the various Holy Lands. From another perspective, they were the guardians left behind by the Sage in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
Their purpose of staying here was to help the Sage do things. Many times, they even had conflicts with their own race.
For example, the Mountain Crushing Race wanted to know the purpose of their existence and the purpose of the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. They even wanted to be like the foreign martial artists and one day be able to escape from the Forbidden Land of God Refinement and enter the God Domain.
The Prophets were very clear that this was impossible, but they didn't tell the people under them. They even misled their own people many times.
After all, this was the world created by the Sage. Luo Zheng wouldn't casually say too much.
After this test, Lei Toad and the Mountain Crushing Race didn't go out to hunt for several days. Instead, they prepared in the race.
After about four days, the Mountain Crushing Race finally finished their preparations. All the adults were divided into several groups and prepared to set off.
The hunters of the Mountain Crushing Race were divided into four groups: Snake, Tiger, Dragon, and God.
The Snake Hunting Team was the weakest. The martial artist who had just obtained the title of "Extraordinary" joined the Snake Hunting Team. Relatively speaking, this team was the safest. It was a team where the seniors brought the newcomers to gain experience.
The Tiger Hunting Team and the Dragon Hunting Team were the backbone of the Mountain Crushing Race. The animals piled up like mountains on the daily square were all hunted by the Mountain Crushing Race.
As for the God Hunting Team, they rarely took action. There were only about ten of them in the entire Mountain Crushing Race. The three people who defeated the Non-Phase Sky Puppet that day belonged to the Mountain Crushing Race's God Hunting Team.
Luo Zheng obtained the title of Barbarian King and was directly recruited into the God Hunting Team …
After some foreign martial artists passed the test, some chose to leave the Mountain Crushing Race. The Mountain Crushing Race didn't ask them to stay. If they wanted to stay, they had to hunt outside with the Mountain Crushing Race.
After the preparations were complete, the team set off in a grandiose manner.
Han Liusu, He Zuowei, and the others did not enter this group. However, after the Mountain Shattering Clan set off, they also followed along.
After seeing Luo Zheng's great potential, He Zuowei was not as stubborn as before. He did not forcefully bring Han Liusu back, although he still had doubts in his heart.
No matter how powerful Luo Zheng was, the Soul Subduing Cliff was set up by a Saint. In theory, only a Saint could break it!
However, he also knew that if they could really enter Soul Subduing Cliff, it would be very beneficial for the eldest princess in the future. It would only take a bit of time, and there wasn't too much risk involved. He was willing to bring Han Liusu along.
They followed the Mountain Crumbling Race's territory all the way north. The Tiger Hunting Team and Dragon Hunting Team led the way at the front, while the Snake Hunting Team and God Hunting Team followed closely behind.
Not long after, the color of the ground changed. The Silver Cosmic Stones disappeared and were replaced by brown soil and dense forests.
Just as Luo Zheng stepped out of the Silver Cosmic Stone ground and stepped onto the ground, he suddenly felt his entire body relax!
From the moment they stepped into the Mountain Crumbling Race's territory, everyone was burdened with an extremely strong gravitational force.
Resisting this kind of gravitational force was not a small burden for anyone. Therefore, for the martial artists who had just entered, even climbing up was extremely difficult.
However, after getting used to this kind of gravitational force, leaving the Mountain Crumbling Race again, they would feel unprecedentedly relaxed!
It was as if the thousand mountains on their backs had suddenly disappeared!
"Ka ka ka pa pa pa …"
Under the pressure of the gravitational force, everyone's bones were compressed for a long time. Even their height was affected. The joints between their bones were too tight, and they were generally "pressed" by the gravitational force by four or five inches!
After taking two steps, they casually moved their muscles and bones. Under the stretch of their bones, everyone "grew" a few inches in a short period of time …
"Pi pi pa pa pa …"
Perhaps it was intentional, or perhaps it was unintentional, but some of the foreign martial artists deliberately exerted force, causing their bones to crackle.
It was as if someone in the entire team was holding a string of firecrackers, constantly exploding …
"Motherf * cker, it's so noisy. All of you better quiet down. Every year when we bring new people out, it's always like this!" A rough-looking member of the Mountain Crumbling Race cursed angrily.
The members of the Mountain Crumbling Race were used to this kind of feeling. Naturally, no one would be so bored as to deliberately stretch their bodies and cause such a commotion. Normally, only the foreign martial artists would be so bored.
After being yelled at by that member of the God Hunting Team, those sounds instantly became much softer. However, many of the foreign martial artists still revealed satisfied smiles on their faces …
They had been lying in the Mountain Crumbling Race for several years, refining their bodies and growing in the midst of sullenness and temptation. Only they themselves could experience the pain. However, this kind of pain was rewarded. After going in and coming out, their strength had increased by tens or even hundreds of times!
Luo Zheng had not stayed for long, less than a year. At this moment, his mood was naturally quite good. If not for the fact that he was traveling with the team and that he was in the highest position of the God Hunting Team, he would very much like to jump around crazily to see how much his strength had increased.
Just as this team approached the forest ahead, someone in the Tiger Hunting Team suddenly shouted, "There's a mutated beast ahead. It's a Golden Shield Ape!"
The members of the Mountain Crumbling Race were all natural hunters. They had hunted for their entire lives and were very sensitive to smells and traces.
As soon as the voice fell, not a single member of the Mountain Crumbling Race moved. However, the vast majority of the foreign martial artists all rushed out …
Under this rush, a huge explosive force exploded under their feet. In an instant, countless large pits appeared on the ground. Some martial artists were not prepared and fell into those large pits.
According to the rules of the Mountain Crumbling Race, the Tiger Hunting Team had to go through a thorough investigation before surrounding the prey. This way, they could capture the prey to the greatest extent.
However, these foreign martial artists could be said to have been bored to death. On the day of the test, each of them had only thrown out a single punch. Now that there was no gravity, they felt like they were flying freely in the sky. How could they still restrain themselves?
"Humph, this bunch of reckless fools. If they don't follow the rules after encountering a truly powerful mutated beast, then prepare to die!" Someone from the Mountain Crumbling Race sneered.
Under the explosive force of those foreign martial artists, the forest in front of them was instantly turned upside down.
After their strength increased explosively, a single punch could create a canyon. The huge force was unrestrained, and the trees in the forest instantly disappeared without a trace. What replaced it was a wide gully, as well as dirt and tree trunks that flew all over the sky. At the same time, there was also the sound of apes roaring.
Those Golden Gale Apes were at the edge of the Mountain Crumbling Race and were not considered very powerful mutated beasts. Facing this group of aggressive foreign martial artists, how could they have the slightest bit of resistance? Although they were very agile, they still could not escape the fate of being killed.
"Pfff, pfff, pfff, pfff …"
After each Golden Gale Ape was hit, they would immediately explode into a cloud of blood mist. They were not here to hunt, but to kill for the sake of killing.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng also frowned. If a person was suppressed for too long, it was indeed easy for them to lose themselves. At this moment, these martial artists were like wild beasts, only knowing how to vent their strength.
Very quickly, the Golden Gale Apes hiding in the forest were all killed.
Those martial artists also returned to their own teams, but their faces still had unsatisfied expressions.
The Thunder Toad did not stop their actions. As they advanced, the mutated beasts they encountered would become stronger and stronger. Sooner or later, they would be taught a lesson …
The Mountain Crumbling Race continued to lead the team forward.
As they traversed through this forest, the trees in front of them became taller and taller.
At the beginning, the trees in the forest were only twenty to thirty feet tall. Towards the end, those trees covered the sky and the sun, and it was almost impossible to see the sky.
Facing these huge trees that were hundreds of feet thick and hundreds of feet tall, those foreign martial artists could not help but feel their hands itching. When they passed by a tree, they would occasionally punch the tree trunk beside them. The huge tree cracked and the huge tree trunk collapsed, as if a mountain had collapsed …
"This is the Forest of Huge Trees. If we continue forward, we will reach the Crossing Disaster Forest. Don't blame me for not reminding you. If you alarm those huge mutated beasts, don't count on our Mountain Crumbling Race!" Every huge tree that fell would cause a huge tremor. As hunters, the Mountain Crumbling Race finally could not bear it anymore. The Thunder Toad said coldly.
Just as he finished speaking, he heard a rustling sound coming from the front. When all the Mountain Crumbling Race heard this sound, their faces instantly changed.

Chapter 1512
Along the way, whenever they met a few lone mutated beasts, those foreign martial artists would swarm them.
None of the mutated beasts that fell into their hands could remain intact. They were either torn into pieces or beaten into a bloody mist.
It was a pity that they didn't meet many mutated beasts along the way. Many of the foreign martial artists had a body full of strength but didn't have a place to vent it. They only hated that there weren't enough mutated beasts to go around …
Now that they heard the rustling sound, some of the smarter foreign martial artists, such as that little old man Yao An, instantly noticed the expressions of the Mountain Crumbling Mountain Tribe members. Especially those from the Divine Hunters, their expressions didn't look too good …
Not only did Yao An and the rest not rush forward, but they also quietly retreated.
Only a portion of the foreign martial artists heard the sound and naturally thought that there were mutated beasts ahead. Without any hesitation, they flew forward.
Actually, these foreign martial artists were usually very cautious. Those who were too reckless wouldn't be able to obtain the purple trial talisman and enter the Mountain Crumbling Mountain Tribe to cultivate!
Today, it was as though they had been injected with chicken blood. On one hand, it was because their strength had increased greatly. On the other hand, this huge tree forest was closely connected to the Crossing Calamity Forest. They could even smell the faint fragrance here, which strengthened their ferocious nature. To put it bluntly, they were dizzy!
"All of you retreat! That's the True Martial Sacred Beast! If you touch it, you will die! "
Although Lei Toad didn't care about the lives of these foreign martial artists, he wouldn't really send them to their deaths …
All the Mountain Crumbling Mountain Tribe members quickly gathered together. Hearing this, some of the faster foreign martial artists stopped in their tracks and began to retreat.
But Lei Toad's warning was ultimately one step too late. Furthermore, these martial artists had already rushed out a considerable distance!
"Swish swish swish …"
Suddenly, numerous gray tentacles appeared behind the giant trees!
Those tentacles had a metallic luster on them. Luo Zheng stood in the middle of the crowd and noticed these tentacles. His eyes flashed slightly, "This True Martial Sacred Beast is a puppet?"
These steel ropes were forged section by section. No matter how one looked at it, it looked like a puppet made of metal!
"Swish!"
These steel ropes left deep grooves in the ground as they lashed out. After the dozen or so foreign martial artists at the front were whipped, they were wrapped up by the steel ropes, and with miserable screams, they were dragged deep into the forest …
Among these foreign martial artists, there were two who had obtained the title of "Divine Strength". Their strength was not weak, but after struggling for a moment, they were also dragged away!
The remaining cultivators all paled. How could they dare to stay any longer? At this moment, he had to seize the opportunity to flee …
However, there were simply too many of these steel cables. As they continued to roll back, they actually managed to drag away another seven or eight foreign race cultivators!
"Dragon Hunters, open the Demon Sealing Array!"
At this moment, even the Thunder Toad was extremely serious.
The True Martial Sacred Beast was a powerful beast that resided in the Giant Tree Forest. To be precise, half of it was a living being, while the other half was a puppet.
In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, there were some things that were undefeatable. This True Martial Sacred Beast was one of them. Because it was a spirit pet that was raised by the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, it had devoured countless living beings in this place over the years!
The True Martial Sacred Beast had no natural enemies in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Until now, the Mountain Crushing Race had never seen the true face of this thing. Previously, the Mountain Crushing Race did not even dare to enter the Giant Tree Forest because they would definitely die if they encountered the True Martial Sacred Beast.
Perhaps because the Mountain Crushing Race had lost too many people in the Giant Tree Forest, the prophet in the race had taught them the Demon Sealing Array. If they encountered the True Martial Sacred Beast, they only needed to activate this Demon Sealing Array, and they would not be attacked by it.
What the Thunder Toad did not know was that the Demon Sealing Array itself was not powerful. It only contained information that the True Martial Sacred Beast was wary of.
After the prophet communicated with the master, the "master" then bestowed this Demon Sealing Array.
That True Martial Sacred Beast was the "master"'s spirit pet, and the "master" naturally did not wish for the Mountain Crushing Race to be eaten by the spirit pet …
In short, as long as the Demon Sealing Array was activated, the True Martial Sacred Beast would treat this group of people as its own and stop attacking!
For so many years, their Mountain Crushing Race had relied on this Demon Sealing Array to pass through the Giant Tree Forest.
It was also because of the existence of the Demon Sealing Array that passing through the Giant Tree Forest became relatively simple. With such a ferocious beast here, there were no beasts that were too powerful to survive in this forest!
As time passed, the Mountain Crushing Race had almost neglected this "True Martial Sacred Beast". They did not expect that the True Martial Sacred Beast would appear today.
The Thunder Toad was also very confused. It was still daytime, so why would the True Martial Sacred Beast attack them!
Fortunately, the dozens of clansmen in the Dragon Hunting Team instantly activated the divine inscriptions in their hands. After those divine inscriptions were activated, they connected with each other, forming an azure net that completely enveloped the Mountain Shattering Clan and the foreign martial artists within!
"Swish, swish, swish …"
Countless steel ropes crawled over from all directions, like little snakes made of metal, extending and crawling on the ground.
The moment these steel ropes touched the net formed by the divine patterns, it was as if they were electrocuted and instantly retracted.
"Thunder Toad, what is going on?" A member of the Mountain Crushing Race standing in the middle of the Divine Hunting Team asked.
The Thunder Toad looked at the steel ropes that came from all directions. He also could not understand why the True Martial Sacred Beast would choose to attack the Mountain Crushing Race today.
Those steel ropes continued to climb and finally touched the Demon Sealing Array of the Mountain Crushing Race. The moment they touched it, those steel ropes seemed to have been electrocuted and retracted backwards …
Right now, all the other martial artists were holed up in the team and did not even dare to breathe loudly.
They only just remembered that this was the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. This was not a big world and it was crowded with all kinds of powerful beasts. With their strength, it was not enough for them to run rampant here!
"The situation is not right." A member of the Divine Hunting Team frowned and said.
"What is not right?" Luo Zheng asked.
That person was very patient with Luo Zheng. After all, Luo Zheng had the title of Barbarian King and had entered the Divine Hunting Team as an exception. He was on the same level as them and could even surpass them. Plus, they still needed Luo Zheng's help.
"In the past, after the Demon Sealing Array was used, the True Martial Sacred Beast would stop attacking and leave on its own. But today …" That person pointed around.
Steel ropes continued to extend out from those thick trees. When they touched the Demon Sealing Array, they began to retreat. But not long after they retreated, those steel ropes came back again, as if they did not give up.
The Thunder Toad saw this scene and was abnormally ugly. "There should be something that the True Martial Sacred Beast wants beside us!"
Something that the True Martial Sacred Beast wants?
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed and landed on Han Liusu who was not far away.
Han Liusu's face was also very flustered. When she met Luo Zheng's gaze, she immediately pursed her lips.
"Is it her?"
Luo Zheng guessed inwardly. He noticed that the steel ropes were densest in the direction of the tassels. However, the steel ropes seemed to be very afraid of the Demonic Sealing Array. They were unwilling to forcefully break the Demonic Sealing Array and only kept spinning outside.
When they first entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede also chased after this woman.
This Han Liusu was indeed an extremely beautiful woman, but these strange beasts would not lust after her beauty, right? But why did all the strange beasts want to find trouble with her?
Just as Luo Zheng was thinking, those thin steel ropes finally could not hold back and whipped towards the Demon Sealing Array!
"Shua shua …"
These Demon Sealing Arrays could not truly defend against the True Martial Sacred Beast's attacks. It was just that this Demon Sealing Array contained some kind of information that made the True Martial Sacred Beast extremely afraid.
But this time, the Demon Sealing Array was ineffective. The True Martial Sacred Beast seemed to have made a great decision and began to whip crazily!
When the Mountain Shattering Tribe members with the divine runes saw that the situation was not right, they turned around and ran. The originally circular Demon Sealing Array suddenly had a gap!
"Whoosh!"
One of the steel ropes took advantage of the gap!
Just as Luo Zheng had guessed, this True Martial Sacred Beast's goal was Han Liusu!
"Grand Princess, let's go!"
The faces of He Zuowei and the others also became extremely ugly.
They originally thought that following the Mountain Shattering Tribe would not be too dangerous, but they had forgotten about Han Liusu's physique. Her physique had a fatal attraction to any strange beast …
Han Liusu did not leave. At this moment, she did a very correct thing. She jumped up and rushed towards Luo Zheng.
She knew that He Zuowei's great wish had already been used on the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede. Before the wish was fulfilled, he could not use it a second time. It was impossible to fight against this "True Martial Sacred Beast".
Therefore, at this moment, she could only place her hopes on Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1513
Following the wave of the Demon Sealing Formation, everyone starts to panic.
Especially the Mountain Crumbling Tribe.
The Giant Tree Forest is not far from the Mountain Crumbling Tribe. Every Mountain Crumbling Tribe member knows from a young age that there is a huge monster in this forest that they cannot afford to offend. When some Mountain Crumbling Tribe children make mistakes, the adults in the tribe will use this monster to scare them.
Therefore, the Mountain Crumbling Tribe members are very clear about the strength of the True Martial Sacred Beast.
In a panic, Lei Toad immediately roars, "Everyone calm down! Dragon Hunters, maintain the formation! "
The Dragon Hunters' strength is second only to the God Hunters. They are the backbone of the Mountain Crumbling Tribe. Under Lei Toad's orders, most people still maintain the Demon Sealing Formation. However, the hole on the left side is still there.
Someone from the God Hunters wants to make a move but is stopped by Lei Toad.
When that steel cable entered the Demon Sealing Formation and started chasing after Han Liusu, Lei Toad already knows that this girl is what the True Martial Sacred Beast is looking for.
The True Martial Sacred Beast is not a powerful existence that the Mountain Crumbling Tribe can fight against. Moreover, only that steel cable entered the Demon Sealing Formation. If the God Hunters make a move, they will probably anger the True Martial Sacred Beast. At that time, the Mountain Crumbling Tribe will face the danger of being exterminated. It is best not to act rashly at this moment.
The Mountain Crumbling Tribe does not have the obligation to protect foreign martial artists. Moreover, this Han Liusu is not qualified to join the God Hunters!
Han Liusu's movement technique is also very ingenious. That graceful body keeps twisting like a spinning flower, dazzling people. In a flash, she arrives in front of Luo Zheng.
"Save me," Han Liusu says as she blinks her eyes.
As soon as she finished speaking, she very naturally hid behind Luo Zheng's back …
She dodges all the way here with a very calm expression. However, hiding behind Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng feels that Han Liusu is trembling. Previously, she could suppress her fear. However, after finding a safe haven, that fear is completely released.
This scene makes Luo Zheng reveal a bitter smile. This Han Liusu is very sure that I will save her?
"You, hide further away!" Lei Toad roars coldly.
Luo Zheng is the key to their trip. Even if Han Liusu wants to die, she cannot drag Luo Zheng down with her. He also knows that Luo Zheng and Han Liusu knew each other when they entered the Mountain Crumbling Tribe. but he will definitely not allow this to happen.
At this moment, Lei Toad wants to throw Han Liusu out of the Demon Sealing Formation!
However, He Zuowei and the others would not allow such a thing to happen. They took a step forward and stood in front of the Thunder Toad. "The Eldest Princess is the Sacred Emperor's daughter. If she dies and angers the Sacred Emperor, even the Forbidden Land of God Refinement will have to pay the price!"

. "Luo Zheng, don't mess around!" Lei Toad roars.

The power contained in this steel rope is enormous. It is beyond imagination!
"Power!"
Luo Zheng's power erupted. The eight-layered Dao Platform began to shine. The power of the Nine Stars began to crazily draw out …
Luo Zheng's speed of drawing out power could not keep up. The steel rope constantly twisted. Suddenly, it was thrown upwards! Luo Zheng immediately left his hands and shot upwards. In an instant, he passed through countless branches and was thrown several thousand feet away!
Han Liusu, who was hiding behind Luo Zheng, was stunned. She stared at the steel rope in front of her. The fear on her face grew. She never thought that she would die here!
"Whoosh!"
The steel rope coiled and wrapped around Han Liusu's waist!
Seeing that Han Liusu was about to be pulled out of the crowd, the members of the Mountain Shattering Team heaved a sigh of relief. Since it was this woman who had caused trouble, the True Martial Sacred Beast had swept her away. This trouble was probably over.
As for He Zuowei and the others, they were furious.
"With my life of reincarnation, I will make a great wish …"
It had to be said that He Zuowei and the others were indeed loyal. They were able to protect the Eldest Princess into the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. They were completely loyal to Han Jia!
This Great Wish Art only allowed for one wish. After making a wish, one had to fulfill it! He Zuowei had not fulfilled his previous wish. At this moment, he could not use the Great Wish Art again. However, there was a way to escape this restriction. That was to use his life, or even his life of reincarnation to fulfill his wish!
To He Zuowei, this was suicide!
However, before He Zuowei's great wish could be expressed, a human figure suddenly descended from the trees above!
It was just a figure descending, but everyone could feel a powerful aura coming from this person. It was as if countless mountains were stacked together and stomped down!
This person was Luo Zheng!
When he was thrown up by the steel rope, Luo Zheng had already summoned the power of the nine stars. He condensed this power under his feet, forming an extremely sharp energy blade!
Luo Zheng had learned this move from Ji Luoxue's Crescent Moon Edge.
However, what replaced the Crescent Moon Edge was an extremely condensed power!
"Pa!"
The moment his foot landed on the steel cable, the steel cable was directly trampled into two pieces!
Actually, before Luo Zheng stepped on the steel rope, the power had already torn it apart.
The steel rope that was wrapped around Han Liusu instantly lost its power and slid down from Han Liusu's body. As for the other steel rope, it constantly twisted and quickly withdrew from the Demon Sealing Array.
The Mountain Crumbling Clan and the foreign martial artists had already dodged, leaving an empty space in the middle. Everyone looked at Luo Zheng and Han Liusu behind him …
"This time … I'm afraid it's bad," said a Mountain Crumbling Clan member of the Divine Hunters.

Luo Zheng's actions had completely enraged the True Martial Sacred Beast!
"Hualala …" The steel rope wriggled, creating a huge commotion.
Everyone, including the Divine Hunters' Thunder Toad, stood in place, not daring to move.
