The True Martial Sacred Beast hadn't been injured in countless years, but Luo Zheng had actually cut off one of its limbs!
Facing the steel rope that swept towards him from all directions, Luo Zheng took a deep breath. He turned his hand and pulled Han Liusu behind him, "Come closer to me!"
Han Liusu nodded and pressed herself against Luo Zheng's back!
At this moment, the nine stars within Luo Zheng's inner world began to shine with an unprecedented light. As the people of the continent saw this, they all prostrated themselves on the ground, constantly prostrating themselves in worship. To the people of his inner world, this was the divine decree of the World Entrusting Sage. On this day, every baby born would receive the protection of the gods!
A third of his strength …
Using such a terrifying amount of strength at once, Luo Zheng didn't know what would happen.
When his strength reached a certain level, Luo Zheng found it hard to estimate how much strength he should use. To play it safe, he could only use as much as he could!
Extracting a third of the nine stars' strength in such a short amount of time was already Luo Zheng's limit!
The invisible strength spread out, forming a small sphere about one zhang in diameter. Except for the Han Liusu on Luo Zheng's back, everything within the sphere was annihilated by this force. The dead leaves on the ground, the branches, the soil, the scavengers in the soil, they didn't turn into powder but directly disappeared!
"Weng ~"
When this terrifying power was unleashed, it formed an extremely high-frequency roar. The sharp sound caused everyone to temporarily lose their hearing. No one could hear anything. They only felt that the entire world had fallen into silence!
They looked at Luo Zheng in panic. They looked at the Han Liusu on Luo Zheng's back and the invisible force squeezing the surrounding space, forming a sphere!

Chapter 1514
No matter how those steel ropes whipped, as long as they touched the sphere, they would be annihilated and disappear …
Luo Zheng, who was in the center of the sphere, also relaxed slightly.
Currently, he was unable to directly control this kind of power. If this violent power was injected into his body, his body would most likely be annihilated and disappear in the blink of an eye. Therefore, he could only project it over in the form of a "transcendent divine ability".
Using this kind of power might be inefficient and even wasteful, but the actual power was not to be underestimated!
Han Liusu's entire body was already clinging onto Luo Zheng's body, her pair of beautiful eyes filled with surprise.
She was very lucky that her premonition was correct, but she did not expect it to be correct to such an extent … Previously, Han Liusu thought that Luo Zheng could catch up to her brother, but now it seemed that Luo Zheng had already surpassed Han Cangyan in terms of strength by too much.
This person was destined to bring her to the Subduing Soul Cliff!
The corners of her mouth curled up slightly, but she closed her eyes and rested her head on Luo Zheng's shoulder. If he could not bring her to the Subduing Soul Cliff, then there should be no one else in this world who could.
No matter how those steel ropes whipped the sphere, it was all in vain. The steel ropes were constantly annihilated and disappeared. To the True Martial Sacred Beast, it was also a loss. After all, every steel rope was one of its arms.
Not long after, all the steel ropes retreated at the same time.
After those steel ropes retreated, everyone wiped their sweat at this moment. When they looked at Luo Zheng again, their eyes were filled with a trace of horror.
When Luo Zheng was in the Mountain Collapsing Clan, the moment he displayed his strength, he concentrated the power of the nine stars three inches in front of his fist. At that time, everyone thought that it was Luo Zheng's own strength. Apart from Luo Zheng himself, no one could notice this detail.
But this time, Luo Zheng directly spread his strength out of his body. Naturally, everyone felt different.
They did not think that it was pure strength!
After all, when ordinary martial artists used their strength, they had to drive it through their body. It was impossible to simply "force" the strength out of the body and exist independently.
Like Luo Zheng, who turned his strength into a round egg and completely wrapped himself, it looked more like some kind of divine ability.
But what kind of divine ability could have such strength? Luo Zheng was only at the Extreme Divine Realm. How could he block the attack of the Sacred Beast of True Martial Arts?
Among the external human warriors, the strongest were He Zuowei and the other Sector Lords. But even if He Zuowei used Great Ambition, it was impossible for him to block so many steel cables. Moreover, Luo Zheng had destroyed all the steel cables!
It was impossible for them to know that Luo Zheng was using the World Praising Technique to project his power from his inner world to his body.
A long time after Luo Zheng removed the sphere of power, everyone felt a sharp pain in their ears.
Under the high frequency vibration caused by the huge power, everyone's ears were completely numb, and they couldn't hear anything at all.
After the power disappeared and their ears gradually recovered, there was an unbearable pain. Many warriors covered their ears at this moment. Some weaker warriors even had blood flowing out of their ears.
However, just as everyone regained their hearing, the ground began to shake again!
Boom!
Boom!
Boom!
Thunderous footsteps came from far away. The sound was very dense. It was obvious that a huge monster was approaching at high speed.
Hualala …
The trees in the distance fell down one by one.
Every tremor seemed to be trampling on everyone's hearts!
As the vibration got closer, the ground shook more and more violently. With every footstep, everyone would instantly jump two or three inches into the air. Some warriors even purposely kept their feet on the ground.
Rumble, rumble …
As the huge trees in front of them fell to the sides, the huge monster finally appeared in everyone's line of sight.
Everyone looked up at the huge monster.
"This is the Sacred Beast of True Martial Arts?" Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes.
The Sacred Beast of True Martial Arts looked like a huge snake, but it had four legs and a huge crown on its head. Countless tiny steel cables extended from the crown, and these steel cables were constantly wriggling. It turned out that the steel cables were its whiskers.
The strange thing was that half of the body of the Sacred Beast of True Martial Arts was made of the greenish-gray metal. The other half of its body was light green, and the surface of its skin was grid-like, with diamond-shaped scales embedded in it.
In terms of size, the Sacred Beast of True Martial Arts was not particularly huge. It was only slightly taller than the surrounding huge trees, about sixty to seventy feet tall. This size was much smaller than the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede that Luo Zheng had seen before.
However, when this Sacred Beast of True Martial Arts appeared in front of everyone, everyone, including Luo Zheng, felt an intense pressure!
It was as if such a powerful existence should not appear in this world!
Although the Mountain Collapsing Clan was known for their bravery, at this moment, they did not even dare to breathe loudly. They knew that there was a Sacred Beast of True Martial Arts here, but for countless years, the true appearance of this Sacred Beast of True Martial Arts had always been a legend. No one had seen its true appearance. At most, they had only seen the tentacles of the Sacred Beast of True Martial Arts, which were the steel cables.
Luo Zheng's strength might have reached a terrifying level, but he still did not have the strength to fight against the Sacred Beast of True Martial Arts. Strength was very important to a warrior, but in many cases, it was not a decisive existence.
In front of such a powerful existence, Luo Zheng once again felt insignificant …
"Boom!"
The Sacred Beast of True Martial Arts took another step forward. Its pair of blood-red eyes stared at Luo Zheng, as well as the Han Liusu behind Luo Zheng.
This gaze was like a solid substance, giving Luo Zheng a huge sense of pressure.
Even if Luo Zheng's strength had increased countless times, it did not give him any sense of security!
The best choice he could make now was to hand over Han Liusu. However, Luo Zheng had received this woman's help. In exchange, he had also agreed to bring her to the Soul Suppressing Cliff. Naturally, he did not have any thoughts of compromising!
Thus, this exchange of gazes continued.
Not far away, the Thunder Toad gritted his teeth. He also wanted to persuade Luo Zheng, but in the end, he did not say anything.
"Huala …"
A steel rope beard extended from the crown of the Sacred Beast of True Martial Arts, slowly curling towards Luo Zheng.
The steel rope beard's target was still Han Liusu!
However, the moment it extended in front of Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng suddenly waved his hand. Under the burst of energy, it formed a sharp arc.
"Whoosh!"
In an instant, the steel rope was cut off.
Hearing the sound of the steel rope beard breaking, everyone's heart trembled!
If this Sacred Beast of True Martial Arts was enraged, how could they survive?
The most depressed ones were the foreign warriors from the God Realm. Previously, when they heard the Mountain Collapsing Clan mention the Sacred Beast of True Martial Arts, they were still wondering what it was.
Now that they saw the true face of this thing, many warriors recognized it. They cursed in their hearts. This was a motherf * cking Black Shepherd Weasel!
A True Spirit hatched from Ruo Mu!
In the God Realm, there were only seven or eight of them. One of them was heavily injured and was subdued by the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. This Black Shepherd Weasel had never shown itself. No one expected it to obtain half a puppet body and become like this …
The most frightening thing was that this Luo Zheng was planning to compete with this Black Shepherd Weasel?
An extreme God Realm brat competing with a True Spirit?
Was he crazy?
Many of the foreign warriors were thinking this, including the little old man, Yao An. He was also secretly regretting. He originally planned to leave the Mountain Collapsing Clan. This time, he followed the team to secretly observe Luo Zheng. He didn't expect to encounter such a situation. He was extremely unlucky.
"Luo, Luo Zheng … Hand over the woman behind you," one of the warriors advised.
Luo Zheng turned a deaf ear. His black eyes were filled with determination as he continued to stare at the Black Shepherd Weasel.
After cutting off a strand of the Black Shepherd Weasel's beard, the other party still didn't choose to attack. Luo Zheng's determination wasn't reckless. This thing seemed to be afraid of something. Or perhaps it wasn't willing to fight with him?
After a while, the Black Shepherd Weasel once again extended its steel-like beard, curling towards the Han Liusu behind Luo Zheng.
Seeing the steel-like beard curl towards him, Luo Zheng felt his back tighten. The warm and fragrant jade behind him was tightly strangling his throat. He could feel Han Liusu's nervousness and fear.
The hearts of He Zuowei and the others were also in their throats. As the Saint Emperor's men, their knowledge was much greater than ordinary God Realm warriors. They were even more clear on the origin of this Black Shepherd Weasel. If the Eldest Princess was really swallowed by this Black Shepherd Weasel, the Saint Emperor wouldn't be able to do anything.
However, Luo Zheng still punched out. An invisible crescent moon suddenly condensed and cut off the steel-like beard. Luo Zheng didn't have the slightest intention of retreating.
After being blocked twice by Luo Zheng, the Black Shepherd Weasel suddenly raised its head.
"Aooo!"
A sharp roar burst out, and all the trees in the Giant Tree Forest began to sway. Even the martial artists present were unable to stand firm. A large number of the martial artists from the Snake Hunting Team and the Tiger Hunting Team suddenly fell to the ground, and some even fainted!
Luo Zheng also felt a throbbing pain in his head. But, he still stood in place, motionless.
As the Black Shepherd Weasel roared, it stretched its long head forward. Only when it was right in front of Luo Zheng did it stop …
Luo Zheng tightly clenched his fists. He stared at the Black Shepherd Weasel that was close by. He could smell the fishy smell coming from its nose, and his heart began to crazily beat.

Chapter 1515
Under such gazes, Luo Zheng felt a great amount of pressure.
However, facing the scarlet eyes of the Black Shepherd Weasel, Luo Zheng didn't feel any strong killing intent other than the pressure.
If this Black Shepherd Weasel was really a spirit pet raised by a Saint, it would have many ways to kill him. It wouldn't be so troublesome. Perhaps with one move, it would be able to kill everyone present.
However, Luo Zheng didn't know what the Black Shepherd Weasel was planning. It would be a lie if he said he wasn't nervous.
At this moment, Lei Toad had already decided to give up on Luo Zheng. This young man was too impulsive. However, if this person died in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, the power source that he obtained would still be returned to this world.
After staring at each other for a while, the Black Shepherd Weasel suddenly raised its head and roared towards the sky. Its scarlet eyes glared at Luo Zheng before it disappeared into the forest.
Even though the figure of the Black Shepherd Weasel had completely disappeared before everyone's eyes, no one dared to move. They could only hear the heavy footsteps gradually moving further and further away. After it had completely disappeared, everyone's heart that was in the air finally calmed down.
"Hu …" Lei Toad exhaled. However, his expression didn't look good. However, as the leader, it wasn't good for him to say anything.
However, a member of the Mountain Shattering Tribe suddenly jumped up. His hands crossed like an agile monkey as he extended his hand towards Han Liusu's head.
This member of the Mountain Shattering Tribe actually wanted to kill Han Liusu directly!
The Mountain Shattering Tribe believed that Han Liusu was Han Cangyan's sister. At the same time, they also knew the status of the Han Family in the Divine Realm. This colossus was on the same level as the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. However, so what?
If they couldn't leave the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, everything would be in vain.
Luo Zheng was the hope of the Mountain Shattering Tribe. With this troublesome person by their side, it would be a hidden danger.
"Chi! Don't be impulsive! "Lei Toad's expression changed.
This member of the Mountain Shattering Tribe called "Chi" clearly didn't have a good temper like Lei Toad. They had almost been killed by this woman. At this moment, they only wanted to kill Han Liusu.
However, he didn't consider Luo Zheng's thoughts.
Fortunately, before he could get close to Han Liusu, He Zuowei and the others had already blocked his path.
"Get lost!" Yu said coldly.
"You're courting death!" He Zuowei didn't think that the Mountain Shattering Tribesmen would suddenly make a move.
The strength of this "Hui" in the Mountain Crumbling Race was comparable to the Thunder Toad. Perhaps it was unable to bear the Mandate of Heaven in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, but in terms of pure strength, this "Hui" was already comparable to a Heavenly Venerable who specialized in body refinement!
Under the impact of his body, how could the few sector lords block him?
Two of the sector lords were lightly touched by the "Hui" and groaned, their internal organs were instantly shifted and they were thrown to the side!
However, He Zuowei and the other Sector Lords were ultimately from the Divine Realm!
In the eyes of the martial artists of the Realm of the Gods, strength was only a standard to measure a martial artist. But in a battle between martial artists, what mattered most was their comprehensive strength. Even if one's pure strength was too great, it didn't mean they were invincible. They had many methods to resolve or even weaken their opponent's strength!
He Zuowei flicked his finger and an emerald green scepter appeared in his left hand. He pressed his finger on the scepter and drew in the air. Soon, a fishing net appeared in front of him. This fishing net was light as if it was weightless and drifted in the air like flying cotton. Very quickly, it enveloped the "Hui".
The moment the "Hui" was enveloped, all the strength in his body was exhausted. The momentum of his charge came to a halt and his entire body went limp. It seemed that even standing up was extremely difficult!
"This fishing net …" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed.
If Luo Zheng didn't use the power of nine stars, his strength would be far inferior to this Divine Hunter. However, this fishing net was extremely mysterious. It was actually able to drain the strength of the Mountain Shattering Tribesmen?
"That's the Force Wheel Rope Net." After noticing Luo Zheng's expression, Han Liusu didn't miss the opportunity to explain. "This is a grandmist treasure. It can completely absorb strength and reincarnate it into every thread."
In other words, after the Force Wheel Rope Net absorbed the strength of the "Hui" and then applied it to the "Hui", under this reaction, the greater the strength of the Hui, the greater the strength of the net would be applied to him. Naturally, he wouldn't be able to break free from this fishing net.
Seeing this scene, Lei Toad also let out a sigh of relief. This "Hui"'s temper was too fiery. It had almost ruined their plan.
Just now, Luo Zheng was willing to risk his life to protect Han Liusu. How could Luo Zheng back down in front of the Hui?
Afterwards, under Lei Toad's intervention, He Zuowei and the others removed the Force Wheel Rope Net. At the same time, they also persuaded the Hui to return …
"Alright, we can continue." Han Liusu smiled faintly. On Luo Zheng's back, she didn't feel nervous at all. On the contrary, she felt a strong sense of security.
Luo Zheng nodded and said, "Then when do you plan to come down …"
Hearing this, Han Liusu stuck out her tongue and slid down from Luo Zheng's back.
After experiencing this True Martial Sacred Beast, the foreign martial artists instantly became much more obedient. It wasn't easy for them to cultivate in the Mountain Collapsing Clan. Who would be willing to lose their lives? This Forbidden Land of God Refinement was originally a rather ridiculous place. If they accidentally died, they could only blame themselves for being unlucky.
This team began to stabilize. In addition, the Mountain Collapsing Clan was very experienced. The rest of the journey was much more stable.
When they encountered a few strange beasts, the Tiger Hunting Team and Dragon Hunting Team would personally go and kill them …
After passing through the Giant Tree Forest, they entered the Crossing Disaster Forest. The number of strange beasts inside had increased by several times. However, as long as they didn't encounter a powerful existence like the Heavenly Witch Sacred Beast, the Tiger Hunting Team wouldn't be afraid of it. Even if it was a strange beast like the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede, the Tiger Hunting Team wouldn't be afraid of it.
After fourteen to fourteen hours, the group passed through the Crossing Disaster Forest again. What appeared in front of them was a strange sight. It was a heavenly river that stretched across the sky.
This river was completely suspended in mid-air. It curved left and right like a snake without a head or tail.
"It's getting dark. Everyone, hurry up. After passing through this Heavenly River, we'll reach the Sky Vault Clan's territory," someone from the Mountain Collapsing Clan commanded.
In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, day and night were two completely different worlds. Powerful existences like the Non-Phase Heavenly Centipede would normally only come out at night. However, the True Martial Sacred Beast was an exception.
"Sky Vault Clan? Why are we going to the Sky Vault Clan? "someone asked.
"This operation is a joint operation of three Cultivation Holy Lands. Why are you asking so much?" Seeing that the person who asked was only a foreign martial artist from the Snake Hunting Team, the Mountain Collapsing Clan member coldly replied.
If it was in the past, the Mountain Collapsing Clan would have returned to the Mountain Collapsing Clan by now. However, it was different this time. They directly passed through the Crossing Disaster Forest and met up with the Sky Vault Clan.
Other than the Sky Vault Clan, the Myriad Elephant Clan would also participate in this operation.
Many years ago, the natives of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement had already planned several operations. However, this time, the three clans would join forces at the same time.
Regarding Luo Zheng, the Mountain Collapsing Clan had already passed the information to the Myriad Elephant Clan and the Sky Vault Clan. They called Luo Zheng the "Fire Repellent", which meant that he was a person who was not afraid of fire. Only Luo Zheng was the key to achieving their goal.
As for where they were going and what Luo Zheng was going to take away, the Mountain Collapsing Clan had always been unwilling to reveal. However, the Thunder Toad had promised many times that the three major clans would do their best to protect Luo Zheng.
After passing through the Heavenly River, everyone instantly felt a strong wave of True Origin. They had arrived at the Sky Vault Clan's territory.
The Sky Vault Clan could cultivate the Beginning and End True Origin. It was a Cultivation Holy Land for martial artists!
However, the foreign martial artists of the Mountain Collapsing Clan mainly cultivated their bodies. A small number of them also cultivated both energy and body. However, they were not very interested in the Sky Vault Clan.
Luo Zheng's situation was rather special. Although he had also constructed an Inner World, the True Origin in his body had been replaced by the Chaotic Qi. The True Origin here was even more useless to him.
However, the Chaotic Qi and True Origin had similar attributes. If the Sky Vault Clan had some special techniques to use True Origin, Luo Zheng would also choose to absorb some of them.
Not long after entering the Sky Vault Clan's territory, several teams came to welcome them.
The Sky Vault Clan's size was not much different from humans. However, their eye sockets were deep, their hair was golden, and everyone had a pair of pure white wings. Looking at their dressing, they were very simple and crude like the Mountain Collapsing Clan. They only used a few pieces of cloth to cover their private parts, revealing their strong muscles.

Chapter 1516
Compared to the Collapsing Mountain Clan's boldness, the Sky Dome Clan's people were more reserved and taciturn.
Under their guidance, everyone stepped into the Sky Dome Clan's tribe.
The Sky Dome Clan didn't have such a terrifying gravitational force, but their buildings were very strange. There were countless tall towers erected in the tribe!
Below the tower was a pitch-black swamp.
At a glance, the top of each tower was covered in white. It turned out that there were many Sky Dome Clan members squatting on top of each tower. When their white wings spread out, it formed a sea of intertwining wings.
From time to time, some Sky Dome Clan members would soar into the sky and land there.
The Collapsing Mountain Clan was a clan that revered power, but the Sky Dome Clan yearned for the sky and freedom.
Unfortunately, the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was only a huge cave. What these clans loved the most was to listen to foreign martial artists describe the vastness of the Divine Territory. The vastness of the territory was so vast that even the Sky Dome Clan couldn't reach the end of it in their lifetime …
All of this made the Sky Dome Clan very envious.
Therefore, compared to the Collapsing Mountain Clan, the Sky Dome Clan was more interested in finding out the purpose of the owner of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. They hoped to get a chance to walk out of this cave.
The Collapsing Mountain Clan's arrival was warmly received by the Sky Dome Clan.
The Sky Dome Clan's towers were arranged in picturesque disorder. Some were high while some were low. Because of this, one could distinguish the status of the Sky Dome Clan at a glance.
The higher the status, the higher the height of the tower they owned.
The difference between the lowest tower and the highest tower was as large as three thousand meters.
The Sky Dome Clan arranged for the Collapsing Mountain Clan to stay at the top of the tallest tower.
When night fell, all the Sky Dome Clan members returned to their nest. The leader of the Sky Dome Clan was wearing a golden crown. He was the leader of the Sky Dome Clan, Gong Yu.
After a simple introduction, Gong Yu's gaze fell on Luo Zheng.
As the key person, Gong Yu felt that it was necessary to personally measure whether Luo Zheng could really resist the Mysterious Flame.
With a light wave of his hand, the True Essence in the surroundings began to surge rapidly, and a bright yellow flame appeared in his hand ….
Although Gong Yu understood that Luo Zheng was the key person, he obviously didn't take this Extreme Divine Stage kid seriously. Without asking for Luo Zheng's permission, he rashly wanted to burn Luo Zheng with this flame.
This scene made Luo Zheng frown slightly.
The flame of the Palace Feather was formed from the power of the Law and the True Essence. Perhaps it was extremely ingenious, but Luo Zheng was not afraid of it. However, Luo Zheng was not a masochist. He held the flame and burned himself without saying a word. What was going on?
After entering the Mountain Crumbling Clan to train, Luo Zheng's strength had undergone a complete transformation.
Previously, he was not strong enough and could not resist the Collapsing Mountain Clan's manipulation. However, the Collapsing Mountain Clan did not have too much malicious intent, so he did not pursue the matter. Now, however, Luo Zheng was not limited to this!
Seeing this situation, the careless Lei Toad took the initiative to stand out, "Brother Gong Yu, hold on!"
Gong Yu's green eyes flashed as he asked curiously, "What's wrong?"
Lei Toad was a little embarrassed. He did not explain Luo Zheng's strength in advance. With Gong Yu's status, there was indeed no need for him to respect a Extreme Divine Stage warrior from another clan. "Luo Zheng belongs to our Divine Hunting Team."
His words were very vague. In fact, he was hinting at Luo Zheng's strength. He was qualified to enter the Divine Hunting Team. In terms of pure strength, he was already comparable to the top of the Mountain Crumbling Clan.
However, Gong Yu did not understand. He only thought that Luo Zheng was able to enter the Divine Hunting Team because he was a Fire Repellent. Otherwise, how could this guy be on the same level as Lei Toad and the others?
"This is not important, right?" Gong Yu said indifferently as the flame in his hand danced.
However, Lei Toad insisted. He even took a step forward and stood in front of Gong Yu, "Luo Zheng was tested in our Mountain Crumbling Clan and received the title of Barbarian King."
Hearing this, Gong Yu's face froze, "Brother Lei Toad, you must be joking. Or does this guy not have the Fire Repellent Divine Ability? Brother Lei Toad, are you trying to make fun of our Sky Dome Clan? The Myriad Elephant Clan will be here in a few days. Brother Lei Toad, you have to take responsibility for making fun of our two Cultivation Holy Grounds! "
Gong Yu did not believe Lei Toad's words at all. In addition, Lei Toad was trying to cover things up, which made Gong Yu even more suspicious. This Luo Zheng might just be a cover. However, he could not understand why Lei Toad would lie to him.
Lei Toad smiled bitterly. He knew that Gong Yu had misinterpreted his words. He could only continue, "I am telling the truth!"
Seeing Lei Toad's serious expression, Gong Yu's eyes were filled with doubt again. He stared at Luo Zheng behind Lei Toad and said, "Whether it is the truth or not, I have to try it myself. Why are you stopping me?"
"I am not stopping you. I just hope that Brother Gong Yu can ask for Luo Zheng's permission," Lei Toad replied.
Lei Toad also knew that Luo Zheng was not a difficult person to talk to. The premise was that you had to show him respect. This was not a conversation between the strong and the weak. He could ignore Luo Zheng's suggestion. Moreover, in Lei Toad's opinion, Luo Zheng's strength was probably much stronger than Lei Toad himself!
Gong Yu finally understood what was going on. He looked down on this Luo Zheng. Was this guy really someone?
In order to get the result, Gong Yu could only restrain his temper. "Kid, it is said that all the flames in the world can't do anything to you. I want to use the Strange Flame in my hand to test you. Is that okay?"
As the leader of the Sky Dome Clan, he had already made an exception to talk to an Extreme God Realm warrior from another clan. In his opinion, he had already shown enough respect.
However, when Lei Toad heard this, he knew that he was in trouble …
Before he could open his mouth, Luo Zheng said with a smile, "I refuse."
There were other higher-ups of the Sky Dome Clan present. When they heard Luo Zheng's words, their expressions became very ugly. They did not expect that this guy would dare to refuse their leader's request.
Gong Yu raised his eyebrows slightly and sneered, "You are just a warrior from another clan. Do you have the right to refuse?"
Luo Zheng's expression did not change at all. "Qualifications are fought for by strength. It is not decided by you or me."
The warriors from the Mountain Crushing Clan and the warriors from other clans who were cultivating in the Mountain Crushing Clan were also helpless at this moment. Why were they at daggers drawn?
However, they had seen Luo Zheng's strength …
Although they did not know where Luo Zheng's terrifying strength came from, when he fought against the True Martial Sacred Beast, he had left a deep impression on them.
Lei Toad wanted to continue mediating, but Gong Yu said coldly, "Then let's see if you have the strength to fight for it!"
In the end, he could not help but make a move!
He stretched out his hand and suddenly pointed at Luo Zheng. The bright yellow flame suddenly turned into a slender fire snake and shot toward Luo Zheng.
The rules in each universe were slightly different, but these rules were made by the Saints. When the Saints built the world, they could not escape the influence of the Divine Realm. Therefore, the differences were not too big!
The Sky Dome Clan members all sneered. This kid from another clan did not know the immensity of heaven and earth. If he could resist Gong Yu's red flame, it would prove that he had the Fire Repellent Body. If he could not, he deserved to be burned to death.
Han Liusu sat obediently beside Luo Zheng. She watched the fire snake roll over and curled the corners of her mouth. The grade of this flame was slightly higher than the flame of the Calamity Avoiding Wood, but compared with her Strange Flame, it was a world of difference. It was impossible for it to cause any harm to Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng would not obediently let the other party burn him, right?
Just as Han Liusu had guessed, Luo Zheng sat upright on the spot and did not move in the face of the fire snake. However, with a thought, the nine stars flickered again.
After using one-third of the power of the nine stars during the day, Luo Zheng discovered that the power of the nine stars was not limitless. When Luo Zheng drew too much power, the nine stars would dim.
However, to resist this fire snake, Luo Zheng did not need to draw too much power. About one-thirtieth of the power was more than enough.
This invisible power turned into a strong shield and blocked in front of Luo Zheng. The moment the fire snake rolled over, it was swallowed up by this invisible power and was directly annihilated and dissipated.
Gong Yu saw this scene and his expression became more and more unsightly. With a stroke of his finger, the fire snake continuously shot out. The color gradually transitioned from bright yellow to deep red. He was serious this time.
However, there was no change. It was as if there was an invisible wall in front of Luo Zheng. Even with the power of the fire transformed from his true essence, he could not cross half a step!

Chapter 1517
In the face of Luo Zheng's question, all the Sky Vault Clan members were silent.
The Mountain Crushing Clan guarded the source of power, while the Sky Vault Clan had the Beginning and End Vital Essence!
Everything in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was formed by Vital Essence, but all the Vital Essence came from here. This was the starting point of the Vital Essence of Heaven and Earth!
With such a unique advantage, the Sky Vault Clan's talent in Vital Essence was much stronger than other clans.
The application of Vital Essence to activate all kinds of laws was also very ingenious.
As the leader of the Sky Vault Clan, Gong Yu's strength was unquestionable. At this moment, he had already activated the power of the Fire Law to the extreme, but he still couldn't do anything to Luo Zheng.
What was worse was that Luo Zheng didn't move at all. He seemed to be very relaxed, revealing an unfathomable aura.
He had never taken the Sky Vault Clan seriously.
At this moment, Gong Yu somewhat believed Lei Toad's words. This guy really got the title of Barbarian King, and it wasn't as simple as a title!
However, his words were so overbearing. As the leader of the Sky Vault Clan, he couldn't back down now.
He couldn't really use assassins to kill Luo Zheng. Lei Toad would be the first to disagree.
Seeing that this fire snake couldn't do anything to Luo Zheng, Gong Yu had no choice but to disperse it. He snorted, "I hope you have the strength! Otherwise, the Emerald Fire will burn everything you have! "
"You don't have to worry about that." Luo Zheng laughed softly. "Remember, I'm just cooperating with the Mountain Crushing Clan!"
These words made Gong Yu's expression turn cold again, but Lei Toad continued to smooth things over. At the same time, he agreed with Luo Zheng's words. "Yes, my Mountain Crushing Clan and Luo Zheng are just cooperating. Brother Gong Yu's attitude is wrong."
"Brother Lei Toad, you!"
Cooperating with warriors from other clans? That would depend on whether Luo Zheng was important enough.
Even when Han Cangyan became the leader, there were all kinds of dissatisfaction in the Mountain Crushing Clan. These six clans were very xenophobic. For example, there were often clansmen in the Mountain Crushing Clan who requested to seal off the Sacred Spring Mountain to prevent warriors from other clans from entering!
This kind of thing often happened in the Sky Vault Clan, Myriad Elephant Clan, and other clans.
However, the rules of the six cultivation sacred grounds were set by the Prophets, and the Prophets followed the orders of the Master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. It was impossible for them to try to change these rules!
Therefore, even though the six great races were extremely xenophobic, they still reluctantly accepted foreign martial artists to cultivate, and reluctantly nurtured foreign martial artists …
This sort of unwillingness deepened the xenophobic hearts of the natives. They believed that these foreign martial artists were here to take advantage of them.
Now that Lei Toad said that he would cooperate with Luo Zheng, lowering himself, Gong Yu almost doubted that he had heard it wrong. But looking at Lei Toad's words and attitude, it was not strange for him to say such words.
Seeing Gong Yu's expression, Lei Toad couldn't help but sigh. He strode forward and whispered something in Gong Yu's ear.
After hearing Lei Toad's words, Gong Yu's expression changed again. He stared at Luo Zheng, and his tense face gradually relaxed. Then, his attitude took a 180-degree turn, and his tone became unusually polite. "I see. Little friend Luo Zheng cooperated with the Mountain Crushing Clan, and my Sky Vault Clan benefited from it. Just now, Gong Mou spoke rudely and offended you. Please forgive me."
After Gong Yu said this, the expressions of the higher-ups of the Sky Vault Clan were quite interesting.
What did Lei Toad say to make their leader's attitude change so drastically in just a few breaths?
The Sky Vault Clan didn't know, but Luo Zheng knew, and so did some shrewd warriors.
Lei Toad must have told Gong Yu about the True Martial Sacred Beast.
The True Martial Sacred Beast was the Spirit Pet of the Master of the Forbidden Land of God, and its status was extraordinary. Not only did Luo Zheng confront the True Martial Sacred Beast, but the Black Shepherd Weasel also chose to leave!
Just this point alone was enough to determine Luo Zheng's extraordinariness.
Although the Black Shepherd Weasel couldn't speak the human language, its intelligence wouldn't be low. It was very unusual for it to let Luo Zheng go, and it was worth guessing!
Who could control the Black Shepherd Weasel? It could only be the Master of the Forbidden Land of God …
Maybe the Mountain Crushing Clan didn't know the Master of the Forbidden Land of God's purpose, and the Prophets kept their mouths shut, but it didn't mean that Lei Toad and the others didn't have their own guesses!
After realizing this, Gong Yu had to bite the bullet and change his attitude even though it was a bit ugly.
After the other party lowered his attitude, Luo Zheng didn't continue to pursue him. He just nodded indifferently. "It's just a small misunderstanding. Leader Gong, you don't have to worry about it."
Gong Yu might be able to put down his face, but the other members of the Sky Dome Clan might not. They didn't know the inside story, and they just hated Luo Zheng to the core …
After this matter was over, it was time to eat.
When the Mountain Crushing Clan came to visit, the Sky Dome Clan's custom was to entertain them with rich food.
Gong Yu waved his hand, and dozens of Sky Dome Clan members flew over with stacks of exquisite plates and wine cups in their hands. They placed the plates in front of the crowd!
However, these plates were empty!
Seeing these plates, Luo Zheng was a bit curious. He didn't know what kind of food the Sky Dome Clan would entertain them with.
Since this place was the Holy Land of Cultivation, the martial artists here were all cultivating True Essence, and they basically didn't need to eat. They just needed to swallow True Essence …
Unexpectedly, what happened next was in line with Luo Zheng's conjecture.
They were indeed different from the Mountain Crushing Clan. The Mountain Crushing Clan needed to hunt large amounts of rare and exotic beasts for food, and the flesh and blood of those beasts had powerful body refining effects.
However, the Sky Dome Clan's food was True Essence!
After those plates were placed one by one, dozens of Sky Dome Clan women appeared again.
These women had slender figures and enchanting figures. Their appearances were naturally extremely beautiful. Compared to those burly Mountain Crushing Clan women, they were like heaven and earth.
When the accompanying foreign martial artists saw these women, they felt that they were a feast for the eyes, and their appetites were aroused!
Those women were facing the plates, clasping their hands together, and closing their eyes to meditate …
"Visualization method …" Luo Zheng was slightly stunned.
Under the woman's visualization, the rich True Essence in this place began to condense unceasingly. From the plates, streaks of jade-like luster began to flicker, and bursts of fragrance also emerged. On the exquisite plates, there were all kinds of fruits and meat, and in the cups, there was also fragrant wine!
Luo Zheng was rather speechless.
Visualization method was the entry-level method to control True Essence!
However, martial artists could directly absorb True Essence and True Essence when they cultivated.
The Sky Dome Clan, on the other hand, maintained their tradition of eating. However, they didn't eat meat paste, and they didn't eat grains either. Thus, they used this method.
These beautiful women were the kitchen maids of the Sky Dome Clan …
The visualization method that they cultivated since childhood was to visualize all kinds of delicious food.
The most delicious food in the world was ultimately within the imagination. After all, a person's imagination was the most beautiful existence in the world.
Under continuous training, the food that they visualized became peerless delicacies!
Of course, when these things were eaten, they were ultimately just a lump of True Essence. It was similar to absorbing True Essence. The only difference was that they were full of food.
"Hahaha! I've been yearning for the Sky Dome Clan's peerless delicacies day and night. Although this lousy stuff isn't filling, the taste is top-notch! Let's eat this meal first, then we'll dissect the prey! "Thunder Toad laughed, already impatiently reaching his hand into the plate.
The Mountain Breaker Clan didn't know how to absorb True Essence at all. To them, eating this food was the same as not eating it at all. However, this delicacy was coveted by their clan. Moreover, they had quite a bit of harvest on their way through the Calamity Passing Forest. Prey that was full would be eaten last. Now, they had to satisfy their appetite.
The Sky Dome Clan woman in front of Luo Zheng finished her visualization and bowed slightly to Luo Zheng. "Please eat." After saying that, she floated down and walked to the next martial artist …
Not long after, there were all kinds of exquisite delicacies in front of everyone's plates.
Hearing Thunder Toad's exaggerated description, Luo Zheng was curious. He casually put some of the visualized grapes into his mouth, and a sweet taste immediately formed in his mouth. Although the sweetness was rich, it didn't feel greasy at all. It was indeed very different from what he had tasted before!

Chapter 1518
In addition to fruits, there was also good wine and meat. After tasting them, Luo Zheng couldn't help but sigh. The Sky Dome Clan really knew how to enjoy life.
These Sky Dome Clan women were also extremely powerful. Their imagination in food simply surpassed Luo Zheng's imagination. They could perfectly combine taste and true essence …
All the Mountain Shattering Clan members and foreign martial artists ate happily.
After finishing the food on the plate, the women would come back and close their eyes to meditate again. They transformed their true essence into food, and each time, the food was different. It was simply everything!
After finishing this special meal, the Sky Dome Clan arranged accommodations for everyone.
They still had to stay in the Sky Dome Clan's territory for a few days. They needed to wait for the Myriad Elephant Clan to come before the three clans started to move.
Luo Zheng stayed in one of the tall towers. After meditating for a night, he once again observed the development of the world in his body.
In these two days, Luo Zheng used the power of the nine stars very frequently. Those nine bright stars were slightly dim …
However, under the evaporation of the sea of chaos, there were traces of chaos energy being absorbed by the nine stars!
Chaos could transform into all things. Everything in the God Domain was derived from chaos, including true essence!
Saints cultivated true essence and transformed the universe. Therefore, everything in the universe was derived from true essence. It wasn't unexpected that the nine stars would absorb chaos energy.
However, thinking about it this way, the creatures in the world in his body were all transformed from chaos. Wouldn't they be of a higher level than him?
Luo Zheng was born in the universe. This way, they were transformed from true essence and could be considered "second-level creatures". The creatures in his body, on the other hand, were of a higher level …
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng couldn't help but smile.
Eighteen hours later, the Forbidden Land of God Refinement welcomed light.
On a tall tower dozens of feet away from Luo Zheng, a graceful figure flashed slightly. That person's toes touched the ground. That person's face was blooming with a faint smile. It was Han Liusu.
"Let's go. I'll take you to a place!" Han Liusu said with a smile. She leaned forward and pulled Luo Zheng up.
"Where?" Luo Zheng asked.
"A fun place!"
After Han Liusu finished speaking, she kept jumping on these tall towers and fled into the distance.
Luo Zheng looked curious and followed them all the way …
The number of towers in the Sky Vault Clan was astounding. As they moved forward, the height of the towers became shorter and shorter. The tops of the towers were almost touching the black water and could only be regarded as stone piers sticking out of the water.
The stone blocks in the distance formed a path that extended above the black water.
Although they could fly here, with Han Liusu leading the way, Luo Zheng still obediently followed behind. He couldn't figure out where this woman was taking him.
As they walked along the short stone pier, a Sky Vault Clan member blocked their path. "The Tidal Surge is behind us! It's the trial ground of my Sky Vault Clan. You can only enter by paying the Trial Talisman! "
Although each Cultivation Sanctuary had different cultivation methods, they all had to pay the Trial Talisman. The Sky Vault Clan was no exception.
How could Luo Zheng take out a Trial Talisman?
However, this matter couldn't stop Han Liusu …
Han Jia had their own city in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. The purple talisman might be precious, and it wasn't easy for the other members of Han Jia to obtain, but for people like Han Liusu and Han Cangyan, it wasn't much.
The corners of Han Liusu's mouth curled up slightly. With a wave of her hand, two purple Trial Talismans flew toward the Sky Vault Clan member!
After the Sky Vault Clan member caught the two Trial Talismans and inspected them, he didn't say anything and directly let them go.
As he continued to walk forward, Luo Zheng noticed that the black water under his feet began to surge. Wave after wave, it kept rolling forward.
At the same time, several wide platforms appeared in front of them. They were like giant mushrooms that stood on top of the black water.
There were thousands of martial artists on these platforms.
Most of them were foreign martial artists. There were also Sky Vault Clan members, but their numbers were very small.
Luo Zheng could understand this.
Martial artists who cultivated their bodies were relatively small in number. Most of them cultivated their true essence!
Most of the martial artists who entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement would set the Sky Vault Clan's cultivation holy land as their first target. After obtaining a purple Trial Talisman, they would naturally enter this place as soon as possible …
If Luo Zheng had been born in a normal place and obtained a purple Trial Talisman, he probably wouldn't have chosen the Sky Vault Clan. After all, cultivating his true essence was useless to him.
When Luo Zheng and Han Liusu entered, the foreign martial artists on the platforms only casually sized them up. When they looked at Luo Zheng, they were expressionless. However, when some of them saw Han Liusu, they frowned slightly, and a trace of fear appeared in their eyes.
However, when they saw that Han Liusu didn't enter the platform, they didn't care too much.
"Where are we going?" Luo Zheng was led by Han Liusu all the way, and he was a little confused.
"Just follow me." Han Liusu laughed mischievously. "However, I have something to trouble you with!"
"What is it?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Help me beat someone up!" Han Liusu said.
"No." Luo Zheng firmly refused. This woman in front of him had indeed helped him before, but Luo Zheng had only agreed to help her enter the Soul Subduing Cliff. He could still do it in the early stages, not to mention that Luo Zheng had saved her life in the Giant Tree Forest.
He wasn't Han Liusu's hired thug.
Han Liusu suddenly turned her head and pursed her lips, revealing a resentful expression. Then, her eyes turned slightly, as if she had thought of something.
As they continued forward, Luo Zheng discovered that these mushroom-like platforms were of different sizes. The deeper they went, the larger the mushrooms became!
At the same time, the strength of the martial artists on these platforms also increased.
In the end, Han Liusu led Luo Zheng up and stepped onto a platform about a thousand feet in diameter. On such a wide platform, there were only a few hundred foreign martial artists, so it seemed very spacious …
The moment Luo Zheng jumped onto this platform, many of the foreign martial artists glared at Luo Zheng, and they all had angry looks on their faces!
Luo Zheng was slightly taken aback. He didn't know why these fellows were so angry at him.
But at this moment, the black water below began to surge violently. A whirlpool appeared below each platform, and the one Luo Zheng was on had the largest whirlpool.
"Hua la la!"
As the sound of fierce waves spread out, the foreign martial artists who were originally glaring at Luo Zheng and Han Liusu no longer had the mood to pay attention to them.
Round divine runes began to appear on the platform. One also appeared below Luo Zheng and Han Liusu's feet.
"Quick, pour your true essence into this divine rune," Han Liusu reminded.
"Why?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"Try it!" Han Liusu urged.
At this moment, Luo Zheng had already guessed what was going on. He wasn't a fool. From the moment he entered, the martial artists on the platforms had already revealed vigilant expressions.
Clearly, the resources on these platforms were limited. According to Luo Zheng's estimation, these resources should be comparable to the power source. After all, the Sky Dome Clan was a cultivation holy land on the same level as the Mountain Shattering Clan. They also had to pay purple trial talismans.
And the resources on each platform were definitely limited. That was why the martial artists were so vigilant and unwilling to let newcomers join.
Now, Luo Zheng's platform was the widest, but there were only a few hundred martial artists on it. This was even more abnormal. The only possibility was that ordinary foreign martial artists didn't dare to step onto this platform!
In addition, Han Liusu had said that she would help him beat people up. Luo Zheng already understood 80% of what was going on.
However, Luo Zheng didn't care too much. He was originally curious about this place and wanted to see what this Surge of Tides was.
Luo Zheng naturally couldn't inject Quintessential Essence into the divine pattern. However, it was extremely easy for him to substitute it with Primal Chaos Aura.
With a thought, Luo Zheng drew out a trace of Primal Chaos Aura. It flowed through the meridians in his legs and poured into the divine pattern under his feet.
Han Liusu was also very focused. Her ten slender fingers intertwined with each other and produced a streak of colorful Quintessential Essence that poured into the divine pattern under her feet.
The instant the Primal Chaos Aura poured in, the divine pattern started to rotate slowly. It was as if something was jumping out from it.
"Hualala!"
At this moment, the black water below was surging more and more violently. The glow of the divine pattern was also becoming more and more intense!
After injecting a streak of Primal Chaos Aura, it was quickly swallowed up by the divine pattern. Luo Zheng could only add another streak of Primal Chaos Aura.
For ordinary divine patterns, injecting a streak of Primal Chaos Aura was more than enough. However, Luo Zheng didn't expect that after injecting several streaks of Primal Chaos Aura, they would all be absorbed by the divine pattern under his feet. It was like a bottomless pit!
A look of curiosity crossed his face, but he didn't know what use this divine pattern had. However, the amount of Primal Chaos Aura in his inner world was massive, so this wasn't much at all. Thus, Primal Chaos Aura continued to pour into the divine pattern. Although the amount of Primal Chaos Aura he poured into the divine pattern wasn't little, it was still a drop in the ocean of Primal Chaos.
The black water's tidal waves lasted for about the time it took for an incense stick to burn, and then it quickly returned to its original calm state.
Just as the black water calmed down, the divine pattern under everyone's feet began to shoot out black crystals!
These black crystals were about the size of a finger. As they fell to the ground, they emitted crisp tinkling sounds!
As the black crystals shot out, many martial artists revealed looks of joy. They immediately grabbed them in their hands, as if they were afraid that they would run away.
Every martial artist had three or four black crystals by their side. Most of them chose to swallow them as soon as possible …
The larger the platform, the more black crystals could be extracted!
The platform that Luo Zheng and Han Liusu were on was the largest platform, at least seven or eight times larger than the surrounding platforms. In addition, there weren't many martial artists on this platform, and each time the divine runes beneath their feet were refined, there were at least 30 to 40 divine runes!
But in this round, including Han Liusu, none of the hundreds of martial artists had extracted a single black crystal …
At this moment, all of them were staring at Luo Zheng, as if they wished they could kill Luo Zheng. In addition, there were several complex emotions in their widened eyes, such as bewilderment and disbelief!
This was because the divine pattern under Luo Zheng's feet was spitting out these black crystals with all its might. In the blink of an eye, the black crystals around Luo Zheng had piled up into a small mountain. There were at least four or five thousand black crystals …
Feeling the intense hatred of these martial artists, Luo Zheng revealed an innocent smile. He shrugged his shoulders in apology, indicating that he didn't know what these things were.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1519
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that he had committed an enormous taboo by stepping onto the largest platform.
Although the Six Great Cultivation Holy Lands were on equal footing, the Sky Vault Clan was undoubtedly the most popular!
Not only were there many martial artists cultivating the True Essence system, but the most important thing was that the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement cultivated the Beginning and End True Essence, which was extremely famous in the Realm of the Gods!
Absorbing the Beginning and End True Essence here was equivalent to being personally taught by a Saint!
In the vast Realm of the Gods, how many Saints would hand over their personal teachings to outsiders?
Thus, the Sky Vault Clan had become an important place in the eyes of many forces. They would all think of ways to occupy a place in the Sky Vault Clan.
Even the hundreds of people on the circular platform that Luo Zheng was on were considered to have transcendent status in the Realm of the Gods!
They occupied the largest platform, so who would dare to fight with them?
Everyone was clear about the amount of Beginning and End True Essence that could be extracted from this platform, but they could only look on enviously. They simply didn't have the qualifications to join this platform …
Luo Zheng stepped onto this platform unknowingly.
If it were any other time, Luo Zheng would be the first to be attacked. The martial artists on this platform were all ruthless characters. They would definitely gang up and attack him. Any martial artist that joined this platform would have to pay a considerable price.
However, Luo Zheng and Han Liusu stepped onto this platform at a rather coincidental time. It was just when the Tidal Surge was activated.
Everyone was busy refining the Beginning and End True Essence, which was that kind of black crystal.
This didn't mean that they would let Luo Zheng go. They would wait until they extracted the Beginning and End True Essence. Every piece of Beginning and End True Essence was incomparably important. No one was willing to waste it.
In their opinion, Luo Zheng and Han Liusu could only extract ten to twenty pieces of Beginning and End True Essence. In a while, they would just obediently hand it over.
However, the result was completely out of their expectations …
The Sky Vault Clan had their own reasons for setting up these refining divine patterns on these platforms. After all, this was a Holy Land of Cultivation. With confrontation and competition, everyone's speed of improvement would be faster.
Whoever poured in the most True Essence would be able to extract the most Beginning and End True Essence.
When the tide came, all the black water would gather at the pillar below the platform. The pillar was specially made of a special material that could sift out the Qi contained in the black water.
Then, they were refined by the divine patterns and distributed to the hands of many martial artists.
The stronger one was, the more Beginning and End true essence one could refine. Therefore, even if they were on the same platform, a martial artist could refine 50 Beginning and End true essences while another martial artist could only refine 10. This was the embodiment of the difference in strength.
However, Luo Zheng's chaotic energy was clearly many times stronger than Zhen Yuan!
If Heaven and Earth Yuan Qi was the foundation of the entire Divine Realm, then Chaos Energy was the foundation of the entire Chaos Energy!
After Luo Zheng injected such a large amount of Chaos, it produced an unacceptable result.
He had almost monopolized all of the Beginning and End Vital Essence. The black crystals that should have been scattered under the feet of many martial artists were now all piled up beside Luo Zheng …
"Han Liusu!" An angry voice transmitted over.
In the center of the platform was a woman wearing a purple robe. The woman's skirt was long and slender, and it spread out from her waist, revealing a pair of slender white jade legs. This woman was fiercely glaring at Han Liusu and Luo Zheng, her extremely beautiful face filled with rage.
Beside this woman was a young master wearing a jade-colored brocade robe. This young master's eyes were also emitting a cold light.
"Hey! Little sister, what business do you have with me? " Han Liusu's face was smiling. With a casual wave of her hand, a series of clinking sounds rang out. Dozens of Beginning and End true essence gathered in her hand. She gently blew a breath of air, and those black crystals instantly melted, transforming into streams of black light that were absorbed into her body through her nose.
"Little sister?" Hearing Han Liusu's response, Luo Zheng was slightly stunned.
Whoosh!
The purple-robed woman's figure flashed, and she appeared in front of the two. Her beautiful eyes glared at Han Liusu, "Who's your little sister!"
Han Liusu was just about to speak when the young master in brocade robe appeared. He frowned as he sized up the pile of black crystals beside Luo Zheng, but he smiled at Han Liusu, "Big sister has found a strong helper, but it seems to be only a secondary life form?"
"Humph, who's your big sister!" This time, it was Han Liusu's turn to deny.
The young master in brocade robe didn't seem to mind. He smiled at Han Liusu, but turned to stare at Luo Zheng. His tone had a 180-degree change, "You're just a small secondary life form. You don't have the qualifications to enter this place. Do you want me to kick you down, or do you want to roll down yourself?"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's brows immediately furrowed.
No matter what Han Liusu's plan was, he didn't want to participate.
Even though this pile of black crystals seemed to be quite precious, they were almost useless to Luo Zheng. He didn't need any True Core Strength.
However, the young master in brocade robe's words were too harsh. Even though Luo Zheng could remain calm under any provocation, it didn't mean that he was afraid of trouble!
Just as Luo Zheng was about to answer, he didn't expect that another figure would fly up from behind him. Then, he steadily stopped beside Luo Zheng and patted Luo Zheng's shoulder, "Brother Luo Zheng!"
"Dugu?" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed.
It was Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao, who had finally obtained a purple Trial Talisman two months ago. It had been two months since he entered the sacred cultivation place of the Celestial Vault Clan.
"Haha, I didn't expect you to come so late. I thought you would be the first to obtain a purple Trial Talisman!" Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao said with a smile, "Ku Deng and Ji Luoxue are also here!"
While he was talking, another two figures landed on the platform. They were Ji Luoxue and Ku Deng.
After arriving, they greeted Luo Zheng one by one. However, Luo Zheng also noticed that the three of them were faintly vigilant against the warriors on the platform. Obviously, these Dao Disciples didn't want to become enemies with them.
After saying this, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao immediately put on a smile, "Brother Qin, this is the first time my friend has come here. He doesn't know the rules here. He's innocent. Please don't be angry with him."
The three of them had been cultivating here for a while, so they were familiar with the rules here.
Ji Luoxue and Ku Deng were God's favored sons in the universe. However, there was no Celestial Master's protection here. Moreover, these guys' backers were all experts in the God Realm. They couldn't be compared with second-level creatures like them!
What was worse was that these people all had powerful backers.
For example, this Young Master Qin in a brocade robe. His family directly occupied a birthplace. Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao and Ku Deng were both born in that birthplace. They knew that this Young Master Qin's background was too unusual …
At first, the three of them were busy refining the Beginning and End Real Essence, so they didn't notice Luo Zheng.
When they were almost done, they suddenly found that the atmosphere here wasn't right. At first glance, they saw a warrior refining a large pile of black crystals next to him. However, there were only a few other warriors. They were wondering who this guy was.
After looking at him carefully, they found him familiar. Wasn't that Luo Zheng? This was a huge problem!
Therefore, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao hurried over and apologized to Young Master Qin.
Young Master Qin didn't pay too much attention to Luo Zheng. At first, he just told Luo Zheng to get out. However, after noticing the pile of black crystals, he immediately changed his mind. He smiled coldly at Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao and said, "You know the rules here?"
"I don't know. Young Master Qin, please enlighten me." Seeing Luo Zheng's gloomy face and wanting to say something, Young Master Qin suddenly pulled Luo Zheng and said with a smile.
At the same time, Ku Deng and Ji Luoxue muttered in their hearts, "This is troublesome. I'm afraid this Young Master Qin won't be kind."
In the end, these platforms belonged to the Sky Vault Clan. Although they were currently occupied by the martial artists of the God Realm, there were no "rules" in place. Those who didn't know what was going on would usually be driven away by the Sky Vault Clan if they jumped onto the platforms at will. If there were some troublemakers who were unconvinced, they would probably be beaten up and thrown into the black water below.
Now that some rules had suddenly appeared, this guy must be up to no good.
"Anyone who casually steps onto this platform must first break their legs. This is the rule." Young Master Qin's face suddenly revealed a trace of viciousness. "If the four of you want to leave, break your own legs first!"
The four people he was referring to were naturally Luo Zheng, Ji Luoxue, Ku Deng and Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao.
He didn't dare to break Han Liusu's legs!

Chapter 1520
That Young Master Qin suddenly set this rule, and the other martial artists on the platform all revealed strange smiles.
Those who had the qualifications to enter this platform to refine the beginning and end true essence naturally had an extraordinary background.
Perhaps they were very angry that Luo Zheng had snatched the beginning and end true essence from this round of Tidal Surge, but if this brat was tactful and left the beginning and end true essence behind, they wouldn't find too much trouble. After all, they didn't have any actual losses.
This was the territory of the Sky Dome Clan, after all. Many forces in the God Realm did their best to befriend the Sky Dome Clan, and indeed obtained many benefits, but they couldn't be too arrogant and despotic here.
Now that Young Master Qin suddenly stood out and inexplicably made up such a rule, with this group of people's quick thinking, they instantly understood.
Young Master Qin's goal naturally wasn't really to chop off these guys' legs.
They were just a few second-level life forms, who knew which corner of the universe these guys came from. What was the point of chopping off their legs? It would be too troublesome to even make a move.
As for what these second-level life forms had in their hands, it couldn't possibly catch their eye.
The only thing that caught their eye was the method Luo Zheng had just displayed!
Every martial artist who stood on the platform would obtain a divine pattern under their feet when the Tidal Surge appeared.
There was no way to investigate who had set up these platforms, but refining the beginning and end true essence from these divine patterns was the only way.
The size of the platform and the strength of one's strength would determine the amount of black crystals refined.
The martial artists on this platform were basically all from the God's Domain. Occasionally, the younger generation of the Sky Vault Clan would come in, but the gap between them wasn't that big. At best, they could refine 30 to 40 pills, and at least, they could refine 10 to 20 pills.
But that guy that Han Liusu had brought was a bit outrageous!
Under this round of refinement, he directly produced three to four thousand crystals, which was to say that he had taken away the beginning and end true essence of everyone on the platform …
The group of God Realm martial artists was also rather speechless.
It was just that they had always looked down on second-level life forms, so how could they be willing to show any more shock? But the shock in their hearts couldn't be contained, and at the same time, they began to ponder where the problem lay.
Since Young Master Qin had made up a rule to chop off these people's legs, his goal was most likely to target Luo Zheng!
They were right.
Young Master Qin was indeed tempted by Luo Zheng's ability. If he could control this ability, he would be able to monopolize a platform, and the speed at which he absorbed the Beginning and End True Essence would increase by a hundredfold. Why not?
He didn't need to stay on the largest platform. He just needed to occupy a platform that wasn't too big. After all, no one else could compete with him.
As for Ku Deng, Ji Luoxue, and Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao, their faces darkened when they heard this.
After entering the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, they also understood the status of second-class living beings. In order to cultivate and increase their strength, they had no choice but to endure … Even after entering the Sky Dome Clan, they still put in a lot of effort.
Among them, Ji Luoxue had spent three months, Ku Deng had spent two months, and Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao was the slowest, spending a total of four months to obtain a precious purple talisman.
Even after coming in, he was still bullied.
They didn't even dare to think about occupying the largest platform. In the beginning, even the small platforms at the edge of this place were chased away by warriors.
But, these people's talents were extraordinary. Even within the Divine Realm, those that could cultivate the body of a Dao Child were existences that couldn't be casually ignored. Their greatest weakness was that their cultivations were too low. This was because most of the people here were Divine Transformation realm martial artists, but these three people were stuck at the middle and late Divine Realm.
Gradually, their strength grew, and they started to change platforms frequently. They also obtained more and more Beginning and End True Essence.
But in the end, they still didn't dare to step onto the centermost platform …
Because of Luo Zheng's appearance, they all came forward to take Luo Zheng away. They didn't expect that Young Master Qin would say such a thing. It was too much of a bully!
Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao forced a smile. "Young Master Qin, you must be joking. We won't take away the Beginning and End True Essence. You don't have to make it sound so serious."
Young Master Qin's expression became more and more serious, and a trace of malevolence appeared at the corner of his mouth. "I, Qin Tianze, never joke. How can you second-level creatures come up to this platform?"
After saying this, he gently waved one hand, and a bit of True Essence began to condense, condensing into a sword as thin as a cicada's wing. "Do you want me to do it myself, or do you want to do it yourself?"
The brilliance of the sword was restrained, and there was a hint of sharpness, but Young Master Qin didn't have the slightest killing intent. In his eyes, these second-level creatures were insignificant characters that weren't worth mentioning. They would immediately submit after being intimidated!
But he had to put on a show. Otherwise, how could he use this as a threat to interrogate Luo Zheng?
If these guys really didn't know what was good for them, Young Master Qin had another plan in mind. If he really killed these people, the Sky Dome Clan probably wouldn't find trouble with him. After all, the Sky Dome Clan still had many requests for his Qin Family …
The Sky Dome Clan cultivated the Beginning and End True Essence, which was fundamentally different from the Mountain Crushing Clan.
The Mountain Crushing Clan only cultivated the body, and they only needed to use the Origin of Power to irrigate their bodies. What's more, the clan had sufficient food supplies, and they didn't have any requests for foreign warriors. Therefore, the clan treated everyone equally and rarely favored anyone. This time, Luo Zheng was an exception.
However, the Sky Dome Clan had a considerable demand for some techniques and even weapons from the God Area, so the forces in the God Area naturally catered to them. Therefore, the Sky Dome Clan naturally took special care of warriors like Young Master Qin.
He thought his strength was enough to crush them, and with the help of the Sky Dome Clan, where could these guys escape to? If they dared to escape from the Sky Dome Clan, it would be better … The Qin Family's forces in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement could almost do whatever they wanted!
Seeing that Young Master Qin was really going to take action, the spirals in Ji Luoxue's pupils suddenly spun, and the hidden killing intent gradually flashed!
Ku Deng put his palms together with one hand, and the strength of the hand holding the Zen staff also increased a bit!
Compared to the other two, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao knew the guys in the God Area better. After all, the broken sword in his hand came from the God Area, but he knew that if he fought with Young Master Qin, he was afraid that he would really die. However, if Young Master Qin insisted on taking action, they couldn't possibly be fish on the chopping board.
"Young Master Qin, you're too ruthless." Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao was already quite helpless.
Young Master Qin took two steps forward. Seeing that the time was right, he smiled coldly, stared at Luo Zheng and said, "Of course, it's okay to not be ruthless. Just now, your friend has some small tricks to refine such a large amount of Beginning and End True Essence. If you tell me this small trick, I can let you go! As for the pile of Beginning and End True Essence, I'll give it to you! "
Coercion and bribery …
On one hand, he threatened to take action, and on the other hand, he would give them Beginning and End True Essence. This person's timing and means were quite accurate.
After he finished speaking, the warriors of the God Area on the platform suddenly showed an expression of "I've already guessed it", and quietly waited to see how this matter would develop.
Ji Luoxue and the other two looked at Luo Zheng at the same time. Young Master Qin had taken a big detour, and finally revealed his original purpose!
Han Liusu knelt on the ground. She gently moved her fingers, and the Beginning and End True Essence flew into her hands. Then she held it with both hands and put it into her mouth. Like eating melon seeds, she ate the black crystals one by one, and looked at the scene in front of her with a smile.
She already knew Qin Tianze's temper, but she didn't expect the effect to be so good. He really forced Luo Zheng to take action. This guy was afraid that he would suffer a big loss. At this moment, her mood was naturally very good!
Luo Zheng stood in the same place. Just now, he didn't say a word, but just coldly stared at Young Master Qin.
He had long seen through Han Liusu's plan. She wanted to use him to teach this Qin guy a lesson. Luo Zheng didn't want to do as she wanted, and from the beginning, he wanted to settle things peacefully …
Unfortunately, Luo Zheng still underestimated the domineering nature of some people. When they saw his methods, they were determined to obtain it through coercion and bribery. Under this aggressiveness, they even involved Ji Luoxue and the other two. Now, Luo Zheng didn't intend to shrink back.
At this moment, Luo Zheng still didn't pay attention to Young Master Qin. Instead, he stared at the smiling Han Liusu and said, "So this is what you wanted?"
Han Liusu smiled and didn't speak. She casually ate another black crystal.
However, Luo Zheng suddenly stretched out his finger, his thumb hooked around his index finger, and made a gesture of flicking his finger. "I don't like others using me. I hope this is the last time."
After saying this calmly, from his fingertip, a terrifying power suddenly condensed!
Luo Zheng's entire body's power, including the power of his eight Dao Pillars, all gathered in his index finger. This index finger was only three inches away from Han Liusu's forehead. In an instant, it gave Han Liusu a tremendous amount of pressure!
If Luo Zheng flicked his finger, her entire head would turn to dust, and she would die without a doubt!
In the face of this pressure, Han Liusu's face instantly paled. She saw that Luo Zheng's expression was extremely cold. He didn't seem to be joking.
"Pa!"
He flicked his finger and tapped on Han Liusu's forehead.

Chapter 1521

In other words, he had the confidence to fight against everyone on this platform!

In the Surge of Tides, in order to refine as much true essence as possible, martial artists always hoped to monopolize a platform as much as possible. Because of this, disputes often occurred, so the Sky Vault Clan had no choice but to send people to guard this place in turns.

He had already seen that Luo Zheng was also a Body and Energy Dual Cultivator. For a Body Refining Cultivator, the most important thing was the Dao Platform. For a Cultivation Martial Artist, the most important thing was the Dantian. If both were destroyed, it would be worse than death!

The martial artists behind Qin Tianze clamored.

At this moment, they also hoped that Qin Tianze could get his hands on this method. If Qin Tianze was willing to share it, they could each occupy a platform. In this way, they could almost monopolize the entire platform on the Tide Surge and take all the resources in their pockets.
"Kacha!"
Han Liusu popped another black crystal. She glanced at the group of idiots and shook her head slightly.

Chapter 1522

Ji Luoxue and Ku Deng had entered the Holy Land of Cultivation very quickly. After absorbing the Beginning and End Zhen Yuan, they felt that they had made great progress. But now, after seeing Luo Zheng's performance, their confidence, which had been slightly inflated, was severely damaged.
Luo Zheng's speed of progress was unimaginably fast.

Chapter 1523

Chapter 1524
After the Sky Vault Clan members rushed into the sky, streams of black Vital Essence emerged and condensed into black javelins that shot toward Luo Zheng!

Although these javelins were sharp, it was not easy for them to hurt Luo Zheng!

Those black javelins fell like raindrops. Faced with this rain-like attack, Luo Zheng's fist radiance flickered, accompanied by bursting sounds, and all the javelins that came close to Luo Zheng exploded in an instant!
Seeing that Jing Xuan on the ground still wanted to resist, Luo Zheng kicked out and twisted his hand. Like an eagle catching a chick, he pinched Jing Xuan's wings in his hand and used him as a shield in front of himself.
The Sky Vault Clan members did not dare to throw any more javelins. They scowled coldly and immediately started cursing again.
"No one dares to start a fight in the Sky Vault Clan!"
