Hearing these words, Luo Zheng could not help but roll his eyes.
The Sky Vault Clan's civilization was not developed very well. He did not know who they had learned these words from, but they came out of their mouths so easily, making him quite speechless.
To others, these threats might be quite effective, but Luo Zheng only found them funny. "They're right. They're right.
"They're right. If you let me go now, you still have a chance to live. If you're too late, no one will be able to save you. You know where you are," Jing Xuan said coldly. The people of the Sky Dome Clan were arrogant. Having one's wings grabbed from the back was like having one's face stepped on by a foot. This was a rather taboo action. Jing Xuan wanted nothing more than to skin Luo Zheng alive. However, his life was in the hands of this person, so he didn't dare to act rashly.
"Is that so? I just want to see who is qualified to take my life, "Luo Zheng said with a smile.
The Sky Vault Clan members in the sky realized that their threats had no effect at all. They exchanged glances and finally thought of calling for reinforcements …
The hearts of Ji Luoxue and the others were still hanging in the air.
How would Luo Zheng deal with the mighty figures of the Mountain-Crushing Clan when they came later?
But they still had hope when they saw Luo Zheng's calm expression. This guy had always been unexpected. Could it be that he had other trump cards up his sleeve?
Not long after, another group of people from the Sky Vault Clan shot over. When they saw Jing Xuan in Luo Zheng's hand, they didn't act rashly and just stood guard in the air.
Gradually, more and more Firmament Clan members gathered in the sky, but none of them recognized Luo Zheng. They only thought that Luo Zheng was some foreign martial artist who did not know the immensity of heaven and earth.
Whoosh!
At this moment, a piercing sound suddenly rang out, causing everyone's ears to go numb.
That figure left a trail of light in the air. Without any pause, he charged straight at Luo Zheng!
"Let go of my son!"
With a roar filled with anger, the man's wings flapped and a blood-red spear appeared in his hands. The spear was a visualization item, and when he thrust it forward, the spear extended like crazy and headed straight for Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng could have used Jing Xuan as his shield, but he did not do so. His expression was calm as he quietly activated his power!
Luo Zheng had already determined the opponent's strength. He could be considered a top expert of the Sky Vault Clan. In the Mountain-Crushing Clan, he could join the Divine Hunters.
Luo Zheng's physical body and Dao Platform were not enough to resist him. When Luo Zheng pointed at his opponent, a formless power appeared on his fingertips. That power was drawn from the Nine Stars!
The moment his fingertips touched the tip of the spear!
The man's expression changed drastically. He felt an irresistible power coming from the tip of the spear. His hands could not hold it at all!
Swoosh!
The spear flew out of his hand and split into Vital Essence in the air!
Seeing this scene, the eyes of many Sky Vault Clan members in the air almost popped out of their sockets.
After Jing Xuan was captured by Luo Zheng, these Sky Vault Clan members split up to call for reinforcements.
Even though Luo Zheng was causing a ruckus, they did not inform the higher-ups of the Sky Vault Clan. Luo Zheng did not have the qualifications to alarm them …
One of them went to inform Jing Xuan's father, one of the elders of the Mountain-Crushing Clan, Jing Chen.
Jing Chen's strength could be ranked in the top ten of the Sky Vault Clan. His strength was naturally not to be underestimated. After waiting for a long time, Jing Xuan's father finally came. They also felt that this farce was coming to an end. That savage brat on the platform would face the most severe punishment from the Sky Vault Clan.
Everyone was waiting to watch a good show.
They never expected that the guy below would not dodge. Instead, he would fight head-on and use a finger to welcome the spear!
Just this one finger had broken Jing Chen's Visualization Spear …
They suddenly realized that they thought too simply of Luo Zheng. Who sent this guy to cause trouble for the Sky Vault Clan?
But even if he wanted to cause trouble, it was impossible for him to barge into the Sky Vault Clan's territory so brazenly. No matter how powerful he was, he could not be a match for a clan. Besides, it was meaningless to cause trouble for them!
As an elder of the Sky Vault Clan, Jing Chen should have attended the Mountain-Crushing Clan's visit yesterday.
But recently, the Qin Clan sent over a True Form Wonder Technique. This technique's understanding of Vital Essence was refreshing, so he chose to go into seclusion for a month. Therefore, he did not see Luo Zheng!
"Brat, tell me your name! I'll let you die a quick death! " Jing Chen saw that his son's wings were pinched and could not move. It was as if he was pinching a rooster. The anger in his heart overflowed.
Luo Zheng sighed in his heart. Compared to the Sky Vault Clan, the Mountain-Crushing Clan's character was really good. The Sky Vault Clan looked beautiful and had a pair of pure white wings, but they were all unreasonable and domineering. They were all clans born in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Why was there such a big difference?
Luo Zheng did not answer. Jing Chen had lost his patience. He waved his hands and streaks of black Vital Essence gushed out from his body, forming mysterious patterns. Each streak of Vital Essence turned into a strange Mysterious Crystal, revolving around Jing Chen.
The crystals were white and flawless, and they contained the supreme power of the Great Dao. As they continued to combine, they formed a huge array.
A martial artist below saw this scene and murmured, "Formless Divine Dao …"
The Sky Vault Clan members could not become Dao Disciples, but they could cultivate the techniques of the 3,000 Divine Daos. These techniques of the Sky Vault Clan were naturally passed down by the people of the God Realm. Among them, most of the techniques were given by the Qin and Qin Clans. Therefore, the disciples of the two clans often received special treatment in the Sky Vault Clan.
After that person finished speaking, he slapped his head and shouted, "Run!"
The foreign martial artists below felt something was wrong. When this guy exerted his full strength, his power was comparable to that of a Celestial Master. Moreover, the range of this kind of Vital Essence technique was very wide. If they stayed here, they would inevitably be implicated …
Therefore, all the martial artists rose into the air, trying to escape.
However, at this moment, a large group of Sky Vault Clan members rushed over from not far away …
One of them said faintly, "Jing Chen, stop!"
Although the voice came from far away, it was transmitted by Vital Essence and resounded in everyone's ears.
In a rage, Jing Chen would not stop easily no matter who tried to persuade him. However, he could not ignore this person's words because it was the leader of the Sky Vault Clan, Gong Yu!
"Leader!"
Jing Chen glared at Luo Zheng unwillingly. He could only let this guy live a little longer.
Seeing that all the higher-ups of the Sky Vault Clan had arrived, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao, Ku Deng, and Ji Luoxue felt a burst of bitterness. This time, Luo Zheng was doomed. Perhaps they would also be unlucky.
Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …
Under Gong Yu's lead, this group of people, including Thunder Toad and members of the Mountain-Crushing Clan, landed on the platform.
At this moment, except for Han Liusu, almost everyone thought that Luo Zheng was doomed.
Even Jing Chen himself thought so!
However, Gong Yu's behavior made Jing Chen feel that something was very wrong!
"Young friend Luo Zheng, why didn't you inform me in advance that you were going to cultivate in my Tide's Surge? Why did you make such a big fuss? "Gong Yu asked faintly.
Jing Chen's expression was quite interesting at this moment. He could not figure out who on earth Luo Zheng was to make the leader so respectful.
Not only him, but also Jing Xuan, who had been captured by Luo Zheng, was completely dumbfounded when he looked at his father …
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1525
Since Gong Yu was here, Luo Zheng snorted coldly and threw Jing Xuan several feet away. Then he said, "You Sky Vault Clan have such strict rules. Ask him yourself!"
Luo Zheng had used a hidden force when he threw Jing Xuan. Jing Xuan wanted to jump into the air after he landed, but when he stood up, he rolled a few times. Naturally, he looked very embarrassed.
"You're courting death!" Jing Xuan's father, Jing Chen, was very angry when he saw his son being thrown out like a dead dog. He was about to attack again.
"Jing Chen," Gong Yu reminded him coldly.
Jing Chen was not a fool. He knew Gong Yu's character very well. Someone who could make Gong Yu so polite must have a very important background …
However, it was hard for Jing Chen to figure out Luo Zheng's identity. After all, even the so-called "Fire Reprimand" could not make Gong Yu so polite!
In the entire Forbidden Land of God Refinement, the six major clans did not show any respect to outsiders. After all, this was the territory of the six major clans. Even people like Qin Tianze had to put in a lot of effort to be taken care of by the Sky Vault Clan.
He was curious. What was the background of this Luo Zheng?
After Jing Xuan got up, Gong Yu said, "Jing Xuan, tell me what happened. How did you offend our distinguished guest?"
The words "distinguished guest" made Jing Chen, Jing Xuan, and the others' hearts sink. Even the warriors from the Realm of the Gods were stunned.
This group of warriors from the Realm of the Gods used all kinds of methods to please the Sky Vault Clan every year, but they did not become the "distinguished guest" mentioned by Gong Yu. This Luo Zheng was just a second-level creature. How could he become a distinguished guest?
They were also speechless at this moment.
Ji Luoxue, Ku Deng, and Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao behind Luo Zheng looked at each other again and saw the shock in each other's eyes.
They had been worried about Luo Zheng and the revenge of the Sky Vault Clan. Now, this worry disappeared. This was the leader of the Sky Vault Clan, Gong Yu!
What did Luo Zheng do to make the leader of the Sky Vault Clan be so polite to him?
Ji Luoxue could not help but sigh in his heart. He had worked so hard to achieve this. He had entered the Sky Vault Clan in a limited time and absorbed the vital essence. Originally, he had been happy about his speed and luck. But compared to Luo Zheng, it was a joke!
In the Illusory Battlefield, Ji Luoxue had been defeated by his subordinates. Maybe he had been defeated. But now, Ji had been defeated. They were all Daoists. There was always room for improvement in their strength.
What Ku Deng and Dugu Jian were thinking was almost the same as what Ji Luoxue was thinking.
Jing Xuan was not willing to tell the truth. After being thrown by Luo Zheng, his Qi and blood were surging, and his Inner World was surging. He felt very uncomfortable. He said, "Leader, I just followed the rules of the clan and forbade this group of warriors to fight. Luo Zheng fought with others in the territory of the Surge of Tides. I should stop him and expel him from the Sky Vault Clan. I didn't expect that he would attack me as soon as he arrived …"
Hearing his son's defense, Jing Chen naturally added fuel to the fire. "My son was just following the rules of the clan. He did not do anything wrong. I hope the leader can uphold justice and bring this man to justice!"
Gong Yu frowned. He did not believe Jing Xuan's one-sided story. He looked at Luo Zheng again and asked, "Little friend Luo Zheng, is Jing Xuan telling the truth?"
The warriors present had seen the whole process. Naturally, they knew that Jing Xuan was lying. They also understood that Gong Yu's attitude was biased toward Luo Zheng. Or rather, Gong Yu did not want to offend Luo Zheng. If Luo Zheng explained himself and confronted Luo Zheng face to face, Jing Xuan and Jing Chen would not be able to do anything to Luo Zheng …
Luo Zheng looked at Jing Xuan and laughed. His eyes showed a touch of madness. "Hahaha, you're right. It's exactly as Jing Xuan said. How does Leader Gong want to deal with this?"
Luo Zheng openly admitted to Jing Xuan's lies. Everyone present was stunned again.
"Is there something wrong with Luo Zheng's head?"
"He didn't defend himself. Instead, he admitted it!"
"I don't know what this guy is thinking …"
In their opinion, Luo Zheng's reasoning was reasonable. Jing Xuan's favoritism towards the Realm of the Gods was something they were well aware of. It was an open secret in the Sky Vault Clan. Everyone, including Gong Yu, knew about it. Some of the rules were even set by Gong Yu!
With Gong Yu's intelligence network, how could he not know that Jing Xuan was lying?
In this Surge of Tides, there were only a few trivial things that happened back and forth. It was easy to understand …
He had asked Luo Zheng to give Luo Zheng a chance to defend himself. If he gave him a reason, everyone would take advantage of the situation. He would then symbolically punish Jing Xuan, and this matter would be over.
He never imagined that Luo Zheng would not argue. Instead, he openly admitted to it.
"Chuyue, do you see? This kind of man is a thousand times better than that Qin Tianze. Elder sister just wants you to see that there are many good men in this world, but that Qin Tianze is just a clown!" Han Liusu suddenly opened her mouth and said.
The purple-robed woman, Han Liusu's younger sister, was called Han Chuyue.
At this moment, her eyes were wide open. She didn't understand what Luo Zheng was doing. She forgot about her conflict with her sister and asked foolishly, "Why did he agree with Jing Xuan?"
Han Liusu smiled. "Because he doesn't care."
"Doesn't care about what?" Han Chuyue asked again.
"He doesn't care about whether he's right or not, so he doesn't need to admit it. Because regardless of whether he's right or not, Gong Yu won't pursue it." Han Liusu smiled. "The so-called right is just a line drawn by the strong for the weak. The weak will be drawn into it, and the strong … will ignore it!"
Han Chuyue's thoughts were much simpler than Han Liusu's. She was still at the age of puberty. She didn't understand everything Han Liusu said, but she understood a lot. Now, she had to re-evaluate the young man not far away.
To put it simply, Luo Zheng was telling Jing Xuan, "Yes, I bullied you. I didn't follow the rules. What can you do about it?"
Hearing Luo Zheng's answer, Gong Yu cursed in his heart. He cursed Luo Zheng to be struck to death by lightning when he went out.
This guy just had to put him in a difficult position!
"How dare you. Since you dared to take responsibility for your actions, do you want to end your own life, or do you want me to do it myself?" Jing Chen didn't think so much. Anyway, Luo Zheng had already admitted it. This guy was looking for death, so Jing Chen was naturally happy to send him on his way.
Jing Chen's behavior only made Gong Yu even more embarrassed. Gong Yu was looking for a reason to let Luo Zheng go. It would be best if he could stabilize the dignity and rules of the Sky Vault Clan without causing any discomfort to Luo Zheng, because he was destined not to touch Luo Zheng.
Unfortunately, Jing Chen was still adding fuel to the fire. Gong Yu was helpless in his heart. He could only say with a gloomy face, "Jing Chen, take your son and get out of the Surge of Tides!"
"Why, why …" Jing Chen stared at Gong Yu in disbelief. In any case, he was an elder of the Sky Vault Clan!
"Jing Chen, the leader wants you to leave, so just leave. We'll talk later," someone beside Gong Yu advised.
Seeing several elders giving him meaningful looks, Jing Chen finally realized that this matter wasn't simple. He gritted his teeth and turned back to glare fiercely at Luo Zheng. He wished his gaze could turn into a knife and cut Luo Zheng into pieces. Then he grabbed Jing Xuan, raised his wings, and flew away.
Gong Yu was in a dilemma. He even wanted to grit his teeth and leave, but there was no shortage of scheming people in this world.
At this moment, a foreign warrior suddenly jumped out and cupped his hands toward Gong Yu. "Leader! Just now, I witnessed the entire process. It was Young Master Qin who provoked Luo Zheng, and Luo Zheng retaliated in anger! Jing Xuan was partial to Young Master Qin, so he had a conflict with Luo Zheng … "
After hearing this explanation, Gong Yu couldn't help but nod. His expression also calmed down. "I see. This matter was Jing Xuan's fault. Little friend Luo Zheng has been wronged! I'll punish him later! "
Luo Zheng didn't cause any more trouble. Gong Yu had been partial and accommodating from beginning to end. Besides, Gong Yu wasn't the one in the wrong. If he continued to cause trouble, he would be aggressive.
This disturbance was over. Before Gong Yu prepared to leave with his people, he turned back and asked, "What's your name?" He was asking the warrior who had jumped out to reconstruct the whole process.
"My name is Han Ping!" That person was surprised and delighted. He cupped his hands and smiled.
The foreign warriors in the Surge of Tides immediately regretted their actions. They had all witnessed the entire process, but only Han Ping had jumped out at the critical moment. He had seen through Gong Yu's intentions and helped Gong Yu out of the predicament. In the future, Han Ping would definitely receive a lot of care from the Sky Dome Clan.

Chapter 1526
Luo Zheng had kicked many Divine Realm martial artists into the black water. After they fell into the water, they quickly moved to other platforms …
But, there was one person that didn't appear. That was the first person to fall into the water, Qin Tianze!
Qin Tianze's personality was originally incomparably vicious. Moreover, he was a martial artist that came from a large influence. His face was more important than the heavens.
This time, he had suffered a great loss at the hands of Luo Zheng. His face had been pressed to the ground by Luo Zheng. Besides wanting Luo Zheng to die, there was no other request in his heart!
As the black water wildly surged, some martial artists shouted out, "The tide is coming again! Everyone, prepare to refine it!"
Here, the tide only surged seven times a day. Each time was incomparably precious to a martial artist. It wasn't easy for them to enter this place, so they naturally wouldn't let go of any opportunity.
However, there were also martial artists that were puzzled. This tide should be all of the black water surging at the same time, so why was there only one side of the central platform surging?
"It doesn't seem like the tide is surging. There must be something beneath the black water!"
A giant vortex appeared in the black water and began to crazily sink in. It was like there was a monster opening its maw and sucking in the black water. The water level at the center of the vortex was a full 10 feet lower than the edges!
Huala!
A waterspout suddenly appeared in the center of the vortex, shooting straight into the skies. And atop this waterspout was a martial artist. It was Qin Tianze!
Qin Tianze was pressed to the ground by Luo Zheng. Half of his face was covered in blood and flesh, and there were even some places where his bones could be seen. In addition to his current expression, he looked extremely fierce!
"Mm?" Gong Yu, who was planning to leave, turned back and saw this scene. "That Qin Family Young Master?"
"It looks like his anger isn't light. I fear he's about to go all out. Should we stop him?" Someone beside Gong Yu asked.
Gong Yu waved his hand and shook his head. "Let's wait and see …"
Currently, Gong Yu didn't have a complete understanding of Luo Zheng's strength. But, this boy was able to block his fire serpent and didn't seem to be struggling at all. He indeed had the qualifications to enter the Mountain Shattering Clan's Divine Hunter Squad. And, his status in the Divine Hunter Squad was equal to an Elder of the Sky Vault Clan.
If Luo Zheng really had this strength, then the Divine Transformation realm Sir Qin shouldn't be Luo Zheng's match.
At this moment, the young master of the Qin Clan angrily made a move. He was able to assess Luo Zheng's strength, but if Qin Tianze was not his match, he would be in for a bad time …
The Qin Family would definitely get involved with the Sky Vault Clan because of this matter, but Gong Yu did not care.
The races of the Six Great Cultivation Sacred Grounds were all confident because this was the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Only others would beg them, they didn't need to beg others.
"Me! Qin Tianze made a great wish! In the name of my life, in the name of my heart's devil, I swear to destroy everything before me. Grandmist artifact, heed my command! "
Qin Tianze shouted as he soared into the sky. Then, he began to use the Great Wish Technique.
The Great Wish Art was also one of the Three Thousand Divine Daos. Moreover, it was the most widespread Divine Dao.
Because cultivating this Divine Dao didn't conflict with other Divine Daos. Conversely, the Grand Aspiration Art was something one could rely on. When one faced a life and death crisis, utilizing the Grand Aspiration Art would allow one's strength to multiply!
Thus, although He Zuowei's cultivation was only at the World Overlord realm, he could use the Great Wish Art to temporarily block the Formless Heavenly Centipede!
Of course, everything had its pros and cons. The more one obtained, the greater the restrictions. This was the principle of equivalent exchange. If the Great Wish Art was not enough to defeat the other party, then in this life, one would have to face the process of repaying the wish.
If this wish was not repaid, then it would be difficult for one's cultivation to improve in this life.
After He Zuowei and the others saved Han Liusu, they didn't have the strength to kill the Formless Heavenly Centipede. This was a considerable hidden danger to them, but for the Eldest Princess, they had to pay this price.
Now that Qin Tianze was in a violent rage, how could he think too much?
As soon as he executed the Great Ambition Art, a grandmist artifact artifact hanging from his chest was activated!
It was a gray bead. It was like a water droplet, sparkling and translucent. It was round and smooth.
"Screech!"
The moment this bead was activated, a loud and sonorous sound suddenly resounded through the sky!
All the clansmen on the tower in the entire Sky Dome Clan heard this scream at the same time. Nervous expressions appeared on all the clansmen's faces, including Gong Yu and the various Elders of the Sky Dome Clan!
This kind of fear came from their innate instinct. No matter how powerful the Sky Dome Clan was, they couldn't avoid it.
The Sky Dome Clan's bloodline was fused with the bloodlines of several kinds of divine birds, and Qin Tianze's bead was sealed with the blood essence of a Black Vulture Wu. The Black Vulture Wu was also a true spirit of the Divine Realm and could be called the Lord of All Birds.
In the nearly endless deep space of the Divine Realm, there were countless powerful vicious birds, including mythological beasts such as the Kunpeng and Phoenix, but the peak of all birds was undoubtedly the Black Vulture Wu! It was the ultimate of all birds!
Therefore, when this Black Vulture Wu's cry spread, the Sky Dome Clan felt a strong suppression. All of the Sky Dome Clan's faces were unsightly, but the Mountain Crumbling Clan's people, the foreign clan's cultivators, and Luo Zheng didn't feel this way.
This bead was called the Female Heavenly Bead. Although it was a grandmist treasure, it was a one-time use magic treasure. It was obvious how precious it was!
Qin Tianze was powerless against Luo Zheng. The strength that Luo Zheng had just displayed was not something that he could contend against.
However, Qin Tianze, who was born in a prestigious family, still had a lot of tricks up his sleeve …
When he was bestowed this Female Heavenly Bead, he was repeatedly warned by his clansmen that he could only use this Female Heavenly Bead in desperate situations. It could be seen that this Female Heavenly Bead was a rare item to the Qin Clan. At least, it couldn't be used carelessly.
In today's situation, if Qin Tianze could endure this humiliation and take a step back, Luo Zheng wouldn't be so ruthless.
Unfortunately, Qin Tianze's temperament had never been tempered. Under this humiliation, he no longer had any scruples. He only wanted to get rid of Luo Zheng …
However, he didn't think about whether Gong Yu or Lei Toad would tolerate it.
After that long cry, the Female Heavenly Bead suddenly cracked, and a drop of jade essence flowed out from the bead!
It was like a drop of green ink dripping into water. This jade essence turned into wisps of green liquid and spread out. Then, it shot towards Qin Tianze's forehead!
In an instant, Qin Tianze's entire body was dyed with a green light, and the aura he emitted suddenly increased by a hundred times!
This wasn't Qin Tianze's own aura, but the aura of his true spirit!
"Ah … Qin Tianze is going all out." Han Liusu's face revealed a trace of shock. "Luo Zheng, you better be careful!"
Han Liusu also realized that she had gone a bit too far this time. She knew that Qin Tianze's temperament was perverse, but she didn't expect that he would directly use the Female Heavenly Bead because of this matter. This Female Heavenly Bead was only used at the last moment of life and death. Han Liusu also knew this.
She thought that even if Luo Zheng taught this guy a lesson, he wouldn't really kill him and wouldn't force Qin Tianze into a desperate situation.
However, she forgot that with Qin Tianze's personality, he would do anything if he was forced into a corner. Why would he have any scruples?
"Hahaha, secondary creatures are secondary creatures in the end. No matter how strong you are, it's useless! What do you have to fight with me! I will let you understand what it means to be crushed! " Qin Tianze laughed wildly. He spread his arms and rushed towards Luo Zheng like a roc spreading its wings!
Luo Zheng looked up at Qin Tianze, also secretly shocked in his heart. The richness of the Divine Realm's foundation was indeed not what he could imagine!
With Luo Zheng's current strength, Qin Tianze simply didn't have the qualifications to fight with him. If Qin Tianze fought with Luo Zheng, the only result would be the same as before. He would be beaten to the ground by Luo Zheng …
But now that he had used the bead on his chest, his strength could be described as explosively rising. He might even be much stronger than He Zuowei when he used the Great Wish Art!
And he was only at the Divine Transformation realm!
To be able to rise to such a degree in such a short period of time was unimaginable in the universe …
Of course, this was also because of Qin Tianze's special status. It was impossible for an ordinary divine realm martial artist to have such treatment, nor would they have a Female Heavenly Bead with them.
The group of divine realm martial artists that had been taught a lesson by Luo Zheng smiled once more.
Luo Zheng had finally angered Qin Tianze. The consequences of angering this madman were severe. Seeing that the Sky Vault Clan hadn't moved to protect Luo Zheng, they relaxed. Although Luo Zheng's strength was tyrannical, facing Qin Tianze in his current state, it was impossible for him to win.
But Luo Zheng exceeded their expectations once more. He didn't retreat in the slightest.
As the power of the Nine Stars crazily gushed out, Qin Tianze instead shot up and rushed forwards!

Chapter 1527
Luo Zheng wasn't really afraid of breaking the platform beneath his feet.
It was just that Luo Zheng's current strength was completely dependent on the power of nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier, so it wasn't suitable for long-distance battles.
If Qin Tianze relied on nimble movement techniques to launch long-distance attacks at him, Luo Zheng probably wouldn't be able to gain an advantage.
Fortunately, Qin Tianze's Hollow Sky Pearl seemed to be a strengthening tool, and he actually recklessly pounced at him, which was exactly what Luo Zheng wanted.
Luo Zheng, however, didn't know that Qin Tianze had the same thoughts as Luo Zheng.
This Hollow Sky Pearl could greatly increase his strength and speed in a short period of time. Under such strengthening, he, who was in the Divine Transformation Tier, had the ability to fight against a peak stage Realm Master — there were no Empyrean Venerables in the Divine Realm. Any Realm Master could think of ways to condense a godhood, and there were many ways to obtain a godhood.
Thus, Qin Tianze was also afraid that Luo Zheng would flee or seek protection from the Sky Vault Clan!
The Hollow Sky Pearl's duration was extremely short, only thirty to forty breaths of time. Once Luo Zheng dodged, his strength would rapidly decline, and he would no longer have a chance to turn the tables!
Seeing that Luo Zheng didn't intend to dodge, Qin Tianze was overjoyed!
"Since you're in such a hurry to die, don't blame me!"
In addition to this trace of joy, Qin Tianze's heart was also unwilling.
What was a second-level living being? In Qin Tianze's eyes, those second-level living beings were existences that could be killed at any time, not worth mentioning. There were many second-level living beings in the Qin Clan's birthplace, and this group of trash could fight to the death for a few Trial Seals. In Qin Tianze's impression, they weren't even qualified to be on the stage!
Yet this second-level living being in front of him not only required him to use all his strength, but also the Grand Wish Art, and even the Hollow Sky Pearl …
Besides this unwillingness and helplessness, Qin Tianze was left with a deep killing intent. He had to kill Luo Zheng in order to be quick!
The instant the two approached each other, Qin Tianze clenched his teeth, and the green light on his opened hands instantly condensed into a pair of claw shadows. The surface of the claws was covered with wrinkles. These wrinkles were the divine patterns evolved by the True Spirit for countless years. This kind of divine pattern was the highest level of divine patterns, because it could appear on the next generation through bloodline inheritance.
"Die!"
Qin Tianze's eyes widened in anger. In his life, situations like this were extremely rare. After all, many crises didn't require him to take action.
In contrast, Luo Zheng's face was filled with a calm smile..
He continued to extract the power from the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier and gathered it in front of Luo Zheng. The powerful power continuously distorted the space, and the way everyone looked at Luo Zheng was also distorted. So, in their eyes, Luo Zheng's entire body was distorted in a strange way, as if he was looking through a mirror, looking very strange.
However, no one thought it was funny. On the contrary, everyone's eyes were solemn …
Previously, Luo Zheng's cultivation was at the pinnacle of the God Tier, and he was unstoppable on this platform, throwing so many Gods and martial artists into the black water was already inconceivable. Now, Luo Zheng's power skyrocketed again, and no one could see his limit at all!
This time, Luo Zheng directly transformed that invisible power into the shape of a shield, holding it in front of him, and collided head-on with Qin Tianze in the sky.
Through that transparent shield of power, Luo Zheng suddenly cracked open the corners of his mouth, revealing a hearty smile!
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, Qin Tianze didn't have time to be stunned. The sharp claws wrapped around his hands had already collided with Luo Zheng's shield of power.
This pair of sharp claws condensed from green light was condensed from Xuan Mi Wu's blood essence. The sharp claws of this kind of living creature could almost cut through everything. Even the sturdiest Golden Fork Strange Stone of the Mountain Crumbling Clan could be cut into two by him.
In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, there shouldn't be any substance that could block this pair of sharp claws.
However, the shield of power formed by Luo Zheng's nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier wasn't material at all. It was only an invisible power.
Generally speaking, power could only be displayed to the extreme with the help of a medium.
The most common medium was a martial artist's own physical body and fists … Some Body Refining martial artists used large hammers and battle axes, and these weapons could also be considered as a medium.
However, the part of the Saint's power that Luo Zheng controlled was too powerful. With Luo Zheng's current strength, no matter what medium it was, it couldn't withstand it. If his own physical body withstood it, his physical body would collapse. If he withstood it with a weapon, perhaps even a Primordial Treasure would shatter inch by inch.
He could only unleash his power with the Transmigration Theurgy. Using his power like this was a huge waste, but it was also an act of helplessness. Otherwise, it would be like the power of dragon scales he had before, where he could light up too many dragon scales but was unable to use them at all.
However, even if this power was purely released, it was more than enough to resist Qin Tianze!
"Ga …"
In the instant that those sharp claws reached into this invisible shield, this pair of incredibly sharp claws and Luo Zheng's power intersected, releasing a sharp cutting sound!
The invisible power was like flowing water. When a piece was cut, the power on the side would replenish it …
Therefore, this kind of cutting power couldn't destroy this shield of power at all. On the contrary, under Qin Tianze's control, those sharp claws continuously penetrated deeper into the shield of power, and were instead swallowed by this shield!
This process only lasted for less than half a breath's time.
Qin Tianze's expression suddenly darkened. He watched as the tip of the sharp claws began to bend, and cracks continuously appeared on the surface of the claws!
"This … is impossible!"
After using the Empty Heaven Pearl, his information increased explosively, and he felt that he was an invincible existence.
When a martial artist's strength increased greatly in a short period of time, they would often have this kind of illusion. For example, the group of martial artists from the Collapsing Mountain Race who had just left the Sacred Land of Cultivation after passing the trial would also have their confidence increase explosively …
In this kind of situation, martial artists would often overestimate their own strength, and under this overestimation, they would lose caution, thus making a wrong judgment and choosing the wrong opponent.
Qin Tianze was like this.
If he had enough observation skills and a strong state of mind, he would have chosen to endure and retreat at this moment. He would have been patient enough to crawl and give Luo Zheng a fatal blow at the right time. Only in this way would he have a chance to kill Luo Zheng.
However, his arrogance and prejudice made him lose his mind, and he chose the wrong time to attack Luo Zheng.
"Zi zi zi …"
The strong power contained in that shield of power finally shattered Qin Tianze's sharp claws, turning them into countless green powder and annihilating within it …
When this pair of sharp claws shattered, the green light that enveloped his whole body was like a bubble, instantly bursting and disappearing completely!
That Empty Heaven Pearl was forcibly broken by Luo Zheng, and it was in the most barbaric way possible …
"You can't …"
That Empty Heaven Pearl was Qin Tianze's spiritual pillar, and it was the last resort of the Qin Clan's disciples. Countless Qin Clan disciples had used this method to reverse many life-and-death battles and create many miracles.
Now that the Empty Heaven Pearl was easily broken by Luo Zheng, his spiritual pillar had collapsed. Moreover, he had been played by Luo Zheng in a very embarrassing manner before. At this moment, all the fighting spirit in his heart dissipated completely and was replaced by deep fear.
He didn't think that he would die. He suddenly realized that when he was about to face the Grim Reaper, no one could suppress the fear in his heart!
"You can't kill me!" Qin Tianze subconsciously said this sentence. "I …"
Just as he was about to open his mouth to explain the pros and cons of the situation, Luo Zheng had completely crashed into him …
There was no need for Qin Tianze to speak. Luo Zheng could already guess what this person would say next. He had heard too many such words along the way, and he was too lazy to give Qin Tianze a chance to defend himself.
The moment that shield of power collided with Qin Tianze, an invisible force began to annihilate Qin Tianze's body. If nothing unexpected happened, this person's body would be instantly annihilated by this powerful force. Regardless of whether it was his hair, flesh, bones, or internal organs, they would all be crushed into the tiniest powder by this force!
However, what happened next was completely out of Luo Zheng's expectations!
It seemed that at this moment, the brown wooden ring on Qin Tianze's finger suddenly broke. At the same time, a cloud of smoke suddenly rose from the surface of Qin Tianze's skin and enveloped him. A strong power of the Law of Space came from this smoke, and his entire body disappeared, replaced by a wooden puppet!
This puppet was lifelike and was no different from Qin Tianze, but it was definitely not Qin Tianze himself.
The puppet was instantly crushed into wood dregs by the shield of power and fell down in pieces. Luo Zheng floated in the air and looked around. He sneered and returned to the platform.
This Qin Tianze really had a lot of protective treasures. Before he died, he had such a substitute treasure! Not only could he replace his body with a puppet, but he could also activate space transmission!

Chapter 1528
At the southern end of the hills in the center of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, there was a place of birth. About 15% of the martial artists who entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement would be born here.
After the Qin Family occupied this place of birth, it was renamed Qin City. Then, the Qin Family constantly sent World Lord powerhouses here, gradually consolidating this place …
In the sky above a fort in the center of Qin City, a blazing white light suddenly appeared. This was the effect of the Space Laws.
This kind of Space Laws was passed down from the Realm of the Gods. The space passage constructed was relatively stable, and it was almost a perfect space. The blazing white Space Laws constantly intertwined, forming a large snowflake pattern. After spinning a few times in the air, a person fell straight down from this snowflake!
In the fort, a World Lord soared into the sky and caught the falling person. That World Lord's eyes were filled with shock. "Young Master, who dared to injure you so severely!? Did you encounter those powerful mutated beasts in the wild? "
The falling person was without a doubt Qin Tianze.
At this moment, his entire body, especially his front, didn't have an intact piece of flesh. There was a huge crack in his chest, and one could even see the bones within.
However, Luo Zheng's energy shield had eroded Qin Tianze's left hand.
On the little finger of his left hand, there was a ring made of green wood. This ring could replace him with the wooden puppet when he was in danger. At the same time, it could teleport him to another place. If he was in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, he could teleport Qin Tianze back to Qin City.
When the World Lord carried Qin Tianze into the fort, it immediately alarmed many people.
"How is Young Master Tianze!?"
"How could he be injured so severely?"
"He wasn't injured by a blunt weapon. It should be caused by some kind of corrosive energy!"
Soon, a World Lord set up a large array, activated the divine runes, and injected a dark green light into Qin Tianze's body.
When Realm of the Gods martial artists fought, as long as there was still a breath of life left, no matter how severe the injury, it could often be saved.
The terrifying wounds on Qin Tianze's body were rapidly healing. Countless flesh tendrils intertwined, and the wounds were constantly healing …
Soon, Qin Tianze opened his eyes, but his aura was still very weak.
"Young Master, what kind of exotic beast injured him?"
These Sector Lords stayed in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement for a long time, and the six great cultivation sacred grounds wouldn't make a move for no reason. Moreover, Young Master was in the territory of the Heaven Vault Race, and their Qin Clan often moved around, so they had the closest relationship with the Heaven Vault Race.
Most people speculated that the Young Master must have been injured by an exotic beast while out training.
Hearing the Realm Lord's words, Qin Tianze's face immediately became gloomy. He gritted his teeth and said angrily, "It wasn't a beast that injured him!"
"Not an exotic beast? Could it be that Young Master has a conflict with the Sky Dome Clan? "The Sector Lord was shocked.
"It was … by a secondary life form!" Qin Tianze also felt that this was a very shameful thing. However, he still lost to Luo Zheng even after using the Female Heavenly Pearl. This was no longer a problem with his strength. It was completely because Luo Zheng was too strong! As much as he didn't want to admit it.
"Secondary life form?"
"How is this possible?"
"Is Young Master talking nonsense …"
The moment he said this, everyone was shocked.
They were very clear about the strength of secondary life forms. There were many of them in Qin City. After the Qin Clan occupied this place of birth, they were also responsible for distributing trial talismans. They knew that this group of secondary life forms would fight to the death for a few low level trial talismans!
Now, their Young Master was injured by a secondary life form. They couldn't help but feel that it was inconceivable.
"I, I also used the Great Wish Technique!" Qin Tianze said again.
The Sector Lords' faces changed again. He even used the Great Wish Technique and was still injured to this extent. This secondary life form's origin was probably not simple.
"What about Black Raft? And Mu Zhan? They didn't protect Young Master? "A Sector Lord asked coldly.
Qin Tianze naturally had guards accompanying him when he entered the Heaven Vault Race. The two guards were both Sector Lords, but they caused Young Master to suffer such heavy injuries. Their responsibility wasn't small …
However, the two guards didn't enter the Tidal Surge. After all, with Qin Tianze's strength, there was no need to protect him in the Tidal Surge!
"And I also used the Female Heavenly Pearl." Perhaps to cover up his embarrassment of losing to a secondary life form, Qin Tianze said everything in one breath.
"What!"
"This …"
The Sector Lords present were all dumbfounded.
Qin Tianze's cultivation might not be as high as theirs, but after using the Great Wish Technique, he could still compete with the Sector Lords. His strength was on par with theirs.
But after using the Female Heavenly Pearl … with the support of this secret treasure, Qin Tianze's strength should be far above the Sector Lords present!
The problem was that Young Master was still defeated after using the Female Heavenly Pearl. He even activated his escape ring to save his life. This meant that this secondary life form's strength was at a level they couldn't comprehend.
They couldn't believe that such a thing could happen.
Seeing their expressions, Qin Tianze was also angry. "You all doubt me?"
"This subordinate doesn't dare to doubt Young Master, but this matter is truly unbelievable!" A Sector Lord replied.
"No matter who it is, we must go to the Sky Dome Clan to capture this person and let Young Master fulfill his wish!" Another Sector Lord said.
Qin Tianze lost to Luo Zheng, but his Great Wish Technique was used to target Luo Zheng. If he didn't kill Luo Zheng, Qin Tianze's cultivation might not be able to improve in the future. So no matter who it was, this person had to die, and it had to be killed by Qin Tianze himself.
"Don't be hasty. This person's origin is very problematic. Even the Sky Dome Clan is protecting him. I'm afraid it'll be very difficult for us to take him down," Qin Tianze said with a frown. Rashly using the Great Wish Technique had planted a hidden danger for him!
Originally, once a martial artist used the Great Wish Technique, it would be a situation where they would fight to the death. They had to kill the other party to avoid the backlash of the Great Wish Technique …
"The Sky Dome Clan will protect a secondary life form!"
At this moment, many martial artists of the Qin Clan realized the seriousness of the problem. Their Qin Clan could do whatever they wanted in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, but they still had to behave themselves in front of the Six Great Cultivation Holy Lands. After all, this was their home ground, and they could only curry favor with them.
After Qin Tianze escaped from the Tidal Surge, the people of the Sky Dome Clan and the Mountain Shattering Clan finally left.
Luo Zheng returned to the platform. At this moment, the black water below the platform surged again, and the second Tidal Surge finally appeared.
"Hualala …"
Accompanied by the rapid sound of water, numerous whirlpools began to converge below the platforms.
The martial artists who were originally spectating, after exclaiming in surprise, turned their attention back to their own feet. No matter how powerful Luo Zheng was, it had nothing to do with them. Everyone still obediently refined their own true essence.
However, the group of martial artists of the God Realm who had been driven off the platform by Luo Zheng now occupied the other platforms. Some of the foreign martial artists were angry, but they didn't dare to say anything. They didn't have Luo Zheng's skills, so how could they resist the group of martial artists of the God Realm?
As for the central platform, Luo Zheng didn't drive Han Liusu and Han Chuyue away. As for Ji Luoxue and the other two, they naturally stayed on the platform, refining their own true essence with Luo Zheng.
Ji Luoxue, Ku Deng, and Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao were extremely excited at this moment.
The largest central platform was a place they didn't dare to set foot on before!
Now, not only were they standing on the platform, but the other martial artists of the God Realm had also been driven away by Luo Zheng. The few of them occupied an empty platform. This kind of treatment was simply unimaginable!
Under the continuous refining of the divine patterns below their feet, many black crystals were refined around each of them. This time, Luo Zheng only injected a small amount of Chaotic Energy. Anyway, these black crystals were useless to him, so he just treated it as helping Ji Luoxue and the others.
Thus, Ji Luoxue and the other two obtained around a thousand black crystals …
Before, they could only refine several black crystals at a time, less than a hundred a day. But now, they refined nearly as much as they had refined in the past three months.
The three of them were extremely excited. They constantly swallowed up that precious true essence, adding to their inner worlds! The density of true essence in their bodies increased at a visible rate.
The three transformations of the Incarnation Transformation were the changes to their inner worlds.
As their inner worlds constantly expanded, their cultivations became more stable. If this continued, they would be able to attack the Divine Transformation realm in just a few days!
After absorbing the true essence, the three of them naturally gathered around Luo Zheng, curiously inquiring about what Luo Zheng had seen and heard along the way.

Chapter 1529
On this platform, Luo Zheng and the others chatted about the Forbidden Land of God Refinement while refining their Beginning and End True Yuan.

Luo Zheng's request was very predictable.
Three days later, while Luo Zheng was still cultivating in the towers, Jiang Zhengyi and Luo Xi Xuan entered the Sky Vault Clan.
As Secondary Life Forms, after paying the Trial Talisman, they smoothly entered the Tidal Surge without encountering Luo Zheng.

In reality, it was not an easy task to obtain a Purple Trial Talisman in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Some martial artists had to spend years or even decades to obtain one. This also proved how outstanding these ten people were. They were indeed the top ten martial artists in the universe.

Just as the two of them were hesitating, they suddenly heard someone shout, "Luo Xi Xuan!"
The two of them turned their heads at the same time and saw that there were only three people standing on a platform not far away. It was Ji Luo Xue's group of three.
Most importantly, there were only three of them on that platform. The platforms here were all overcrowded, but this one was completely empty. The two of them immediately felt that it was very strange.
However, they were naturally overjoyed to be able to meet their old friends here, so the two of them flew towards the platform!
"Luo Xue, why is such a large platform for you to monopolize?" Luo Xi Xuan asked, puzzled.
Perhaps she hadn't figured out the rules of this place, but with Luo Xi Xuan's keen senses, she knew that there were definitely some unusual resources on these platforms, so the foreign race cultivators weren't willing to share them with her.
However, there were only three people on this spacious platform, which was unusual.
Ji Luo Xue naturally understood why Luo Xi Xuan was so strange, so he just smiled faintly, "It's all thanks to Luo Zheng."

Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao shook her head and then explained the whole story of this place and how the three of them obtained this platform. Finally, she said, "That guy probably doesn't care about the Origin and End True Qi here, so I don't know if he has left the Sky Vault Clan or not …"
These words amazed Luo Xi Xuan. This cultivation Holy Land was full of talents. Not only were the geniuses of the universe gathered here, but there were also people from the Realm of the Gods, but that guy was still able to stand out among them and obtain such benefits. It could be said that he had paved the way for them.
After the Tide's Surge appeared, Luo Xi Xuan and Jiang Zhengyi finally understood what it meant to obtain such a large platform …
Luo Zheng hadn't left the Sky Vault Clan.
These few days, he stayed in the Sky Vault Clan, waiting for the arrival of the Yuan He Clan.
Taking advantage of this idle time, Luo Zheng continued to observe the world inside his body.
After the power of the nine stars was extracted by Luo Zheng, they appeared extremely dim at first …
The source of power couldn't be overdrawn infinitely. Ordinary Body Refining martial artists, even if they opened nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier and exhausted all their power, would still need a long time to nurture the nine stars to the peak state. Sometimes, this time could even be as long as several months.
Thus, Body Refining martial artists had a huge weakness after cultivating to the peak — their battle prowess couldn't last long.
This was because no one could directly transform True Qi into pure power.
However, this Primal Chaos Qi was simply a living thing. After all, this Primal Chaos Qi was the source of everything, and everything evolved from it. Thus, these nine stars hung above the Primal Chaos Sea, but could be nurtured by the Primal Chaos Qi. The recovery speed of the power of these nine stars was something that other martial artists, and even Saints, couldn't compare to. After all, Saints constructed a universe, and this universe didn't contain Primal Chaos Qi, so they couldn't use this Primal Chaos Qi for their own use.
However, cultivating the Primal Chaos Secret Technique to this point, Luo Zheng also encountered a more serious problem.
He didn't know how to expand his territory …
The living beings in the world inside his body were still growing rapidly. Dynasties changed one after another, and all kinds of living beings with God Tools were born. It was full of vitality.
The area of this Primal Chaos Sea was slowly shrinking, but the continent in the center of the sea was constantly expanding.
The problem was that the area of the entire Primal Chaos Sea didn't increase! If he cultivated True Qi, Luo Zheng could very well stay in the Sky Vault Clan to absorb the black crystals and try to expand the world inside his body.
The size of the world inside one's body was also a standard to measure one's cultivation. If Luo Zheng couldn't expand the world inside his body, then he would lose the method to increase his cultivation.
Unfortunately, Luo Zheng couldn't find the answer in the Primal Chaos Secret Technique, so he could only slowly explore it himself. A path that others had never walked before was destined to be filled with too many risks and uncertainties. However, he would also obtain astonishing harvests and unexpected results.
In addition to exploring the world inside his body, Luo Zheng also needed to deal with the two sisters from Han Jia.
That Han Liusu was still alright. Although she had used him once, she still knew when to advance and when to retreat. However, that sister of hers wasn't the same. She was a little girl who didn't understand the ways of the world. She constantly pestered him and asked him all sorts of questions. She even asked Luo Zheng which universe he came from.
"I also don't know which universe I came from." Luo Zheng didn't understand much about the powers of the Realm of the Gods, nor did he understand how the universes were divided. Perhaps Hua Tianming and Old Man Feng knew a little, but they had always hidden some things from him.
"Who is the Custodian!" Han Chuyue suddenly asked.
"What is a Custodian?" Luo Zheng naturally didn't know what this girl was referring to.
Han Chuyue proudly showed off, "So you don't even know what a Custodian is … the universe really is a backward place!"
"Chuyue!" Han Liusu felt that her sister's words were a bit rude.
However, Luo Zheng waved his hand and said, "It's fine. I don't know if the Custodian you are referring to is the person controlled by the Heavenly Dao?"
At this time, Luo Zheng's interest was piqued. Some information needed to be grasped by oneself. These two girls weren't very scheming people, so he naturally wanted to take this opportunity to find out a thing or two.

Chapter 1530
Under her sister's rebuke, Han Chuyue did not take offense and continued, "It is, the whole universe is under the control of the saints! What qualifications do other people have to be called the controller! "
Hearing Han Chuyue's explanation, Luo Zheng understood.
Since the universe is the saint's inner world, then the saint is the controller of the universe. This logic is very simple.
But what role does that Jiang Yu 'er play? Just like what Han Chuyue said, she is the so-called "custodian"?
"Custodian, in the end what is going on?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Han Chuyue blinked her eyes. Compared to Han Liusu, her eyes were more round and watery, but they lacked Han Liusu's charming and lonely characteristics.
Girls her age have a natural worship of the strong. Others may be curious about Luo Zheng's origin, but whether Luo Zheng is strong or not is not important, as long as he does not provoke her.
Han Liusu is slightly more mature. She wants to use Luo Zheng to do her own things, such as entering the Subduing Soul Cliff, or letting Luo Zheng attack Qin Tianze.
But after Han Chuyue became interested in Luo Zheng, she wanted to dig up everything about Luo Zheng.

Luo Zheng hesitated for a moment, then opened his mouth. In fact, he only knew her name, "Jiang Yu 'er."
