Han Liusu's eyes flashed, revealing a meaningful expression, as if she was getting to know Luo Zheng again.
Luo Zheng saw the reaction of the two women and knew there must be something hidden, "What?"
"That is the Great Yan Universe," Han Chuyue said.
"Great Yan Universe … …" Luo Zheng muttered these words. This is the name of the universe he is in?
Han Chuyue looked suspiciously at Luo Zheng, "But the saint who built the Great Yan Universe seems to be in a bad situation, he seems to be dead!"
"Chuyue, what are you talking about?" Han Liusu corrected her. "Saints and the universe are interdependent. As long as the universe is not destroyed, the saints will not die."
"Indeed, it's about time. Wasn't there news that they were already preparing to take action? If the Great Yan Universe is broken down, then wouldn't the Saint be dead … "Han Chuyue continued.
Hearing Han Chuyue's words, Luo Zheng was sure that they were not lying.
It was a fact that Huan Yu was being invaded by the Holy Clan. Hua Tianming had also informed him that Huan Yu's master was on the verge of death …
Strictly speaking, Luo Zheng and his group of people were geniuses who were born after Huan Yu sensed danger. They were given the destiny of a great era. According to Hua Tianming, all the talk of fate was actually deliberately arranged!
Luo Zheng might have met with some accidents during his cultivation. For example, entering the Holy Realm Dwellers' Mansion was an accident, and obtaining the Secret Skill of Primal Chaos was another accident.
But his path was still following the destiny that Huan Yu had arranged.
Including the guidance of the Nine True Dragons, including the fact that Luo Yan was taken away, this fact became an obsession that led Luo Zheng forward.
The ultimate goal was to let Luo Zheng go to the Divine Realm and save everything …
Of course, such a large Huan Yu could not place all its hopes on Luo Zheng alone. The probability was too small. Therefore, batch after batch of geniuses were born in Huan Yu. Countless geniuses fought in the Illusory Battlefield, and these ten people were born. They carried an irresistible destiny on their backs as they moved forward.
According to the current information, Luo Zheng could only infer this much. But he still had many questions in his heart. "This is a battle between the Saints?"
Han Liusu nodded, but before she could open her mouth, Han Chuyue spoke first: "Of course it is, but the outcome of this battle has already been decided, now it is no longer a battle, but rather the finishing stage, the master of Great Evolution Universe has been thoroughly defeated!"
"Since it's a battle between the Saints, why don't the Saints directly destroy the universe? With the strength of the Saints, they should be able to do so, right? "Luo Zheng asked again.
Han Chuyue faintly smiled. "That won't do. The Divine Realm is not what you think it is! The Saint Hall will not agree! "
"Saint Hall … what is that?"
Realizing that Luo Zheng was terrifyingly ignorant about the Divine Realm, Han Chuyue could only patiently explain.
In the end, she still had the intention to show off to Luo Zheng. "You don't even know this? Let me tell you in detail. The Divine Realm … I'm afraid it's very different from what you think it is!"
Along the way, Luo Zheng had seen many civilizations.
The Demon Night Clan was an absolute god system. The three kings controlled everything. They were the gods of the Demon Night Clan, so they could use the faith pond.
On the other hand, ordinary Holy Lands followed the typical Sect system. Regardless of whether it was a sixth-grade Holy Land or a tenth-grade Holy Land, the difference between these Holy Lands was quite big according to the difference in strength. However, the rules and culture of these Holy Lands were no different from that of a low-grade Sect.
Aside from the Holy Lands, there were also Divine Kingdoms!
Divine Kingdoms were dominated by the royal family. All the large and small forces had to rely on the Divine Kingdoms. When the Divine Kingdoms developed to a certain extent, they evolved into the Ji Family, the Xuanyuan Family, the Ji Family, and the Lie Family.
In this kind of civilization, the monarch and the family were the most powerful. The top inheritances and foundations were all poured into the outstanding children of the family.
And in such a big Divine Realm, after countless years …
This countless years was roughly equivalent to the creation of the world, which was the day when the Good Fortune Axe split open the primal chaos.
Time had become another concept here. It was a concept that ordinary people found difficult to understand.
After all, time was also a type of law. In the entire Divine Realm, "time" hadn't been created for a very long time. So at that time, time was still. It was precisely because of this that the Divine Realm was in a state of deathly stillness for a very long time!
From the time that the rule of "time" was created, the Divine Realm had already passed 860 quadrillion years. The scale of time was so long that it was hard for anyone to imagine it. It had become an illusory term.
In these ancient years, countless lives and races constantly perished. Mortal kingdoms were born, and mortal sects were born.
As the martial artists of these sects rose in strength, these sects quickly rose above the mortal kingdoms. Gradually, they developed into Holy Lands, giving birth to Divine Kingdoms, and so on. The situation was the same as in the universe. Different races and different life forms found it difficult to get along with each other.
In these countless years, the Divine Realm had always been in a state of chaos and chaos. The number of deaths and injuries was incalculable. There were some races that were eliminated as soon as they appeared. There were some races that had tens of billions of people. They didn't even have the qualifications to leave behind a trace in the history of the Divine Realm. They were like a splash on a wave, disappearing in the whirlpool of history in the blink of an eye.
The races that were able to survive in this chaos were worthy of being proud. They were the strongest and most tenacious races.
Endless war could only bring destruction and destruction. As civilization developed, there were eventually people who were willing to stand up and solve this problem.
Thus, many saints joined hands and formed the Holy Halls. They were bestowed with the highest authority in the entire Divine Realm.
The appearance of the Holy Halls finally stabilized the entire Divine Realm, continuing to this day.
But the struggles between races, the struggles between martial artists, and even the struggles between saints, had never stopped …
But these struggles were all within the rules.
If someone violated these rules, after the Holy Halls passed a resolution, they would join hands and punish the person who violated the rules!
The entire process naturally had no lack of schemes and traps.
"The master of the Great Evolution Universe and his disciples should have fallen into the other party's trap and finally received their punishment. But the matters involved are very complex and it's hard to explain in a short time. Also, many rumors may not be true." Han Liusu suddenly inserted herself into this topic. Han Chuyue glared at her elder sister with dissatisfaction. At the most critical moment, her elder sister had snatched the opportunity to speak first!
"Then what is the name of the master of the Great Evolution Universe?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
At this moment, Luo Zheng asked this question out of pure curiosity. He knew that it was the saints who had constructed this universe. Moreover, he had seen one of the saints' disciples, the "Star Tail" hidden within the Demi-Immortal race.
But he knew nothing about this saint.
"The master of the Great Evolution Universe is called Luo Xiao. He has the same surname as you," Han Liusu lightly replied.
"What!"
Han Liusu's reply caused Luo Zheng's blood to surge wildly. His powerful heart beat even more wildly, as if someone was beating a drum. Even the two sisters of Han Jia could hear it clearly.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1531
No name from Han Liusu's mouth would cause such a huge shock to Luo Zheng!
The Great Yan Universe, the master of the universe Luo Zheng was in, the Saint of the Divine Domain, Luo Xiao …
For a moment, Luo Zheng's mind was in a mess.
Perhaps it was just a coincidence. Although the name Luo Xiao was not common, it was not a particularly rare name either.
But it was not reasonable to simply regard it as a coincidence!
Thinking back carefully, did the things Hua Tianming and the others deliberately hide also contain these secrets?
There was also the problem of his own fate. At that time, Luo Zheng had displayed great talent, but that was only relative to the martial artists of the Vast Thousand Domains. If compared to the entire universe, the Luo Zheng at that time could only be said to be not worth mentioning.
But that young man who appeared out of nowhere, on what basis did he kidnap Luo Yan? Just because she was the so-called Compass of Fate?
Thinking about it carefully …
This was no more than an excuse made up.
To emphasize the inevitability of fate, to make him stride towards the Divine Domain …
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng felt that his deduction was becoming clearer and clearer.
Assuming that young man was a True God who could descend into the Great Yan Universe at will, he was most likely a disciple of a Saint.
Ordinary True Gods could not return to the universe. At least, the only True God Luo Zheng had seen with his own eyes was Xing Wei of the Celestial Race, who was one of his disciples.
When his strength was only at the False Tribulation Realm, he was taken care of by a True God disciple.
And his dead father was called Luo Xiao, and the Saint's name was also Luo Xiao …
Deducing from this, the so-called coincidence became inevitable!
If his father was really the creator of the universe, then he was an omnipotent existence in this inner world, turning into a Xiantian lifeform and giving birth to the Luo Family in some corner of the universe. Then he began to nurture Luo Zheng from a young age, and when his mind was close to maturity, he arranged a tragic situation at will, causing him to "fall".
That way, he would grow rapidly in the midst of hatred!
Luo Zheng's breath became colder and colder as he continued to deduce endlessly!
In addition, Luo Zheng also had a lot of other evidence in his mind. When Luo Zheng attacked the "little monster" of Yu the Great Divine Kingdom with his soul, he devoured his opponent's soul and directly inherited the will of the Boundless World!
At that time, he had inherited it so easily. It was as if the will of the Great Wide World had originally belonged to him.
It was just that other people's research on the will of the world was too rare, and Luo Zheng couldn't find other things to compare it with, so he didn't feel that it was strange. Instead, he thought it was reasonable. Now that he thought about it, he could easily inherit the will of the world. Perhaps it was related to Luo Xiao?
Furthermore, when Luo Zheng was transcending the tribulation in the Xin Huo inheritance and encountered the Luck Destroying Bamboo Snakes, a gigantic figure appeared and directly tore the Luck Destroying Bamboo Snakes apart and devoured them …
These things could not stand up to careful scrutiny. They appeared inexplicably, and were not Luo Zheng's own abilities at all.
It was as if there was an invisible protector behind him that would always appear at critical times and protect him successfully through difficulties.
However, this kind of protection did not appear every time. At least, when Luo Zheng had experienced the limits of life and death several times, it had not appeared. Perhaps it was to train his mind.
Of course, these were still Luo Zheng's deductions!
It was just that the possibility of this deduction being true was quite high. The remaining part was for Luo Zheng to verify with his own hands!
Thinking that his father might not have died, Luo Zheng's mood was quite complicated. After all, whether it was Hua Tianming's words or Han Liusu's words, they all knew that the builder of the Dayan Universe was in a dying state.
Even if it was confirmed that this Saint Luo Xiao was the same person as Luo Zheng's father, then the burden on Luo Zheng's shoulders would be even heavier.
This burden was destined to fall on Luo Zheng's head from the moment he was born. It was originally something that Saint Luo Xiao could do …
With all kinds of thoughts in his head, he was stunned for a long time and did not speak.
"Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng …" Han Liusu called out a few times. Luo Zheng did not respond, only staring blankly into the distance.
Han Chuyue stretched out her white fingers and waved them in front of Luo Zheng several times, then asked, "Sister, could it be that this person is stupid?"
Han Liusu shook her head. In her eyes, Luo Zheng's state of mind was extremely strong. In the face of death, he did not panic. The problem must be with the name "Luo Xiao". These two people had the same surname, and Luo Zheng's talent was so outstanding. Could it be that they were father and son?
This kind of conjecture seemed to be untenable …
If Luo Zheng was really the son of a Saint, how could he be just a second-level creature?
And which Saint Emperor would arrange for his children to cultivate in the universe? After all, second-level creatures had many innate defects. The laws they comprehended were only the laws of Saints. The world they came in contact with was only a world created by True Yuan. They even had to comprehend the Divine Dao and become a True God before they had the qualifications to move about in the Divine Domain.
This kind of gap was very difficult to make up for.
Taking a step back, any creature in a martial artist's inner world could be counted as their people …
Just as Han Chuyue was waving her hand, Luo Zheng suddenly waved his hand. With a "pa" sound, he slapped Han Chuyue's hand away, then jumped up and stood on top of a tower in the distance.
Probably because she was hit, Han Chuyue pouted and looked at her sister, feeling wronged.
Han Liusu did not feel bad. Her sister's temperament was pretentious. After all, she had learned a bit of the ways of the world.
"Hmph, even if this Luo Xiao is really his father, is there a need to be so agitated?" Han Chuyue clearly saw through it and said with dissatisfaction.
"In the end, you are not him … If our father was ordered to be killed by the Saint Halls, and our Han Family's disciples were also hunted down, how would you and I feel?" Han Liusu asked back.
Han Chuyue shrugged her shoulders, indicating that she could not think of anything.
The Saints Hall was a rather stable organization. Every Saint had their own rights. Under normal circumstances, it was almost impossible for Saints to attack each other. Back then, when they joined forces to kill the Saint of Evolution, Luo Xiao, there were many hidden secrets. For a time, there were many different opinions, and there were all kinds of conspiracy theories.
However, the real reason was never revealed.
Perhaps the father of the two sisters knew, but such a big matter could not be told to the two little girls of the Han Family who had not yet become True Divinities.
The Forbidden Land of God Refinement was extremely large. Although one could see the top of the cave from here, this cave was still as large as several great worlds.
Looking into the distance, what Luo Zheng needed the most right now was peace.
Previously, he seemed to have fallen into layers of mysteries, but now that he had obtained this news, it was like a sharp knife that completely cut open the doubts in his heart!
Perhaps he was a creature that had been deliberately created!
When he was young, his father's teachings were still clear in his mind. That Luo Xiao might just be an "incarnation" of the real Luo Xiao. Strictly speaking, he could not be considered his father.
Luo Zheng had also condensed an incarnation in his body. It was not his main body, but it still represented his will. In the end, he had poured his feelings into him.
He was still his father!
Even if he could be created, he had to repay this kindness.
If he wanted to save Luo Xiao, would he have to face a colossus like the Saint Halls?
Compared to a Saint, the current him was so small that he could be ignored.
The Saint Race was eroding the Great Expansion Realm. Since Luo Xiao had already sent him to this step, the rest of the journey would need to be walked by Luo Zheng alone. This road was destined to be difficult, but Luo Zheng still did not have the slightest intention to retreat. His eyes faintly revealed a look of excitement!
He stayed in the Sky Vault Clan for another two days. During this time, he went to the Surge of Tides to visit and met Luo Xi Xuan and Jiang Zhengyi.
Six of the Ten Children of the Heavenly Dao appeared in the Sky Vault Clan, but the remaining four had entered some cultivation holy land. Especially Xi Youqin … Luo Zheng was still a little worried. This girl was impulsive and very willful. He hoped that she could safely complete the trial in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
Two days later, the Yuanhe Clan, who controlled the Heart Refining Tower, finally arrived at the Sky Vault Clan!
Compared to the Sky Vault Clan, the Yuanhe Clan's appearance was very ugly. Their bodies were covered with snake-like scales, their noses were high, and their eyes were like triangles hanging upside down on their faces. If it were not for their behavior, Luo Zheng was more inclined to think that they were strange beasts …

Chapter 1532
The Heart Refining Tower controlled by the Yuan He Clan was a sacred cultivation ground for tempering one's state of mind.
Perhaps it was because their state of mind was stable and lacked that trace of ruthlessness, allowing them to maintain an indifferent attitude at all times. That was why it was so easy to get along with them.
Now that the three major races had gathered, it was time to set off.
The Mountain-collapsing Clan and the Yuanhe Clan had sent their strongest elites this time. The Sky Dome Clan had also prepared for this. In addition to these elites, there were also some warriors from other clans who were willing to train with them.
When the sun rose on the second day, everyone set off for the north of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement!
At this time, Han Jia had also sent more people. Including He Zuowei and the others, there were more than 20 Sector Lords. They brought along Han Liusu and Han Chuyue as they set off with the three races …
To the three major races, this was an extremely important exploration trip. No one knew what methods Han Jia had used to get them to agree to go with them.
The three major races had different attitudes toward warriors from other races. The Mountain Crumbling Race was the most domineering and only recognized the position of the strong. Therefore, warriors from other races like Han Cangyan could be qualified for the position of leader. However, even if Han Cangyan had been the leader of the Mountain Crumbling Race, they wouldn't give Han Jia any special treatment. After all, Han Cangyan was the strong one.
The Yuan He Clan and the other races got along very well. It was almost as if they didn't care. Whether it was secondary life forms or God Realm warriors, they were all treated equally.
On the other hand, the Sky Dome Race cared a lot about the exchange of benefits. Therefore, it was the Sky Dome Race's decision to allow Han Jia to explore with the three major races this time.
Advancing north from the Sky Dome Race's territory, there were still many tall towers. After walking for about ten miles, the densely packed towers suddenly disappeared. At this point, they were considered to have left the Mountain Crumbling Race's territory.
In front of everyone, there was a clear dividing line!
On one side of the demarcation line was a calm black water, but the black water suddenly disappeared here, as if someone had drawn a knife on the ground. Beyond the demarcation line was a boundless ice field.
The white world of ice and snow extended all the way to the end of the world.
"Stop!"
Along the way, the Sky Dome Race had been leading the way. At this moment, the leader of the Sky Dome Race, Gong Yu, suddenly waved his hand and everyone stopped.
Luo Zheng also felt strange. He was used to seeing strange things in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Although the ice field in front of him was spectacular, it was relatively ordinary. However, Gong Yu's expression was very serious.
However, the other party was a native of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement after all. There was definitely a reason for them to be cautious. These reasons were even bought with a bloody lesson.
Under Gong Yu's command, the Sky Vault Clan's martial artists took out some blood-red fruits and started to distribute them.
... this place........................ of the Golden Sun Fruit................................ But this place can also be considered a place of trial. The deeper you go, the stronger the frost flame poison. If you martial artists from other races want to comprehend the profound meaning of the Ice Laws, this place is the most suitable! "
The surface of the blood-red fruit was covered with dense veined patterns. The veined patterns emitted a crimson light as if lava was flowing within them. One of the Sky Vault Clan members flew to Luo Zheng's side and tossed a fruit to him. The fruit looked fresh and juicy, but when Luo Zheng held it in his hand, he could feel a trace of scalding heat from it.
Even Luo Zheng could feel a trace of scalding heat coming from the fruit. When the other cultivators caught the fruit, they almost constantly tossed it back and forth in their hands. Finally, they wrapped the fruit in their true essence and stuffed it into their spatial rings!
When everyone heard about this frost poison, they didn't take it seriously.
After all, most of the martial artists present were at the peak of the Divine Transformation realm. They simply didn't place such an extremely cold place in their eyes.
"Little Sister, put this on first." Han Liusu took the lead and took out a robe embroidered with fire clouds. She handed it to Han Chuyue. Their Han Family had been operating in the Forbidden Land of God for many years. They were very familiar with the Forbidden Land of God and naturally knew just how terrifying this Frost Flame Icefield was.
It could be said that the master of the Forbidden Land of God had recorded all of his comprehensions of the Ice Laws on this ice field.
Although Saints were proficient in all Laws, it was impossible for them to miss out on the Laws when constructing a universe. However, the master of the Forbidden Land of God mainly cultivated the Ice Laws and had his own comprehensions of the Laws. Thus, this Frost Flame Icefield couldn't be underestimated …
Han Liusu put on the fire cloud robe for Han Chuyue. Then, she walked in front of Luo Zheng and whispered, "Since they want to cross the Frost Flame Icefield, their destination should be the inner world of the Forbidden Land of God. I don't know what they want you to take away, but I must remind you to be careful. Don't force yourself and put your own life above all else."
Luo Zheng nodded. He had received the Mountain Collapsing Clan's grace and had also agreed to their request. He would naturally do as he was told.
But, the premise of this was that they were in a relatively safe situation. If they were in a desperate situation, it was impossible for Luo Zheng to risk his life for them. Since both sides were using each other, they could only use an equivalent exchange.
This wasn't to say that Luo Zheng was being realistic. It was just that the pressure he was shouldering was unprecedentedly heavy. Just his fate in the Great Evolution Universe was enough to give Luo Zheng a headache, much less the fact that he still needed to enter the Realm of the Gods in the future.
In other words, Luo Zheng's life was more precious than anyone else's. He couldn't let himself die here. That would be an irresponsible behavior.
"Inner world, what is that?" Luo Zheng had just heard another unfamiliar term.
Han Liusu continued to send a true essence sound transmission, "The Forbidden Land of God is divided into the outer world and the inner world. Everything you see, including this massive cave, is only the outer world. The inner world is the core of the Forbidden Land of God …"
Han Jia had sent many subordinates into the Forbidden Land of God. After being rooted here for so many years, they understood the Forbidden Land of God much better than ordinary martial artists. And Han Liusu, as the princess of the Han Family, naturally had a natural advantage.
Besides martial artists who comprehended the Ice Laws, very few people would go to the Frost Flame Icefield.
However, to ordinary martial artists, the Frost Flame Icefield was a land of death. Without the Sky Vault Clan's Golden Sun Fruit, they would quickly be severely injured by the cold poison …
As for the martial artists of the Realm of the Gods, they could directly comprehend the complete Ice Laws of the Realm of the Gods, not the Laws constructed by the Saints.
Perhaps a Saint's comprehension of the Laws was in a perfect state, but every Saint's comprehension was different and their arrangement of the Laws was different. There would always be a slight deviation. They could go to the Realm of the Gods and comprehend the power of Laws; there was no need for them to come to the Forbidden Land of God.
Thus, there was only one possibility for passing through the Frost Flame Icefield, and that was to enter the inner world …
Frankly speaking, the joint operation of the three great races had fulfilled Han Liusu's wish. This was because the Soul Subduing Cliff she had always wanted to go to was in the inner world!
Wanting to enter the inner world wasn't easy for Han Jia.
In the past, her big brother Han Cangyan had once organized the Han Family, the Qin Family, and other Realm of the Gods influences of the Forbidden Land of God to enter the inner world.
Because of this, they had paid a terrible price. Just the Han Family alone had lost over 20 World Lords, and Han Cangyan had been severely injured.
Most importantly, after such a large expedition, not a single person had been able to enter the Soul Subduing Cliff. As for the Sunflower Water Ancestral Land and the Paramita Sand, there was no way to enter. This caused Han Cangyan to return empty-handed and be depressed for a long time …
When Han Cangyan was sent to the Forbidden Land of God, it was hoped that he would be able to open the Soul Subduing Cliff. Unfortunately, even after Han Cangyan had succeeded in his cultivation, he still hadn't found a way to enter the Soul Subduing Cliff.
This time, it was Han Liusu's turn.
In everyone's eyes, Han Liusu's talent was far inferior to Han Cangyan's. The only thing that made Han Liusu stand out was her uncontrollable Deviant Flame talent.
Thus, when Han Liusu shouted that she wanted to enter the Soul Subduing Cliff, others only treated it as a joke.
Even He Zuowei and the others, although they verbally supported Han Liusu's idea, in their hearts, they didn't believe that she had the qualifications.
After finishing their preparations, the Sky Dome Clan took the lead to cross the obvious snow line. Then, everyone else followed.
No matter who it was, the moment they entered the Icy Flame Icefield, their hair, clothes, and even their eyebrows and eyelashes bloomed with blue icy flames!
Although these icy flames were condensed, they weren't solid. They were like icy blue flames swaying.
However, these icy flames didn't give off the slightest feeling of warmth. Instead, they gave off a bone-chilling burning sensation!
Luo Zheng didn't swallow the Golden Sun Fruit. After entering the snow line, his entire body seemed to be frozen. Looking at the icy flames all over his body, Luo Zheng was a bit surprised. The icy flames here were indeed domineering.
As his body abruptly shuddered, the glacial flames were completely extinguished.

Chapter 1533
After the frost flame poison entered Luo Zheng's inner world, it started to fall like snowflakes.
When these snowflakes fell on the brown Chaos Sea, they released biting cold air as if they wanted to freeze the Chaos Sea …
However, just as the frost flame poison released its cold air, it was instantly swallowed by the Chaos Sea like a sheep that fell into a pack of wolves.
Ever since Luo Zheng gave the Chaos Sea the ability to swallow, it could transform almost anything. Luo Zheng also had a clear understanding of the terror of the Chaos Sea.
However, Luo Zheng was currently more inclined towards body refinement. As for the Yi God Sword, although it made use of the Chaos Sea, the power of the Yi God Sword was displayed more by Luo Zheng's comprehension of the Divine Dao. He didn't display the Chaos Sea to its limit. In other words, Luo Zheng lacked a cultivation technique that could fully display the power of the Chaos Sea!
The frost flame poison was still drifting in his inner world. Most of it fell into the Chaos Sea and disappeared instantly. However, a small portion fell into the main continent of his inner world. There might be a few unlucky creatures that came into contact with the frost flame poison and were instantly frozen into ice. However, overall, the effect on Luo Zheng's inner world was completely negligible.
Compared to Luo Zheng's relaxed expression, the other cultivators didn't have it so easy.
Although the Sky Dome Clan distributed the Golden Sun Fruits, most of the cultivators present didn't choose to swallow them …
The Sky Dome Clan Leader said that one could comprehend the profound meaning of the Ice Law in the frost flame ice plain.
In fact, these cultivators from the God Realm knew better than Gong Yu that the so-called profound meaning of the Ice Law was the famous consummate skill of the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, the Ice Soul Devil Flame that shook the God Realm 30,000 years ago!
The Ice Soul Devil Flame was a profound meaning created by the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. None of the cultivators before him had comprehended this profound meaning.
When the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement entered the Saint Realm and was crowned as a new Saint, he suddenly built a Forbidden Land of God Refinement and announced to the entire God Realm that everyone could enter the Forbidden Land of God Refinement to cultivate and even absorb his power of origin. The entire God Realm was in an uproar.
Besides the Origin energy, he also said that his understanding of the Ice Soul Devil Flame was also sealed in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, which was the Frost Flame Icy Plain under everyone's feet!
However, after so many years, many people had entered this ice field, but no one was able to comprehend the profundity of the Icy Soul Devil Flame …
To the warriors of the other clans and the God Area, it was a chance to comprehend the power Upanishads.
As for the Golden Sun Fruit's ability to resist the Frosty Flame Poison, he would only use it when he couldn't hold on any longer.
Most of the warriors were in the same situation as Luo Zheng. The Cold Flame Poison lingered around their bodies, following their meridians and constantly invading their Inner Worlds, causing snowflakes to flutter in their Inner Worlds.
However, their inner worlds couldn't compare to Luo Zheng's. After all, no matter how overbearing this Frostflame Poison was, it was ultimately formed from True Essence. It was no more overbearing than Chaos Qi.
The temperature of the martial artists' inner worlds began to plummet.
The Inner Worlds of the Divine Transformation realm warriors, especially those at the peak of the Divine Transformation realm, were relatively complete. Some of them even had Fourth or Fifth Grade sects within their Inner Worlds, and some even had Divine Core warriors, or even Void Tribulation warriors …
But as the temperature plummeted, those living beings suffered. The snowflakes formed from the Frostflame Poison released extremely low temperatures, causing their Inner Worlds to fall into a harsh winter. Some of the warriors even had layers of ice forming above their True Essence, causing devastating damage to the living beings in their Inner Worlds.
This was the fate of all second-grade living beings. They lived in someone else's Inner World, unable to control their own fate. They might be frozen to death by the sudden harsh winter when they were just born, or they might be infected by the Frostflame when they stepped into the Xiantian realm, instantly freezing to death!
This kind of situation didn't only happen in the Inner Worlds, even the Inner Worlds constructed by Saints had the same problem. If Saints were to fight, even the stable Inner Worlds would be affected, and it would bring about an earth-shaking crisis of extinction.
Just like the Great Evolution Universe, the Great Evolution Universe would undergo a cycle of reincarnation every 3.6 billion years, and it would bring about a great calamity of extinction. However, this great calamity wasn't caused by Luo Xiao fighting with others, but by some other hidden reason.
It was just that the Empyreans within the Inner Worlds had concluded that this was the opposite of what was to come. They even tried to control this law and prevent the Inner Worlds from going extinct …
This was the sorrow of second-grade living beings. It was impossible to completely control one's own fate, and even one's vision was limited. Even someone as powerful as an Empyrean couldn't control their own fate; they were still second-grade living beings.
For instance, those living beings within their Inner Worlds that had been corroded by the Frostflame Poison might have thought that the Heavenly Gods were enraged and were sending down a calamity of ice and snow. They all knelt to the ground and prayed for the Heavens to calm their anger. What they didn't know was that this calamity was nothing more than the master of their Inner Worlds wanting to undergo a tempering experience!
Hu hu hu …
The cold winds of the Frostflame Ice Plains were swift and fierce.
As the cold winds blew past, everyone's bodies were suddenly 'decorated' with lanterns and decorations. This was because the Frostflame Poison that hung on their bodies was an even brighter blue, as if they were all wearing sparkling jewelry. It was a very beautiful sight. At the same time, the amount of Frostflame Poison that invaded their bodies would greatly increase …
This process was quite painful. That kind of coldness went from the outside to the inside. After it wreaked havoc within the Inner Worlds, it went from the inside to the outside. It was bone-chilling.
It was precisely because of this that the Frostflame Poison left behind in their Inner Worlds left behind the imprint of laws!
Although no one had completely comprehended the profound meaning of the Icy Soul Devilish Flame in all these years, the Icy Flame Poison had indeed brought tangible benefits to cultivators. At the very least, the speed at which the ice elemental laws in everyone's inner worlds were perfected was extremely fast. At their level of cultivation, they needed to use the laws they had comprehended to construct the laws in their inner worlds.
Of course, this didn't mean that everyone could endlessly absorb the Frostflame Poison. After all, this thing could be fatal.
Some of the weaker martial artists' faces were deathly pale. A gust of cold wind blew past, causing them to involuntarily curl up.
"If you can't bear it, don't force yourself! After swallowing the Golden Sun Fruit, you can withstand it! "someone from the Sky Vault Clan reminded.
Those martial artists couldn't bear it and could only swallow the Golden Sun Fruit in one gulp! After the Golden Sun Fruit entered their bodies, it quickly escaped into their Inner Worlds and turned into a small golden sun. It released a scorching warm current that warmed everything within their Inner Worlds. As for the secondary creatures struggling on the edge of life and death within their Inner Worlds, they were naturally saved at this moment. After enduring the cold winter, their Inner Worlds were once again flourishing.
This warm current flowed from the inside to the outside, washing away the coldness in their meridians. It also allowed those martial artists' faces to return to their normal ruddy complexion.
However, at this moment, even more martial artists were still resisting the discomfort and bitterly persevering!
On this trip, the most relaxed one should be Han Chuyue.
She was wearing a Fire Cloud Robe, so the Frostflame Poison couldn't enter her body at all. She was curious about everyone's reactions. Looking at the constantly swaying Frostflame Poison on the faces, hair, and beards of her sister, she couldn't help but laugh.
Everyone maintained a low altitude and their speed wasn't considered slow. After advancing like this for four hours, they had traversed nearly a hundred thousand miles of distance.
From this hundred thousand miles of distance, one could see the disparity in everyone's strength.
At ten thousand miles, only a few weaker foreign race cultivators had taken the Golden Sun Fruit. At thirty thousand miles, 20% of the cultivators had taken the Golden Sun Fruit, and when everyone reached seventy thousand miles, the number of cultivators who had taken the Golden Sun Fruit had reached 90%!
As for the hundred thousand mile journey, among the martial artists present, only the Mountain Shattering Clan's Divine Hunting Team, the Sky Dome Clan's seven Elders, and several elites of the Yuanhe Clan could bear the burden.
Of course, there was also Luo Zheng and Han Chuyue …
As for Han Liusu, if she activated her Heavenly Flame talent, she would be able to resist the Frostflame Poison. However, people who were proficient in fire were often more afraid of the cold. She had already swallowed a Golden Sun Fruit when she was thirty thousand miles away.
"Just as Brother Lei Toad said, Luo Zheng not only can resist the Heavenly Flame, but also the cold?" Jin Yue, the leader of the Yuanhe Clan, said as he looked at Luo Zheng with a hint of surprise in his eyes.
He led the Yuanhe Clan here, so he naturally heard about this "Fire Repulser" from Gong Yu and Lei Toad. Like Gong Yu, he didn't expect that the "Fire Repulser" mentioned by the Mountain Shattering Clan a few months ago could improve his strength to such a level in such a short time …
However, seeing once was better than hearing a hundred times. Gong Yu and Lei Toad's descriptions were so vivid that he had some doubts in his heart. At this moment, he saw that Luo Zheng was just like them, able to move freely in this world of ice and snow without consuming the Golden Sun Fruit. He was indeed quite capable.
"Hehe, you and I can resist the Frostflame Poison at the hundred thousand mile stage. Do you think this guy can resist the Frostflame Poison at the hundred and fifty thousand mile stage?" Gong Yu suddenly said.
One hundred and fifty thousand mile …
That was the deepest part of the Frostflame Icefield. Even the three leaders had to swallow the Golden Sun Fruit.

Chapter 1534
At this stage, the cold flames below the ice field burned fiercely. The azure light was constantly refracted, making it difficult to look at directly. Everyone felt as if they were above the sea of fire, but they couldn't feel the slightest bit of warmth from the azure flames.
"Swoosh!"
A member of the Mountain-Crushing Race's Divine Hunter Team suddenly fell into the ice flames below!
Gong Yu reacted extremely quickly. He spread his wings in the air and turned into a streak of light, circling below and saving the Mountain-Crushing Race member.
"No one can save you if you fall into the ice flames! If you can't hold on, don't force yourself, "Gong Yu reminded.
The higher-ups of the three races also had the mentality of a competition. None of the Mountain-Crushing Race's Divine Hunter Team swallowed the Golden Sun Fruit. In fact, as pure body refiners, they couldn't possibly understand the profundities of the ice laws.
Using extreme cold to refine one's body was a path, but there was no need to take such a big risk.
"Toram! Don't force yourself, "Lei Toad also ordered.
The Mountain-Crushing Race's warrior called Toram nodded and swallowed the Golden Sun Fruit in one gulp.
Apart from Toram, another person from the Sky Vault Race also swallowed the Golden Sun Fruit without forcing himself. Gong Yu continued to lead the way while the rest followed behind.
As more and more cold flame poison entered Luo Zheng's inner world, it became denser and denser. The snow in that world also became heavier and heavier …
The living beings in his inner world were experiencing an unprecedented winter.
Although the Chaos Sea wasn't frozen, the temperature inside suddenly dropped by a lot …
If the temperature continued to drop, the living beings in his inner world would probably face a large-scale death.
Losing a portion of the living beings didn't affect Luo Zheng too much. After all, Luo Zheng didn't rely on the power of faith of these people at this stage. However, he still didn't want the people he created to die.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's thoughts moved slightly.
Countless schools of fish suddenly appeared in the Chaos Sea. The moment they jumped out of the water, they spread their wings and flew straight into the sky!
A dense school of fish flew through the air. This scene was witnessed by the fishermen in his inner world, and some of them recorded it down. Countless years later, people would praise it as the Fish of the Northern Underworld, transforming into the body of a Kun Peng, saving the world from the snow, saving the world from the heavens …
He didn't know that this was just Luo Zheng's idea.
After those small fish leaped into the air, they charged towards the Frost Flame Poison. The moment they came into contact with the Frost Flame Poison, they turned into Chaos Qi and devoured the Frost Flame Poison!
Luo Zheng tried his best to keep the Frost Flame Poison in the air. At the same time, the nine stars hanging high in the sky began to move.
"Nine Stars Shine Together!"
Luo Zheng didn't extract the power from the stars. Instead, he let the nine stars move with his own power.
Drilling wood to make fire was the process of producing heat while the material was moving at high speed. Only then could the wood be drilled into flames. All heat was produced during movement!
The hotter the star, the more intense the activity inside.
The power of the nine stars was so strong that even Luo Zheng himself felt terrified. One could imagine the heat emitted when this power was mobilized!
Therefore, the temperature, which had dropped suddenly, began to rise slowly under the illumination of the nine stars!
After settling down his inner world, Luo Zheng was still calm and unhurried. He continued to move forward with the large group.
120,000 kilometers …
At this stage, only two people in the Divine Hunters hadn't devoured the Golden Sun Fruit.
The Sky Dome Clan's performance was slightly better. As cultivators, their ability to understand and adapt to the laws was much stronger than the Mountain Crushing Clan's. However, the Mountain Crushing Clan's members had relied on their physical bodies to withstand the attacks. It was not a simple comparison between the stronger and the weaker ones.
As for the Yuanhe Clan, only one martial artist was left. It was their leader, Jin Yue.
The Yuanhe Clan was calm. Perhaps there were still a few people in the clan who could persevere. However, they didn't like to compete with others and knew that there was no need to force themselves. Many people had left early and swallowed the Golden Sun Fruit.
Even the three leaders, Lei Toad, Jin Yue, and Gong Yu, were becoming more and more tired as they resisted the corrosion of the surging cold flames!
In order to resist the cold flame poison, they became more and more focused. At first, they were still paying attention to Luo Zheng, but in the end, they could only concentrate on moving forward while using all the strength in their bodies to protect themselves!
As for the other martial artists, they were all paying attention to Luo Zheng!
"When Sage Jiu stepped into the Sage Realm and was conferred as one of the three new Sages, he didn't accept a single disciple!"
"According to convention, when a Sage has a new understanding of the laws in the Realm of the Gods, he would record his own understanding on a tablet and pass it down to the world! After all, at their level, laws are nothing more than floating clouds, and the space in the Realm of the Gods to explore new laws and mysteries is getting smaller and smaller! "
"However, Sage Jiu broke the convention and refused to pass down the tablet to the world. Instead, he sealed the mysteries of the Icy Soul Devil Flame in this place and led countless geniuses to enter to comprehend it, but they all returned in failure! None succeeded! "
"It's said that a certain Sage went to question Sage Jiu! However, Sage Jiu just nodded and smiled, saying that he would definitely keep his promises! "
"… …"
In the group, a martial artist wearing a purple crown was standing atop a flying shuttle, speaking loudly and leisurely. Some of the Realm of the Gods martial artists had already heard him speak many times, and some of the secondary life forms from the universe were listening with rapt attention.
The disparity between secondary life forms and Realm of the Gods martial artists wasn't just a matter of resources and birth, but also a great disparity in information. Thus, all of the secondary life forms, including Luo Zheng, were extremely cautious and did their best to gather information about the Realm of the Gods.
As the purple crowned martial artist saw the foreign martial artists' rapt attention, he was also happy to show off. "In fact, according to my speculations, if you want to truly comprehend the Icy Soul Devil Flame, you have to rely on your own strength and not use the Golden Sun Fruit to pass through the Icy Soul Icy Plains! Hehe, it's a pity that no one has succeeded so far! Even Han Cangyan was only able to last 130,000 miles before he was defeated! That Han Cangyan is still a little bit lacking … "
Hearing the purple crowned martial artist's words, Han Liusu and Han Chuyue glanced at each other. From the side, He Zuowei coldly said, "Someone has already proposed this point of view. To say that it's your own speculation is just too shameless. My Han Family's young master is indeed a little bit lacking, but I wonder how much you're lacking?"
After being choked by He Zuowei, the purple crowned martial artist was a bit embarrassed.
After all, for Han Cangyan to be able to walk 130,000 miles was already an astonishing result. The key point was that at that time, Han Cangyan hadn't broken through to the World Lord realm and was only at the Divine Transformation realm.
But after Han Cangyan became a World Lord, he actually didn't want to come.
The profound meanings of the Icy Soul Devil Flame were indeed fierce, but the dual body and energy cultivator Han Cangyan was still the young master of the Saint Sovereign's side. He might not have placed it in his eyes.
Although Luo Zheng had divided a portion of his attention to resist the Icy Soul Devil Flame Poison, he was still paying attention to the purple crowned martial artist.
Had he reached the end of the Icy Soul Icy Plains?
Now, he had already traveled a hundred and twenty thousand and a half miles. The remaining distance was only twenty thousand miles.
If he really reached the end by himself, would the purple crowned martial artist's words really happen? Would he be able to comprehend the Icy Soul Devil Flame?
Luo Zheng wasn't sure if this profound meaning was strong or not. But, since it was the unique skill of a Saint, it shouldn't be weak, right?
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's interest was piqued. Before this, he was still considering whether or not it was necessary to continue walking like this. If this was just a pure test without any rewards, Luo Zheng wouldn't force himself to continue. He wasn't a masochist …
But now, it seemed that he had to give it a try.
Not long after, everyone finally broke through 130,000 miles!
The moment they entered 130,000 miles, an accident occurred.
The Yuan He Clan's Jin Yue immediately announced that she was giving up. At the same time, she swallowed a Golden Sun Fruit. The Mountain Shattering Clan only had Thunder Toad left. As for the Sky Vault Clan, there were still two people left. They were Gong Yu and another Elder called Fei Chen.
At this moment, Fei Chen silently fell downwards!
In order to resist the Icy Soul Devil Flame Poison, he had no time to divert his attention. By the time he noticed the problem, the Elder had already fallen.
"Fei Chen!"
"Elder Fei!"
Someone screamed. More and more people rushed towards Fei Chen, but they were still a bit too slow!
Everyone watched as Elder Fei Chen was touched by the Icy Soul Devil Flame below. Then, Fei Chen's entire body turned into a block of ice and fell down stiffly!
"Crash!"
It wasn't that Fei Chen didn't try to save himself. The moment he fell, his entire body emitted raging flames. He wanted to resist the Icy Soul Devil Flame, but the bright yellow flames were only formed by True Essence. How could they resist the power of the Icy Soul Devil Flame?
In an instant, the raging flames disappeared in front of everyone's eyes. Then, everyone heard a crisp "ping ping" sound. It was the sound of glass smashing on the ground. Fei Chen's body directly shattered …

Chapter 1535
Although people died every time they passed through the Frost Flame Icefield, it was very rare for an elder of the Sky Vault Clan to die.
After all, the Sky Vault Clan mainly cultivated true essence. They were also exploring the speculations of the Realm of the Gods.
Almost every once in a while, the Sky Vault Clan would try to pass through the Frost Flame Icefield, hoping that a miracle would happen and they would be able to comprehend the mysteries within.
But what the Sky Vault Clan didn't know was that as early as the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, the Realm of the Gods martial artists called Saint Jiu, had created the six great clans, he had already created a boundary that was impossible to cross!
Whether it was Lei Toad, Gong Yu, or Jin Yue of the Yuanhe Clan …
Their performance was outstanding, and their strength improved rapidly. But when they reached a limit, they couldn't advance any further.
It wasn't that their talent wasn't enough, nor was it that their willpower wasn't enough. It was because this boundary had been planted from the day the six major clans were born.
So for so many years, the elites and elders of the Sky Vault Clan couldn't cross the Frost Flame Icefield with their own strength no matter how hard they tried. They were destined to be infinitely close!
After that elder died, Gong Yu and Lei Toad also gave up. Both of them swallowed a Golden Sun Fruit, and their complexions immediately improved.
Now everyone's eyes gathered on Luo Zheng again. Among the hundreds of people in the team, only Luo Zheng hadn't swallowed a Golden Sun Fruit!
At this moment, even Luo Zheng became cautious …
Under the crazy influx of the Frost Flame Poison, the power of the nine star spheres in Luo Zheng's body flowed faster and faster!
But purely relying on the radiance of the nine star spheres couldn't completely melt the snowflakes. Although the temperature of the Inner World was stabilized within a limit, the temperature was still slowly dropping!
Starting from 130,000 miles, the power of the Frost Flame Poison increased a bit after flying a thousand miles forward. The threat it brought to Luo Zheng also increased.
The power of the nine star spheres wasn't limitless. It would take a long time to completely recover the power source, and passing through the Frost Flame Icefield was only the first step …
The others didn't speed up to pass through the icefield because of the threat of the Frost Flame Poison.
Although the Golden Sun Fruit had the power of a Yang Flame, it was still in danger of being extinguished in the face of a large amount of Cold Flame Poison, so everyone maintained a relatively uniform speed. This speed was neither fast nor slow.
Since Luo Zheng wanted to challenge the Frostflame Icefield, and he could withstand the corrosion of the Frostflame Poison, there was no need for him to maintain the same speed as the others!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng's figure suddenly flashed. He broke through the air and rushed forward a distance of several hundred feet. The moment he passed through the space, his speed completely exploded. He turned into a straight line in front of everyone and advanced at an unimaginable speed!
"Sou!"
"Ah! Is that kid crazy? "
"He just broke Han Cangyan's record, and he's already risking his life to advance!"
"If he can really endure the Frost Flame Poison, then doing this can reduce the pressure on me. After all, the shorter the time it takes, the less True Essence will be consumed …"
Gong Yu, Lei Toad, and Jin Yue looked at each other.
"This Fire Expeller is really extraordinary," Jin Yue could not help but praise.
"Maybe he can really pass through safely," Lei Toad said, staring at the thin line that was gradually fading away.
Gong Yu's face showed a worried expression, "What if this kid can't withstand it and dies here?"
The reason why they had mobilized so many people this time was because of the existence of Luo Zheng. If Luo Zheng died, there would be no need for them to continue moving forward. However, they had mobilized so many people this time, so it seemed even more inappropriate for them to just go back like this.
"Let's follow and see," Lei Toad nodded. No matter what, Luo Zheng's life was the most important. As for whether he could comprehend the mysteries of the Frostflame Icefield, it was not important.
After the three of them made up their minds, they also quickly chased after him. However, as they chased after him, they found that the distance between them and Luo Zheng was actually getting farther and farther. After chasing for about ten thousand miles, the three of them showed bitter smiles on their faces. This kid had actually run away without a trace.
Fortunately, the Frostflame Icefield was only a vast plain. Apart from the ubiquitous frostflame poison, there were no other dangers …
The whistling cold wind was like an axe, slashing Luo Zheng's body. At this moment, Luo Zheng had completely disappeared. Countless blue frostflames completely enveloped him, making him look like a large ball of flames flying through the air!
In addition to wholeheartedly rushing forward, Luo Zheng put more attention on the world inside his body.
An unprecedented storm was rising above the Central Continent of his Inner World. This storm only contained sixty percent of the power of the Law of Wind, which was Luo Zheng's own comprehension of the Law of Wind. However, when combined with the Chaotic Qi, the power it displayed was naturally extraordinary!
This storm directly covered the entire Central Continent, as well as the World Tree. The reason he raised this storm was to protect the entire Central Continent. Within his Inner World, the frostflame poison had turned into more and more snow. If this snow really covered the Central Continent, then all the living beings within his Inner World would likely die!
All the snowflakes were blown into the surrounding Chaos Sea. The Chaos Sea, which was originally surging with waves, was now showing signs of being frozen. Pieces of floating ice appeared on the brown seawater, and these floating ice pieces were about to connect into one.
If it were a martial artist that cultivated true essence, then everything within their Inner World would have already been frozen. But in the end, this Chaotic Sea was not something that a Saint could seal. Moreover, Luo Zheng had given the Chaotic Qi the ability to devour!
After rapidly swallowing the snowflakes, the nine stars began to emit a dazzling light that covered the entire Chaotic Sea!
Within his Inner World, the countless living beings could already feel the aura of doomsday. Millions of martial artists were incessantly praying …
"We hope that the World Supporting Sage will expel this deadly poisonous snow with his great Saint strength!"
"Bless our people for all eternity!"
"The Sage's great supernatural ability will protect our people from this crisis!"
Luo Zheng could sense every single word that reverberated within his Inner World. This was the instinct of a builder. At this moment, Luo Zheng had no time to pay attention to them.
"Go!"
Whoosh!
140,000 miles!
Soon, Luo Zheng rushed out of the 140,000 mile boundary …
The flames on the Icy Plains had already been extinguished. However, this wasn't true extinguishment. When matter reached a certain limit, it would change its appearance. For example, when water's temperature dropped to a certain limit, it would turn into ice!
The frostflame here also changed its state, turning into regular icicles that were like sharp swords inserted into the ground.
This was a world of absolute low temperatures.
There was no aura of life. Even the flow of energy and the transformation of Laws had come to a standstill.
There was no wind, and even sound couldn't be transmitted.
Everything around was incomparably quiet. Only Luo Zheng was silently moving forward in the air …
The frostflame poison didn't just freeze Luo Zheng's Inner World. Even the flow of his blood, the beating of his heart, and his thoughts were frozen and rigid!
"Go!"
Under this temperature, Luo Zheng almost stopped thinking. There was only one thought in his mind, and that was to rush forward!
At this moment, a strange change occurred in the vast Icy Plains.
These dense icicles were originally ice blue, but a part of them suddenly turned orange. The two colors formed a sharp contrast, and this part of the orange icicles outlined huge runes!
When Luo Zheng flew over these runes, those orange icicles began to fade little by little, turning back into ice blue …
Luo Zheng galloped forward. The runes below him also began to fade, as if those different-colored icicles were a welcome carpet laid out for Luo Zheng. After he passed by, this road began to dissipate by itself.
Although Luo Zheng's thoughts were extremely rigid, he still noticed the strange phenomenon below. After all, in his field of vision, he could see an orange rune extending into the distance. Unfortunately, he had no time to look at these runes. He could only rush forward!
Not long after, Luo Zheng finally arrived at the end of the Icy Plains. Those runes had completely disappeared.

Chapter 1536
This mirror was like a massive lake of mercury, placed in front of Luo Zheng and blocking his path.
Luo Zheng slowly approached this massive mirror.
He could see that there was an identical version of himself in the mirror, and it was also slowly approaching him.
Finally, Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and gently pressed it against the mirror. Ripples immediately appeared where his fingertips touched, spreading out in all directions.
"This really is a lake of mercury!"
Standing there, Luo Zheng did not act blindly without thinking.
If his guess was correct, this should be the entrance to the Inner World that Gong Yu had spoken of.
He knew nothing about the so-called Outer World and the Inner World. It was best to wait until those natives arrived.
As Luo Zheng approached the mirror, the Cold Flame Poison also completely vanished. The temperature of Luo Zheng's inner world began to rapidly rise, gradually calming down the storm within his body. The nine stars within his body also gradually stopped revolving.
If Luo Zheng continued to madly revolve the Nine Stars, they would release an intense heat that would burn all the living beings within his inner world to death.
Just as Luo Zheng was attempting to restore his inner world to normal, he suddenly discovered that there seemed to be something on the Primal Chaos Sea!
"Incantations?"
Luo Zheng's expression suddenly froze.
Just now, as he was rushing over, he had seen a path of incantations appear in front of him. As he continued to advance, this path continued to disappear along with him …
Now that he looked back, he saw that the incantations had already completely vanished.
At the time, Luo Zheng's thoughts had been frozen by the cold of this place. He simply didn't have the mind to examine them one by one.
But who could have imagined that this string of incantations spanning several thousand li had already been imprinted on the Primal Chaos Sea!
"These incantations are the mysteries of that Saint Jiu?"
Luo Zheng's gaze focused on the numerous wrinkles. Under the cover of this layer of incantations, they formed a circular strip that extended several thousand li above the Primal Chaos Sea, rising and falling on the surface of the sea!
These incantations were similar to the Sanskrit that Luo Zheng had seen before!
There were many types of Sanskrit. For example, Buddhist scriptures were also recorded in Sanskrit. However, Luo Zheng had only seen three types of Sanskrit.
The first kind was the Sanskrit that appeared on Luo Zheng's body. It was the Sanskrit that recorded the Highest Weapon Refining Method. Luo Zheng had seen this kind of Sanskrit many times. Every time his body was refined by the strange flame, the golden Sanskrit would appear on his body.
As for the second type of Sanskrit, Luo Zheng had seen it once when Luo Xi Xuan used her full strength …
However, the two Sanskrit fonts were very similar. They were almost identical, but the arrangement and content of the Sanskrit characters were different.
The key point was that Luo Zheng had never come into contact with this kind of culture, so he naturally couldn't understand it.
Now, the incantations Luo Zheng saw were similar in form to the first two types, but the writing method was completely different. It seemed to lack a certain charm.
"Right, these three types of Sanskrit should have come from the same culture. I didn't have time to carefully examine them before, but now it seems that these incantations should be the same type of Sanskrit. It's just that the writers are different …"
Everyone's writing method was different. The same sentence could be written by different people. Some were like dragons flying and phoenixes flying, while others were like willow bones.
The incantations in front of Luo Zheng should have been written by the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, that 'Sage Jiu'! Inferring from this, this Sage Jiu should have also obtained a Sanskrit, and the Ice Soul Devil Flame was recorded in this Sanskrit?
Luo Zheng had too little information, so he could only infer to this extent.
If this was true, then the Ice Soul Devil Flame might not be as simple as an Ice Law Profound …
Just as he was thinking this, the belt of light formed from Sanskrit suddenly floated above the Primal Chaos Sea. Like a living creature, it floated towards Luo Zheng's Central Continent!
"Eh?"
As he saw these Sanskrit characters begin to move, Luo Zheng became vigilant.
However, his inner world wasn't as fragile as the inner worlds of other martial artists. Primal Chaos Qi was an extremely tyrannical thing. Strictly speaking, it was the highest level of source matter. If these Sanskrit characters moved in the slightest, Luo Zheng would use Primal Chaos Qi to instantly swallow them up.
What Luo Zheng was worried about had happened, and it was even beyond his control. As these Sanskrit characters flowed for thousands of miles, they circled a sea region on the other side of the Central Continent. Then, the temperature of this sea region began to plummet!
As these Sanskrit characters began to emit a cold air, Luo Zheng immediately used the characteristics of Primal Chaos Qi to swallow these Sanskrit characters!
But Luo Zheng soon discovered that his actions were futile!
These Sanskrit characters simply couldn't be swallowed up. Even the Primal Chaos Sea that was circled by these Sanskrit characters couldn't be controlled by Luo Zheng. At this moment, it seemed that there was a part of the Primal Chaos within his body that wasn't under Luo Zheng's control.
He had actually lost control of his inner world. This was something that had never happened before.
In just a moment, the Primal Chaos Qi in this sea region began to freeze. At the same time, it began to transform into an extremely solid layer of ice. At first, these layers of ice were brown. After all, they were formed from the Primal Chaos Sea. But soon, they turned bluish-white, just like the Icy Flame Icefield!
"Rumble, rumble …"
This icefield wasn't small. It was almost a third of the size of the Central Continent!
With such a huge icefield appearing on one side, the entire Central Continent began to shake under the pressure. The creatures living on the Central Continent naturally panicked in the face of such a violent earthquake!
Fortunately, the tremors quickly disappeared. When the ice sheet was formed, the earthquake stopped.
At the same time, an icicle suddenly appeared in the center of the icefield. As soon as this icicle appeared, it began to grow crazily. As it continued to grow, the top of the icicle began to split into countless branches …
In the blink of an eye, the icicle turned into a huge ice tree that stood erect on the icefield!
"Now there's another continent …" Luo Zheng muttered to himself.
This icefield that suddenly appeared formed a compression force in his inner world, pushing the Primal Chaos Sea away. Luo Zheng felt his dantian faintly expand. This was the expansion of his inner world. It was also a manifestation of his cultivation increasing!
The ice tree continued to grow. It was originally only a thousand feet tall, but after a few dozen breaths, the ice tree had grown to several thousand feet tall. It was almost the same size as the World Tree on the other side of the Central Continent!
At the same time, a magnificent Icy Flame Flower bloomed at the top of this ice tree.
If one looked down from the sky at this moment, on the west side of the Central Continent, there was a green World Tree that reached into the clouds. On the other side, there was a sparkling and translucent ice tree. The two were opposite to each other, but also resonated with each other from a distance …
Luo Zheng still didn't know what this meant.
However, in the past, after the Good Fortune Axe gave the Divine Realm a chance of survival, it also received the Weapon Grandmaster Heaven Splitting …
Ruo Mu, Builder Mu, Fusang Mu, Xunmu.
Perhaps these Four Great Trees weren't amazing by themselves …
It was just that these Four Great Trees had been planted in the first Divine Era. Their roots directly penetrated the Divine Realm and extended into the Primal Chaos. They had the ability to absorb Primal Chaos for their own use. After absorbing for countless years, they finally had the qualifications to become Great Trees!
These Four Great Trees stabilized the Divine Realm. At the same time, they also relied on the Divine Realm to stabilize their own positions, becoming the four unshakable pillars of the Divine Realm.
Luo Zheng had previously used a World Tree to give birth to the first Great Tree. Now, on the icefield, another Ice Tree had appeared. It could also absorb Primal Chaos Qi and grow. In the future, it could also grow into a Great Tree!
As for that Icy Flame Flower …
Luo Zheng withdrew his consciousness from his inner world. Then, he opened his five fingers!
"Hiss –"
A cold flame burned in Luo Zheng's hand.
As expected!
Luo Zheng revealed a smile. Then, he poured Primal Chaos Qi into the cold flame!
"Huala!"
In the blink of an eye, this cold flame expanded by more than ten times. It instantly surrounded Luo Zheng. The cold air emitted by this cold flame even froze the mercury on the nearby mirror.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1537
The freezing power of this Ice Flame was extremely terrifying. Most importantly, this Ice Flame Profound was inherited from the Ice Principle of the God Realm, and could be considered a complete Principle.
It was unlike many Principles of the universe, which relied on the Sage's own comprehension and often had deviations and imperfections!
After experimenting for a while, Luo Zheng gently squeezed his hand, and the Ice Flame instantly dissipated in his hand. A faint smile appeared on his face. This Profound cultivated by the Sage could be his trump card at a critical moment.
As for how powerful this Ice Flame was, he didn't have the time to test it out, so he could only wait in front of the mirror.
While waiting, Luo Zheng pondered for a while, then took out the Golden Sun Fruit and swallowed it.
He didn't know what this Ice Soul Devil Flame meant, and whether it was really just a Profound of the Ice Principle, but as a precaution, Luo Zheng didn't want to spread this matter.
If he didn't swallow this Golden Sun Fruit, those people would inevitably ask him about it, and he was afraid that he would have to fabricate another excuse. He might as well swallow the Golden Sun Fruit right now.
Not long after, Gong Yu, Lei Toad, and Jin Yue finally passed through the Ice Flame Icefield, and saw Luo Zheng standing alone in front of the mirror. After sizing him up, Gong Yu couldn't wait to ask, "Luo Zheng, you swallowed the Golden Sun Fruit?"
Luo Zheng nodded slightly.
After receiving Luo Zheng's confirmation, the three of them showed disappointment on their faces …
Without a doubt, Luo Zheng was the last person who could pass through the Ice Flame Icefield without using the Golden Sun Fruit. Previously, when Luo Zheng was running wildly, the three of them couldn't find any trace of Luo Zheng. They even thought that Luo Zheng had died in the Ice Flame Icefield.
They didn't expect that Luo Zheng would still reach the end of the Ice Flame Icefield, but it was a pity that he used the Golden Sun Fruit.
"How long did you last?" Gong Yu asked again.
Luo Zheng smiled, "When there were still five thousand li left, I couldn't hold on any longer, so I swallowed the Golden Sun Fruit into my stomach. It was a failure at the final stage."
"Alas …" Gong Yu sighed deeply. He didn't know if it was because he didn't get to see the secret solved or because Luo Zheng didn't persist until the end.
Jin Yue's state of mind was stable, so she looked at this matter indifferently. "It's already not easy to persist for 145,000 miles. At the very least, no one has ever reached this stage before!"
Other than the warriors in the Third Transformation of the Divine Realm, there were many Sector Lords who tried to break through the Frost Flame Ice Field. However, the cultivation of a Sector Lord was limited after all. Therefore, someone once concluded that only a True God could pass through the Frost Flame Ice Field with their own strength!
Under normal circumstances, True Gods were not allowed to enter the universe.
As for the fact that there were no Heavenly Venerables in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement and no True God had ever been born, who could truly rely on their own strength to cross this icy plain?
Thunder Toad chuckled. "If Luo Zheng enters the inner world and his cultivation reaches the Divine Transformation realm, he can return to Frostflame Icefield! There really is hope! "
"Yes." Gong Yu's eyes flashed.
It had to be said that when the six great races were created, the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement bestowed them with powerful innate talents, nearly perfect bodies, and unique cultivation grounds. However, their master had taken away their right to explore the Forbidden Land of God!
In a complete universe, there were countless lives and countless races that struggled for survival. On one hand, there was the danger of survival, but on the other hand, there was an intense thirst for knowledge!
The six great races didn't face any danger of survival. As the natives of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, their status was naturally higher than that of outsiders. Even the martial artists from the Divine Realm were lower than them. But, their thirst for knowledge couldn't be satisfied in the slightest.
Were they created just to serve those martial artists from other races? There was even a prophet assigned to each race to monitor them. If so, then what was the purpose of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement?
To wild beasts, these questions were meaningless. But to intelligent life forms, these mysteries were enough to drive them insane.
Whether or not Luo Zheng obtained the inheritance of the Ice Laws wasn't important. What was important was that it could solve the doubts in their hearts. Thus, Gong Yu and the others were even more enthusiastic than Luo Zheng himself …
The four of them waited here for a while. Then, the brigade behind them finally caught up.
Even if they swallowed the Golden Sun Fruit and formed a sun in their dantian that constantly released heat, it was still difficult for them to pass through the last 10,000 miles.
As they passed through the last stretch of the journey, there were still three people who were permanently frozen in this icy plain because their strength wasn't enough. Two of them were martial artists from other races and one was a martial artist from the Sky Vault Race.
The stronger martial artists were in a slightly better situation. As for the weaker martial artists, their complexions were all pale. The Ice Laws contained in that silent world could even freeze the sun. It wasn't an exaggeration to say that they had escaped death.
But this time, venturing into the inner world was their own choice. Whether it was the martial artists from other races or the three great races, they had all mentally prepared themselves!
To the martial artists from other races, the inner world was a great opportunity. If they followed these natives, they could reap unimaginable benefits. To the three great races, this was a desperate attempt to cut off all means of retreat!
After everyone had arrived, Gong Yu raised his voice. "This Mirror Lake is the entrance and exit between the Inner World and the Surface World. These are two completely different worlds. Once you enter the Inner World, the danger level will increase by several times. Our three races will do our best. Everyone, please proceed with caution and follow our arrangements! If you act rashly and lose your life, it's your own business! "
Many people nodded silently.
Then, Gong Yu flapped his wings and walked toward the Mirror Lake with his head raised!
