Hearing the loud noise behind him, Gong Yu knew that he couldn't be distracted at this moment, but he couldn't help but turn his head to look at this scene. His breathing involuntarily quickened. When he looked at Luo Zheng again, there was even a trace of awe in his eyes.
In his mind, Luo Zheng was just a secondary creature that wasn't worth mentioning at all. It was impossible for him to lead their exploration this time. He was just a key at a critical moment.
On the first day Luo Zheng entered the Sky Vault Clan, Gong Yu changed his opinion of Luo Zheng. This person's strength was not to be underestimated, and he already regarded Luo Zheng as an existence on equal footing with him! This was an extremely rare action in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
After all, the entire Forbidden Land of God Refinement was the territory of these aboriginals. The six major races had never put the foreign warriors in their eyes.
Just like how Han Cangyan became the leader of the Mountain-Crushing Clan, but he didn't really conquer the entire Mountain-Crushing Clan.
Now Gong Yu understood that he had really underestimated this Luo Zheng. That day, when he probed the Sky Vault Clan, this Luo Zheng had still held back. Otherwise, with the strength this kid had displayed, it was possible for him to destroy the entire Sky Vault Clan with his strength alone.
Of course, this situation couldn't happen. The prophet of the Sky Vault Clan wouldn't allow this to happen …
It wasn't just Gong Yu who improved his opinion of Luo Zheng.
Lei Toad had seen Luo Zheng's strength. When he fought against the True Martial Sacred Beast, Luo Zheng's methods were already extremely terrifying. Lei Toad estimated that Luo Zheng had almost reached his limit at that time. Even if he hadn't reached his limit, he should be close to it.
This Ultimate God Realm youth possessed such terrifying strength. It was already quite terrifying and even incomprehensible.
At this moment, Lei Toad and the members of the Mountain-Crushing Clan were looking at Luo Zheng with an infatuated expression!
That day, when they tested Luo Zheng's strength with the Golden Fork Strange Stone, they hadn't been able to test it because the distance on the plaza wasn't enough. Now they understood that even if the distance on the plaza was enough, they still wouldn't be able to test his limit.
This kid's strength alone was able to block dozens of Formless Heavenly Centipedes and then casually throw them out. How strong was this strength?
If this strength was used on the Golden Fork Strange Stone, it was likely that the stone would be thrown infinitely far away. This kind of test was completely meaningless to Luo Zheng! The so-called Barbarian King title was just a joke to Luo Zheng.
The members of the Yuan He Clan focused on cultivating their hearts. Under the pressure of the Heart Refining Pagoda, their hearts were extremely strong.
Ordinary matters of life and death couldn't even cause their hearts to waver. Therefore, the Yuan He Clan's performance was the most stable. Even though hundreds of Formless Heavenly Centipedes were rushing at them with terrifying momentum, the Yuan He Clan's elites didn't show much panic on their faces.
Heart state didn't seem to affect a martial artist much, but it was extremely useful at critical times. This was because once they panicked, it meant that they had completely failed. Only by maintaining absolute calm could they find a way to resolve the crisis.
However, the strength Luo Zheng displayed at this moment still set off perilous waves in the hearts of the Yuan He Clan's elites!
"This strength shouldn't appear on a Ultimate God Realm martial artist. No, it's even more impossible for it to appear on a second-level life form!"
From what Gong Yu and the Thunder Toad had told her, Jin Yue knew that Luo Zheng wasn't someone to be trifled with. But the problem was that Luo Zheng wasn't someone to be trifled with. He was a monster that defied common sense.
That unimaginably huge strength only lasted for a few breaths of time before it quietly dissipated …
Just a moment ago, Luo Zheng's complexion was still rosy and full of vitality.
However, in the next moment, his legs gave way and he lay flat on the ground, without any strength left in his body.
With his strength completely exhausted, Luo Zheng couldn't even lift his head, his eyelids couldn't blink, and his heart couldn't keep beating. After losing the pumping power of his heart, the blood in his body flowed slowly and his face was deathly pale.
"Luo Zheng!" Han Liusu was the closest to Luo Zheng. She was the first to go up and help Luo Zheng up.
"Did this guy overuse his strength?" Lei Toad and the others also came over.
Overuse of strength usually only happened to body cultivators. This was because no matter how much strength was consumed, a person's strength would never truly be completely exhausted.
Cultivators usually fought with vital essence. The strength of their physical body was only secondary. It was even more difficult for them to exhaust their strength to such an extent. It was only possible if they fought continuously for several days or even months.
However, the situation of body cultivators was more complicated. Body cultivators had more precise control over their strength. After many years of cultivation, they already knew how to squeeze out all their strength.
Most importantly, if a body cultivator used up all the strength in the eighth layer of Dao Pillars, the eight Dao Pillars scattered around the body would continuously absorb the cultivator's strength. This kind of absorption was unstoppable and was also an instinct of body cultivators!
However, Luo Zheng's situation was a bit special.
What he used up was the strength of the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier. Just now, on impulse, Luo Zheng directly drew out ninety-nine percent of the strength of the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier. He didn't use the strength of the eighth layer of Dao Pillars, nor did he use the strength of his own body.
However, Luo Zheng didn't think that there was a price to pay for draining his strength.
No matter if it was the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier or the eighth layer of Dao Pillars, it was a taboo to completely drain their strength. If they lost the nourishment of the source of strength, the nine stars and eight Dao Pillars would be unable to continue. After a long time, they might even directly collapse.
The strength of the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier was almost completely drained. The nine stars didn't have time to replenish their strength through the primal chaos, so they could only draw on the strength of the eighth layer of Dao Pillars to replenish it. Compared to the strength of the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier, the eighth layer of Dao Pillars contained a minuscule amount. The two weren't even on the same level.
As a result, the strength of Luo Zheng's eight Dao Pillars instantly dried up. These eight Dao Pillars then directly drew on the strength of Luo Zheng's physical body …
In this situation of drawing on one cycle after another, how could Luo Zheng's own physical body have so much strength?
After instantly draining the strength of Luo Zheng's physical body, he naturally blacked out and fell directly to the ground.

Chapter 1542
Luo Zheng had already done everything he could. Now, he could only place his hopes on Gong Yu.
Luo Zheng's interception just now had caused the Formless Heavenly Centipedes to fall into chaos. After the Formless Heavenly Centipedes fell into chaos, they all tried their best to crawl out of the mess. However, the more they tried, the more tangled the mess became. Some of the Formless Heavenly Centipedes' long bodies even tangled together.
However, there were still many Formless Heavenly Centipedes that rushed out...
However, at this moment, the runes of Gong Yu's Heavenly Imperial Jade Seal finally covered the entire black sphere!
As the green light flowed along the lines, Gong Yu shouted, "It's done!"
Many martial artists stared at the Formless Heavenly Centipedes warily. Seeing Luo Zheng lying on the ground, they could only choose to fight with their lives on the line for the next wave.
Hearing Gong Yu's shout was probably the most beautiful celestial music in the world!
"Let's go!"
"Quickly go in!"
Almost everyone rushed into the black sphere, fighting to be the first to enter!
Under the protection of He Zuowei and the others, Han Liusu also carried Luo Zheng into the black sphere...
The moment everyone rushed into the black sphere, the Formless Heavenly Centipedes also rushed in. However, after everyone entered, the green runes on the surface of the black sphere instantly disappeared!
The Formless Heavenly Centipedes didn't have high intelligence. They relied on their instincts to attack the black sphere.
However, no matter how the Formless Heavenly Centipedes collided with the black sphere and tried to crush it with their enormous bodies, the black sphere floating in the air remained motionless. It slowly moved on its own orbit and wasn't affected by any external forces, even if they moved the black sphere by an inch.
Luo Zheng quietly lay on Han Liusu's back. He had lost all sense of the outside world, even his sense of touch and smell. This kind of exhaustion of the body's strength was quite uncomfortable. The key was that after a cultivator's true essence was exhausted, they could replenish it with true essence stones. But a person's strength was a gradual process of recovery. Body cultivators thirsted for strength much more than cultivators.
At this moment, green dragon scales lit up in the depths of Luo Zheng's mind. It was the Azure Dragon that was silently transmitting its power to him.
At the same time, a heroic voice rang out. "Haha, I forgot to remind you. Unless faced with a life and death crisis, body cultivators should try their best not to squeeze out the last bit of their strength!"
It was the crimson dragon's voice.
In fact, this was the norm for martial artists. When faced with all kinds of difficulties, their true essence and strength were ultimately insufficient. Preserving a bit of strength was equivalent to preserving a bit of opportunity.
Luo Zheng's desperate gamble, but it brought serious consequences...
....................... Luo Zheng's body body.. the exhaustion.......
But as soon as this energy entered Luo Zheng's body, it was sucked dry by the eight Dao Pillars. Luo Zheng could feel the energy within the Dao Pillars being sucked dry by the Nine Stars!
Luo Zheng was on the verge of tears when he realized this.
How much power did the nine stars need to be filled!?
Qinglong remained silent, but he endlessly poured power into Luo Zheng. However, this power was like a stone sinking into the sea, unable to solve this problem.
Fortunately, the recovery speed of Luo Zheng's nine stars was still quite astonishing.
After all, the Nine Stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier existed above Luo Zheng's Primal Chaos Sea. Aside from absorbing the power of Luo Zheng's eight Dao Pillars, it could also endlessly absorb Primal Chaos Qi. The speed at which it replenished its power with Primal Chaos Qi was naturally much faster than when it absorbed Luo Zheng's power.
One side was replenished by the power of dragon scales, the other side by chaotic Qi. Under the replenishment of both sides, the originally dim nine stars began to brew with a faint seven-colored light …
At this time, the Spiritual Pedestal Nine Stars finally stopped this crazy action, no longer absorbing the power of Luo Zheng's eight Dao Pillars, and the eight Dao Pillars also stopped absorbing the power of Luo Zheng's physical body.
However, Luo Zheng also noticed that the lit dragon scales on Qinglong's body were already dim, almost the same as the unlit dragon scales.
Among the nine dragons, Luo Zheng had the best relationship with Qinglong. After all, on his path of growth, he spent most of his time with Qinglong. After the nine dragons awakened, Qinglong remained silent. At this moment, he silently poured power into Luo Zheng, and Luo Zheng was also very moved.
Previously, he might have felt some resentment towards the nine dragons for deceiving him, and was even more furious at them for using Luo Yan to threaten him, but after knowing the origins of the Great Evolution Universe and Luo Xiao's identity, this resentment completely disappeared.
They had no intention of harming him after all!
Although this power recovered a little and was still very weak, it could at least support Luo Zheng to move freely.
Just as he was concentrating on absorbing the power of dragon scales, everyone had already entered this black sphere. At this moment, Luo Zheng opened his eyes. His surroundings were still pitch black.
"Put me down..."
After his power gradually recovered, Luo Zheng smelled a faint fragrance. Soft hair swayed on his face from time to time. Only then did he realize that he was lying on Han Liusu's back.
"Oh, your power recovered so quickly?" Although Han Liusu had never refined her body, she also knew that Luo Zheng was in a state of exhaustion just now.
"It shouldn't be too big of a problem." Standing up, Luo Zheng activated his Clear Eyes Spirit Eyes in the darkness, but he still couldn't see through the darkness.
Luo Zheng had encountered this situation several times. This place should be filled with strong dark laws.
If it was personally arranged by a Sage, then no one present should be able to see through the darkness. Just as Luo Zheng thought, everyone moved forward in the darkness, and everyone was close to each other.
No one dared to run wildly in the darkness. They didn't understand the space inside this black sphere. In this pitch-black space, everyone was no different from a blind person. At most, they could release their perception to explore the surrounding terrain. However, everyone was walking on a flat surface. There was nothing around them to explore.
After moving forward like this for a long time, someone finally asked, "Leader Gong, are you sure this black sphere is connected to the other black spheres?"
In the darkness, everyone waited for Gong Yu's answer.
After a moment of silence, Gong Yu said, "Yes, last time I entered one of the black spheres, I passed through it and appeared in another one."
"Then what do you think the black spheres are here for?" someone asked.
"I'm not sure... actually, I don't know much about these black spheres." Gong Yu also didn't know the purpose of these nine black spheres floating in the inner world.
At this moment, He Zuowei's voice faintly floated over. "If this old man guessed correctly, these nine black spheres should be the Sage's nine stars!"
Luo Zheng blinked in the darkness. This He Zuowei thought of the same thing as him. He also thought that these nine black spheres were the Sage's nine stars.
"Nine stars? It's said that after entering the Saint Tier, the nine stars are indeed moved into the inner world. However, the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier are the place where the power source gathers. There is no power source in these black spheres, "a Realm of the Gods martial artist questioned.
"This I do not know. This Saint Jiu has always been an extremely independent individual. It is very strange for him to place the source of his power into the Mountain-Crushing Race. However, the thoughts of a Saint are not something existences like us can understand," He Zuowei calmly said.
A Sage was a peak existence in the Realm of the Gods. Perhaps there was a reason for such an arrangement, but ordinary martial artists couldn't guess the purpose.
Luo Zheng also couldn't figure it out. Placing the power source in the Sacred Spring Mountain was equivalent to wasting one's own nine stars. This was equivalent to having no path to take...
"Yi!"
He was pondering when he suddenly noticed a faint flash of golden light not far away.
That golden light appeared for a very short time. However, for a golden flash to appear in such darkness, it was as dazzling as a firefly in the night. Luo Zheng saw it quite clearly!
"What's wrong?" Han Liusu asked from the side.
Luo Zheng was even more curious. "You didn't see it?"
"See what?" Although he couldn't see Han Liusu's face, he could tell that she was very puzzled.
"Nothing..."
In that instant, Luo Zheng really did see a golden flash. It definitely wasn't an illusion.
The light was so dazzling in the darkness, but no one present had any reaction. This could only mean one thing: only he saw that golden flash. Others couldn't see it.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng carefully left the group. He wanted to see clearly what that golden flash was.
Soon, another dazzling golden light appeared in Luo Zheng's pitch-black vision. This time, Luo Zheng saw the golden flash clearly. It was actually a golden Sanskrit character!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1543
Luo Zheng was quite familiar with Sanskrit.
The moment this Sanskrit character appeared, Luo Zheng recognized it.
The overall structure of this Sanskrit character was very smooth. Each stroke was like a tadpole, but the framework was very complex!
A Sanskrit character often contained 30 to 40 strokes!
The biggest purpose of a race's language was to pass down its culture. Generally speaking, the simpler the language, the better it was. After all, learning a language required a lot of time and resources. If the language was simple and easy to understand, it would naturally be easier to learn.
Luo Zheng had come into contact with many races along the way. He had more or less seen their languages, but he had never seen any race's language as complex as this Sanskrit.
According to this inference, the race that used this Sanskrit must be an extremely intelligent race. Perhaps this race existed in the Realm of the Gods …
The golden Sanskrit character only flashed for a moment, then disappeared completely. At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly realized that the group was getting farther and farther away from him.
He frowned slightly, feeling a little strange.
In the darkness, it was impossible for the group to move so fast …
After realizing the strangeness, Luo Zheng quickened his pace and chased in that direction.
In the process of chasing, Luo Zheng could hear their low voices, including He Zuowei, Han Liusu, Thunder Toad, Gong Yu, and the others. They were all whispering something, but their voices were getting softer and softer. This meant that they were getting farther and farther away from him.
"Gong Yu! Thunder Toad! Han Liusu! "Luo Zheng shouted loudly.
But the people in front of him didn't respond. They were still whispering something as they kept moving farther and farther away.
"This …"
Realizing this problem, Luo Zheng didn't have any way to solve it. Besides, his strength hadn't recovered yet. Now, the strength in his physical body was only 10% recovered after absorbing the power of the dragon scale.
"If they find out that I'm not here, they should stop to look for me. But from the looks of it, these people haven't found anything wrong." Luo Zheng's brows were tightly knitted.
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that there was one more person in that group.
This person's aura, appearance, and even voice were exactly the same as Luo Zheng's. Martial artists like them could use their perception to scan their surroundings, but even Han Liusu didn't sense anything strange. She only felt that Luo Zheng had suddenly become very quiet, and thought that he was too lazy to speak because he had exhausted his strength.
After chasing for a while, Luo Zheng stopped. He knew it was futile.
Luo Zheng was completely immersed in the darkness. Everything around him seemed to have turned into nothingness. Occasionally, a golden light flashed, and another golden Sanskrit character appeared in front of him!
It was another Sanskrit that was completely different from the previous ones. After the Sanskrit appeared for a brief moment, it instantly dissipated into the darkness.
Luo Zheng let out a sigh of relief and began to ponder.
The other cultivators were not left behind. He was the only one left behind. There must be some reason for this.
"Because I can see those golden Sanskrit characters?"
In the darkness, Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched slightly. From the time he entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, the owner of this place had always been selecting certain cultivators.
Since he treated the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier like this, there must be a reason.
Just now, Luo Zheng saw that golden Sanskrit character, but the others didn't have any strange reaction. This meant that only Luo Zheng could see it here!
The key reason should be here.
As for why Luo Zheng couldn't catch up to the main group, it should be that someone deliberately isolated him!
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng actually calmed down.
With his strength exhausted, Luo Zheng was afraid of encountering a strange beast that he couldn't resist. The strange beasts in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement were all quite powerful. In this state, he probably couldn't resist.
At this moment, he stood in place and didn't chase in vain. In any case, he couldn't walk out of this space no matter how he ran.
A golden light flashed, and another complicated Sanskrit character appeared before his eyes, then disappeared instantly.
These Sanskrit characters appeared and disappeared continuously. Luo Zheng didn't know what connection there was between them, but he firmly memorized every Sanskrit character.
After about half an hour, Luo Zheng suddenly heard a light breathing sound!
"Hu …"
This breathing sound was quite light, but in this completely silent environment, Luo Zheng still caught it clearly.
There was someone!
In this instant, Luo Zheng expanded his perception to cover an area of several hundred meters.
As expected, he discovered that someone was slowly walking towards him in the darkness.
Luo Zheng's fists clenched tightly at this moment, and the sea of chaos in his inner world gradually rippled.
In a situation where he couldn't use the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier, Luo Zheng could only use the aura of chaos. However, cultivation level limited Luo Zheng's strength. With his cultivation level of the God Tier, it was easy to fight against a Divine Transformation Tier, but it would be quite troublesome if he encountered a Realm Master.
"Don't panic, I have no hostility," an indifferent voice was heard from nearby.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's fists loosened slightly. He indeed didn't feel the other's killing intent, so he asked straightforwardly, "Who are you?"
"Me? Hehe … "The person in the darkness laughed." You just took away about a third of my power source, shouldn't you thank me first? "
In the darkness, Luo Zheng's face was filled with astonishment.
The power source he took away naturally belonged to the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. On the way here, those God Realm martial artists called this person "Saint Jiu."
"You … are Saint Jiu?"
If Luo Xiao was excluded, the strongest person Luo Zheng had seen was the young man who took away Luo Yan that year.
Now that Luo Zheng was suddenly facing a Saint, he felt a great deal of pressure in his heart. Although he didn't feel any aura from this person, he was still an existence at the peak of the God Realm. He should be on the same level as Luo Xiao and his own master.
"That's what outsiders call me. My name is very, very long. Most people can't remember it, so they call me Saint Jiu," the person replied.
"Very long? How long? "Luo Zheng curiously asked.
The person in the darkness spat out a long string of words …
These words were in a language that Luo Zheng had never heard before. It took him nearly twenty breaths of time to spit out this string of words. His voice was sometimes high and sometimes low, as if he were singing.
"Remember it? That's my name, "the person said with a faint smile.
"… …"
Luo Zheng was also speechless.
This string of names had several hundred syllables. It was a bit too long.
From beginning to end, Luo Zheng had only heard one word clearly, and that was the last word of this name, "Jiu."
Presumably, this was the origin of the name "Saint Jiu."
After a moment of silence, Luo Zheng opened his mouth and said, "I'll just call you Saint Jiu … …"
Saint Jiu faintly smiled, "A name is just a symbol. There's no need to care so much about it."
"Mm." Luo Zheng nodded. His heart was full of doubts, but since Saint Jiu had found him alone, he must have a purpose. Was it possible that he had locked him in this dark place just to play tricks on him?
Saint Jiu could roughly sense Luo Zheng's thoughts. He immediately said, "From the moment you entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, I noticed you. Luo Zheng from the Great Evolution Universe, so I changed your birthplace … …"
Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat. After wandering around the Mountain-Crumbling Clan and the Sky Vault Clan, Luo Zheng also understood that there had been a deviation in his birthplace. Nine of the ten Dao Children had appeared in normal birthplaces. Only he had appeared in a desert, which was said to be an abandoned birthplace.
"I also arranged for Masked Saint's youngest daughter to meet you and make her believe that you are her true Son of Heaven." Saint Jiu faintly smiled, "It seems that the effect is not bad. Both sisters have a chance."
"… …"
Luo Zheng was rather speechless. The first sentence was serious, but in an instant, he had jumped to such a topic. This Saint's words were very awkward.
"Don't think that I'm joking. Han Liusu is very important to Masked Saint, not just because of the Strange Flame she was born with. If you have a Saint as your father-in-law, it will be very beneficial," Saint Jiu continued.
"Lord Saint Jiu, I already have a wife. In my life, Luo Zheng has never sought connections with others." Although he was facing a Saint, Luo Zheng could not help but curl his lips.
"Heh, I don't need to listen to your thoughts. There is nothing I cannot do in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement," Saint Jiu said with a smile. This Forbidden Land of God Refinement was created by Saint Jiu. Here, he was an omniscient and omnipotent existence. No one could stop him from doing what he wanted to do.
"Saint Jiu, you're too kind. You won't change my mind, will you?" Luo Zheng said in a low voice.

Chapter 1544
As soon as Saint Jiu said this, Luo Zheng's expression suddenly changed.
"What do you know?" Luo Zheng coldly asked.
Speaking to a Saint in such a tone was very rude, but Saint Jiu naturally didn't care about Luo Zheng's attitude. He had even expected Luo Zheng's reaction.
"As you know, Luo Xiao is indeed your father," Saint Jiu replied.
Luo Zheng had come up with this conjecture from his conversation with the Han Jia sisters in the Sky Vault Clan's tower.
It seemed that Saint Jiu had been paying attention to him ever since Luo Zheng had entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Otherwise, Saint Jiu wouldn't have known that he had come up with this conjecture. Saint Jiu was omniscient and omnipotent in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, just like how Luo Zheng was in his own inner world. There was nothing he didn't know and nothing he didn't know. Nothing about the millions of beings in his inner world could be hidden from Luo Zheng because the entire inner world had been constructed by Luo Zheng.
Before, Luo Zheng had only been speculating, but Luo Xiao's identity hadn't been confirmed. After all, there were certain coincidences, such as the problem of having the same name …
Now that he heard Saint Jiu say it himself, the situation was completely different.
Luo Zheng didn't believe that a grand Saint had nothing better to do and was just teasing him.
Moreover, from Saint Jiu's words, he seemed to have a deep relationship with his father and was helping him!
Thinking about it more deeply, Luo Zheng's fortuitous encounters in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement could be solved. Saint Jiu had set up the Forbidden Land of God Refinement to open up all of his origin energy. This might be a very generous move.
But no Saint would give away a third of their origin energy. That wasn't generosity, it was idiocy.
Luo Zheng had forcibly absorbed so much of his Origin Energy without encountering any resistance. This was something that couldn't be justified.
In the darkness, Sage Jiu took another step towards Luo Zheng. The darkness was unable to obstruct his gaze. His gaze seemed to materialize as it landed on Luo Zheng's body. That gaze seemed to pierce through Luo Zheng's soul, seeing the nine true dragons in Luo Zheng's mind, and also the enormous furnace. Then, it pierced through Luo Zheng's heart and entered Luo Zheng's dantian …
But in the end, his gaze could not penetrate Luo Zheng's Inner World. His gaze was blocked.
An ordinary martial artist's dantian was connected to their inner world. As the saying went, one flower, one world. If a dantian could contain a great world, then the inner world would indeed exist within the dantian!
But Luo Zheng's inner world was completely different. His inner world existed in the primal chaos, a place outside the Realm of the Gods!
Aside from Luo Zheng using the inconceivable 'Transcendence Divine Ability' to communicate, there was no other way to communicate with it.
Sage Jiu was a peak powerhouse who had stepped into the Saint realm. He also possessed the 'Transcendence Divine Ability', but his Transcendence Divine Ability could only communicate with the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement he had created. It had no effect on Luo Zheng's inner world.
This was also why Sage Jiu was unable to see through Luo Zheng's inner world …
With Sage Jiu's gaze, in the entire Realm of the Gods, only the inner worlds of the Saints he was unable to see through. Thus, it was impossible for the Saints of the Realm of the Gods to spy on each other's inner worlds.
"What a wonderful inner world. A world constructed from the primal chaos can be called a new Realm of the Gods." Sage Jiu's words were filled with gratification.
Luo Zheng already had a preliminary understanding of his inner world. Luo Zheng was not surprised by Sage Jiu's conclusion. It seemed that Sage Jiu knew about him, and the information he knew far surpassed his imagination.
What Luo Zheng was interested in was information about Luo Xiao. "What is your relationship with my father?"
Sage Jiu waved his hand in the darkness. "There are some things that are too long to explain. Where should I start?"
At that moment, several more golden lights lit up nearby. Sage Jiu looked back and continued, "That's right, do you know why I destroyed Nine Stars and created such a powerful Black Law here?"
"I don't know." Luo Zheng shook his head.
"I was observing something," Sage Jiu said.
"Those Sanskrit characters," Luo Zheng replied.
Sage Jiu nodded. "Yes, but also no … To be more precise, I was observing some living beings. These living beings' existences are beyond ordinary imagination. These complex Sanskrit characters belong to them. Only in absolute darkness can one find the clues they left behind. In order to create this perfect Law of Darkness, I spent a great deal of effort."
Although Luo Zheng understood Sage Jiu's words, he was still confused.
The Law of Darkness within this black sphere was extremely dense. It was impossible to see anything in this pitch-black place, so what kind of living beings could be inside?
Moreover, Sage Jiu was the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Since these living beings existed in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, how could he not know?
Sage Jiu could roughly guess the doubts in Luo Zheng's heart and continued, "These living beings are not ordinary living beings. If we call the living beings born in one's inner world secondary living beings, then the martial artists of the Realm of the Gods can be called equal living beings. Then, the living beings I was looking for are surpassing living beings!"
"Surpassing living beings?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
Sage Jiu nodded. "Their existences are unimaginable to us."
"They created the Realm of the Gods?" Luo Zheng blurted out.
This was a simple inference. If the Saints of the Realm of the Gods were able to construct the universe, then the life forms within the universe would be secondary life forms. Strictly speaking, Luo Zheng was also a secondary life form, so the life forms within a secondary life form's inner world would be considered secondary life forms?
On the other hand, since these surpassing living beings were above the martial artists of the Realm of the Gods, then they must be living beings outside of the Realm of the Gods.
"Your inference is reasonable." Sage Jiu shook his head. "Whether or not these living beings created the Realm of the Gods is still unknown. However, their level of existence is higher than ours, and the things these surpassing living beings display are far from something we can understand … just like the Sanskrit characters you saw."
As soon as his voice fell, another golden light flashed in the darkness, and another Sanskrit character appeared.
"Just like this Sanskrit character. In fact, it contains a great deal of information, but you can only see the surface of the Sanskrit characters. You cannot decipher the information within," Sage Jiu said.
Luo Zheng frowned. Although he wanted to know more about his father, he was also attracted by Sage Jiu's words.
Indeed …
The structure of these Sanskrit characters might be incomparably complex, but it was not difficult to draw and imitate them. It was just that when he glanced at these characters, there was always an indescribable feeling, as if there was something hidden behind these characters that he could not imagine.
"The race that created these Sanskrit characters was able to interpret them at will," Sage Jiu said excitedly. "This is because the way the world appears in their eyes is completely different from ours! I have a more appropriate description! When we see a flower, it is just a flower. When we see a grain of sand, it is still just a grain of sand. But in the eyes of these surpassing living beings, they can see every detail of the flower with a single glance. The meridians in the center of the flower, everything inside the stamen … "
"Is this something amazing?" Luo Zheng asked in a muffled voice. He was also confused by Sage Jiu's theory. Not to mention, it was not difficult for some martial artists to see through the inner workings of things.
"Quite amazing!" Sage Jiu's tone became more and more excited. "They are a higher level of life, an existence that we cannot understand at all. In the past, your father, I, and North Sage, who taught you the 'Primal Chaos Secret Technique', inadvertently found traces of these living beings and began a new exploration …"
As Sage Jiu spoke of these matters, he completely lost the demeanor of a powerhouse and instead became very excited.
"North Sage?" Luo Zheng's eyes sharpened.
From Sage Jiu's narration, Luo Zheng learned that his master's name was Gu Bei. In the universe, he was known as' North Sage ', and his father, Luo Xiao, was known as' Firmament Sage 'in the Realm of the Gods.
Sage Jiu was a new Sage. Comparatively speaking, Firmament Sage and North Sage could be considered his seniors. The three of them had a very good relationship!

Chapter 1545
Listening to Saint Jiu's explanation in the darkness, Luo Zheng was finally able to connect all the clues.
From the time he obtained the first section of the Exalted Tool Refining Technique to the time he cultivated the Primal Chaos Secret Technique, all the reasons appeared …
Gu Bei, the master of the Immortal Mansion and Luo Zheng's master, did not come up with the Primal Chaos Secret Technique by chance. According to their inference, there were higher-level creatures and civilizations in this world, but those creatures were hard to grasp, and they could not even recognize their forms.
After Luo Xiao was ordered to be killed by the Saints, he fell into a half-dead state …
There was a saying among the Saints that as long as the universe was not destroyed, the Saints would not die. To a certain extent, this was indeed correct.
However, the universe was not a world isolated from the rest of the world. No matter how intricate the structure of the universe was, the true essence within it would constantly leak out. Without the replenishment of true essence by the Saints, the universe would eventually be exhausted.
However, the wisest of the three, Northern Sage Gu Bei, helped Luo Xiao think of a way to let Luo Xiao's universe exist forever!
That was the 3.6 billion era calamity!
In other universes, there was no such thing as an era of 3.6 billion years, and there was no such thing as a great extinction every 3.6 billion years!
After the Saints constructed the universe, the entire universe would be relatively stable. They only needed to maintain it regularly and breathe in and out true essence to maintain the operation of the universe.
However, Luo Xiao could not do this in his state, so he listened to Gu Bei's idea and arranged the 3.6 billion era calamity … Every 3.6 billion years, the universe would undergo a great extinction.
Apart from the Nine Stars, all the stars would fall, and all living beings would wither. Mountains and rivers would collapse, and lakes and seas would overflow. From the Great Thousand Worlds to the Great Realm, no place would be spared!
During the great extinction, the entire universe would begin to shrink!
The space inside the universe would shrink to two-thirds of its original size.
During this shrinking, the universe would undergo a great extinction, and after the great extinction, the universe would begin to expand …
Because the universe was attached to the edge of the Divine Realm, after contracting and expanding, under the effect of the universe's enormous space, it produced a powerful suction force. As it continued to expand, it was able to absorb the true essence of the Divine Realm into the universe!
This Great Expansion Space was like a heart that was constantly contracting and expanding, beating once every 3.6 billion years to absorb True Essence.
The amount of true essence absorbed each time was enough to offset the consumption of the universe for 3.6 billion years.
This kind of contraction and expansion was carried out a total of 14 times.
Fourteen Era Evolution had passed …
In this new era, Luo Xiao's plan was finally implemented, and Gu Bei gave his immortal mansion to Luo Zheng.
To the three of them, it was almost impossible to defeat the Saints. Even if they nurtured to cultivate Luo Zheng to become a Saint, they would still not be able to change the final outcome!
The only way was to nurture Luo Zheng was to to to to become a lifeform Surpassing Level Realm! In essence, it was a being that was a level higher than all other living beings.
Lifeto was to be To was Creation Realm Lifeforms
To be to be to be To to to be to to to to,, to be to be to be Realm

This was a test between Gu Bei and Luo Xiao. Whether or not the final result would be like this, no one could say for sure, because in the endless history of the Divine Realm, such a crazy thing had never happened before!
From the time Luo Zheng was born until now, their ideas had been steadily developing. As for whether Luo Zheng's fate could reach their expectations, no one could say for sure.
The Saints didn't think that Luo Xiao would be able to struggle at death's door for so many years. Although the Divine Realm's Time Laws were different from Huan Yu's, and the flow of time in Huan Yu was far faster than in the Divine Realm, Luo Xiao had clearly lived for far too long!
They realized that if they continued to wait, Luo Xiao would not die on his own. Therefore, within the rules, they sent another Saint to fight and started a war between worlds. The Saint Clan from another universe would directly invade the Great Yan Universe!

Before, Luo Zheng had thought that Saint Jiu was just joking. He hadn't expected him to be so serious.
No matter how great the difficulty, Luo Zheng would not place his hopes on others. This life was still short, but in the struggle of the martial path, he did not hesitate at all. He constantly sharpened himself and became stronger.
But this time, was Luo Zheng really going to curry favor with a little girl for the sake of his goal?
He fell into hesitation … …

"Saint Jiu?"

Chapter 1546

Luckily, the warriors present didn't have too many suspicions. They only thought that Luo Zheng's luck was heaven-defying. After resting for a while, they continued on their way.

Chapter 1547
In the surface world of the Forbidden Land of Gods …
Myriad Elephant Clan, Annihilation Valley.

The lowest level recorded the first level of the Laws of Thunder. Although the power of the Laws above was rich, it was still easy to understand.
The higher one went, the more intense the reaction of the Laws of Thunder became. From the fourth level onwards, blue lightning began to constantly revolve. Every time a bolt of lightning appeared, it would be like a spirit snake that rapidly crawled upwards. As it spread, the power of this bolt of lightning became stronger and stronger. Its color also constantly changed.
It changed from bright white to azure blue, then from azure blue to dark purple, and finally to turquoise green …
These colors represented the level of the Laws of Thunder.
When this bolt of lightning spread to the top, it would turn into a hole in the shape of a wild bolt of lightning. It would emit a violent roar before disappearing without a trace!
Hua Tianming's time was tight.

Creating a Rule Stele was a common practice.

No wonder...

He didn't even need to use the Laws of Space, because this space was created by him and revolved around his will. Thus, he directly used a ball of air in this tent to replace his incarnation, which could create the shocking effect just now.
Hua Tianming was very astute and instantly came to this conclusion.
Saint Jiu nodded and waved his hand at the Prophet. The Prophet's eyes lost their light and he slowly walked out like a puppet.
The Prophets of the Six Great Cultivation Holy Lands were different from ordinary living beings.
The members of the Six Great Races could be said to have been born independently and possessed their own consciousness and souls. However, these Six Prophets were beings that Saint Jiu had personally created, and their souls and consciousness had been bestowed upon them by Saint Jiu himself. In other words, these Six Prophets could be considered his six incarnations …
Thus, the Prophets of the Six Great Cultivation Holy Lands were unimaginably strong and held important positions in the Six Great Cultivation Holy Lands.
Saint Jiu stared at Hua Tianming and calmly said, "Draw your sword."
Hua Tianming showed no hesitation. The Sword of Tengshe on his back was already in his hand.
Saint Jiu continued, "When Gu Bei left the Weapon Grandmaster's treasure to you, it could be considered a backup plan. I want to see how far you've cultivated. Come at me."
Hua Tianming's eyes suddenly flashed. The Sword of Tengshe flickered with resplendent light. A surging sword intent spread out and surged in the small tent.
The tent looked like it was made of canvas, but the surging sword intent did not tear it apart.
After brewing for a while, Hua Tianming's long sword suddenly stopped. The Taoist Connotation that had been condensed to the extreme burst out. Then, the long sword pierced towards his opponent.
"Pu!"
Saint Jiu did not dodge. The tip of the Sword of Tengshe instantly pierced his body.
The sword pierced through Saint Jiu's skin. The sword intent spread through the sword and wreaked havoc in Saint Jiu's body … He was not worried about Saint Jiu's safety. After all, he was only facing one of Saint Jiu's incarnations. It would be a joke if Saint Jiu, a Divine Realm martial artist, could injure him.
However, Hua Tianming's sword only managed to pierce through less than an inch. His sword couldn't advance any further.
Saint Jiu shook his head. "It's not enough. Your understanding of the Sword of Beginning is not enough."
Hua Tianming was not surprised to receive such an evaluation. "Please enlighten me, Saint Jiu."
Saint Jiu stared at Hua Tianming's heroic face and said, "What I want to impart to you is the realization of certain death. Can you accept it? If you can't, I can give up on you. With your cultivation, talent, and strength, you can still live well in the Divine Realm in the future. "
Hua Tianming might be extremely outstanding, but the three of them had already planned for someone else.
"Yes," Hua Tianming calmly replied.
Ever since he had inherited Weapon Grandmaster Chiyou's treasure trove, Hua Tianming had understood his mission.
"Why is that?" Saint Jiu asked. From his point of view, Hua Tianming's attitude was very important. If this person was truly afraid of death, then their plan would be missing an important part.
Hearing this question, Hua Tianming smiled. "Saint Jiu, do you know what I was thinking when I learned that True Gods had unlimited lifespans?"
"Speak," Saint Jiu said.
"I was thinking that unlimited lifespans might be the greatest punishment in the world," Hua Tianming said, looking straight at Saint Jiu.
Everyone's pursuit of life was different. Some mortals chased after meat for three meals a day. Some martial artists chased after the recognition of their sect. Some experts chased after an even stronger path.
Before Hua Tianming had understood the meaning of his existence, he had indeed chased after such a thing.
But after entering the Weapon Grandmaster's treasure trove, his state of mind had undergone an intense change.
He had obtained an unimaginable amount of information, and through this information, he had been able to view the entire Divine Realm.
This was a highly civilized world filled with Laws. Eternal life was an illusory pursuit for the people of this world, but it was not difficult to achieve in the Divine Realm — at least for Hua Tianming.
He had once tried to climb to the peak of martial arts and used that eye to take a look at the peak. However, he sighed and thought that it was nothing more than that. At least when the mysterious veil was lifted in front of him, the scenery behind the veil did not attract him.
On the contrary, he had developed a strong interest in Northern Sage's plan to stay behind.
It was as if this plan had been tailor-made for him!
He knew that when this plan was realized, it would be the moment of his death, but he had agreed to this plan without hesitation.
Not only for Luo Zheng, but also for himself.
"I like this answer," Saint Jiu said. "Living for so long is indeed quite boring … In this trip to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, I will do my utmost to increase your strength."
The situation in the Divine Realm was rather complicated, and Saint Jiu did not want to talk about it here.
"Where's Luo Zheng?" Hua Tianming asked.
"Him?" Saint Jiu faintly smiled. "The blacksmith and crafting furnace have already been prepared. He just went in, and we will soon begin to completely refine his body."
Thunder Toad, Gong Yu, and Jin Yue were in a rather good mood. This trip to the inner world had been rather unlucky at the beginning, encountering the pursuit of so many Formless Heavenly Centipedes, but they had not expected to profit from this disaster and reach their destination so quickly …
They had not imagined that this place was just a crafting furnace that Saint Jiu had carefully prepared for Luo Zheng. He was going to completely refine Luo Zheng's body here!
