Chapter 1550
After saying that, the one-eyed man suddenly swung the golden sledgehammer and smashed it on the ground!
This big golden hammer suddenly smashed down, making a muffled "dong" sound.
When the hammer smashed into the ground, the bottom of the cave was not destroyed, but a faint halo spread out.
Three meters …
Three hundred meters …
Three thousand meters …
Ten thousand meters …
Three million meters …
Deep underground, high up in the sky …
The inner world, the surface world, the entire Forbidden Land of God Refinement was shaking at this moment!
"Old Jiu! Come out! "the one-eyed man shouted.
Seeing that there was no reaction, the one-eyed man smashed the hammer into the ground again.
The second hammer smashed down, causing the entire Forbidden Land of God Refinement to shake even more.
At this moment, Saint Jiu, who was talking to Hua Tianming, stiffened and then revealed a bitter smile.
"The blacksmith is already impatient," Saint Jiu said helplessly. He had no choice but to end the conversation for now.
Hua Tianming felt the ground shaking and felt a little confused. What kind of existence was that blacksmith to be able to make the entire Forbidden Land of God Refinement shake?
The Forbidden Land of God Refinement might not be as big as a universe, but such a big commotion was clearly not something an ordinary person could do …
Hua Tianming nodded, "I will continue to stay here and wait for Saint Jiu."
"No need, you come with me," Saint Jiu said and reached out to grab Hua Tianming.
Without any warning, Hua Tianming felt the world spin and the scenery around him changed.
He suddenly appeared in a cave.
"You'll know once you knock!" Saint Jiu said unhappily.
The one-eyed man in the cave chuckled and hugged the golden hammer in his hand. He licked the tip of his nose and laughed, "My hammer is already unable to endure the thirst! That kid has already come in! "
"Do I need you to remind me?" Saint Jiu rolled his eyes. He could clearly sense any movement in his inner world. Even a group of ants moving house or a withered leaf falling, he could instantly sense it.
"Then can I make my move? Kill all the trouble around us? "asked the one-eyed man.
In the eyes of this one-eyed man, apart from Luo Zheng who was his treasure, everyone around Luo Zheng was not worth mentioning. He could wipe them all out with a casual move...
"Among them are Han Jia's children. Do you want to make a move?" Saint Jiu sneered.
The one-eyed man snorted. "If it's a side branch of the Han Family, then it doesn't matter."
The Forbidden Land of God Refinement took care of the martial artists of the Divine Realm's large clans to a certain degree. At the very least, Saint Jiu wouldn't allow the children of these important figures to die so casually. He didn't want to provoke any trouble.
"Then you can try. In any case, I'll pretend I didn't see it." Saint Jiu shrugged.
The one-eyed man also sensed the meaning behind Saint Jiu's words. It seemed that Luo Zheng had an important direct descendant of the Han Family by his side. He immediately picked up his golden hammer and walked out of the cave. "I understand. No one will die …"
The one-eyed man took a single step and suddenly appeared at the entrance of the cave. Hua Tianming carefully observed him but didn't sense any Space Laws. This person wasn't Saint Jiu, so Hua Tianming naturally couldn't see how he did it.
"Is this the blacksmith that Lord Saint Jiu spoke of?" Hua Tianming asked.
Saint Jiu nodded. "I paid a considerable price to invite this fellow here!"
Even a Saint would find it difficult to invite this one-eyed man. This fellow was extremely picky. Most of the time, it wasn't just a matter of price. With his strength, there were far too few things in the Divine Realm that could catch his eye. Most of the time, he acted out of pure interest. In addition to his eccentric personality, it was extremely difficult to invite him.
However, Saint Jiu had successfully piqued this fellow's interest …
Whether it was in the divine realm or the universe, there were all kinds of strange divine weapons, magical treasures, and evil weapons. All kinds of unimaginable refining methods were common. Magical treasures could speak, help their masters take revenge, and even take human form to marry their masters. There were even such strange things as magical treasures taking human form and marrying their masters!
However, Saint Jiu was certain that refining a human body into a magic treasure was unprecedented. This was also one of the reasons why the one-eyed man was willing to help.
The one-eyed man carried his golden hammer and walked up the cave. His target was naturally Luo Zheng.
At this moment, the ice butterfly diagram in Gong Yu's hand was almost completely used up.
"Quick, let's charge over!"
The number of butterflies that condensed became fewer and fewer. Everyone felt their surroundings become hotter and hotter!
The lava on both sides of the path continued to transform into countless creatures that pounced at the group.
"Illusory Frost Chant!"
"Snow Seal …"
"Mysterious Ice Sky!"
After feeling the pressure from Gong Yu, the various Sector Lords of the Han Family also made their move.
At this moment, the entire cave shook. On the narrow path, a few fighters slid down the lava on both sides.
Luckily, these fighters reacted extremely quickly. They instantly flew into the air and avoided the tragedy of falling into the lava.
"Earthquake?" Everyone's eyes revealed surprise.
This earthquake came without any warning signs …
They were in the cave and naturally didn't know that this tremor had affected the entire Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
After shaking three times, the entire cave stabilized.
Not long after, everyone saw a person at the bottom of the cave.
It was a one-eyed man. He carried a golden hammer and walked towards them. His footsteps were strange and his face was expressionless. The key was that the path below took a turn. This one-eyed man didn't follow the path. Instead, he walked in a straight line and slowly walked over the lava.
The green flames in the lava didn't cause any damage to him!
"Who … are you?"
Gong Yu and Thunder Toad took the lead and blocked in front.
They had already walked more than half the way. As long as they sent Luo Zheng into the cave, they would be able to take the door. This one-eyed man who suddenly appeared didn't seem to have good intentions.
This one-eyed man didn't emit any aura at all. They couldn't estimate his cultivation level at all!
Facing such an unfathomable person, they were naturally nervous.
The one-eyed man didn't even bother to reply to Gong Yu. He walked towards them. In his eyes, there was only Luo Zheng …
If it wasn't for Jiu Sheng being unwilling, he would have killed all of them.
To an existence like the one-eyed man, these living beings were no different from a ball of true essence. They had no value. However, he promised Jiu Sheng that he wouldn't kill them.
"Stop." Thunder Toad's face sank. He directly blocked the one-eyed man. His tall and sturdy body was like a door in front of him. His muscles were somewhat stiff.
The Mountain Shattering Tribe was proud of their strength. Thunder Toad was also the leader. However, in front of this one-eyed man, Thunder Toad didn't have much confidence.
Therefore, Thunder Toad didn't attack. He only wanted to use his body to block in front of this person!
This operation was too important to Thunder Toad and the others. He wouldn't allow anyone to disrupt their plan.
The one-eyed man didn't react to Thunder Toad's warning. He continued to walk towards Luo Zheng.
The two of them didn't give way to each other. In the end, they still collided!
The one-eyed man maintained his pace from beginning to end. When he collided with Thunder Toad, it was as if he had collided with a curtain. The one-eyed man's speed didn't slow down or change at all.
On the other hand, Lei Toad's face was ghastly pale. He let out a muffled groan and his burly body fell to the ground. He almost fell into the magma. Lying on the ground, he couldn't even get up! His heart, however, was surging with waves!
In the collision just now, Thunder Toad had secretly accumulated more than ninety percent of the strength in his body! Under such a collision, Thunder Toad felt as if he was a piece of paper. He couldn't obstruct the one-eyed man in the slightest!
This meant that their strength wasn't on the same level. Thunder Toad was just an ant standing in front of an elephant. He didn't have the qualifications to block the road at all!
Gong Yu and Jin Yue's expressions were quite ugly. Gong Yu couldn't help but attack. However, when he saw Thunder Toad suffer, he realized that the one-eyed man's strength was beyond imagination.
If all of them rushed forward, they wouldn't even be able to resist one finger of this person!
Gong Yu resisted the urge to attack and only asked in a low voice, "Senior, may I ask what you want?"

Chapter 1551
This person's casual grab seemed to have completely sealed off all of Luo Zheng's escape routes. There was simply no room to dodge …
The nine stars in Luo Zheng's inner world were constantly transforming Primal Chaos Qi. However, the amount of Primal Chaos Qi in his inner world was limited. If he absorbed too much Primal Chaos Qi at once, his inner world would face the problem of drying up.
Although Luo Zheng guessed that this person in front of him might have been arranged by Saint Jiu, he knew nothing about this arrangement.
When this person grabbed him, Luo Zheng's body suddenly twisted, wanting to break free from this person's grasp!
However, this person grabbed Luo Zheng's shoulder and twisted his hand, dissolving Luo Zheng's movement technique. Then, he exerted some force and directly lifted Luo Zheng onto his shoulder.
Whether it was in terms of strength or control of strength, Luo Zheng was far inferior to this person.
This person carried Luo Zheng on one shoulder and the golden hammer on the other. He strode forward, minding his own business. As for the group of martial artists behind him, they were completely ignored by this one-eyed man.
"Let go of me!" Luo Zheng coldly said.
Although his hands were being held by this person, this didn't mean that Luo Zheng had no way to resist.
"I advise you to be obedient. Resistance is meaningless," the one-eyed man coldly said.
Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed and the Primal Chaos Qi in his body surged out. Then, a circle of light quietly appeared under the one-eyed man's feet. He was about to use the Thunder Tribulation Annihilation. However, the heavenly tribulation above their heads brewed for a long time, but the heavenly lightning didn't fall …
The one-eyed man looked up and coldly smiled, "There is no heavenly tribulation in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. The Thunder Tribulation Annihilation naturally can't be summoned. I already told you not to struggle in vain."
Hearing this, Luo Zheng slightly frowned. This Forbidden Land of God Refinement wasn't a complete universe. Some cultivation methods couldn't be used. For example, the Stellar Battle Body couldn't be used.
However, as soon as this one-eyed man's voice fell, a stream of Primal Chaos Qi surged out and transformed into beautiful ice flowers.
It was the Ice Soul Devil Flame that Luo Zheng had just comprehended!
Extremely beautiful ice flowers fluttered in the air and drifted towards the one-eyed man, immediately releasing an intense chill.
The one-eyed man was originally moving forward at an extremely fast speed, but the instant the Icy Soul Devil Flame bloomed, it was as if he had fallen into a swamp, and his speed rapidly decreased …
"Ice Soul Devil Flame?" The one-eyed man's face revealed a look of surprise. He never thought that Luo Zheng would be able to learn Sage Jiu's spirit power.
The might of the Icy Soul Devil Flame, which was powered by Primal Chaos Qi, was naturally no small matter. The one-eyed man was careless for a moment and allowed the cold flame poison within the Icy Soul Devil Flame to invade his body. At this moment, it was extremely difficult for him to move.
But in the end, Luo Zheng's cultivation was far too low. If this method were used on a martial artist of the same level, or even a Divine Transformation realm martial artist or World Lord, the effects would naturally be different. But to this one-eyed man, it wasn't even worth mentioning.
His body flashed with a halo of light and waves of water vapor were forcefully squeezed out from his body. His originally sluggish body was instantly able to move freely.
At this moment, Luo Zheng used the Icy Soul Devil Flame again. The cold flame poison within was no longer able to drill into the one-eyed man's body.
However, the one-eyed man didn't seem to want Luo Zheng to cause any more trouble. He casually condensed a faint halo of light and slapped it towards Luo Zheng's abdomen. After his hand left Luo Zheng's abdomen, he left behind a spider formed from light in his dantian.
This spider was vivid and lifelike. It lay on Luo Zheng's abdomen, its eight legs easily piercing through Luo Zheng's abdomen and wrapping around Luo Zheng's dantian. He instantly felt as if he had lost contact with his inner world!
"It's just a Truth Sealing Art. However, your inner world should be similar to a Sage's universe. It has the ability to transcend worlds. Thus, I've temporarily modified this Truth Sealing Art. I advise you not to carelessly use it. If you really do cut off your connection with your inner world, then I'll have sinned greatly." The one-eyed man looked ahead.
This time, Luo Zheng was truly at his wit's end.
Not only was this person unimaginably powerful, but he also understood him so well. He even knew that his inner world was different from ordinary people's.
However, Luo Zheng was able to relax for now. Although this fellow's appearance was unfriendly, he had not killed a single person. Moreover, this fellow knew Luo Zheng very well, so he should be someone that Saint Jiu had deliberately arranged. However, why did this person kidnap him?
"Follow him!"
Gong Yu and the others weren't willing to give up. They also knew that they were far from being this person's match. They could only follow from a distance. None of them were willing to rush to their deaths.
The two Han Family sisters led the rest of the Han Family to follow behind. Han Liusu stared and said, "Sister, why do I feel that this person is somewhat familiar?"
Han Chuyue shook her head. "I don't recognize this person …"
"What about He Zuowei?" Han Liusu asked again.
He Zuowei and the other Sector Lords shook their heads at the same time. These Sector Lords didn't have a high status in the Han Clan. In fact, if Han Liusu was cultivating in the Divine Region, her guards would basically be True Gods. They wouldn't even have the chance to see the Realm Lords. However, True Gods couldn't descend in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Han Clan could only send Sector Lords to act as their guards. With the status of these Sector Lords, it wasn't easy for them to even catch a glimpse of the Saint Emperors.
"Reporting to Eldest Princess, this subordinate doesn't know." He Zuowei shook his head.
Han Liusu frowned and thought for a moment, then lightly said, "Six years ago, after the Sage Hall's meeting, father invited the Sages to a banquet to pick the shoots of the Fusang Divine Tree. I seem to have seen this person at that banquet …"
"What!" He Zuowei and the others' expressions suddenly changed.
To be able to be personally entertained by the Sage Emperor, he had to be a Sage. At the very least, he had to be an upper True God!
No wonder this person was so strong …
"If I remember correctly, this person seems to be that Craftsman Saint." Han Liusu continued.
"Ah!"
Many Sector Lords' expressions became even more shocked!
"Impossible! That Craftsman Saint is incomparably reclusive. How could he personally appear in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement? "
"Back then, even the Lord Sage Emperor couldn't invite this person …"
"But rumor has it that the Craftsman Saint refined one of his eyes into a chaos treasure called the Devil Star's Eye. That Craftsman Saint was indeed a one-eyed dragon!"
This group of Sector Lords might not have the qualifications to meet those top figures, but it didn't mean that they had never heard of these figures. In fact, the legends of every Sage were widely spread in the Divine Realm. Some cultivators could be said to be familiar with them.
Especially the legends of this Craftsman Saint, there were countless of them.
It was said that this Craftsman Saint was only a very ordinary blacksmith back then. Although he had a little reputation in the Divine Realm, his cultivation base was only in the lower True God realm. He could be considered a borderline True God.
At that time, blacksmiths were very particular about being orthodox. Back then, the Craftsman Saint's temperament and character were very eccentric. He was not tolerated by the orthodox and was expelled from his own blacksmith sect.
On the path of refining grandmist treasures, the Craftsman Saint didn't have great achievements.
Fortunately, blacksmiths were a highly sought after profession anywhere. Some famous blacksmiths were much wealthier than cultivators of the same realm. With their own skills, they could live comfortably in a small corner.
However, the Craftsman Saint had always been brooding about being expelled from his sect. Perhaps he didn't have any great breakthroughs on the path of refining grandmist treasures, but he set his sights on chaos energy!
He knew that chaos energy could be obtained from the borderline of the Divine Realm. However, chaos energy was extremely toxic to cultivators. Their dantian couldn't contain it. What if they sealed chaos energy into a weapon?
In fact, many blacksmiths had the idea of refining a chaos treasure, but no one had put it into action.
After the Craftsman Saint came up with his hypothesis, he walked on this path without hesitation.
As a lower True God, it wasn't easy to obtain chaos energy. In fact, in the entire Divine Realm, only Saints dared to wander in the chaos. However, this didn't mean that chaos energy didn't exist in the Divine Realm.
The roots of the East Pole Fusang, the West Pole Ruo Wood, the North Star Building Wood, and the Southern Dipper Seeking Wood were buried in the borderline of the Divine Realm. The roots of these four divine trees extended for millions of miles to the periphery of the Divine Realm. They went deep into the chaos and continuously absorbed chaos energy.
The Craftsman Saint had visited the four divine trees before. In the end, he paid a considerable price and exhausted almost all of his wealth to obtain chaos energy from the Southern Dipper Seeking Wood!
Then, according to his own ideas, he refined this chaos energy into a weapon.
However, the Craftsman Saint didn't succeed on his first try.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1552
When some of the blacksmiths in the divine territory heard of this news, they all mocked him for being childish.
Using the Primordial Astral Nova as the foundation of the blacksmithing method had been passed down for countless years. It was also the orthodox way of the blacksmiths in the divine territory!
This person was indulging in wild fantasies. He wanted to open up a new path and use the primordial chaos to refine weapons. He simply did not know the immensity of heaven and earth.
The first failure exhausted all of the Craftsman God's wealth.
At that time, the Craftsman God had been silent for a long time. The social structure in the divine territory was quite complete. All blacksmiths had to be registered in the blacksmithing guild and become a blacksmith of the guild. They would be issued an exclusive nameplate before they were qualified to sell and auction the weapons they refined.
The Craftsman God had been expelled by the Blacksmithing Guild. The weapons and treasures that he refined in private could not be sold legitimately. They could only be traded in some black market auctions. The prices were also quite low …
According to the Craftsman God's situation at that time, it would be extremely difficult for him to accumulate a considerable amount of wealth in exchange for the primordial chaos aura.
However, the Craftsman God did not give up.
In his opinion, since he had an endless lifespan, time was the cheapest thing. He would accumulate slowly and there would always be a second chance.
However, the Craftsman God did not expect that his second chance would come so quickly. This was because he met "Gu Bei", also known as "North Sage" in the divine territory.
At that time, Gu Bei and Luo Xiao had already begun to explore the primordial chaos.
He had unintentionally heard about this "young" refiner and became interested in this refiner.
In the end, they found the Craftsman God and expressed that they could provide him with the primordial chaos aura …
The Craftsman God was extremely touched at that time!
Although the Craftsman God was determined to refine a primordial chaos treasure, with the speed at which he accumulated wealth, it would be many years before he could get a second chance. Moreover, who could guarantee that he would succeed the second time?
Of course, he could try a third time, a fourth time …
However, the time it would take to succeed would be extended infinitely.
Reality proved that this kind of success was not so easy.
Before refining the first primordial chaos treasure, the Craftsman God failed 76 times!
This could be considered as "North Sage" who had done some research on the primordial chaos and concluded that it was enough to refine primordial chaos treasures. Strictly speaking, the primordial chaos energy was at the level of vital essence. However, the primordial chaos energy exceeded this level of energy. It was the origin of all things. There was no reason that it could not be refined successfully.
On the 77th time, the Craftsman God finally refined his first primordial chaos treasure!
When this Chaos Supreme Treasure was revealed, the entire Divine Dominion's Weapon Forging World was shocked. At first, the Weapon Forging Guild refused to acknowledge this treasure and tried their best to erase this fact. In the end, it was only after North Sage intervened that the Divine Dominion was forced to acknowledge this treasure.
Relying on this method, the Craftsman God had created a sect that was comparable to a grandmist artifact artifact!
After that, the Craftsman God went out of control and explored a new path in refining Chaos Supreme Treasures. He then established his own sect and vaguely showed signs of being on par with the Craftsmen's Union.
The Craftsmen's Union was also quite helpless about this. This huge union formed by craftsmen had existed for countless years, but chaos treasures had become a new trend. Whether it was Upper True Gods, Venerables, or True Saints, all of them scrambled madly for chaos treasures. As for grandmist treasures, their value fell again and again. This was something that had never happened before.
............ and..........
Later on, he did something rather crazy, which shocked the entire Divine Realm once again. Even many Saints were shocked.
At that time, the Craftsman God took one of his eyeballs and refined it into the Devil Star Eye!
Although the eyeball was a primordial chaos treasure, it was not an ordinary treasure used to defend against enemies. It was a huge world, a world similar to the universe!
After the Devil Star Eye was refined, it was placed at the edge of the divine territory like the other universes!
The Craftsman Saint had never stepped into the Saint Realm, and he himself had not cultivated to the Saint Realm. Even after many years, he was only at the Middle True God Realm. However, with the Devil Star Eye, he was qualified to be ranked among the Saints!
It was the first time in the history of the divine territory that someone had been conferred a Saint in such a unique way.
Only at this time did the words "Craftsman Saint" appear in the Divine Domain!
He was the first and perhaps the last person to be conferred a Saint as a refiner.
Although Saint Jiu had paid a great price to invite the Craftsman God, the most important thing was that the Craftsman God still cherished their old friendship.
The Craftsman God might not be able to help Luo Zheng fight against the Saints, but he could help Luo Zheng to the greatest extent. Moreover, he was very curious about Luo Zheng's body. He had refined many primordial chaos treasures, but he had never seen a warrior who could take the primordial chaos for his own use.
"If it really is the Craftsman God, why did he take Luo Zheng away?" He Zuowei asked.
Han Liusu blinked. "He can't be trying to refine him, right?" She knew that Luo Zheng's body was special, so she came to this conclusion. But it was only a guess.
"It's possible." He Zuowei nodded in agreement. "I'm afraid Luo Zheng is doomed."
Although the legend of the Craftsman God was widespread, everyone knew that this person was quite eccentric. It was not surprising that he would do something like this.
However, they thought that the Craftsman God would refine Luo Zheng, but in reality, the Craftsman God wanted to refine Luo Zheng. The difference of one word was like the difference between heaven and earth.
Ever since Luo Zheng started cultivating the Exalted Tool Refining Method, he had always been very passive in refining his body. The Primordial Astral Nova had only refined his body a few times with the help of the crimson dragon. However, the crimson dragon was not a tool refiner, and Qinglong was not proficient in this field.
This time, the Craftsman Saint was going to personally refine Luo Zheng's weapon.
The Craftsman God already had a rough idea, but this was his first time refining a living person. Moreover, Luo Zheng not only had a special body, but also cultivated a special cultivation method. Even the Craftsman God himself could not be sure what the final result would be.
Han Liusu was unwilling to give up. She lightly tapped the ground with the tip of her foot and dashed out of the crowd. She shouted, "Uncle Craftsman God! I am Han Liusu from the Han Family! "
When she called out the Craftsman God's identity, the Realm of the Gods cultivators were dumbfounded.
Who would have thought that the one-eyed man in front of them was the Craftsman God who had created the Chaos Treasure?
The one-eyed man turned his head and glanced at Han Liusu. He had already determined that this woman was the eldest daughter of the Hidden Sage, the Eldest Princess who possessed the Blood of Impure Yin.
Hearing this shout, the Craftsman God's face was wooden. He did not respond at all. His footsteps were extremely fast, as if he was teleporting. He shuttled downwards …
Han Liusu could not help but pout. Seeing the Craftsman God carrying Luo Zheng, she could only continue chasing.
The cave was not very deep. After a short while, the Craftsman God arrived at the bottom of the cave. There was another curved cave entrance there. Green tongues of fire were wildly spewing out from this cave entrance.
The Craftsman God did not hesitate at all. He carried Luo Zheng and rushed into the cave.
This cave was the one that Lei Toad and Gong Yu had discovered.
When Lei Toad and Gong Yu saw this scene, their feelings were extremely complicated. They had hoped that Luo Zheng would enter and bring out the door.
Now, Luo Zheng had indeed entered. However, he did not enter by himself. Instead, he was dragged in by the so-called Craftsman God. The situation had suddenly become complicated!
"Dong!"
Lei Toad punched the wall, but could only stand there anxiously. They had brought Luo Zheng from thousands of miles away to their destination. However, this inexplicable situation had occurred. This caused the three clan leaders to feel extremely disheartened.
The cave was filled with green flames. The one-eyed man carried Luo Zheng inside and casually threw him to the ground.
The moment Luo Zheng fell to the ground, he rolled and stood up. He frowned at the one-eyed man.
The green Strange Fire was constantly burning Luo Zheng. His body was already a dazzling gold. He began to frantically absorb the green flames.
"What the hell are you doing?" Luo Zheng asked coldly.
The one-eyed man did not answer Luo Zheng's question. He only moved his body. Suddenly, he waved the golden hammer in his hand and fiercely smashed it towards Luo Zheng's chest!
The speed was so fast that it exceeded the limit of Luo Zheng's reaction. Before he could see it clearly, the golden hammer had already smashed into his chest.
Accompanied by a muffled sound, an extremely strong force spread out from the golden hammer and transmitted into Luo Zheng's body!
Although the force was strong, it was very uniform. A faint halo spread out from the hammer and dispersed the force of the golden hammer throughout Luo Zheng's body.

Chapter 1553
If the hammer's power hadn't been evenly distributed throughout Luo Zheng's body, but had struck Luo Zheng in the form of a single point, he might have been directly smashed into two pieces.
But this power clearly wasn't meant to take Luo Zheng's life …
At this moment, the Craftsman Saint's eyes had become incomparably focused, and there was even a trace of madness in them.
In his eyes, Luo Zheng wasn't a living person, but a weapon waiting to be tempered!
After the Craftsman Saint became a Saint, his refining techniques had reached the pinnacle.
Naturally, there were high and low refining techniques for refiners, and this depended on the degree of control of power.
No matter how hard an ore or material was, it couldn't withstand the tempering of a strange fire. For example, the Golden Fork Strange Stone was a type of divine artifact ore that couldn't be touched by oil or salt. But after being burned by a special water flame, its characteristics would change, becoming loose and brittle. If the power was slightly greater, the entire Golden Fork Strange Stone would be scrapped.
Therefore, refining techniques were extremely important here.
And these techniques were divided into Small Perfection and Great Perfection in the Realm of the Gods. Small Perfection spread the power evenly throughout the area, and it was relatively easier to grasp. Many of the mature refiners in the Realm of the Gods had grasped this technique.
But in the Realm of the Gods, there were very few who could use the Great Perfection tempering technique.
This Great Perfection technique didn't only require a fine control of power, it also required a great amount of power.
Previously, the Craftsman Saint had smashed the ground three times with his hammer, almost shaking the entire Forbidden Land of God Refinement!
If it was another person, they might have created a several thousand mile wide crater, but there wasn't even a small crater under the Craftsman Saint's feet!
This was because the power of this hammer was evenly distributed throughout the entire Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
Now, the Craftsman Saint was using the Great Perfection tempering technique. Although the power of this hammer was extremely strong, when it was evenly distributed, Luo Zheng's body could easily withstand it.
At this moment, Luo Zheng felt as if a pot of lava had been poured into his body, and it was scalding hot!
This scalding hot feeling didn't come from the green strange fire, but from the Primordial Astral Nova.
Arms, arms, stomach, heart, head …
Every pore, every blood vessel, and even every crack in his bones had a vast amount of Primordial Astral Nova gushing out.
His body was like a dam that had been opened. The Primordial Astral Force was rushing down at an unprecedented speed and gathering in his body!
If he could produce a small amount of Primordial Chaos Celestial Force, it could reduce Luo Zheng's pain. Therefore, he didn't feel any pain when he was beaten. Instead, he felt unusually comfortable.
However, if there were too many Primordial Dippers, it wouldn't be a good thing.
With such a massive amount of Primordial Astral Force gathering in Luo Zheng's body, Luo Zheng felt an intense burning sensation. Apart from that, there was also a wave of aching and numbing pain …
However, the Craftsman Saint was not satisfied with this little amount of Primordial Astral Force. With this strike, Luo Zheng was sent flying into the air. After a somersault in the air, his body involuntarily fell back down.
Before Luo Zheng could land, the Craftsman Saint had already swung his hammer a second time. This time, the hammer directly smashed onto Luo Zheng's head!
Boom!
The huge golden hammer directly smashed onto Luo Zheng's head.
With the Craftsman Saint's Perfection Stage technique, smashing his head was actually the same as smashing one of Luo Zheng's fingers. After all, he had spread his own strength evenly throughout Luo Zheng's body. Therefore, the location was not important. What was important was that he didn't let Luo Zheng down at all!
With this second strike, Luo Zheng was like a rocket that shot straight into the sky. He flew all the way up, crashed into the ceiling of the cave, and fell straight back down …
"Again!"
The third strike!
The strength of each strike was very evenly distributed, squeezing out the Primordial Astral Force in Luo Zheng's body!
By the fourth strike, the Primordial Astral Force that Luo Zheng's body could withstand was already in a state of bursting. If people looked at Luo Zheng now, they would see that his skin and flesh were bulging. His originally thin face looked as if it had been beaten swollen. His pair of round cheeks looked like a pig's head.
"So painful!"
The Primordial Astral Force was also a kind of strength. This kind of strength was extremely strong and tough. Now, a huge amount of Primordial Astral Force was squeezing Luo Zheng's body, and at the same time, not being pushed out by Luo Zheng, he was naturally in great pain.
Moreover, this kind of pain was even more unbearable than when he had been squeezed!
"The fifth strike!"
While the Craftsman Saint was hammering, he was also sizing up Luo Zheng's situation.
Although he saw Luo Zheng as a magical weapon, he also had to consider Luo Zheng's uniqueness. After all, he was a living person. He was completely different from a piece of lifeless Essence Iron.
A piece of Essence Iron was a whole inside, but Luo Zheng's body contained all kinds of organs, bones, and spaces …
If he was hammered like ordinary Essence Iron, the only result would be that Luo Zheng's body would explode and he would die.
This time, Sage Jiu had paid a great price to invite him, and this youth was the son of an old friend. Perhaps he didn't seem to care in front of Luo Zheng, but he still had to be somewhat cautious.
After five strikes, the Craftsman Saint stopped.
He reached out his hand and gently flipped it. A ray of light from the Law of Space flickered in his hand. When the light disappeared, there was a golden wine pot in his hand! He tilted the mouth of the wine pot toward Luo Zheng. From the mouth of the pot, bejeweled nectar immediately flowed down and poured over Luo Zheng's body!
"Slurp!"
It was unknown what was in the wine pot. Even though the place was burning, the bejeweled nectar wasn't affected at all. When the nectar touched Luo Zheng's body, it instantly rose into a white mist.
Luo Zheng was lying on the ground, already on the verge of death. He felt that he could explode at any time. There were too many Primordial Astral Stars. They were not under Luo Zheng's control at all and were running amok in his body. Some of them even rushed into his brain, causing him to be unable to think clearly for a while.
But at that moment, he suddenly felt a cool feeling!
It was like a thirsty man on a desert journey seeing an oasis in front of him. Suddenly, he rushed into the oasis and drank like a whale. His body began to desperately absorb the bejeweled nectar!
At the same time, the Primordial Astral Stars in his body also surged toward the bejeweled nectar. They were constantly melted by the liquid and completely integrated into his muscles and bones.
"What is this?"
After regaining his senses, Luo Zheng opened his eyes and saw that the one-eyed man was aiming a wine jug at him. At this moment, he even had an impulse to snatch the wine jug and drink it all in one gulp …
However, he also knew that in his current state, any action would be futile. At that moment, he could only obediently lie on the ground and hope that this torture wouldn't last too long.
Reality proved that the more one feared something, the more it would happen.
A godly weapon was forged through countless rounds of tempering. Luo Zheng had only been struck by the golden hammer five times ….
After the bejeweled nectar was absorbed by Luo Zheng, the golden hammer in the Craftsman Saint's hand made a circle and suddenly hit the ground next to Luo Zheng. A huge force bounced Luo Zheng off the ground again!
"Dong!"
This time, Luo Zheng also let out a muffled groan!
Before he could react, the Primordial Astral Stars in his body were like a flood, crazily flowing around Luo Zheng's limbs and bones!
"Dong, dong, dong, dong!"
In an instant, another five hammers were hit …
Luo Zheng, who was rapidly "losing weight", suddenly became "fat" at this moment.
The pain of this repeated torture was far beyond imagination. However, no matter how painful it was, it was not as painful as Luo Zheng's pain when he cultivated the Primal Chaos Secret Technique. After all, this pain only came from the physical body, but the pain of cultivating the Primal Chaos Secret Technique was all-encompassing …
"Huala …"
The Craftsman Saint took out the wine jug again and poured the bejeweled nectar on Luo Zheng's body, causing a large amount of white mist to rise.
As this tempering process was repeated, the number of times the Craftsman Saint hit Luo Zheng's body increased …
At first, it was five hammers in a row. After repeating it three times, it was six times in a row. After repeating it three times, it was seven times!
Time slowly passed as the tempering continued!
The crowd outside the cave had been waiting for a long time. On the other side of the cave, Hua Tianming and Saint Jiu stood to the side, silently watching Luo Zheng in the distance undergo this painful tempering.
"I see that Luo Zheng is in a lot of pain. How long will this torture last?" Hua Tianming asked.
Saint Jiu said indifferently, "If you want to do something that ordinary people can't, you have to bear pain that ordinary people can't."

Chapter 1555
As Saint Jiu said, this piece of godhood had an extremely special origin.

Even if a lower True Divinity's godhood was condensed, it would be impossible to destroy it no matter what method was used. Even if a Saint used their strength, they still wouldn't be able to destroy it …

In the past, the person who wielded the Good Fortune Axe and opened the Realm of the Gods.
Time would thoroughly bury some truths, but there would always be some clues left behind …

The Divine Realm that had just been split open was in an empty state, without any life being born. Then, where did the legend of the Good Fortune Axe come from?
Who was the person who wielded the Good Fortune Axe in the past?
In the center of the Divine Realm was the vast and boundless Sea of Time, and at the end of the Sea of Time was a flat and vast plain.

They called this godhood the origin of godhood. This was because they speculated that the godhoods of the 3000 Great Daos were created from this origin of godhood.
Moreover, this godhood could fuse together with all other godhoods. Whether it was a True Divinity or a Saint, it would increase their strength to a considerable degree.

It was originally a battle between two Saints, but many Saints in the Divine Realm were completely dragged into it …
The process of the battle was not described. This massive origin of godhood was eventually divided into countless pieces and divided among the Saints.

This was because people at that time believed that this godhood might contain the ultimate secrets of the 3000 Great Daos. Some people even asserted that the lost Great Daos were hidden in this godhood.
However, countless years had passed. The Saints who had snatched away the shards of godhood had not been able to find anything from it. In the end, they had all devoured this origin of godhood one after another …
After so many years, the secrets of the origin of godhood had never been uncovered. However, there were fewer and fewer of them left in the world, and they were increasingly rare.
Tens of thousands of years ago, the Craftsman Saint had used a top-grade chaos treasure to exchange for a piece of origin of godhood with a Saint of the Holy Hall.
The Craftsman Saint had not been able to unravel the secrets of this origin of godhood. However, not only could this origin of godhood be fused with godhood, but it could also be refined into weapons.
However, the Craftsman Saint had never found an opportunity to try it out. Origin of godhood was rare. Even with his wealth, he did not dare to squander it casually.
However, the Craftsman Saint was actually so generous this time. He had actually taken out the origin of godhood! It was no wonder that the Craftsman Saint had been so hesitant …
The Craftsman Saint had his own reasons.
In his opinion, it was indeed necessary to cultivate Luo Zheng at this stage.
Although the Craftsman Saint was quite well-regarded in the Divine Dominion and was a guest of honor in front of the Saints' doors wherever he went, the Craftsman Saint's relationship with those old fellows in the Weapon Refining Union was not as good as it seemed.
Although the Craftsman Saint had reconciled with the Weapon Refining Union, it was only a temporary compromise between the two. The root of the conflict had not been resolved.
The Weapon Refining Union had repeatedly expressed that the Craftsman Saint should hand over the cultivation method for chaos treasures. However, other than the two disciples he had taught it to, there was no fourth person in the entire Divine Dominion who had the method to refine chaos treasures!
When he had wanted to try it back then, these people had treated him as a joke. Now that he had finally achieved great things, how could the Craftsman Saint be willing to hand over the forging method for no good reason?
This conflict had always been buried here. Over the past hundred years, several Saints had vaguely mentioned this matter to him, hoping that the Craftsman Saint would make the method to refine chaos treasures public to benefit the entire Divine Dominion.
The Craftsman Saint was still no match for those who had stepped into the Saint Realm. Even if he had refined his eyes into a small universe and become a member of the Sacred Hall, he had not stepped into the Saint Realm. Chapter 1556

Chapter 1558
These strange birds circled around in the sky, not knowing what fatigue was.
Every one of these strange birds had a wingspan of several million miles. Every time they flapped their wings, they could create a world-destroying storm. Occasionally, they would let out strange screeches, like the wails of ghosts and howls of wolves.
These strange birds were called the Ancestor Birds.
The Ancestor Birds started flying when they were young and never stopped. They circled in the sky for millions of years before falling. When they fell, it was the end of their lifespan. That was why there was a saying that they only flew once in their lives.
In millions of years, countless creatures were born on the backs of these birds, forming a large world. When the Ancestor Birds fell, the countless creatures in the large world were also destroyed.
Below these unimaginably large birds, there was an even larger mountain!
Compared to this mountain, the Ancestor Birds were very small.
At the top of the mountain, a young girl was sitting on a rock looking up at the sky. She seemed to have noticed something and suddenly stood up.
"It's falling …"
She saw an Archaeopteryx descending.

An Ancestor Bird needed millions of years to fall from the sky. If it fell, it meant the destruction of a world.
Ever since she came to this mountain, the shadows of those strange birds had been hovering in the deep sky above her head. They didn't know fatigue and didn't stop … Although Qi Leng told her that these birds were very, very big, so big that she couldn't imagine it, but in her eyes, these birds were no different from ordinary eagles!

The shadow of the strange grey bird kept growing bigger and bigger until it filled the young girl's eyes. It was like Mount Tai was pressing down on her. Above her head, she could only see a patch of grey. This wasn't a bird, but a world that was pressing down on her.
From her angle, the young girl could even see some of the creatures on the Ancestor Bird's body.
Such a large bird, even if it fell, it would be a long process. The young girl's face didn't show any signs of panic.

It was a pitiful thing to fall along with the fall of its master.
The young man smiled. "But you forgot the problem of time. The life of an ant is very short. The average lifespan is only half a month. But for an ant, this half a month is a lifetime of birth, old age, sickness, and death."
"It's still not fair." The young girl stood up. She was very stubborn about her conclusion.
"So the warriors in the universe can break free from this limit through their own efforts. After they understand the Divine Dao, they can raise their level of life and become a life form of the same level." The young man said.
"The bridge … is too narrow." The young girl said.
How many life forms in the universe could surpass the limits of the Heavenly Dao and enter the divine realm?
Among the billions of life forms, there were only a few people …
In the end, the young man could only smile helplessly and admit the young girl's point of view. "This world is not fair. If there really are those 'life forms of a higher level', then in the eyes of those 'life forms of a higher level', we are also small and sad existences. How many people can leave the divine realm and explore the chaos?"
The young girl pursed her lips and nodded …
"Let's go. The sound of that big bird crashing down is still very loud." The young man grabbed the young girl and left.
Unexpectedly, the young girl shook her head and said, "Uncle Qi, let's move this strange bird. Let it fall down the mountain."
The height of this mountain was difficult to describe. If someone were to free-fall from the mountain, they would still be a young man on the mountain. By the time they fell down the mountain, they would most likely be a white-haired old man …
If this strange bird fell down the mountain, it could buy the countless life forms on the bird dozens of years to live.
Qi Leng shook his head helplessly. He could only comply with the young girl's request.
He suddenly waved his hand and an invisible force cut through the sky. He wanted to move a bird larger than the boundless world …
But to him, this was not difficult. It was just a matter of lifting his hand.
The big bird above their heads changed direction under the force of the force. It started to fall to the other side. This way, it could ensure that this Progenitor Bird would not fall on the mountain …
The young girl looked at the Progenitor Bird that was flying diagonally. Her face revealed a gratified smile. She had at least helped the life forms of a world to buy dozens of years. Although dozens of years later, this world would still fall.
Seeing the smile on the young girl's face, Qi Leng's face revealed a trace of hesitation.
The arrival of this little girl was like a fairy. She was deeply loved by this group of people.
He could feel her melancholy and worry, but she always carefully hid those emotions. She tried her best not to reveal them in front of everyone.
After thinking about it, Qi Leng still said, "The Great Extreme Universe has started to corrode the Great Evolution Universe."
Hearing this, the young girl's body slightly trembled. She stared at Qi Leng and asked, "Where's brother?"
"He's already entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement," Qi Leng replied.
The young girl wanted to say something, but in the end, she held back …
Actually, she wanted to ask why Qi Leng did not directly bring Luo Zheng to the mountain and directly pull Luo Zheng into the Realm of the Gods. But at this moment, she thought of her father's situation …
If the Great Extreme Universe completely corroded the Great Evolution Universe, then her father's situation would be dangerous. He would really fall.
Only her brother could save her father. So her brother could not leave the universe!
Unfortunately, from the beginning to the end, Luo Yan was like a spectator. She could not help at all. She could only watch as her brother continuously struggled. Then, Qi Leng brought him to the mountain and told her everything.
Little did she know, she would rather not know anything …
"Whoosh whoosh …"
The huge Progenitor Bird had already slowly swept past the mountain peak. When its huge body fell, it still pushed away the air, creating a strong wind that blew towards them …
Under this strong wind, there was an extremely strong storm. And within that storm, there were countless wind blades.
"It's a bit cold," Luo Yan said indifferently. Some wind blades flew towards Luo Yan.
These wind blades were light gold. The power of the wind element law had already reached the seventh level. Moreover, the law here was not the universe, but the Realm of the Gods. So, it was a perfect law. It was enough to threaten a martial artist at the Third Transformation Stage.
She stretched out her finger and casually fiddled with it. Specks of purple light flickered and those wind blades stopped at her fingertips. Then, they quickly reverted from wind blades to gentle wind. The wind element law within them constantly collapsed, turning into true essence and slowly dissipating in this place …
Qi Leng took out an emerald green cloak and placed it on her shoulder.
Luo Yan quickly walked forward. Her legs were straight as she walked towards the other side of the mountain peak. Her beautiful face was especially cold, but the melancholy in her eyes had completely disappeared. She faintly revealed a swift and decisive expression.

Chapter 1559
Purple Deviant Fire raged at the cave entrance, as if an evil dragon was hiding in the depths of the cave, constantly spitting out dragon's breath.

He suddenly stretched out his hands and dense true essence turned into a small vortex. The vortex was like a black hole that constantly sucked in the surrounding true essence. As the true essence condensed, the vortex bloomed with blazing white space laws!
The Eye Transformation Technique was not difficult for martial artists. For example, the Blue Cloud Sect of the Eastern Region had once created a "letter tablet", which was created by fusing an array with True Essence.
However, the Purple Heavenly Flame was so fierce that ordinary methods couldn't enter it at all. Only the Eye of Nothingness could barely enter!
After a short while, a strange eye condensed in Gong Yu's hand. The eye floated in the air, looking faintly discernible, but in reality, it was constantly shuttling back and forth in space to create such a scene!
"Crackle!"
A blazing white space law turned into a bolt of lightning and entered one of Gong Yu's eyes. The Eye of Nothingness was connected to Gong Yu's left eye. The scene he saw in his left eye was the scene transmitted back by the Eye of Nothingness.
"Go!"
With a thought, the Eye of Nothingness burrowed into the rock stratum to the side.
The Eye of Nothingness could only last eight to nine breaths of time in the Purple Heavenly Flame. Naturally, he wouldn't foolishly enter the cave through the opening. After passing through the rock stratum, what Gong Yu saw was endless purple flames!
This scene was exactly the same as what Gong Yu saw last time.
In just a breath's time, cracks began to appear on the surface of the Eye of Nothingness under the burning of the purple Deviant Fire.

With his previous experience, Gong Yu instantly saw the door in the depths of the cave!
At the same time, Gong Yu's other eye suddenly widened as if he had seen something unbelievable.

"The door, the door is open …" Gong Yu said loudly.

"Fire, flames …" Gong Yu was stunned.

The purple Heavenly Flame was constantly spewing out from the entrance of the cave. Naturally, the cave was filled with purple Heavenly Flame. It was just a bit of Heavenly Flame. What was there to be surprised about?

In the vision of Gong Yu's left eye, he saw bits of black flames. They were like demons swarming out …
These purple Heavenly Flames were already fierce enough. But in front of these black flames, they were like subjects in front of their monarch. All the purple Heavenly Flames seemed to have gained intelligence. They all avoided the door and the black flames that surged out from behind the door.

He saw Hua Tianming and Jiu Sheng.

Last time, when Gong Yu released the Eye of Nothingness, it lasted for nine breaths of time. This time, Gong Yu came prepared. It shouldn't be shorter than last time.
"I don't know. It's two strangers. One of them crushed my Eye of Nothingness." Gong Yu's face was also filled with anger.
Their journey into the inner world this time could be considered smooth sailing. Who would have thought that strange things would happen again and again when they reached their destination? Where did these strange fellows come from? Why were none of them afraid of the purple Heavenly Flames?
Inside the cave …
A small eyeball appeared in Jiu Sheng's hand. He sighed and said, "They thought that this door would allow them to transcend the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. However, they didn't know that this door was only a crafting furnace prepared for Luo Zheng. In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, they have no worries. Why do they have to transcend?"
Hua Tianming silently looked at the eye and said softly, "These people … Are they the people Jiu Sheng created?"
Jiu Sheng nodded, "Most of them are. After I created them, they never stopped."
Hua Tianming smiled faintly, "Jiu Sheng's actions are wrong."
"Why do you say so?" Jiu Sheng stared at Hua Tianming and asked.
"Although I have never been to the Divine Realm, regardless of whether it is the Divine Realm or the universe, under the constant evolution of living beings, their greatest motivation is to pursue a higher level. Even the current Jiu Sheng cannot avoid this, right?" Hua Tianming asked.
Jiu Sheng's gaze revealed a trace of surprise. Hua Tianming's words were irrefutable.
"This is the meaning of life," Hua Tianming said as he stared at the eyeball in Jiu Sheng's hand.
"This meaning is not great." With Jiu Sheng's wisdom, he clearly did not need Hua Tianming to teach him this simple and easy to understand principle. He glanced at Hua Tianming indifferently.
"The meaning of a beggar starving to death and obtaining a bun is perhaps greater than the meaning of a True God becoming a Saint." Hua Tianming did not care about Jiu Sheng's gaze at all. A confident and calm smile appeared on his face.
Hearing this, Jiu Sheng's fingers, which were holding the Eye of Nothingness, suddenly stiffened. He was somewhat speechless.
Entering the Saint Realm and establishing the universe meant that there was nothing one did not know about the vast world. However, he could not refute Hua Tianming's words.

Chapter 1561
"The 3,000 Great Daos can all condense a divine spark. It's just that the Forbidden Land of God Refinement does not have many Heavenly Dao Laws, so it's difficult for you to become a True Divinity. In truth, with your strength, condensing a divine spark is relatively easy for others …"
For example, Lei Toad, Gong Yu, and some other elites of their clans had strength that far surpassed a World Lord and were comparable to an Empyrean. But in terms of cultivation, they did not have the Mandate of Heaven to bear, nor did they condense a divine spark. In truth, their strength was still at the level of a World Lord.
However, they were World Lords that could display a certain ability to the extreme.
They were much stronger than those peak World Lords and did not lose to Empyreans at all.
If they cultivated a Divine Dao that suited them, it would be much easier for them to break through …

The eyes of the natives of the Forbidden Land of Divine Refinement were shining brightly at this moment. They were completely focused on Saint Jiu, afraid that they would miss a single detail or mishear a single word. This was probably their only chance, and also their last chance.

With that, he pointed his finger slightly, and that little bit of energy turned into little bits of colorful light that surged into the minds of all the members of the Mountain Crushing Clan present …
In their minds, a colorful palace of power appeared!
In the Realm of the Gods, the vast majority of body transformation martial artists mainly cultivated the strength path. Moreover, most of them focused on forging the eighth level of the Dao Foundation and condensing the ninth star of the Divine Pedestal. But, this didn't mean that one had to walk this path in body transformation. The method that Saint Jiu had passed on was to forge the strength path. When combined with the Mountain Collapsing Clan's strength source, the chances of one becoming a True Divinity were not small!
The members of the Collapsing Mountain Clan instantly bowed and expressed their gratitude, their expressions extremely sincere.
Gong Yu and Jin Yue looked at Sage Jiu anxiously …
This was especially true for the people of the Yuan He Clan. Although their hearts were stable, they were unable to remain calm when faced with such a situation!
This time, Sage Jiu was a good person and gave them advice one by one …
Actually, compared to other universes that were sealed by the Heavenly Dao, the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was an open world.
Just like the shackles of the Great and Great Expanse Lotus Flower. Only those that had opened the Great and Great Expanse Lotus Flower had the qualifications to comprehend the Divine Dao and become a Dao Child. To a martial artist, this was a kind of shackle!
In the past, Luo Zheng had not opened the Great Expanse Lotus Flower and was unable to break free from the shackles of the Heavenly Dao, thus he was unable to become a true Dao Child.
As for the natives of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, they did not have the shackles of the Heavenly Dao. Thus, in theory, they were all Dao Children and had the qualifications to cultivate the Divine Dao. But, without this shackle, they had lost the ability to comprehend the Divine Dao!
Only under Sage Jiu's personal guidance would they be able to catch a glimpse of the supernatural powers of the Divine Dao. From this point, it could be seen that the shackles of the Heavenly Dao were also a kind of motivation for martial artists. Once they broke free from these shackles, they would naturally be able to comprehend the Divine Dao.
Facing the grace of their Lord, every native of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was filled with gratitude.
"Thank you, Lord. If we can really cultivate to become True Gods, we will do our best until we die!" Gong Yu expressed his gratitude excitedly.
Saint Jiu, on the other hand, remained calm. Every rock and every living being in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was created by him, so this gratitude did not mean much to him. "I'm doing this purely for the sake of the young man beside me. Soon, he will also be heading to your Holy Land to cultivate. If you want to thank someone, thank him."
As his voice fell, whether it was Thunder Toad, Gong Yu, or Jin Yue, all of them were filled with deep veneration.
Thunder Toad immediately said in a clear voice, "My Mountain Crushing Clan is always open to you!"
"You seem to focus on cultivation. The Sky Dome Clan will definitely be a Sacred Land that you must enter. As the leader of the Sky Dome Clan, I will respectfully await your arrival in the clan …"
"…"
Their words came from the bottom of their hearts and were not fake in the slightest.

At the very least, he had not completely comprehended the nomological laws in Extinguishing Wind Valley. Heading to the Sky Dome Clan could only be the next step.
The martial artists of the three clans were satisfied. At least, the things they obtained this time far exceeded their expectations.
"You may leave." Sage Jiu immediately ordered them to leave.
Thunder Toad, Gong Yu, and the others did not linger at all. They immediately led their clansmen and quickly left, leaving behind a group of martial artists from other clans and the Realm of the Gods.
These people's six senses had been sealed by Sage Jiu. They stood in place, unable to move at all …
"How should we deal with these people?" Hua Tianming asked.
"Don't worry about them." Sage Jiu glanced indifferently at the Realm of the Gods martial artists. Then, he entered the cave with his hands behind his back.

… …
Behind the door was a world of nothingness.
The Divine Flame Cauldron had already been separated from Luo Zheng's body …
The nine True Dragons' soul essences constantly circled around the Divine Flame Cauldron. Luo Zheng seemed to have lost consciousness. His hands hung down and he floated motionlessly in the void.
Within the massive Divine Flame Cauldron, black flames slowly rose. These black flames seemed capable of swallowing everything.
Even the Craftsman Saint could not casually expose his body to the black flames. There was a bright yellow light on his body that completely protected him.
"Nine True Dragon Soul Essences … you were all contracted beasts of Luo Xiao in the past," the Craftsman Saint said indifferently as he gazed at the nine True Dragon Soul Essences. He had a total of forty-nine True Dragons … Do you want to restore your bodies, or do you want to offer your soul essences to Luo Zheng?"
Luo Xiao liked to raise contracted beasts, especially dragons. When he created the universe, he also created a separate True Dragon World on one side of the Great Evolution Universe. This True Dragon World could be considered Luo Xiao's Spirit Beast Field!
In the Craftsman Saint's opinion, the nine True Dragon Soul Essences were decent materials. If he refined them and gave them to Luo Zheng, they would be of help.
"We … refuse," the five-clawed golden dragon suddenly said.
The Craftsman Saint was interested in the nine True Dragons. "Luo Xiao is your master. Why don't you want to offer your souls to him?"
"There's a problem in our True Dragon World. They've instigated a rebellion among our clansmen. We can't offer ourselves up just yet …" the five-clawed golden dragon replied.
Dismembering a universe created by a Saint wasn't an easy task.
This was because the universe itself could sense danger and plan a counterattack. Even if the Sacred Race had the upper hand, it wouldn't be easy to conquer the Great Evolution Universe. This was because the Great Evolution Universe's consciousness would create a very unfavorable environment for the invaders …
It was like an army invading a country. Everything in the country, including the land, vegetation, and rivers, would launch a silent counterattack.
Therefore, the Sacred Race had prepared a foolproof plan for this invasion.
Aside from the Ancient Shamans, the Yao Night Clan and the Demon Race were also problematic. Therefore, the Demi-Immortals didn't really believe in those two races. As for the True Dragon World, they'd already begun to act. They'd already thought of a way to instigate forty True Dragons to defect, and nine of them had lost their physical bodies!
It wasn't that the True Dragons didn't want to help Luo Zheng, but they still had a great grudge to settle in the True Dragon World.

Chapter 1563
The black Godly flames were like little snakes. They slithered into Luo Zheng's body bit by bit, before being spat out by Luo Zheng.
It was said that chaos first transformed into Yin and Yang, and then Yin and Yang transformed into all things!
This Great Yin Yang Transference Fire Formation was derived from the Great Yin Yang Art. The Great Yin Yang Art itself was incredibly powerful, and using it to refine treasures was much easier …
However, many True Gods in the Realm of the Gods used the Great Yin Yang Art in a straight line.
This was because True Essence and chaos had very similar characteristics. If chaos was described as "equal energy," then True Essence was actually a type of "secondary energy."
With the Great Yin Yang Art in a straight line, True Essence replaced chaos and transformed into the Eight Trigrams. When the Eight Trigrams came crashing down, it could destroy the heavens and earth. It was extremely domineering.
However, the Craftsman Saint's Great Yin Yang Transference Fire Formation was in a straight line …
This was actually one of the keys to refining Absolute Chaos Treasures.
In a straight line, True Essence would transform chaos into Yin and Yang, not True Essence …
If the Craftsman Saint were to refine a Chaos Supreme Treasure himself, he would naturally need to prepare his own Chaos Qi. However, there was no need for him to 'refine' Luo Zheng this time. After all, Luo Zheng's dantian had an endless supply of Chaos Qi …
While the Great Yin Yang Transference Fire Formation was operating, the world within Luo Zheng's body was constantly evolving!
The concept of time became extremely subtle for Luo Zheng.
After he fell into a deep slumber, his body grew older by the day.
The lifespan of a Divine Sea Realm martial artist was tens of thousands of years, while that of a Paramount God Realm martial artist was hundreds of thousands to millions of years old. Eighty thousand years wasn't enough for Luo Zheng to age excessively and enter Immortal Decay …
As for Xun, after accompanying Luo Zheng for a long time, she entered a very focused state as well.
A thin, long needle floated within her soul. It was a needle that was embedded deep within her soul. Its surface was engraved with extremely mysterious patterns. The craftsmanship behind it was at least impossible to refine in a secondary universe like Huan Yu. It was an extremely profound refinement method.
Every King of the Night Demon Clan who was sealed would hold a rather mysterious ceremony when they ascended to the throne.
This ceremony was the highest level of secrecy in the Fiend Night Clan. Apart from very few people in the Fiend Night Clan, no one else knew about it …
This ceremony had been passed down from the ancient Fiend Night Clan. As for the purpose of this ceremony, back then, Xun didn't know.
It wasn't just Xun; Yao and Qing Yu didn't know either.
But after the end of the Dream Battlefield, the long needle that had been inserted into the soul began to move. The long needle had two parts, one to the left and the other to the right. At the same time, it released a little bit of information.
Xun Xun by Xun Xun by Xun, she couldn't even resist it.
Xun by Xun's by Xun. by Xun by Xun! by Xun by Xun by Xby Xun 'by by Xun.

However, this instinctive disgust and the feeling of seeing Luo Zheng as her natural enemy constantly affected Xun, and she couldn't ignore it at all.

As more and more information was released by Xun's needle, she understood more and more!
Later, Xun discovered that she didn't only hate Luo Zheng, but also all humans …
The needle in her soul was a method of inheritance, just like the Demi-Immortal Blood Transformation that Luo Zheng had absorbed.
Of course, the Demi-Immortal Blood Transfusion only contained important information within the golden blood. At the same time, it gave the owner of the blood some sort of hidden ability. The Demonnight Clan's ceremony, on the other hand, involved inserting some sort of legacy into the soul.
This method was even more tyrannical because not only did Xun obtain an ability, but she was also affected by Xun's needle. Even her thoughts were changed by the needle!
She felt like she was a mouse and a snake in nature. From the moment she was born, they were each other's natural enemies. This inheritance that came from the soul was simply impossible to resist …
After realizing this, Xun became even more silent when facing Luo Zheng!
She didn't want to leave Luo Zheng's body, and she didn't want to become enemies with Luo Zheng. Thus, she curled up and made herself fall into a deep sleep … At the same time, she was also quite frightened because she didn't know what would happen to her under the influence of the needle.
In fact, with her intelligence, she faintly understood that the Fiend Night Clan and the human race had different standpoints. The existence of this needle was enough to explain everything.
The Ancient Sorcerer Clan wasn't the only spy in the Great Evolution Universe. The Fiend Night Clan might be another one, and even the Devil Race might be one … Moreover, the Demi-Immortal Clan seemed to be on guard. When they gathered the Celestial Venerables to fight against the Devil Race, they intentionally isolated the Fiend Night Clan and the Devil Race.
This needle that had pierced into the depths of Xun's soul had become her nightmare. She didn't even dare to confide in Luo Zheng about this matter. She could only let herself fall into a deep sleep, an endless deep sleep …
This time, after Luo Zheng entered the door to train, Xun woke up again.
This time, the needle began to spin once more, and the information that emerged from it gave Xun a comprehensive understanding of the situation.
Why did the Great Evolution Universe fall apart? The Saints had secretly created the Fiend Night Clan and used some special method to place them in the Great Evolution Universe. This was a foreshadowing that had been buried for more than three billion years!
However, for the sake of caution, they didn't dare to openly use the Golden Bloodline like the Demi-Immortal Clan had done. Instead, they used the method of "Soul-Sealing Needle" to pass on their inheritance.
The Bloodline Inheritance could only hide important information within the blood, but it couldn't change the thoughts of the person receiving the blood.
When the time was right, the blood would awaken, allowing Luo Zheng to understand the information hidden within the blood. However, if he didn't want to do it, the blood wouldn't be able to force Luo Zheng to do it. The choice was still in Luo Zheng's hands, and the same went for the other Demi-Immortals.
The Soul-Sealing Needle, on the other hand, could directly control the person receiving the needle.
Comparatively speaking, the Soul-Sealing Needle was much more difficult to create, and it could only affect a limited number of people. It couldn't be used on a large scale like the Demi-Immortals.
As a result, the Fiend Night Clan had a unique social structure.
From the Celestial Exalts to the commoners, everyone in the Fiend Night Clan believed in their supreme Kings. With the three Kings as the core, everyone in the Fiend Night Clan had to listen to their orders!
This way, all they needed to do was plant the Soul-Sealing Needles in the three succeeding Kings. The three Kings themselves didn't know what their true destinies were, but when the time was right, the Soul-Sealing Needles would be activated. After the three Kings were affected, they would be able to control the entire Yao Night Clan.
In order to destroy an entire universe, they had created an entire clan and used such methods and schemes. Was it really so troublesome to kill a Saint?
"I … will destroy this needle!"
Previously, Xun had already had this idea, and she had tried to pull the needle out.
However, the Soul-Sealing Needle had penetrated deep into Xun's soul. It had even fused with her soul, making it impossible to pull it out. Because of this, if the Soul-Sealing Needle wanted to completely control Xun's thoughts, she wouldn't be able to resist.
Since she couldn't pull it out, she would destroy it.
That perfect face revealed an unparalleled resolute expression ….
She had plenty of time. 80,000 years was more than enough to destroy this Soul-Sealing Needle. The rest would require a great deal of willpower.
Xun's soul had been damaged once before. At that time, she had only possessed a wisp of her soul. Since she had been able to recover it last time, she would be able to recover it this time.
Her soul had originally been hidden within Luo Zheng's Sword Spirit. It would be very easy to separate the two.
However, the difficulty of destroying the Soul-Sealing Needle was far beyond Xun's imagination.
Last time, Xun's soul had been destroyed, but her memories hadn't been affected. The Soul-Sealing Needle had been injected into the depths of her soul and was tightly connected to her memories.
As Xun endured the pain of her soul being destroyed and destroyed the Soul-Sealing Needle, her memories rapidly declined ….
The speed of this decline was astonishing. Towards the end, Xun couldn't even remember why she was torturing herself like this. There was only one obsession left in her soul, and that was to completely destroy the Soul-Sealing Needle ….
While Xun's soul was damaged, Xun's statue in the Great Expansion Realm changed once more. The light within it dimmed once more.
However, the Fiend Night Clan members weren't paying attention to the changes in Xun's statue. The entire Fiend Night Clan members were in a state of panic. This was because they had received a new order on the day the Saints invaded, an order that they couldn't understand.
