Chapter 1564
The situation in the Great Evolution Realm was beginning to shake violently.
The Sacred Race's army continued to push forward with irresistible force. Every day, the universe was filled with noise and roars and shouts filled the sky.
This war concerned the entire universe, every race, and every living being. Whether you were a commoner or a cultivator, whether you were a human or of a different race, you couldn't stay out of this!
The Yao Night Clan had been actively preparing for war at the start.
All of the cultivators of the sacred lands began to gather at the core realms of the Yao Night Clan ….
Realm Lord Jun Lan of the Frigid Autumn Realm led her subordinates through the realm passageways. Her destination was the Xingming Realm, the realm where Yao Yao's Palace of Punishment was located!
She led the elites of the sacred lands, cultivators at the third transformation of the Spiritualized Realm. Even the weakest of them was at the Spirit Sea realm.
The enemy they were facing this time was too strong. Cultivators below the Spirit Sea realm were of little use.
Ever since she'd become the realm lord of the Frigid Autumn Realm, Jun Lan had only been to the Xingming Realm once. The universe was so vast that even as a realm lord, it wasn't easy for her to travel through hundreds of great realms. It was a long journey.
As she traveled through the realm passageways, she'd occasionally run into groups from other sacred lands of the Yao Night Clan. Most of them looked worried and worried.
Taunting voices would occasionally ring out in her ears. Holy Son Maha of the Sacred Beast Clan had somehow gotten his hands on a roaring command. He'd been taunting them almost every day ….
"Heh heh, the seven realm lords I killed today were all cultivators of the Pure Blue Realm of the Human Alliance. They were Song Chuanhe, Zhao Si, Long Can …. Tsk tsk, they couldn't even withstand a single blow.
"Holy Son is mighty!"
"Holy Son is mighty!"
Right at this moment, Holy Son Maha's voice resounded once more ….
"How arrogant!" Jun Lan's expression was as dark as water.
Actually, the people Maha had killed were from the Human Alliance, and human cultivators at that. Not only were they from a different faction, they weren't even from the Yao Night Clan. Logically speaking, Jun Lan shouldn't have been so furious.
However, she wasn't the only one who was furious. The elites of the sacred lands she led all had ugly expressions on their faces. Everyone in the universe didn't look too good either.
The Sacred Beast Clan's target wasn't just the Human Alliance ….
From the very beginning, they had declared that the entire universe was in their hands. They were the enemies of the entire universe.
Therefore, when the Human Dao Alliance was attacked, the Yao Ye Clan would naturally unite against a common enemy!
During this period of time, Maha's reputation had spread far and wide.
Although this person was only a sector lord, his strength seemed to be far above normal sector lords. Every time he killed a sector lord, he would record the name of the sector lord and broadcast it to the entire universe through his roar command!
When the trillions of living beings in the universe heard this guy's provocation, they all gnashed their teeth in hatred.
But there was nothing he could do.
It was said that this person truly relied on his own strength to kill those sector lords. At least in the universe, there wasn't a single sector lord who could defeat him in a one on one battle.
"Lord Jun Lan, why do you think no Empyrean acted to kill Maha?" A martial artist beside Jun Lan asked.
This martial artist was very young. Her name was Yuan Ling, and she could be considered a god level genius in the Yao Ye Clan. After participating in the Dream Battlefield, her cultivation had risen a level and she had stepped into the Extreme Divine realm. However, because she was young, she was deeply favored by Jun Lan.
Jun Lan looked straight ahead and lightly replied, "Perhaps … they're planning something. Since this Maha dares to be so arrogant, he definitely has no lack of Empyreans protecting him. The situation isn't as simple as you think."
To raise the morale of their Sacred Beast Clan's Holy Son and suppress the morale of the entire universe, this move was indeed effective.
At the very least, all of the lower and middle class martial artists in the universe had already begun to complain endlessly!
Ever since the Sacred Beast Clan invaded Windbreaker World, many Empyreans had responded. They had gathered all the races of the universe together to resist the Sacred Beast Clan. But even now, not a single Empyrean had acted!
The result was that they could only watch on helplessly as the Sacred Beast Clan invaded great world after great world, slaughtering them one after another.
In just a few days, the number of sector lords that had fallen had already reached over a hundred!
But whether it was the Demi-Immortal Clan or Gods Without Thought, none of them had acted. Even the Empyreans of the Human Alliance remained silent …
This was an inconceivable matter in the past!
In the past, because of a border dispute between the Human Alliance and the Demon Race, the Empyreans of both sides had come to blows. Especially Empyrean Mirage Sea, who had just inherited the Mandate of Heaven, he had nearly caused the death of a demon Empyrean!
At that time, the factions of the universe didn't think highly of the Human Alliance. This was because the foundation of the Human Alliance was inferior to Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Realm and Gods Without Thought. They were originally a pile of loose sand that had joined together to form a colossus.
It was also because of that dispute that the Human Alliance rose to become a first tier faction, causing the races of the universe to look at them in a new light.
The Human Alliance's Empyreans remained silent. This sort of abnormal situation puzzled countless martial artists. After all, to remain silent at this time was to damage one's morale …
In their grief and indignation, some of the World Lords of the Human Alliance even used their Roar Orders to criticize Empyrean Mirage Sea and Empyrean Oracle.
But they still maintained their silence.
"If they don't act, then Maha will fly to the heavens." Yuan Ling angrily said.
Jun Lan smiled faintly. "It's always hard to guess the thoughts of those important figures. I believe that they will defeat the sacred race in the end."
Not long after, Jun Lan finally led the elites of her sacred land into the Punishment Realm.
There were several hundred great realms gathered in the Punishment Realm. Over two thousand of the elites of the Fiendnight Clan's tenth rank sacred lands were gathered here. Just the World Lords alone numbered over six thousand, not to mention the Divine Transformation Realm, Divine Pinnacle Realm, and Divine Sea Realm martial artists!
With so many Fiendnight Clan experts gathered here, they were all waiting for their ruler's orders. Almost everyone in the Fiendnight Clan believed that they would launch a counterattack against the sacred race.
Within the Punishment Palace.
Beneath the enormous domed ceiling, azure tassels hung from the hem of her dress to the ground. Her dress dragged on the ground for hundreds of feet. Above her beautiful figure was an exquisite face. Beneath her luxurious makeup, she appeared incomparably noble and gorgeous, but it still couldn't conceal her ferocious aura.
Yao stood in the center of the hall with her eyes closed. The needle in the depths of her soul was still slowly spinning.
She was different from Xun. Xun deeply hated this soul sealing needle. She would rather sacrifice herself than be controlled by it.
But Yao gladly accepted the soul sealing needle's intentions. She even had a feeling that they were on the same page.
She was an extremely ambitious woman, but she didn't have the strength to support her ambitions. Even if she became the ruler of the Fiendnight Clan, it was impossible for her to lead the Fiendnight Clan to dominate the entire universe. Compared to the three great human influences, the Fiendnight Clan was still a bit weak, especially when it came to the Empyrean level.
So, the moment the soul sealing needle was activated, she discovered an inconceivable secret. She screamed in excitement and even gathered several men she loved to do it with for several nights.
She was someone who loved to see the world plunged into chaos. To her, this was an excellent opportunity. Moreover, the Fiendnight Clan was actually joining forces with the sacred race!
On the smooth and hard floor in front of her, a speck of green light suddenly appeared. Then, a green vine began to rapidly grow and sprout. In several breaths of time, a giant flower bud emerged.
"Bo!"
After the flower bud bloomed, a blue-clothed Whisper walked out from within.
Compared to the other Dao Children, Whisper's cultivation rose the fastest. In a short period of time, she had reached the peak of the Divine Transformation realm. It seemed that she wasn't far from breaking into the World Lord realm.
Yao looked at Qingyu with a smile on her face. "Ying, you're late."
Whisper coldly glared at Yao.
She didn't like Yao too much. She had always been immersed in her own research and didn't pay too much attention to the matters of the Fiendnight Clan.
Not too long ago, she had discovered the mysteries of the tenth level Laws. But then, a sacred race appeared out of nowhere and caused such a great disturbance, completely disrupting her progress … and what was even worse was that the soul sealing needle in her mind had released a pile of information that had nothing to do with her!
It was only now that she understood the mission of the Fiendnight Clan. Although she wasn't happy about this, she had no choice but to obey the soul sealing needle's intentions.
What left her even more depressed was that through the soul sealing needle's information, she finally understood why she was able to control the tenth level of the Life Laws.
This was because the person who had created the Laws of the universe was on the verge of dying. This was why there were so many loopholes in the Great Evolution Universe. She, Whisper, had only found these loopholes by chance and obtained the tenth level of the Life Laws!
"This time, they sent ten Dao Children to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, but they didn't send me. In truth, the Demi-Immortal Race has long been suspicious of our Fiendnight Clan. They don't even trust the Demon Race," Whisper said. "So, don't think that you can catch them off guard."

Chapter 1566
Yao's explanation wasn't very convincing.
After all, none of the Yao Ye clansmen present had soul sealing needles in their souls, so they didn't know the origins of the Yao Ye clan.
However, as the ruler of the Yao Ye clan, the totem of faith that couldn't be overturned, Yao only needed to give an explanation. As for whether or not this explanation was true or absurd wasn't important, because the Yao Ye clansmen didn't have the right to question their ruler.
"No matter how confused, doubtful, confused, or angry you are, it won't change my and Ying's decision. Realm Lords, listen up. Reorganize your troops and wait for further orders in the Xingming Realm. I will lead the Yao Ye clan into a new era of prosperity!"
With that, Yao turned and left. The multicolored door slammed shut. She still had to explain things to the Yao Ye clan's Celestial Sovereigns.
The Celestial Sovereigns weren't as easy to talk to as the Yao Ye clansmen, but Yao could have them inspect the soul sealing needles. This wasn't a difficult problem to solve. What the Yao Ye clan needed to do next was wait. At the most critical moment, they would deal a fatal blow to the humans.
… …
… …
In a short period of time, the Human Alliance had already lost two hundred great realms.
The Sacred Race's army was unstoppable. They didn't encounter any decent resistance at all. At this rate, the Sacred Race would be able to swallow up the entire Human Alliance in two years at most.
In the core great realm of the Demi-Immortal Race.
Suddenly, a sharp aura was emitted from the huge palace.
The aura was like countless thin blades that swept through everything in the palace.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …
Under the scouring of these blade-like auras, countless tiny marks were cut into this magnificent palace.
The walls, the floor, the ceiling, and the statues made of all sorts of rare materials were instantly covered in tiny scratches.
If someone were to be in the palace and come into contact with the aura, they would probably be reduced to a bloody mess. It would be comparable to death by a thousand cuts.
"How much longer … do we have to endure?" A delicate and pleasant female voice rang out.
The speaker was Celestial Sovereign Oracle of the Human Alliance. Her black gem-like eyes seemed to burn with two balls of fire. The aura just now had been unintentionally released in her anger.
In addition to Celestial Venerable Shen Yu, there were more than 20 human Celestial Venerables in the hall. However, none of them said a word.
After the Sacred Race passed through the Wall of Sighs, their first stop was the Tang Chen Clan. At that time, the people of the Human Dao Alliance had already realized that if the Sacred Race started a war, the first to suffer would be the Human Dao Alliance.
The Human Alliance wasn't weak. Celestial Venerable Oracle was one of the four upper Celestial Venerables of the universe. Apart from her, Celestial Venerable Mirage Sea, Celestial Venerable Cloudfox, and the others also had decent Heavenly Wills.
If they had resisted with all their might, they wouldn't have lost more than two hundred great realms in such a short time.
Now, the Human Alliance was infamy. All intelligent beings in the universe were mocking them for their cowardice.
In particular, there were some World Lords that were killed by the saints. Before they died, they even used their Roar Command, cursing the Empyreans of the Divine Realm for being cowardly before even fighting, saying that they were shameful leaders of the Human Alliance.
Although the Realm of the Gods Empyrean was a woman, her personality was also fiery to the extreme.

Old Man Feng sighed softly. "For now, we can only endure …"
"Still the same explanation?" Celestial Venerable Oracle sneered.
Celestial Venerable of Original Sin suddenly said, "We've just sent out our hope. We can exchange space for time …"
"What time? Time for the ten Dao Children to grow? "Celestial Venerable Oracle questioned.
Celestial Venerable of Original Sin was speechless.
The ten Dao Children were geniuses that had gathered all the resources in the universe to be born. Almost all of them had perfect talent in their own fields. Even the Celestial Venerables present in their youth couldn't compare to them.
However, no matter how talented a Dao Child was, they still needed time to grow!
If the Dao Children were given a hundred years to grow, the current situation would be very different.
But only a little over a year had passed, and the Sacred Race had already made their move.
How many years would it take for the Dao Children to become True Gods?
That was undoubtedly a pipe dream. No matter how much Celestial Venerable Oracle thought highly of Luo Zheng, he knew that it was impossible.
Therefore, many Empyreans were well aware that the so-called exchange of space for time was just wishful thinking. They could only watch helplessly as the universe was nibbled away by the Sacred Race.
"My master naturally has his reasons for making such arrangements. As his descendants, we should choose to believe in him. If Huan Yu survives this crisis, we will repay our debt to the Human Dao Alliance tenfold!" Old man Feng continued.
Celestial Venerable Oracle didn't say anything. A handsome young man next to her sneered. "We can't wait until then. The Human Alliance will not sacrifice ourselves to buy time for the Demi-Immortal Tribe! This one will take his leave! "
With that, the young man turned his head and split into two illusions, disappearing before everyone's eyes.
He was Celestial Venerable Mirage Sea of the Human Alliance!
The Demi-Immortal Tribe's starting point was different from the Human Alliance's. The Demi-Immortal Tribe considered everything from the perspective of the entire universe.
Even with the Sacred Race's relentless pursuit, they'd only nibbled away two hundred great worlds. It would take a considerable amount of time for them to completely occupy the thousands of great worlds in the Human Alliance.
But to the Human Alliance, this was unbearable.
Everyone was gathered by the Demi-Immortal Tribe to join forces against the Sacred Race. Now that the Sacred Race had attacked, the Demi-Immortal Tribe was just going to watch from the sidelines? Not only did they watch from the sidelines, but they also wanted them to give up resistance. That was too much.
Although the Celestial Venerables understood that the Sacred Race's grudges stemmed from the Divine Realm, which was a struggle between higher life forms, and the Demi-Immortal Tribe represented one of the factions. It could be considered a way for the Lord of Great Expansion to redeem himself. But it was hard for them to accept such a method.
"Hahaha, we killed four realm lords today. They were He Shuiming, Yan Kai … Celestial Venerable Oracle, you and I didn't have the fortune to meet that day. I've never forgotten your beauty. If you're willing to come out and drink with me, I can reorganize my troops and postpone the attack for three months. How about it?"
At this moment, Maha's voice rang out through the roaring command once more. All the Celestial Venerables heard it clearly. Old Feng frowned. This Holy Son's provocation was becoming more and more excessive.
"You're going too far. Do you think we can tolerate this?" A strange smile appeared on Celestial Venerable Oracle's face. He was beyond furious. He glared fiercely at the Celestial Venerables present, then turned and stepped into the void.
The Human Alliance's Celestial Venerable Cloudfox followed suit.
Seeing the three representatives of the Human Alliance leave, Celestial Venerable of Original Sin couldn't help but sigh. "Perhaps our decision was wrong."
Thunder Punishing Celestial Venerable, who'd been silent all this time, shook his head. "Time is indeed too short. It's unrealistic to pin our hopes on the little fellows …"
The old patriarch's expression was conflicted.
If they allowed Celestial Venerable Oracle to lead the Human Alliance against the Sacred Race, the result would be obvious. If the elites of the Human Alliance were completely wiped out, it would be a huge loss to the human race.
The old patriarch sighed again.
This expression hadn't appeared on his face for many years.
Ever since he'd led the Demi-Immortal Tribe, their development had been smooth sailing.
The Demi-Immortal Tribe's rapid rise, the ten Dao Children's smooth ascension, and Luo Zheng's rapid growth … Everything seemed to be going according to plan.
He'd even thought all of this was a matter of course. The Demi-Immortal Tribe was a disciple's plan, an arrangement made by the Lord of Taien. It was impossible for anything to go wrong!
He hadn't expected a fatal mistake to occur. The Sacred Race had broken through the Wall of Sighs prematurely, resulting in the Dao Children not having enough time to grow.
"Perhaps it's time to launch a counterattack. I'll go see Master Xingwei." Old Feng disappeared from the palace.

Chapter 1567
The Demi-Immortal Tribe was caught between a rock and a hard place.
In the beginning, both Lord Xingwei and the old patriarch had planned to trade space for time, delaying the Sacred Race as much as possible.
The universe was so big that even if the Sacred Race had a huge appetite, digesting it wouldn't be an easy task ….
In the end, they would have to sacrifice the Human Alliance.
Because of the Sacred Race's invasion, the three great powers of the human race were tied together. The grudges between Exalted Oracle and Exalted Original Sin had been written off, but there was no way Exalted Oracle and the others could just watch as their territory was slowly nibbled away.
But if the Human Alliance were to fight the Sacred Race alone, the consequences would be even worse. In their current situation, rather than fighting them one by one, it was better to work together and fight with everything they had!
But it was a difficult choice.
"Lord Xingwei." Old man Feng cupped his hands in greeting after entering the secret realm.
In the secret realm, an old man floated cross-legged in the air. Specks of starlight constantly streaked past him, as if countless meteors were falling around him.
Xingwei didn't say anything. His eyes were closed, as if he was using the starlight to communicate with something.
Old man Feng stood to the side and didn't interrupt Xingwei's thoughts.
Soon, more and more starlight appeared around Xingwei. Every ray of starlight turned into a bright white line, outlining a complicated pattern.
The pattern grew in number until it finally gathered at the top of the secret realm, forming a white belt of light that slowly pierced through Xingwei's head!
As the light spread, all the stars in the universe simultaneously shot out light!
It was currently night time in the universe. All the starlight bloomed, illuminating the night like it was daytime!
Although Old man Feng was in the secret realm, he could clearly sense the changes in the universe. It seemed that Lord Xingwei was using some kind of secret technique.
Xingwei was the only true god of the Demi-Immortal Tribe. He was the one who had personally activated the bloodline of the Demi-Immortal Tribe. In addition, he was a disciple of a Sage. From his perspective, the situation in the universe should be clearer.
As the ribbon of light above Lord Startail's head continued to draw closer to his head, the speed at which the ribbon of light extended became slower and slower, and more and more difficult!
Xing Wei, whose eyes were tightly shut, frowned slightly. He suddenly stretched out his hands, and a resplendent light suddenly emitted from his Dantian. A light blue crystal suddenly flew out and circled in front of Xing Wei.
The crystal was octagonal in shape and looked like an Eight Trigrams Talisman. However, this Eight Trigrams Talisman gave off a feeling of eternity and indestructibility.
"This is Lord Startail's Godhead! The Godhead has left the body! "Old Man Feng raised his eyebrows slightly. This was the first time the Old Patriarch had seen a Godhead with his own eyes.
After the Godhead spiraled out, the starlight emanating from Lord Startail's body grew even brighter, and the speed at which the belt of light above his head extended increased by several times!
"Condense!"
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
The specks of starlight shot towards Star Tail's head and gathered in the belt of light. It seemed that Lord Star Tail wanted to pull the belt of light into Star Tail's mind.
When the belt of light was only two inches away from Star Tail's head, a cracking sound suddenly came from the belt of light.
"Ka …"
The belt of light suddenly shrunk like an injured snake head of a ferocious beast. It shrunk back into the patterns above Star Tail's head. Very quickly, the patterns outlined by the starlight disappeared completely!
Star Tail's face instantly turned ugly.
"Why, why …" Star Tail's eyes revealed a tinge of frustration.
To be able to make a True God feel frustrated, it was obvious that what he had just done was extremely important!
"Lord Star Tail, this is …" Seeing Star Tail open his eyes, Old Man Feng hurriedly asked.
Star Tail glanced at Old Man Feng indifferently and sighed, "What I used just now was the Great Star Technique. It is able to fully utilize the power of all the stars."
This Great Star Technique belonged to the Star Divine Dao of the Three Thousand Divine Daos.
"Utilizing the power of the stars? What is Lord Star Tail trying to do?" Old Man Feng did not understand.
Star Tail continued, "I want to take over the entire Great Evolution Universe. If I can control the entire universe, I can use the hundred thousand great worlds in the universe as my substitute. My strength will increase greatly, and if I succeed, even the True God of the Sacred Clan will not be able to do anything to me."
"You want to take over the control of the universe and seize the Heaven Way Skill?" Old Man Feng revealed a shocked expression.
Xing Wei nodded, a strange expression on his face. "Honorable Master is now in a half-weakened state. Even his consciousness doesn't exist. Logically speaking, he shouldn't be able to control the entire universe. But why was it that when I tried just now, I was repelled by a strange force?
This was not the first time he had tried.
Old Man Feng's eyes flashed and he said, "Previously, the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe secretly set up a formation at the back of the Great Thousand World in order to seize the Heaven Way. However, in the end, it was all in vain! There seemed to be a power that stopped it! "
Previously, when they had killed the Candle Dragon, the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe had activated the formation and wanted to integrate the will of the Great Thousand World.
However, at the last step, they were repelled by a mysterious power.
Back then, they had suspected that there was a traitor among the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe. However, as the remaining members of the Ancient Sorcerer Tribe were killed, there was no problem with the Heaven Way. Hence, their speculation was disproven. However, until now, they had not found the reason.
Now, Lord Star Tail had tried to integrate the Heaven Way with the Great Star Art, but he had failed.
He was also repelled by a power. It was as if there was a master hidden in the Heaven Way that blocked everyone else.
What Old Man Feng did not expect was that the person who had integrated with the Heaven Way was Luo Zheng!
After he fused with the wills of the Great Thousand Worlds, those wills followed the Immortal Ascension Passage and spread into the universe. Although Luo Zheng had left the Great Expansion Universe and his main body was in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, it was impossible for the Star Tail to invade or even control the Heavenly Dao.
After failing, Star Tail revealed a vexed expression.
If he had successfully integrated with the Heaven Way and controlled the entire Great Yan Universe, he would have a stable position in the Great Yan Universe. Even if the Sacred Race sent several True Gods at the same time, they would not be able to do anything to him.
"What do you want?" Star Tail stared at Old Man Feng and asked.
Old Man Feng reported the reactions of the Human Alliance's Heavenly Venerables to Star Tail truthfully.
Hearing Old Man Feng's words, Star Tail couldn't help but shake his head. "How preposterous. The Human Alliance has less than ten Heavenly Venerables. Even if they gather all the world lords of the Human Alliance, fighting against the Sacred Race is like throwing an egg against a rock."
"But letting them watch as the Sacred Race invades their homeland is too cruel." Old Man Feng shook his head.
Frankly speaking, not to mention Heavenly Venerables Oracle and the others, even Old Man Feng himself could not do it.
How could Star Tail not understand Old Man Feng's words? He sighed and said, "Forget it. Since they want to go, we can't just stand by and watch. This time, I will activate the Celestial Star Technique and do my best to help you fight back."
"We're not waiting for the Daoists to return?" Old Man Feng asked.
The legends of the Daoists and even sending them to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement were all provided by Star Tail. According to Star Tail, the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was indeed an extraordinary cultivation holy land. However, if they were sent out in such a hurry, there was no telling when they would return.
"We can't just wait here. We must do something and cooperate with the Human Dao Alliance and the Sacred Race. At the very least, we can slow down the Sacred Race's advance." Star Tail revealed a worried expression.
As Luo Xiao's disciple, Star Tail had extraordinary trust in his master.
His master's plans and arrangements would definitely be able to turn danger into safety. This was the common belief of all his disciples.
No matter how bad the situation was, Star Tail would never lose confidence.
But this time, Star Tail didn't have much confidence.
As a True God, he understood the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. It was indeed a cultivation holy land built by Sage Jiu of the divine realm.
However, no matter how godly the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was, it couldn't raise the strength of the Daoists to the point where they could save the Great Yan Realm in such a short time. This was too ridiculous.
The Great Yan Realm and the Forbidden Land of God Refinement's flow of time were synchronized.
Just as Star Tail made this decision, Luo Zheng's eyelids trembled slightly. Then, he abruptly opened his eyes. A golden afterglow spun rapidly in his pupils and gradually dissipated.
Luo Zheng revealed a puzzled expression. What time was it?

Chapter 1569
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, the Craftsman Saint disapproved. "It's just a Sword Spirit. A Sword Spirit of this level is of no help to the current you!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng coldly glanced at the Craftsman Saint.
With the strength of these two Saints, they could have prevented this from happening, but they just watched with folded arms …
But soon, Luo Zheng felt relieved.
Whether it was the Craftsman Saint or Jiu Saint, they were just outsiders in the end.
This Craftsman Saint had arranged for him, and Jiu Saint had agreed because Luo Xiao had lent him a hand.
Even in the Realm of the Gods, existences like Saints were lofty existences. To countless creatures, they were illusory legends.
The fact that Jiu Saint was willing to help him was already a great help.
He could only rely on himself!
Thinking of this, the fluctuation in Luo Zheng's heart quietly disappeared, and his expression also calmed down.
Previously, he had wanted to seek Jiu Saint's help to see if there was a way to restore Xun's erased memories, but now, Luo Zheng remained silent.
"Quick, show me your ability!" The Craftsman Saint didn't want to discuss the Sword Spirit at all. He only wanted to see what level Luo Zheng, this living weapon, could reach.
Any Craftsman would have the urge to 'test the blade' when they first formed a Divine Weapon. After making the most outstanding work, they naturally wanted to see the strength of this work!
Ever since Luo Zheng woke up, he had been paying attention to Xun. Hearing the Craftsman Saint's words, he began to examine himself.
In fact, after waking up, Luo Zheng had already sensed that his body had undergone an earth-shaking change!
At this moment, he felt as if there was a transparent membrane around his body, and this membrane seemed to have a connection with his soul.
With a slight thought, a transparent membrane appeared on the surface of his body!
"What is this?"
Luo Zheng revealed a trace of curiosity.
The Craftsman Saint didn't say anything. He only watched as Luo Zheng slowly fumbled around.
The thin film was like a layer of water, constantly surging on the surface of Luo Zheng's body. Soon, Luo Zheng discovered that he could control this thin film with his thoughts. This thin film was like a layer of Luo Zheng's skin covering his body.
"What is this?" Luo Zheng stared at the Craftsman Saint and asked.
The Craftsman Saint chuckled. "This thing doesn't have a name yet!"
After all, no one had ever refined an Origin Divine Spark into a defensive artifact before this. However, the Craftsman Saint quickly added, "However, this artifact is up to your liking. I want to name this artifact the Concentric Garment!"
"Tongyi?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. Under the influence of his thought, the thin film turned into a piece of clothing that covered Luo Zheng's body. This thing did indeed change according to Luo Zheng's state of mind, but at this moment, the clothing was completely transparent and could not be used to cover his body.
Seeing that Luo Zheng was completely unaware of what kind of artifact this was, the Craftsman Saint couldn't help but remind him, "It can change into any color or shape according to your thoughts!"
"It can change color too?"
As soon as the Craftsman Saint finished speaking, the originally transparent layer of clothing changed color and turned into a long black gown.
In terms of appearance, this long gown was almost no different from an ordinary long gown. When a gentle breeze blew, the lapel could even flutter.
"Interesting …" The corner of Luo Zheng's mouth twitched slightly.
The Craftsman Saint looked left and right, then turned his head to Hua Tianming and said, "Little friend, you can attack Luo Zheng with all your strength."
Hua Tianming was slightly taken aback, but his gaze swept past Jiu Sheng.
Jiu Sheng nodded at Hua Tianming, indicating that Hua Tianming could try his best.
With two Saints present, Hua Tianming no longer had any misgivings. He unsheathed his longsword without any hesitation, and his gaze instantly became extremely sharp.
He stared at Luo Zheng and smiled slightly. "Brother Luo Zheng, I'm coming!"
After he finished speaking, the sword in his hand blossomed with a powerful light. The purple Mysterious Fire in the entire cave was pushed away by the light, rolling and tumbling.
Seeing the sword in Hua Tianming's hand, the Craftsman Saint revealed a look of surprise. He hadn't noticed Hua Tianming before, and only now did he realize that this brat was actually holding Tengshe's Sword, the sword of a Weapon Grandmaster!
Almost all of the artifact forging techniques in the Divine Realm were passed down to Weapon Grandmasters.
Sabers, swords, axes, hammers, and halberds … these were all invented by Weapon Grandmaster Chi You. His successors only continuously improved them, but it was very difficult for them to truly innovate.
For countless divine epochs, they had always walked the path of primordial treasures.
Only the Craftsman Saint had walked a new path, and that was the path of primordial treasures. This was why the Craftsman Saint was so popular in the Divine Realm. To be able to open a path that Weapon Grandmasters had never walked before was indeed an amazing feat.
However, Tengshe's Sword was still one of the famed swords that Weapon Grandmasters had personally forged. Seeing it this time, the Craftsman Saint couldn't help but sigh in admiration. It seemed that a portion of the inheritance that Chi You had left behind had fallen into Luo Xiao's hands, and had been inherited by this young man.
Hua Tianming slightly straightened his longsword, and a sharp light spread out from the sword. Layers of light formed a bridge that connected the space between him and Luo Zheng. Then, he charged at Luo Zheng with his longsword in hand!
"Rip!"
At the same time that Hua Tianming charged forward with his sword, the tip of the sword at the front was like a pair of scissors, directly cutting the space in half!
In just an instant, Hua Tianming's sword was already pressed against Luo Zheng's chest. The tip of Tengshe's Sword was directly pressed against the surface of Luo Zheng's robe.
The originally soft fabric became extremely firm under Luo Zheng's will. It was like a thin piece of metal blocking Luo Zheng's chest.
"Whoosh!"
As the longswords collided, a melodious sound erupted. The tip of the sword immediately slid to the side.
This sword really couldn't pierce through Luo Zheng's concentric robe. However, it still left a scratch on the surface of Luo Zheng's Divine Realm robe!
However, the concentric robe was like a living thing. The scratch was instantly repaired.
"Whoosh whoosh!"
The longsword circled around Hua Tianming's hand once before returning to its scabbard. Hua Tianming cupped his hands at Luo Zheng and smiled. "Congratulations, Brother Luo Zheng."
Luo Zheng nodded. Then, he cupped his hands at the Craftsman Saint and said, "Thank you, Senior, for bestowing me with such a wonderful treasure."
He was naturally grateful for this person's help. However, Luo Zheng also understood that he was merely exchanging relationships with these people. They didn't help him because he was Luo Zheng, but because of other motives.
He was indeed grateful to this person now. However, he would definitely find an opportunity to repay this favor in the future.
The Craftsman Saint smiled faintly. "Helping you refine your body this time is also an experiment for me. However, at this stage, your body has yet to reach the level of a first-rate Chaos Treasure!"
Hearing the Craftsman Saint's words, Luo Zheng's face revealed a trace of doubt.
"You will know soon." The Craftsman Saint didn't reveal the secret of Luo Zheng's body. He even changed Luo Zheng's instincts to a certain extent. He believed that Luo Zheng would discover this secret soon.
Despite the Craftsman Saint's words, Luo Zheng discovered that the power he could control had greatly increased. At the very least, he had used nearly one percent of the Nine Stars' power source and applied it to his body. However, his body was still as sturdy as before and showed no signs of collapsing.
The Craftsman Saint didn't stay for long. He had entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement precisely to refine Luo Zheng. After saying a few words, he turned into a wisp of smoke and left the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
After the Craftsman Saint left, Saint Jiu glanced at Luo Zheng and Hua Tianming, as well as the Nine True Dragons circling around the cave. "I just received news that the fake Sacred Clan has begun to act."
"They've already begun?"
Hua Tianming's eyelids suddenly twitched. Luo Zheng's eyebrows also rose. As for the Nine True Dragons, they all had different expressions.
Saint Jiu nodded.
The Saint Jiu that Luo Zheng and the others saw was only one of Saint Jiu's incarnations. The real Saint Jiu was far away in the Realm of the Gods. He had only just received news that Mu Haiji had captured the Great Yan Realm.
"You don't have much time left," Saint Jiu said.
Once the Sacred Clan occupied the entire universe and disintegrated it, Luo Xiao's Sacred Position would be completely erased and he would truly perish.
If there was enough time, this group of ten Dao Children could stay in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement for dozens of years or even a hundred years.
To a martial artist, a hundred years was an extremely short period of cultivation.
When Luo Zheng left the Great Yan Realm, he also understood that the Sacred Clan wouldn't give them much time. But now, only a year had passed. They had far too little time!
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1570
In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Sage Jiu was an omniscient and omnipotent existence, but he couldn't influence the Great Evolution Universe.
He might be good friends with Luo Xiao, but he couldn't oppose the Templars. He couldn't break the rules of the Templars and directly help Luo Xiao …
Sage Jiu could only participate in nurturing the Ten Dao Children.
And Luo Zheng was the most important one, which was why he was so generous as to share his power source with Luo Zheng.
If it were any other Saint, this would be unthinkable …
Although at the level of the Saint Realm, the contest between Saints wasn't decided by strength, in many specific situations, strength still played a role that couldn't be ignored.
In this situation, Sage Jiu could only compress the time without limit!
He wasn't just responsible for Luo Zheng.
Xi Youqin, Ji Luoxue, Jiang Zhengyi, and the other Dao Children all had their own arrangements, but the resources given to them naturally wouldn't be as much as Luo Zheng and Hua Tianming …
Sage Jiu had never been to the Great Evolution Universe, so he didn't have a deep understanding of its situation. He only knew that Mu Haiji had made his move, urging the creatures in his inner world to attack and occupy the Great Evolution Universe.
Luo Zheng asked Sage Jiu why he didn't try to divine the fate of the Great Evolution Universe.
Sage Jiu said that he couldn't divine it.
In truth, the Dao of Fortune Telling only existed in the universe. In the Realm of the Gods, even Saints couldn't divine the fate of others.
More than a year ago, Hua Tianming had told Luo Zheng that the so-called Dao of Fortune was fake, but there was a deeper meaning behind it.
The so-called Dao of Fortune was nothing more than a path that had been arranged by the Heavenly Dao long ago. As for the divination techniques in the universe, they were actually just a trace of the Heavenly Dao's mysteries.
This was because the master of the universe had the ability to arrange everything. He could make any creature in the universe move according to the will of the universe's master.
For example, every step Luo Zheng took was the result of Luo Xiao's arrangements.
However, the Divine Domain was different. In the Divine Domain, there was no controller or Heavenly Dao. It was a freely constructed world. Everything in that world was in a random state.
However, nothing was absolute. In the legends, one of the lost Divine Daos of the 3000 Divine Daos was the Great Destiny Art. It could alter one's own destiny and even calculate the destiny of every life within the Divine Realm. But up until now, no one had been able to comprehend this Divine Dao.
Although Sage Jiu couldn't calculate the fate of the Great Yan Universe, he gave three years based on the information provided by Luo Zheng and Hua Tianming, as well as Sage Jiu's estimation of the strength of the pseudo-Saint Clan created in Mu Haiji's Great Yan Universe.
He didn't just calculate the Sacred Clan, he also calculated the Fiend Night Clan that Luo Zheng had mentioned. After all, Sage Jiu also knew about the Fiend Night Clan's movements from Luo Zheng's Sword Lingxun …
"Three years is indeed short, but at some point, I will compress the time again. It will be enough for you to cultivate." After saying this, Sage Jiu's originally worried face revealed a slightly vulgar smile. It was hard to imagine that a supreme existence of the Divine Realm would reveal such a smile. "Next, you will still need to bring those two girls from Han Jia to the Soul Subduing Cliff. You still need to seize the opportunity for the girl from Han Jia."
Luo Zheng could only silently roll his eyes. It seemed that Sage Jiu was also a romantic and elegant person in the Divine Realm.
He naturally wouldn't follow Sage Jiu's instructions and pursue Han Liusu. This was not in line with Luo Zheng's original intentions. In other words, with Luo Zheng's personality, he wouldn't do such a thing. However, he still followed Sage Jiu's instructions. After all, he had obtained a Black Trial Talisman and his goal was to go to the Soul Subduing Cliff to cultivate …
In the eyes of foreign martial artists and Divine Realm martial artists, the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was a place where opportunity and danger coexisted.
But the ten Demi-Immortal Dao Children, after nearly a year of tempering, suddenly discovered that the Forbidden Land of God Refinement seemed to have become quite friendly. Every step they took was abnormally smooth, and this smoothness even revealed a trace of strangeness, causing them to become vigilant.
Of the ten Dao Children, only three knew of Sage Jiu's existence: Luo Zheng, Hua Tianming, and Luo Xi Xuan. The others didn't know.
All of this was naturally arranged by Sage Jiu. Although tempering was quite beneficial to these Dao Children, the difference between a martial artist who was forcefully matured and a martial artist who was tempered was like the difference between the heavens and the earth. But in the end, there was only one reason – there wasn't much time.
Han Liusu and the other Divine Realm martial artists were trapped by Sage Jiu, so they naturally had their own ideas. But Luo Zheng didn't directly answer. After all, Sage Jiu didn't want news of his interference to spread to the Divine Realm, so this matter was left unsettled.
After arriving at the Soul Subduing Cliff, Luo Zheng realized that Sage Jiu had sealed away his supreme comprehension of several Divine Daos. So far, only a handful of people had ever entered the Soul Subduing Cliff.
Unfortunately, Luo Zheng didn't find any records of the Severing Emotions Divine Dao. After the selection, Luo Zheng chose the most mature Grand Wish Art.
As for Luo Zheng's choice, Han Liusu was quite disapproving. In her opinion, the Grand Wish Art was not a rare Divine Dao in the Divine Realm. Almost everyone could cultivate it.
The reason why she was so obsessed with the Soul Subduing Cliff was because the Dao Essence of Sage Jiu's Grand Soul Subduing Technique was hidden within.
The Grand Soul Subduing Technique was a supernatural ability that could suppress the soul. After comprehending it, one could suppress the souls of countless powerhouses. It was naturally tyrannical to the extreme. In the Divine Realm, there were very few True Divinities that could cultivate the Grand Soul Subduing Technique to Perfection. And, there was only Sage Jiu who had cultivated the Grand Soul Subduing Technique to the Saint realm!
Thus, by observing the Dao Essence of Sage Jiu's Grand Soul Subduing Technique, one would naturally benefit greatly. It was just that Luo Zheng had gone so deep into a mountain of treasures and yet only cultivated the Grand Wish Art. That was a bit stupid …
Luo Zheng naturally disapproved. He had always believed that what suited him was the most important. There was a price to using the Grand Wish Art. If he couldn't satisfy this price, then it would severely restrict him, even injure him.
But Luo Zheng understood that the problems he would face in the future would be far more severe. First, he would have to face the crisis of the Grand Expansion Realm. Even after resolving this crisis, he would still have to go to the Divine Realm. Then, he would have to face even more powerful existences. He might even have to face a Saint!
With Luo Zheng's current strength, let alone a Saint, Luo Zheng wouldn't even have the strength to fight back against a True Divinity. If he wanted to take advantage of the situation, then he would have to take the risk …
Han Liusu criticized Luo Zheng for wasting his opportunity, but Han Chuyue admired Luo Zheng's actions. At such a young age, Han Chuyue's way of thinking was very different from her older sister's.
Luo Zheng and Han Liusu weren't the only ones to enter the Soul Suppressing Cliff. Han Liusu's younger sister, Han Chuyue, had also obtained the qualifications to enter. Sage Jiu had put in all his effort to create opportunities for Luo Zheng. Perhaps Luo Zheng was well aware of this, but he still failed to live up to Sage Jiu's intentions. Luo Zheng couldn't go against his own heart and truly climb up the ranks of Han Jia's people.
As for the Han Jia's He Zuowei and the other guards, they could only obediently wait outside the Soul Suppressing Cliff. He Zuowei and the others had originally thought that they had a sufficient understanding of the Forbidden Land of God. Although their Eldest Princess had repeatedly said that Luo Zheng could bring her into the Soul Suppressing Cliff, they had believed her a little. They had never imagined that Luo Zheng would be able to bring the two sisters in so easily. Unfortunately, they could only watch with envy. Sage Jiu wasn't so generous as to let just anyone into the Soul Suppressing Cliff.
Half a year later, when they were leaving the Soul Suppressing Cliff, the two sisters of Han Jia tried their best to invite Luo Zheng to the Realm of the Gods.
Luo Zheng didn't even think about it before rejecting the two sisters' request. He was a secondary life form created by Luo Xiao. At this stage, he couldn't enter the Realm of the Gods. The consequence of forcefully entering would be to be reduced to a ball of true essence …
He didn't expect that Han Chuyue and Han Liusu would be able to think of a way.
Secondary life forms that entered the Realm of the Gods would be reduced to true essence. This was the law of the hierarchy of life forms. However, after countless God Epochs, there were still people who could break this law. It was just that the price was rather high.
Han Chuyue expressed that for someone as outstanding as Luo Zheng, her father should be happy to help.
Luo Zheng still refused without hesitation. He was indeed going to the Realm of the Gods, but definitely not now. After all, the Great Yan Universe was still in danger. He wouldn't waste any time.
Time flew by as he cultivated meticulously and tediously.

Chapter 1571
The Time Valley was generally the last stop for outside martial artists in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
The time compression here was very balanced and there were almost no fluctuations. Moreover, the heaven and earth origin energy was in a calm state.
Thus, most people would try to break through bottlenecks here.
But then again, those who entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement were either the top geniuses of the universe or the elite martial artists of the Realm of the Gods. All of them had good talent. The Third Divine Transformation Stage was an insurmountable barrier for ordinary martial artists, but for those who had the qualifications to enter the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, it wasn't difficult at all.
After the ten Dao Children left their respective Holy Lands, they entered the Time Valley one after another and began their year-long seclusion …
The scenery in the valley was beautiful and tranquil. The Dragon Bird Clan used a secret method to create a soul-calming incense that constantly burned, causing its fragrance to spread throughout the valley.
There were large and small caves in the valley, arranged in order. The highest cave maintained a time compression of 80 times, the middle cave maintained a time compression of 50 times, and the lowest cave maintained a time compression of 20 times.
This place maintained a steady flow of time all year round. Naturally, it was impossible for it to be like Luo Zheng's situation in the Gate of God Refinement, where it reached a terrifying 30 million times! After all, even Sage Jiu found it difficult to maintain such a high rate of time compression.
After the Dao Children entered the Time Valley, they were invited by the Dragon Bird Clan's prophet to enter the Time Valley and begin their year-long seclusion. As for the seclusion cave, it was naturally the best location.
This caused a group of Realm of the Gods martial artists to feel indignant. They didn't know what was wrong with the Dragon Bird Clan to give such preferential treatment to a group of second-level life forms …
This year was very peaceful.
According to the time compression of 80 times, a month in the outside world was equivalent to seven years in the cave.
The ten Dao Children had already accumulated a lot of experience in the various Holy Lands. In addition to the several inheritances of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, they had also cultivated their own characteristics to the extreme.
Among them, the ones who broke through the fastest were Hua Tianming and Xuanyuan Chenfeng!
The two of them stepped into the Divine Transformation Realm from the Divine Realm in the first month. Xuanyuan Chenfeng was slightly faster than Hua Tianming.
After breaking through, Xuanyuan Chenfeng left the cave and inquired about the other Dao Children's situations. As he heard that he was the first one to step into the Divine Transformation realm, he was naturally a bit pleased with himself. In his heart, there was still a trace of pride. He believed that he was the strongest of the 10 Dao Children …
However, after the two of them broke through, they only left the cave to rest for a day before continuing their seclusion. A day in the outside world was equivalent to two and a half months in the cave. Their goal was to break through to the world overlord realm. They had no choice but to cherish every second they had.
In the third month, Luo Xi Xuan became the third Dao Child to break through to the Divine Transformation Realm.
In the fourth month, Ji Luoxue, Ku Deng, Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao, Jiang Zhengyi, and the other Dao Children all stepped into the Divine Transformation Realm. At this time, only Luo Zheng hadn't broken through.
The three stages of the Three Transformations of the Divinity Transformation were the process of expanding one's inner world.
After entering the Spirit Sea Realm, the inner world of a warrior was constantly expanding, and many Precepts were constantly being formed.
The key reason why Luo Zheng was lagging behind them was because of his beginning and end True Essence.
The other eight had absorbed a massive amount of beginning and end True Essence from the Sky Vault Clan. This sort of special True Essence could make a warrior's inner world even more condensed, and it was also extremely beneficial for breaking through to the next realm.
Luo Zheng's body was filled with chaos energy. Compared to True Essence, it was much more condensed, and it was naturally much more difficult for him to break through to the Divine Transformation Realm.
However, Luo Zheng was also able to fully utilize the characteristics of chaos energy. In the fifth month, which was nearly 35 years, he repeatedly swallowed the heaven and earth origin energy here, constantly transforming it into chaos energy.
The efficiency of this transformation couldn't be considered high, but it was carried out step by step. On the last day of the fifth month, Luo Zheng finally broke through to the Divine Transformation Realm.
Luo Zheng's inner world had undergone earth-shaking changes.
The area of his inner world had more than doubled, and a World Tree had grown to a height of 100,000 feet. The Heavenly Spirit Race living on this World Tree had become an extremely large race.
As for the Noble Race and the Teng Race, they had already split into two Divine Kingdoms, and they had already given birth to Void Tribulation and Life and Death realm martial artists.
In addition, a strange ice silkworm had been born on the ice tree. This ice silkworm looked like a transparent block of ice, but it was extremely fragile. Once it left the ice tree, it would immediately melt. As for what degree this thing could evolve to, Luo Zheng wasn't sure.
According to the three year agreement, he still had seven months left, which was around 50 years. He wasn't too sure whether or not he could break through to the World Lord realm.
The other Dao Children all had paths paved for them by their predecessors. They only needed to rely on their own talent to madly rush forward. As for Luo Zheng, he had cultivated the Secret Primal Chaos Arts and had relied entirely on himself to reach this point. Not only was it filled with hardships, but it wasn't a problem that talent could solve!
In order to cultivate to the World Lord realm, the requirement was to transform one's body into a world. The standard was to expand one's inner world into a boundless world.
This road had already been walked by countless lives. Every World Lord was the ruler of their own boundless world. But, whether or not the Secret Primal Chaos Arts could expand into a boundless world was still a question.
But when Luo Zheng entered the Divine Transformation realm, his inner world underwent a new change.
The world tree he had planted began to grow. The treetops began to extend upwards without limit, and the roots were deeply rooted in the depths of the sea of chaos!
As these tree roots continued to extend outwards, they actually broke through Luo Zheng's inner world!
If this were an ordinary martial artist, this sort of thing would be forbidden.
The world tree was like a stabilizing needle in the inner world. It could store true essence. If an ordinary martial artist were to plant a world tree, they would have nearly 50% more true essence than an ordinary martial artist.
But, no one would allow the trunk of a world tree to extend outside of their inner world. This meant that their dantian would be pierced through. To a martial artist, this was an enormous damage!
The roots of a world tree were extremely tough and tenacious. Even a small seed could push open a stone and stubbornly grow. This world tree was also the same.
Thus, if a martial artist had a world tree, they would prune the roots from time to time.
But Luo Zheng had allowed the world tree to freely grow. In the end, these roots stubbornly pierced through his inner world.
But, Luo Zheng's inner world wasn't a true inner world. In truth, his inner world existed within the primal chaos. It was only through the supernatural power that Luo Zheng established an inconceivable connection with it!
After the world tree pierced through the inner world's inner world, it actually extended into the primal chaos!
After Luo Zheng discovered this problem, he was greatly interested.
The Secret Arts of Primal Chaos was not a complete cultivation method, and it had never been reviewed by anyone. When Luo Zheng's master, the "Gu Bei" that Saint Jiu spoke of, wrote the Secret Arts of Primal Chaos, he admitted that it was only a concept of his. How far it could go or whether it could succeed was something that could not be guaranteed.
But, the greatest flaw of the Secret Primal Chaos Arts was that it was difficult to supplement the energy of primal chaos.
Whether it was the universe or the Divine Realm, both were filled with heaven and earth origin energy. A martial artist could freely absorb it or replace it with true essence stones. This was how a martial artist cultivated and survived.
But where would Luo Zheng go to find the energy of primal chaos?
Before this, Luo Zheng had thought of an alternative plan. That was to use the special characteristics of the Heavenly Demon God Fist that he cultivated in the past to give the energy of primal chaos the ability to swallow. But, the efficiency of swallowing true essence and transforming it into energy of primal chaos wasn't high. After all, compared to primal chaos, true essence was only a secondary energy.
This was also the reason why Luo Zheng's speed of breaking through to the Divine Transformation realm had lagged behind the other Dao Children.
But this time, the world tree had pierced through his inner world and extended its roots into the endless primal chaos. To Luo Zheng, this was the same as opening up a completely new path!
"It is said that the four divine trees of the Divine Realm can absorb the energy of primal chaos. Then, this world tree will also become a divine tree of my inner world!"

Chapter 1572
The martial artists couldn't help but calm down.
After entering the Valley of Time, the first thing the foreign martial artists did was go into seclusion. They wanted to consolidate the things they obtained in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
As for the Time Valley's Dragon Bird Clan, they were a clan that liked to be quiet.
This strange half-dragon half-human race was like a statue most of the time. They maintained the same posture for several days without saying a word. Only when there was a change in the Valley of Time would they pay attention.
"Pop!"
On this day, the heaven and earth origin energy in the sky above the Valley of Time suddenly began to shake violently. Then, there was an explosive sound.
The originally colorless and invisible heaven and earth origin energy turned into a pale yellow ring that constantly spread out. On the surface of this ring, there were diamond shaped patterns!
"Realm cloud, someone has stepped into the World Lord realm." A Dragon Bird Clan member looked up at the realm cloud and said indifferently.
In the Inner World of a warrior in the Third Transformation of the Demigod Realm, the boundary was very obscure and blurry. There was no obvious boundary line. However, after cultivating to the Sector Lord level, the Inner World would produce the power of the Demigod Realm. Then, there would be an obvious boundary in the Inner World, which would turn into a Boundless World.
And in the process of Realm Transformation, the martial artist would resonate with the heaven and earth origin energy to produce a realm cloud.
In the Valley of Time, one could see the birth of a realm cloud every once in a while. Many martial artists who entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement would strive to cultivate to the World Lord realm before leaving. Most of them would try to break through to the World Lord realm in the Valley of Time.
"This person's realm cloud can spread out to 20 miles. He seems to be a second-level life form." An elder of the Dragon Bird Clan stared at the realm cloud and commented.
The Dragon Bird Clan members could often see realm clouds. Through the extent of the realm cloud's spread, they could understand a martial artist's foundation and potential.
When a martial artist stepped into the World Lord realm, the realm cloud they condensed would spread out to three to five miles. In the Valley of Time, this kind of realm cloud was the most common. Most martial artists could only reach this level.
When some outstanding martial artists became World Lords, they would resonate with the heaven and earth origin energy and produce realm clouds that could spread out to seven to eight miles.
A small number of top martial artists could spread their realm clouds to 15 miles.
However, some geniuses in the Realm of the Gods could spread their realm clouds to 20 miles or even more when they broke through!
This kind of martial artist was extremely rare.
Even in the Valley of Time, only one would appear every few years.
Generally speaking, cultivators who could spread their Boundary Cloud to more than fifteen kilometers had a fifty percent chance of condensing a Godhead in the future. Although there were many ways to obtain a Godhead, such as through inheritance and seizing, condensing a Godhead on its own was the most suitable and the most precious.
If the boundary cloud spread out to twenty miles, such a martial artist would have an eighty percent chance of condensing a godhead. This probability was already quite high.
The key point was that this Realm Cloud actually came from a secondary life form, which was a bit shocking.
"I've already said that this batch of secondary creatures is very special. They must have received the help of a certain Saint to be qualified to condense them," said another Dragon Bird Clan Elder.
The "second-level living beings" that the elder was referring to were naturally Luo Zheng and his group of Daoists …
These people had a common characteristic. They were all second-level creatures, and the Dragon Bird Clan's prophets had personally arranged for them to cultivate in seclusion in the cave on the highest level. The Dragon Bird Clan had never seen such treatment before, so they thought that these people must have extraordinary origins.
Naturally, they didn't know that these Daoists were originally arranged by the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
The World Cloud in the sky did not disperse for a long time. It continued to revolve in the sky, and in a cave at the top of the Time Valley, a handsome youth walked out of the cave. This person was Xuanyuan Chenfeng.
He looked at the Realm Cloud slowly spreading in the sky, and a satisfied smile appeared on his face. "Father is right. When breaking through to the Realm Lord realm, the Realm Cloud produced by the Realm Transformation power can reach twenty miles! This can be considered the greatest in the universe! "
The martial artists who could enter the Forbidden Land of God Refinement were all extremely outstanding. Even in the Valley of Time, it was rare for a twenty mile Realm Cloud to appear. As for the Great Evolution Universe, the largest Realm Cloud produced in this era was only fourteen miles! Xuanyuan Chenfeng had far surpassed this result.
"It seems that I'm also the first to break through to the Realm Lord realm! I wonder when Luo Zheng entered the Divine Transformation Realm? " He had only left the cave when he entered the Divine Transformation Realm, so he naturally didn't know the situation of the other Daoists in seclusion.
However, this sort of information was easy to find out. As he learned that Luo Zheng had spent five months to step into the Divine Transformation realm, he revealed a gloating expression. Luo Zheng's speed was far slower than his own by more than a tiny bit!
It seemed that whether or not Luo Zheng could break through to the Realm Lord realm was a problem …
After Xuanyuan Chenfeng broke through to the Realm Lord realm, he didn't choose to go into seclusion. Instead, he waited for the other Daoists in the Valley of Time. Half a month later, the Valley of Time began to shake once more.
The Realm Transformation power resonated with heaven and earth origin energy, creating a Realm Cloud that constantly spread out in all directions!
"This Realm Cloud is spreading out too quickly!"
Xuanyuan Chenfeng's eyebrows shot up. He had just created an inconceivable result, so he naturally didn't want to be surpassed by others in half a month!
As the Realm Cloud continued to spread out, its diameter also began to lengthen.
Three miles …
Five miles …
Ten miles …
Fifteen miles …
Seeing the Realm Cloud grow larger and larger, Xuanyuan Chenfeng's forehead wrinkled.
The Realm Cloud kept spreading out at a relatively fast speed, which meant that the Realm Cloud would continue to expand!
Eighteen miles …
Nineteen miles …
Twenty miles!
Xuanyuan Chenfeng stared at the Realm Cloud and whispered, "Stop for me!"
The Realm Cloud naturally wouldn't move according to Xuanyuan Chenfeng's will. In fact, as soon as he finished speaking, the Realm Cloud had already surpassed Xuanyuan Chenfeng and spread out to a distance of twenty-two miles!
Seeing this, the Dragon Bird clansmen looked at each other in dismay. Previously, a Realm Cloud of twenty miles had appeared. They didn't expect that in just half a month, someone would create a Realm Cloud of twenty-two miles.
"Twenty-two miles … I don't remember the last time I saw a Realm Cloud of this distance." This time, it was the Dragon Bird clan's patriarch who spoke.
The Dragon Bird clan originally had various guesses about the identity of Luo Zheng and the other Daoists. Now, they felt that it was extremely strange. Could it be that all nine of them could break through twenty miles?
Xuanyuan Chenfeng stared at the cave at the top of the valley. He didn't know that the Realm Cloud was created by Realm Transformation power, but the one he didn't want to see the most was Luo Zheng! At this moment, he naturally paid close attention.
After a while, he saw Hua Tianming carrying his long sword and walking out of the cave. Only then did Xuanyuan Chenfeng slowly let out a sigh of relief in his heart.
Hua Tianming stepped into the Realm Lord realm and created a Realm Cloud that surpassed him. Xuanyuan Chenfeng was quite surprised.
However, he would rather be surpassed by Hua Tianming than be surpassed by Luo Zheng. After all, ever since he participated in the Illusory Battlefield, Luo Zheng had given him the biggest blow. Although he reconciled with Luo Zheng and was even saved by Luo Zheng, there was still a gap in his heart.
At this moment, nine months had passed in the last year. There were only three months left.
After Hua Tianming broke through to the Realm Lord realm, in the following month, no one broke through to the Realm Lord realm. However, in the eleventh month, Ku Deng and Ji Luoxue broke through to the Realm Lord realm on the same day and at the same time!
Under the influence of two Realm Transformation powers, two Realm Clouds appeared in the sky.
Ku Deng's Realm Cloud spread out to sixteen li while Ji Luoxue's Realm Cloud spread out to eighteen li. This result was extremely outstanding even in the entire Divine Realm. However, compared to Xuanyuan Chenfeng's Realm Cloud of twenty li and Hua Tianming's Realm Cloud of twenty li, there was still a considerable gap.
The four gathered together and continued to wait for the rest to come out.
Two days later, Luo Xi Xuan broke through and created a Realm Cloud of seventeen li. Then Jiang Zheng Yi and Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao, and finally Lie Qian Han …
Their Realm Clouds were all above fifteen li. Below twenty li, no one could go beyond twenty li.
Under the influence of curiosity, the Dragon Bird clan also paid close attention to this group of people. In the past, if a Realm Lord could create a Realm Cloud of fifteen li, it was already very outstanding. They didn't expect that this group of people would not be easy to deal with.
"I originally thought that there would be another Realm Lord above twenty li, but I didn't expect that the next few people would be a bit weaker," the Dragon Bird clan's patriarch said lightly.
"Looking at the nine people's cultivation, the cultivator who broke through first would have a much higher comprehension. It's normal for the people who came out later to be weaker than the first two," the Dragon Bird clan's elder replied.
The clan leader nodded. This analysis was also reasonable. Being able to break through faster than others was also a manifestation of one's talent. The realm clouds would naturally spread out even wider.
"It seems that the last person in this group should not exceed twenty li," the patriarch said as he stared at the cave.
The last person he mentioned was naturally Luo Zheng, who was still in seclusion in the cave. At this moment, Luo Zheng was still cultivating with his eyes closed.

Chapter 1573
The Time Valley's cave was completely sealed off. Luo Zheng was completely unaware of the outside world from within the cave.
All these years, he had been using his Inner Body Incarnation to cultivate the entire world within his body.
Most of the time, martial artists didn't need to interfere with their own Inner World. Those creatures would naturally form an ecological chain and thrive within it.
Only in a harsh environment could powerful creatures be born. If Luo Zheng interfered too much and gave too much, then the creatures within his Inner World would become extremely weak. Even their reproduction speed would constantly decline.
Ever since the roots of the World Tree extended into the Primal Chaos, Luo Zheng had been able to continuously extract Primal Chaos Qi through the World Tree. This was extremely beneficial to him …
The Primal Chaos Sea was constantly expanding. The Central Continent's surface area was unknowingly evolving. The time within his Inner World was also rapidly moving forward …
The situation within the continent was also beginning to become complicated.
The Mighty Clan and the Teng Clan had already become the past of the previous era, becoming a mark and legend in history.
The Queen of the Teng Clan that Luo Zheng had saved in his Inner World left behind a glorious Divine Kingdom. This Divine Kingdom was later divided into three, becoming the Three Great Divine Kingdoms of the continent.
As for the Mighty Clan, after being divided, they formed various Holy Lands. Moreover, in one of these Holy Lands, the first Divine Sea Realm martial artist was born!
"Hualala …"
The edge of Luo Zheng's Inner World was still in a state of obscurity.
Some intelligent sea creatures were curious about the edge of this Inner World. They constantly swam forward along the Primal Chaos Sea.
At this moment, a strange phenomenon occurred. No matter how long these sea creatures swam, it seemed as if they would never reach the end. It seemed as if the Primal Chaos Sea was truly boundless.
But as soon as they turned around, they would return to their starting point in an instant. It was as if the edge of this Inner World was the edge of an endless cycle.
Luo Zheng wasn't the only one experiencing this situation. The same was true for the Inner Worlds of other martial artists who focused on cultivation.
But this time, the situation was different. A group of sea creatures swam along the edge of the Primal Chaos Sea and felt a vigorous force descend upon them.
Violent storms began to rise in the Primal Chaos Sea. These storms were like a hundred thousand zhang tall wall that appeared in the sea.
At first, the group of sea creatures were extremely terrified. Soon, although the storm was intense, it only stopped at the edge of the Chaos Sea.
A few months later, the storm vanished, and a grayish-brown screen of light began to condense at the edge of the Primal Chaos Sea. The screen of light began to madly absorb the Primal Chaos Qi in the sea!
"Realm Transformation!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng had also reached a critical juncture. Sitting upright in the cave, Luo Zheng placed his hands on his Dantian, his eyes radiating vigor.
The Secret Skills of Primal Chaos was just a guideline to guide him. Luo Zheng had reached this stage by relying on his own exploration and comprehension.
Now was the most crucial step, to break through to the Sector Lord Realm!
The light screen at the edge of the Chaos Sea was still very weak. Under the impact of the tide, some parts of the light screen were even damaged and cracked.
Fortunately, after the light barrier absorbed the Primal Chaos Qi, it continued to release the power of the World Transformation Realm to repair the damage and the cracks.
At this moment, there was still half a month left before Saint Jiu's one year deadline.
The seven Dao Children had already left the cave and were waiting for Luo Zheng to come out of seclusion in the Time Valley. Although the faint fragrance in the Time Valley could calm a warrior's heart, the seven of them couldn't help but have anxious expressions.
Even Xuanyuan Chenfeng had a trace of worry on his face. After all, the seven of them had gone through the training in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. They had all reached the Sector Lord Realm and obtained many inheritances from the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Whether it was their strength or mental state, they had undergone tremendous changes.
However, only Luo Zheng hadn't broken through yet.
Perhaps Xuanyuan Chenfeng wasn't willing to admit the fact that Luo Zheng was the leader of the ten Dao Children. This trip to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement had taken three years, but Luo Zheng had fallen to the last place. No matter what, it wasn't justifiable.
"Half a month. Magnifying it by eighty times, there are still three years left! Actually, there's no need to be so anxious! " Ji Luoxue smiled. Three of the seven Dao Children had entered the Yuanhe Clan to cultivate, and Ji Luoxue was one of them.
The Yuanhe Clan's Heart Refining Pagoda could train a warrior's mental state. The current Ji Luoxue exuded an additional trace of elegance and calmness, making her seem even more lovely and moving.
"However, Luo Zheng hasn't broken through yet. It's most likely that he's encountered some trouble. Perhaps there are shackles that he can't surpass," Xuanyuan Chenfeng retorted.
Hua Tianming's expression was indifferent …
Among the seven of them, he was probably the one who understood Luo Zheng the most.
During this trip to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Saint Jiu had spent the most effort on two people. One was him, and the other was Luo Zheng. However, Hua Tianming also understood that he was far less important than Luo Zheng.
In his eyes, there was no need to doubt that Luo Zheng had stepped into the Sector Lord Realm. He wasn't worried at all.
Other than worrying about Luo Zheng, the Dao Children were also concerned about the war in the universe.
These Dao Children all had extraordinary backgrounds. Their closest relatives or teachers were all supreme experts in the universe. When the Sacred Race invaded the universe, these supreme experts would inevitably be involved in the war. They were in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, but they couldn't understand the situation in the universe. Naturally, they were worried.
Fifteen days …
Fourteen days …
Thirteen days …
When they were cultivating in seclusion, they felt that time was passing too slowly. Outside the cave, they felt that time was flying. Half a month passed in the blink of an eye.
Five days …
Three days …
On the third to last day of the trial.
A strange phenomenon finally appeared above the Time Valley!
The Qi of Heaven and Earth began to fluctuate violently …
"He broke through!" Ji Luoxue's phoenix eyes revealed a trace of excitement.
"Amitabha, it's indeed the power of the Transformation Realm that caused the resonance. Luo Zheng succeeded in the end." Ku Deng nodded lightly, revealing a gratified expression.
At this moment, it wasn't just the seven Dao Children who were paying attention to Luo Zheng …
The Dragon Bird Clan was also paying close attention. Although they didn't think highly of the person who had broken through last, they were curious about how large the Realm Cloud would be.
In addition to the Dragon Bird Clan, there was another pair of eyes watching this scene from an extremely high place in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. This person was Saint Jiu.
"This little guy cultivates chaos energy, which should be the embryonic form of the Divine Realm. Then Luo Zheng's breakthrough is equivalent to transforming the Divine Realm. I wonder how much energy of heaven and earth he'll attract?" Saint Jiu thought to himself.
He didn't summon his internal incarnation. Instead, he used internal vision to spy on Luo Zheng.
"Pop!"
At this moment, everyone saw a rumble come from above the Time Valley!
When the rumble sounded, it resonated with the energy of heaven and earth. A ring-shaped Realm Cloud appeared in the sky and began to spread outwards.
To be honest, the Realm Cloud didn't spread very quickly. Not to mention Hua Tianming and Xuanyuan Chenfeng, it was much slower than Lie Qianhan and Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao.
However, when the seven Dao Children and the Dragon Bird Clan saw the Realm Cloud, their faces were still filled with shock!
Realm Clouds formed by the energy of heaven and earth were generally light yellow. From Xuanyuan Chenfeng to Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao, they were all like this. However, Luo Zheng's Realm Cloud was different. It was grayish-brown!
"What's with Luo Zheng's Realm Cloud? The color is so strange," Jiang Zhengyi asked, puzzled.
"Why does Realm Cloud have different colors?" Lie Qianhan also asked, puzzled.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng stared at the slowly spreading ring with a complicated expression. "It's said that when a martial artist with the Extreme Purple Yin Body cultivates to the Realm Lord realm, the Realm Cloud they spread out will be slightly different. The Realm Cloud is light purple … but what's with Luo Zheng's grayish-brown Realm Cloud?"
In contrast, the Dragon Bird Clan was even more shocked!
There were many martial artists who had stepped into the Realm Lord realm in the Time Valley. The Dragon Bird Clan's research on Realm Clouds was much more profound than the other clans.
"How can there be someone in this world who has such a strange Realm Cloud?"
"What cultivation technique is this kid cultivating? The commotion is not small!"

Chapter 1574
When a martial artist became a Realm Lord, they would draw a solid boundary for their inner world. It was like a country drawing a boundary for itself.
The Realm Cloud was a layer of clouds formed when one broke through to the Realm Lord realm and resonated with the world's origin energy. It contained a trace of the mysteries of the world's evolution and also recorded a trace of the martial artist's comprehension of the Divine Dao.
Martial artists also recognized the Realm Cloud as a measure of a Realm Lord's potential and strength.
