The Realm Cloud that Luo Zheng had stirred up was not just a matter of color. As the layer of clouds continued to tumble, it emitted an extremely obscure aura, and the patterns contained within the Realm Cloud seemed to contain a profound mystery within them!
The martial artists present were unable to decipher this mystery, but everyone's gaze was firmly attracted by the Realm Cloud.
Facing the mystery released by the Realm Cloud, their eyes were very serious at first, but gradually they became confused. They comprehended everything, but they also didn't seem to comprehend anything.
When Xuanyuan Chenfeng became a Realm Lord, the Realm Cloud he released contained a bit of rhombus-shaped patterns. This was the symbol of the Divine Dao of Truth. If a Dao Child also cultivated the Divine Dao of Truth, they would be able to comprehend Xuanyuan Chenfeng's Realm Cloud.
When Ji Luoxue became a Realm Lord, the Five Element Growth Rings appeared in the Realm Cloud. These were patterns that only those who cultivated the Five Element Divine Dao would have …
However, the patterns in Luo Zheng's Realm Cloud were not regular. They were constantly changing, as if they were a mixture of the three thousand Great Daos of the world, but there also seemed to be nothing!
High up in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Saint Jiu was also silently watching the Realm Cloud Luo Zheng summoned. His eyes revealed an expression of disbelief.
The creatures in the Valley of Time were attracted by this ring of World Clouds because there was an unknown mystery hidden within. They couldn't decipher it, and although they didn't understand it, they were still stunned … ….
However, Saint Jiu was a true Saint. It was just that the universe he created was a bit special.
Before he became a Saint, Saint Jiu had seen something similar!
"Divine Dao General Outline … very similar, very similar!"
The so-called Divine Dao General Outline wasn't actually a book, but rather a mass of rolling brown mist. It was very similar to the Realm Cloud that Luo Zheng was summoning at this moment!
The 3,000 Divine Daos that were cultivated in the entire Divine Realm all came from this Divine Dao General Outline!
However, Luo Zheng's World Cloud still couldn't compare with the true Shinto Overarching Principles. The complexity of the Shinto Overarching Principles was far beyond the imagination of ordinary people!
If an ordinary living being accidentally saw the Divine Dao General Outline, their soul would instantly explode!
Because the information contained in the Divine Dao General Outline was too enormous, and the information that involved the origin poured into his mind like a flood. It was simply irresistible.
If one's soul wasn't strong enough, one could imagine the outcome.
Even an ordinary True Divinity would have to be extremely cautious when facing the Divine Dao General Outline. If they weren't careful, their soul would be severely injured. Of course, let alone a True Divinity, even a Saint would have to be extremely lucky in order to see it. This Divine Dao General Outline was an extremely rare opportunity in the Realm of the Gods. Ever since Saint Jiu had become a Saint, he had only seen it once!
And his Great Soul Subduing Technique had been cultivated through the Divine Dao General Outline.
In Saint Jiu's eyes, the profound principles contained in Luo Zheng's World Cloud were extremely shallow, but they had already begun to take shape. As Luo Zheng's cultivation continued to increase, they would become increasingly perfect!
"It looks like Gu Bei's idea was correct. If this boy is allowed to continue growing, he might really be able to open up a new Realm of the Gods. It all depends on whether or not Luo Xiao can last until that day …"
During this year, the nine Dao Children had all been in closed-door cultivation. They didn't know the situation in the Realm of the Gods, but Saint Jiu's information was naturally incomparably fast.
The Realm of the Great Expansion that Luo Xiao had constructed wasn't optimistic at all …
However, these Dao Children were all in closed-door cultivation, so Saint Jiu wouldn't tell this negative news to Luo Zheng or Hua Tianming. This would only increase the pressure on them and wouldn't do them any good.
When the brown World Cloud spread out, the speed at which it spread was far slower than the others, but the World Cloud was extremely solid and didn't dissipate that easily. Instead, it spread out in all directions at an incomparably steady speed.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng's World Cloud had spread out to a range of ten kilometers, but it had only taken half an incense stick's worth of time before the World Cloud had dissipated.
But now, an hour had passed, and Luo Zheng's World Cloud had only spread out to a range of two and a half kilometers. Moreover, the speed at which it spread out had greatly slowed down. The power of the World Transformation seemed to be showing signs of exhaustion.
"Only a range of two and a half kilometers?" Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao smacked her lips and said.
She worshipped Luo Zheng greatly, but under the resonance of their World Transformation Powers, all of them had spread out to a range of fifteen kilometers, while Luo Zheng's World Transformation Powers hadn't even reached a third of their World Transformation Powers. This gap was rather large.
"Hmph, so what if it's only two and a half kilometers? Can't you see what's hidden in Luo Zheng's World Cloud? I don't think you can understand it either! "Upon hearing Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao's words, Ji Luoxue was very unhappy and said sarcastically.
"As if you can understand it …" Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao replied unconvinced.
"You …" Ji Luoxue was choked by this sentence.
The size of the World Cloud's World Transformation Powers depended on the strength of the World Transformation Powers. The larger the Great Wide World in the Dantian, the stronger the World Transformation Powers would be. When others saw this scene, they could also conclude that Luo Zheng's Great Wide World was relatively small.
Of course, this was because it was compared to the few Daoists in the universe.
In fact, the World Cloud produced by a normal Realm Master originally only had a range of three to five kilometers. It was only because this group of Daoists had all kinds of monstrous talents that Luo Zheng's mediocrity was highlighted.
When the World Cloud had spread out to a range of six kilometers, it was already in a completely immobile state.
However, the looks in everyone's eyes became even stranger.
After the World Cloud stabilized, it would dissipate completely in a short period of time. However, no one was like Luo Zheng, who still tenaciously floated in the sky after it stopped spreading.
Luo Zheng was still sitting upright in the cave, completely meditating.
A layer of light screen at the border of the Chaotic Ocean gradually stabilized, forming a huge circle that enclosed the Chaotic Ocean and the Central Continent.
When ordinary warriors reached this step, their Great Wide World would have already taken shape.
However, the barrier of light at the edge of the Chaotic Sea continued to absorb the chaotic aura. At the same time, it seemed to be brewing something. During this process, tiny strands of World-transformation Force constantly appeared in the barrier of light. It was this World-transformation Force that maintained the ring of clouds above the Time Valley, preventing them from dispersing.
The light screen's demand for Chaotic Qi was extremely high. The Chaotic Ocean surged towards the light screen in all directions. Under the constant absorption, the coastline at the edge of the Central Continent within Luo Zheng's body also began to drop. This even attracted the attention of the Human Race on the continent. In Luo Zheng's inner world, some Human Race warriors on the coast were also curious. Why was the water level of the Chaotic Ocean dropping so quickly?
The World Cloud floated in the sky for a full six hours …
Converted to the time Luo Zheng had spent in the cave, it was 240 hours, 120 days!
120 days later, Luo Zheng's expression changed slightly. The light screen in his inner world suddenly began to roll out rays of light, and then an extremely strong World Transformation Powers gushed out!
"Is this the complete formation?"
After the rays of light disappeared, Luo Zheng looked at the mutated light screen. His mouth was wide open, and his eyes revealed an expression of disbelief.
As for the strong World Transformation Powers that gushed out, it was like a ferocious beast that had been released from its cage, resonating strongly with the World Origin Qi at the peak of the Time Valley!
"Zi ~"
Under this resonance, there was even a sharp cry!
It was as if someone had suddenly thrown a large stone into the water, causing huge ripples to splash out. The originally stagnant brown Realm Cloud rapidly expanded in all directions at this moment.

Chapter 1576
The boundary wall surrounded the entire Primal Chaos Sea, forming a circular "wall" that surrounded Luo Zheng's inner world …
Every set distance, a golden Sanskrit character would be embedded on the boundary wall.
If a living being wandered to the edge of the Primal Chaos Sea, they would see a golden belt of light embedded at the end of this world.
When all the golden Sanskrit characters were in place, Luo Zheng's inner world could be considered to have truly stabilized, and he could be considered to have truly stepped into the ranks of Realm Lords!
As for the Forbidden Land of God Refinement …
Countless martial artists witnessed a miraculous scene today.
Because this world was built into a huge cave, the space inside was not open. There were no clouds inside, so naturally, there was no rain or snow.
But today, the natives of the forbidden land saw the existence of "clouds" for the first time.
The birth of every golden Sanskrit character could stimulate a ring of boundary clouds to spread out. When they overlapped, they were like rings of flowers in the sky, creating a spectacular scene.
The living beings outside the Time Valley didn't recognize that these were "boundary clouds".
Although some people were suspicious when they saw these things that were similar to boundary clouds, there couldn't be so many of them, and the range couldn't be so wide. The largest ring of boundary clouds had already circled around the Forbidden Land of God Refinement!
As for the martial artists in the Time Valley, they were speechless.
Even the two Dragon Bird clansmen who were quarreling before were now staring at each other. They were completely unable to evaluate the boundary clouds in the sky. They even started to doubt whether boundary clouds could really be used as a benchmark to measure Realm Lords.
If they could, then what was going on with so many boundary clouds in front of them …
Not long after, the cave Luo Zheng was in finally opened, and a figure shot out from within. He hovered above the Time Valley for a moment, and with a sweep of his gaze, he saw Hua Tianming and the others. His body suddenly floated down and landed in front of them.
"Luo Zheng, congratulations," Ji Luoxue said with a bright smile.
"You've finally broken through to Realm Lord. Brother Luo Zheng, you're the last one!" Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao said with a smile.
Three days before the deadline, the last Dao Child had also stepped into the Sector Lord Realm. Their goal in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement had basically been achieved. The Dao Children naturally congratulated Luo Zheng.
As for Xuanyuan Chenfeng, he stared blankly for quite a while before going over to congratulate him … He had already been stimulated by Luo Zheng in this way several times, but clearly, he still hadn't adapted to this feeling. Thinking about it, he was still worried whether Luo Zheng's boundary cloud could break through 20 li. If Luo Zheng surpassed him and reached 30 li, or even 40 or 50 li, Xuanyuan Chenfeng would still be able to accept it!
But in the end, such a ridiculous result had appeared. He couldn't understand it at all. This guy was always able to subvert his worldview at the right time. It was simply too much.
Hua Tianming greeted him with a smile. "The commotion caused by Brother Luo Zheng is probably comparable to that of a Saint."
Luo Zheng stared blankly. "What do you mean, Brother Tianming?"
"It's said that when a Saint steps into the Saint Realm, the Wall of Sighs that forms the universe will also trigger a large amount of boundary energy. At that time, the boundary cloud will also be drawn out. I'm afraid it's just as majestic, but I think it's nothing more than that, right?" Hua Tianming said with a smile.
As one's cultivation increased, the number of boundaries in one's inner world would increase, and the range would also increase. In the end, it would form a standard universe.
For example, the "Universe of Great Expansion" was a standard universe. It was divided into the Great Thousand World and the Great World. There were also a few subsidiary planes, such as the Extreme Purple Realm or the True Dragon Realm.
At the same time, there were also some non-standard universes. The Forbidden Land of God Refinement that everyone was in was an example.
Just as he finished speaking, an old man's voice suddenly sounded in everyone's ears. "Hehe, Luo Zheng's boundary energy is indeed far beyond ordinary people, but in the end, it still can't compare to a Saint's universe. However, Luo Zheng is indeed exceptionally shocking. Truly unexpected!"
Luo Zheng and Hua Tianming were very familiar with this voice. Luo Xi Xuan's expression was also calm when she heard this voice. The three of them had all seen Saint Jiu before, so they naturally remembered this voice. However, Xuanyuan Chenfeng, Lie Qianhan, Ji Luoxue, and the others had never seen Saint Jiu's true eyes. They didn't even know this person existed. Saint Jiu had guided their cultivation several times, and he had also secretly asked his spokesperson, the Prophet of the Six Great Cultivation Holy Lands, to act. He himself had never shown up.
"Who is it?" Xuanyuan Chenfeng asked coldly.
Ji Luoxue and the others also looked around, revealing bewildered expressions …
They had just become Realm Masters and had a new keen perception of this world. The person who had spoken seemed to be right next to them, but they hadn't sensed him at all. Naturally, they were very vigilant.
"Hehe." Saint Jiu didn't answer their questions.
Luo Zheng and Hua Tianming also didn't explain anything. Saint Jiu's appearance now meant that he had his own plans.
Not long after, someone from the Dragon Bird Clan appeared and invited the nine Dao Children to meet the Dragon Bird Clan's Prophet.
Xuanyuan Chenfeng and the others were vigilant and originally refused, but Luo Zheng, Hua Tianming, and Luo Xi Xuan expressed that there was no problem, so the Dao Children were persuaded to meet the "Prophet" together.
Inside the tent, there was a cup of green tea in front of each of the nine Dao Children, emitting a lingering tea fragrance.
When Ji Luoxue and the others understood that the old man in front of them was the master of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, they suddenly understood everything they had experienced in the past three years. Almost all the good luck they had in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement had been given to them by this person!
In front of such a Saint, even the most arrogant Xuanyuan Chenfeng didn't have a trace of arrogance. His previous pride came from his own talent and family background. He didn't know Luo Zheng's background. No matter how amazing Luo Zheng's talent was, his background and background were far inferior to Xuanyuan Chenfeng's. After all, his father was one of the top Celestial Venerables in the universe …
But in front of this old man, his father, the Heavenly Lord of Original Sin, was nothing. The old man was a Saint. He couldn't even begin to imagine the true power of a Saint and his status in the universe. It was said that it was extremely difficult for ordinary True Gods to even meet him.
After exchanging some pleasantries, Saint Jiu calmly said, "Three years have passed. It's time for you to return to the Great Yan Universe."
In these past three years, they had been worried about their families, their loved ones, and the situation in the universe.
They didn't have a good channel to understand the problems in the universe, so they were naturally worried. Hearing Saint Jiu's announcement, Ji Luoxue was the first to ask, "Saint, what is the situation in the Great Yan Universe?"
"The situation is not good. It is said that the human race has already lost three Celestial Venerables," Saint Jiu replied.
Saint Jiu hadn't been to the Great Yan Universe. In this delicate period of time, it was impossible for him to enter the Great Yan Universe. He could only help Luo Zheng and the others in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
If Saint Jiu could freely enter and exit the Realm of the Gods, Luo Xiao wouldn't have to go through so much trouble to raise these Dao Children.
In order to raise a successor, Luo Xiao only needed one Luo Zheng.
Saint Jiu's information had been gathered from the Realm of the Gods. The war between the Great Yan Universe and the Great Yan Universe had also received attention in the Realm of the Gods. The Sacred Halls had also constantly spread the news, so it wasn't difficult to obtain this information.
More than two years had passed since the Sacred Race had declared war …
The speed at which the Sacred Race had expanded had already surpassed many people's imaginations. The most crucial point was that the Demon Night Clan had sent troops to assist the Sacred Race!
Because of the Demon Night Clan's participation, many human factions had been attacked from both sides!
"I haven't obtained any specific information. After all, this is a matter between Mu Haiji and Luo Xiao. The other Saints have no reason to interfere, so I can't attack Mu Haiji right now." Saint Jiu was a member of the Sacred Halls, and Luo Xiao had also been one in the past. Now that Luo Xiao was on the verge of death, Saint Jiu couldn't drag himself into this.
"Then what should we do?" Lie Qianhan anxiously asked. The human race originally only had ten or twenty Celestial Venerables. He was afraid that the Lie Family Ancestor had died.

Chapter 1577
In Saint Jiu's opinion, the probability of these Daoists condensing Godhood in a short period of time was simply too low …
The heavy burden of saving the Great Evolution Universe still fell on Luo Zheng, Hua Tianming, and Luo Xi Xuan.
Before Luo Xiao's incarnation disappeared, Luo Zheng was the most important person in his plans. He had chosen a Vast Thousand Realms, arranged everything in the Vast Thousand Realms, and then created the Luo Family. He had planned a path for Luo Zheng's growth, and even poured in his father-son relationship.
As for Hua Tianming and Luo Xi Xuan, Luo Xiao had also spent a lot of effort on them. At the very least, they had grasped things that ordinary martial artists did not have to a certain extent. For example, Hua Tianming had a portion of the Weapon Grandmaster's inheritance, while Luo Xi Xuan had the inheritance of "transcending life forms".
If the three of them occupied the first three positions, then Xuanyuan Chenfeng's importance was ranked fourth. After all, the Xuanyuan Family was once the strongest among Luo Xiao's disciples.
"Saint Jiu, why haven't I seen Xi Youqin?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
Ever since entering the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Luo Zheng had been somewhat worried about Xi Youqin. Although she was not weak, her temper would often attract a lot of trouble …
Saint Jiu's face revealed a trace of helplessness. "Xi Youqin is still somewhere in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement."
"What happened to her?" Luo Zheng noticed the Saint's expression and asked with concern.
Saint Jiu was a genuine Saint. Ordinary questions would not cause him to reveal such an expression. It seemed that Xi Youqin had indeed caused him quite a bit of trouble …
"Hehe, it doesn't count …"
The ten Daoists selected by Luo Xiao each had their own roles. Luo Zheng was the main character of his life, an indispensable existence. Hua Tianming was the assassin, the one who could disregard everything and deliver the most fatal blow. Luo Xi Xuan had other uses.
As for Xuanyuan Chenfeng, he was Luo Zheng's candidate!
No matter how meticulous Luo Xiao's plan was, when it was executed, there would always be some accidents. If Luo Zheng fell, then the one to replace Luo Zheng would be Xuanyuan Chenfeng!
Of course, Luo Xiao did not reveal the division of responsibilities of these Daoists to the people of the Grand Evolution Universe. Even his disciple, Xingwei, did not know. Only Saint Jiu and Gu Bei knew.
If this news were to spread to his disciples in advance, then for the sake of his son, the Lord of Original Sin would do everything possible to kill Luo Zheng so that Xuanyuan Chenfeng could replace Luo Zheng.
Among the ten Daoists, Xi Youqin's role was the most special. According to Luo Xiao's request, Sage Jiu had to help Xi Youqin cultivate to the Realm Lord level. At the same time, he had to help Xi Youqin construct the Extreme Purple Realm within her body. However, he had yet to succeed.
The biggest reason was that Xi Youqin did not cooperate …
As a dignified Great Saint, he could not even handle a little girl. Sage Jiu felt that he had lost face. Sometimes, he could not help but want to directly seize Xi Youqin's soul and tamper with her memories. However, in the end, he restrained himself.
Xi Youqin's personality was incomparably stubborn. When she had gone to the Extreme Purple Realm, Indefinite Old Man had taken great care of her and guided her to stimulate her potential. How could Sage Jiu be in the mood to coax her?
"But it doesn't matter. Soon, I will send her back to the Grand Evolution Universe. There is no need to worry," Sage Jiu replied.
With Sage Jiu's promise, Luo Zheng no longer bothered with this issue.
The Daoists were also anxious to return. They were also worried about the situation in the Grand Evolution Universe …
Before sending off the Daoists, Sage Jiu said with a serious expression, "Sage Jiu has poured countless efforts into the ten of you. In theory, each of you has the qualifications to become a True God. It is only a matter of time. In the Realm of the Gods, even the children of Saints may not receive such treatment. If you can help the Grand Evolution Universe survive this crisis, in the future … we will meet again in the Realm of the Gods!"
When the Daoists saw Sage Jiu's serious expression, they all nodded in unison.
The Demi-Immortal Clan had prepared for a long time to enter the Forbidden Land of God Refinement from the Grand Evolution Universe. However, for Sage Jiu, this was a piece of cake.
…
…
In the Grand Evolution Universe …
A bright yellow crack appeared in the sky, as if a monster had opened its mouth in the void.
A figure suddenly flashed through the crack and plunged downwards!
"Boom!"
Luo Zheng heavily smashed into a tall mountain.
His body was like a giant axe. When he smashed down, he directly split the tall mountain. His entire body left a 300-meter-wide crack on the mountain's surface before sinking deep into the ground.
It was currently raining. The rainstorm, which carried a trace of a fishy smell, poured into the mountain from all directions. It instantly turned into an underground lake and submerged Luo Zheng.
"Huala!"
Luo Zheng's figure burst out from the lake and quickly returned to the ground. When he raised his head, his eyes revealed a trace of astonishment.
Which great world did he enter? The clouds in the sky were actually blood-red, as if they were formed by the convergence of countless amounts of blood. The distant mountains and plains were also blood-red, as if they had been soaked in blood.
The last time he had been transported to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, he had also been transported to a strange place of birth. He didn't expect that his return this time wouldn't go smoothly either …
No, entering that nameless place of birth in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was deliberately done by Sage Jiu. This time, Sage Jiu had personally transported him back, so there was no reason for him to leave him in an inexplicable place.
It seemed that an accident had occurred somewhere to cause such a deviation.
He stretched out his finger and caught a drop of bright red rain. He brought the raindrop to his nose and sniffed it. A familiar smell of blood wafted over.
"It's the smell of human blood," Luo Zheng's eyes flashed and he frowned.
The situation in the universe seemed to be worse than he had imagined. He didn't know which great world he had fallen into.
Just as this question arose in his mind, a battleship shot through the torrential rain not too far away. This battleship was pitch black and its surface was covered in sharp spikes. From time to time, one could even hear the sound of thunder surging from the battleship. This battleship was directly heading towards Luo Zheng, clearly having discovered the disturbance here.
Luo Zheng simply floated in the air, motionless. He didn't know which great force this battleship belonged to, but no matter which great force it belonged to, Luo Zheng had a way to obtain some information, so at this moment he was waiting for the battleship to approach.
His guess was correct.
This battleship had indeed noticed the violent disturbance caused by Luo Zheng's teleportation and had come to investigate.
And this battleship belonged to the Sacred Race.
"The blood of these humans is really stinking. I wonder how long Sir will be able to last," the leading Sacred Race cultivator grumbled.
"It's enough to just crush these little ants to death. Why did he have to spill their blood all over the sky? Just looking at it makes one panic …"
"Don't complain. Sir must have his reasons for doing this!"
"What happened just now? Why did he split apart this mountain in front of us?"
Although something unexpected had occurred here, the several Sacred Race cultivators weren't too vigilant. After all, ever since the saints stepped into the Great Expansion Universe, they had been unstoppable. No one had been able to stop their footsteps. In front of the powerful saints, the several great human influences had no ability to resist at all!
The saints had easily swept through the entire universe. From the start of the war until now, they had never lost a single battle!
"There's a human!"
"Hehe, is that human an idiot? He doesn't even know how to run away. When did the humans become so obedient? "
Several Sacred Race martial artists laughed.
One of the saint martial artists sized up Luo Zheng. This saint martial artist was a bit cautious. "Everyone be careful. That fellow … seems to be a World Lord!"
The saints on this black battleship all had a Divine Transformation realm cultivation. Facing a World Lord was a bit difficult.
"How is this possible!? There's a World Lord that dares to enter this place? "A saint martial artist asked, puzzled.
The great world they were in had already been occupied by the saints. If a human World Lord were to enter this place, that would be the same as sending themselves to their deaths. The several saint martial artists glanced at each other in dismay.
But they didn't fear Luo Zheng at all. From top to bottom, the entire saint race had already lost all sense of awe towards humans.
"Perhaps it's another one that defected?"
In these past three years, from Empyreans to World Lords to ordinary martial artists, the human race had been forced back again and again. Now, there were already several hundred World Lords in the Grand Evolution Universe who had chosen to 'defect', betraying their own race and joining the saints …

Chapter 1579
Luo Zheng still raised his hand, maintaining his previous posture.
The remaining six Saint Race cultivators all stared at Luo Zheng in amazement …
The Saint Race was like a hot knife through butter in the Great Evolution Universe. The human race didn't dare to confront them head-on, and the few times they did encounter them they were defeated. Moreover, this team of theirs had killed a human World Lord before!
In this seven-person team, three of them were proficient in Soul Type attacks, which were the "Yuan Spirit Heart Demons" they had released before. The other three used the "Holy Demon Shackles" to completely bind their opponents, while the last person, who was the leader of this team, used the "Saint Qi Single Slash" to kill his opponent.
In the Saint Race's teams, there were generally seven people in a team, but when the seven of them worked together, their combat strength far surpassed those of the same realm.
This series of methods was used against masters, but if they were facing cultivators of the same realm or a large number of low level cultivators, the seven of them would naturally use a different set of tactics!
The "Elemental Spirit Heart Devil" released by the three of them together was not easy to resist. This was not a pure soul attack, but a soul attack that was mixed with some kind of emotion. It could suppress the opponent's soul and also shake the opponent's mind.
Even if a World Lord's soul was powerful and their mind was stable, their mind would still go blank for a short period of time and they would be unable to resist.
If it were the Luo Zheng from before he entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, he would likely be greatly affected.
Just like when he cultivated the Yi God Sword and comprehended the Emotion Severing Divine Dao, Luo Zheng was unable to withstand the massive amount of Infernal Qi and his entire being would become violent and bloodthirsty …
But the Yuan He Clan's Heart Refining Pagoda was no trivial matter. Luo Zheng's heart of martial arts seemed to be plated with a layer of iron, so how could he be so easily affected?
So when Luo Zheng reacted and counterattacked, he easily killed one of the Saint Race cultivators!
The several Saint Race cultivators were still in a daze, obviously unable to react to the fact that their teammate had been killed.
Luo Zheng still had a faint smile on his face. In the eyes of others, this smile was very warm, as if he were a polite gentleman, but in the eyes of these Saint Race cultivators, Luo Zheng's image was as terrifying as a demon! That smile was filled with malice.
It had been a long time since the Sacred Race felt this fear. They had never faced a race that could surpass them …
Seeing Luo Zheng float towards one of the saint race martial artists, the six people present suddenly realized that this fellow was an existence they couldn't afford to provoke.
"Retreat!"
"Disperse! This person's strength cannot be underestimated! "
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …"
Before they could react, Luo Zheng's originally slow movements had completely changed. His figure flashed slightly, and one of the warriors didn't even have time to turn around before Luo Zheng's hand gently brushed past him.
His finger brushed against the top of the man's head, and a small imprint appeared on the top of his head. The top of his head also caved in.
The Saint Race martial artist's eyes widened as he unwillingly fell down. He crashed into the ground and was quickly covered by a rain of blood...
Luo Zheng didn't stop at all.
There were many times when he would hesitate. In the words of a gentleman, where it was possible to forgive someone, one should forgive them. But this didn't mean that Luo Zheng was a coward.
Those that weren't part of his race were bound to have different hearts. Thus, when facing a race that wasn't part of his race, Luo Zheng didn't feel the slightest bit of pity …
The speed at which these saint martial artists fled wasn't slow at all. Moreover, their teamwork was extremely tight. The remaining five people all fled in different directions. Like this, as long as Luo Zheng chased down one of them, the other four would be able to open up a considerable distance between them and Luo Zheng!
However, after Luo Zheng became a World Lord, his speed had increased by leaps and bounds!
His eyes locked onto a yellow-clothed martial artist and he chased after him.
The yellow-clothed saint race martial artist felt a strong wind blowing behind him and a bitter expression bloomed on his face. There were clearly four other people here, so why was this fellow chasing after him?
But he didn't have time to think about this anymore.
As a Divine Transformation realm martial artist, if he were to face Luo Zheng alone, he had no chance of victory at all …
The truth was that according to the saint race's strategy, when they encountered a powerhouse that was trying to escape, everyone had to scatter. At the very least, the enemy couldn't kill everyone and the majority would be able to escape.
But the saint race martial artist that was being chased suddenly changed his mind. He didn't choose to draw Luo Zheng away, but instead turned around and began to fight back!
Running away meant death, so he might as well fight with everything he had.
This was the nature of many life forms. Even a rabbit would turn around and bite if it was forced into a corner …
"Exploding Essence Art!"
"Hua!"
True essence poured out from the saint race martial artist's body, instantly exploding around him.
This was an arcane skill that burned true essence.
As his true essence violently combusted, it rapidly expanded, turning into spikes that spread out from his body. This saint martial artist suddenly turned into a hedgehog. All of the spikes shot out from his body, making it seem as if countless spears had appeared on his body.
If the martial artist chasing after him didn't react in time, they would be riddled with holes by these spikes.
Luo Zheng's reaction was naturally incomparably fast. He had more than enough time to dodge these spikes, but he didn't need to dodge at all!
He welcomed these spikes head-on. By relying on his mortal body, he smashed apart these spikes formed from burning true essence …
Ka ka ka ka …
With the sound of breaking spikes, Luo Zheng was only a foot away from the saint race martial artist. Then, Luo Zheng's right hand flashed out like a ghost, gently brushing the back of the saint race martial artist's head as if he were brushing off dust from his body.
With this brush, Luo Zheng didn't bother looking at the saint race martial artist a second time. He turned into an arc in the air, continuing to chase after the next saint race martial artist.
The saint race martial artist that was gently brushed by Luo Zheng's finger suddenly shook. He stood in the air for a single breath of time, then like a leaf without a foundation, he drifted downwards …
"Third …"
Turning around, Luo Zheng threw himself at the fourth saint race martial artist.
As the target that Luo Zheng had locked onto saw this not too far away, it was like an earthquake had struck his heart, tumbling around in his chest. As he saw Luo Zheng's eyes lock onto him and their gazes meet, he was immediately frightened out of his wits. He lowered his head and hurriedly flew away.
Although Luo Zheng had spent two or three breaths of time chasing after the third saint martial artist, his figure gently floated through the air. In the blink of an eye, he crossed dozens of miles and arrived in front of the fourth saint race martial artist.
Besides the Yuanhe Clan, Luo Zheng had also gone to Annihilation Valley, eagerly absorbing Saint Jiu's comprehensions of the Laws.
After becoming a World Lord, one could use greater teleportation techniques.
However, at this stage, Luo Zheng's greater teleportation techniques were unable to instantly cross several great worlds like an Empyrean. Even so, moving hundreds or thousands of miles with a single step wasn't difficult for him. Moreover, these Divine Transformation realm martial artists were able to flee hundreds of miles in a short period of time. This was already an extremely good speed!
"You, you … who are you? How can a human World Lord be so strong!? "The saint race martial artist realized that resistance was futile and asked.
"You don't need to know the reason." Luo Zheng faintly smiled. His ghostly figure flashed past the martial artist once more. With a flick of his finger, a blazing white light bloomed beneath his feet and he vanished from where he stood. He began to chase after the fifth saint race martial artist …
Then the sixth, the seventh …
As for the final saint race martial artist, Luo Zheng didn't kill him.
This person was the saint race martial artist that had been looking down on Luo Zheng from atop the black warship. As the captain of this squad, he was the strongest. When the other six team members trapped the other powerhouses, the strongest attack would also be used by him!
Because it took Luo Zheng some time to kill the first six people, he had covered a distance of 200 miles in this short period of time.
As he ran, he had nearly squeezed out all of his strength to the limit.
Compared to the other saint race martial artists, he was the smartest. As soon as he realized that he couldn't defeat Luo Zheng, he didn't turn around, nor did he have any distracting thoughts in his mind. At this moment, the only thought in his mind was to escape!
This saint race martial artist wasn't too sure if his teammates were dead or alive.
But at least he was safe!
"Escaping from death, huff huff …"
Just as this thought of rejoicing appeared in his mind, he heard a voice that scared him out of his wits.

Chapter 1580
As his voice fell, Luo Zheng's figure stepped out from the void, hovering above the sacred race martial artist's head as he looked down …
That powerful aura was like a sovereign descending upon the world, but it also contained an aura of supremacy!
The sacred race martial artists boasted of their noble bloodlines. In the eyes of the sacred race, all other races were equal.
Whether it was the human race or other intelligent life forms, they were no different from pigs, dogs, ants, or other living beings!
Only the sacred race was different. Only the sacred race had the qualifications to stand above all other races!
In the vast universe, any life form, any race, could only crawl beneath the feet of the sacred race, relying on the sacred race's charity and captivity to live their lives.
Had this sacred race martial artist felt such an aura before?
Under the coercion of this aura, this sacred race martial artist suddenly felt incomparably small, as if he couldn't help but submit.
But now, he was facing a human. Because of the glory of his bloodline, this sacred race martial artist instinctively resisted. How could he casually submit to a human?
"What is your name? I can spare your life." Luo Zheng's cold voice floated out once more. The pressure of his soul suddenly doubled, and a faint golden light flashed in Luo Zheng's eyes.
The Forbidden Land of God Refinement was one of the six great cultivation Holy Lands.
The Dragon Bird Clan cultivated in the Valley of Time, the Yuanhe Clan cultivated in the Heart Refining Pagoda, the Mountain Shattering Clan cultivated in the Source of Strength, the Myriad Elephant Clan cultivated in the Wind Destroying Valley, and the Sky Dome Clan cultivated in the Origin and End True Essence …
In addition, there was also the Soul Imprint Clan's Divine Soul Ancestral Land, a place where they cultivated the Divine Soul.
Among the three cultivation systems of a martial artist, the Divine Soul was second only to the Body Tempering and True Cultivation. It was the third fulcrum that supported a martial artist, so it was naturally a very important link.
Of the ten Dao Children, seven had entered the Divine Soul Ancestral Land. Among them, Lie Qianhan, Jiang Zhengyi, and Dugu Jian Xiaoxiao had missed the opportunity to enter the Divine Soul Ancestral Land due to time constraints. They hadn't been able to enter to cultivate and had been directly brought to the Valley of Time.
Apart from the Sky Vault Clan, Luo Zheng also had extraordinary gains in the Five Great Cultivation Holy Lands.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's soul was shining with a golden brilliance. On the surface of his translucent soul, there were arc-shaped golden seals. This was the 'Sovereign Soul Seal' of the Divine Soul Ancestral Land. The one who had such a seal would release a soul pressure that had the aura of an emperor. It would make people feel small and insignificant. It could even force weaker opponents to submit!
In this round of the Great Yan Universe, there weren't many martial artists who had cultivated the Gold Battle Soul. Only Heavenly Venerables and a small portion of Great Sector Lords had cultivated it.
At this moment, Luo Zheng released the power of pressure through the Golden Fighting Soul, and supplemented by the "Emperor Soul Engraving", the effect could be imagined.
Therefore, when he asked this question, the Sacred Race martialist shuddered and subconsciously answered, "My, my name is Long Rong!"
"Stop," Luo Zheng ordered again.
Luo Zheng could have forced the other party to submit, but at this moment, he only used words to make the other party submit. Luo Zheng also wanted to test the ability he had obtained in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
Running away was almost an instinct of the Sacred Race martialists. Luo Zheng's words seemed to have achieved the "law of the land". It made the Sacred Race martialist's heart stop resisting his fear!
Long Rong only looked at Luo Zheng with a pair of frightened eyes. He no longer had any thoughts of resistance in his heart.
"Which Great Realm is this?" Luo Zheng stared at Long Rong and asked.
Long Rong's eyes flickered with hesitation, but he felt his soul being completely suppressed by Luo Zheng. It was as if he was controlled. He couldn't help but answer, "This is the Seven Extreme Realm …"
"Seven Extreme Realm?" Luo Zheng was slightly stunned.
The Seven Extreme Realm was a border Great Realm of the Human Dao Alliance. This border Great Realm bordered the border of Deities No Thought.
When Luo Zheng left the Great Yan Universe, the Sacred Race had only occupied three Great Realms of the Tang Chen Race. If the Sacred Race fought all the way to the Seven Extreme Realm, wouldn't they swallow the entire Human Dao Alliance?
The situation was worse than Luo Zheng imagined.
In Luo Zheng's opinion, no matter how strong the Sacred Race was, it was impossible for them to completely swallow the thousands of Great Realms of the Human Dao Alliance in a short three years. After all, every Great Realm was vast and boundless. Moreover, the Celestial Venerables of the universe and Startail couldn't possibly watch from the sidelines.
Then what about Celestial Venerate Oracle?
Celestial Venerate Cloudfox … and Cloudfall!
Luo Zheng's thoughts spun rapidly. He stared at Long Rong and asked, "What's the current situation in the Great Yan Universe?"
As soon as he finished speaking, a golden light flickered in his eyes. The emperor's aura in the Sovereign Spirit Seal surged out like a tide. Long Rong did not resist at all. His eyes were sunken, and his teeth were chattering. He could not withstand such a strong pressure, but he could not even speak.
When he saw this, Luo Zheng could only withdraw a portion of his soul pressure and continue asking, "What's the situation?"
"Our Sacred Race … the Sacred Race has dismembered the Human Dao Alliance and killed three human Celestial Venerables. The elites of the Human Dao Alliance have retreated to Deities No Thought …" Long Rong hurriedly said.
Luo Zheng nodded. He didn't think this person would lie to him. "What about the Fiend Night Clan?"
"They've already allied with our Sacred Race. They originally planned to declare war on Deities No Thought, but they've been entangled by demons," Long Rong continued.
"They've been entangled by demons?"
Luo Zheng's eyes revealed surprise.
At the end of the day, people liked beautiful things. Luo Zheng had always had a good impression of the Demon Night clan, but he had never thought that the Demon Night clan was actually a spy planted by Mu Haiji in the Universe of Great Evolution. Their goal was the same as the Ancient Shamans, which was to overthrow the entire Universe of Great Evolution.
Luo Zheng's troubles with the demons had never ceased in the lower realms. Although the demons were brave and fierce, they were also crude and cruel. They'd made Luo Zheng their enemy several times. Who would've thought that the demons would actually stop the Fiend Night Clan this time and help the human race? This truly surprised Luo Zheng.
This was the general situation in the Great Yan Universe. Luo Zheng had a rough idea of what was going on now.
He then stared at Long Rong and asked, "What's with the blood rain here?"
"Blood rain … blood rain …" Long Rong hesitated again. He actually stuttered and refused to speak.
"It's fine if you don't want to speak. I have many ways to make you speak, such as … soulsearching," said Luo Zheng indifferently.
If Luo Zheng wanted to obtain this information, he didn't have to go through so much trouble. He could've just soulsearched Long Rong's mind. However, he still wanted to inspect Long Rong's cultivation results.
Long Rong's face immediately paled. Seeing Luo Zheng's decisive expression, he didn't think he was joking.
"The blood rain is human blood. In order to prevent the Human Alliance from taking back this place, we collected the human blood and used the Blood Curse Art to pollute this land …" Long Rong stuttered. He didn't want to answer not because it involved secrets, but because the sacred race's methods were too cruel. He was afraid that Luo Zheng would kill him in a fit of anger.
"How many people did you need to kill to get so much blood?" Luo Zheng asked as he looked at the blood rain falling from the sky.
"A world … a world's worth of humans," Long Rong carefully looked at Luo Zheng.
"Do mortals count as well?" asked Luo Zheng.
Long Rong nodded.
Luo Zheng's pupils slowly rotated, a sharp glint escaping from his pupils. It was as if two inch-long sharp knives were embedded in his pupils. A formless anger rushed into his mind!
Countless races fought in the universe. The lives of martial artists were like grass.
However, there was a bottom line between martial artists. They rarely harmed mortals!
In the eyes of mortals, the world of martial artists was an illusory world. The martial artists flying around were immortals in their eyes!
The battles between martial artists were between immortals and immortals. They had little to do with mortals.
Even when the battle between Gods No Thought and the Human Dao Alliance was at its fiercest, some of the mortal kingdoms in the major realms were still living peaceful lives. Only when Celestial Venerate level powerhouses clashed and tore apart an entire major realm would the mortal kingdoms be affected.
This was a bottom line issue. Even the most brutal demon race wouldn't cross this bottom line. This was because the demon race also had mortals who didn't cultivate …
However, the sacred race was different.
There was no such bottom line in their race. Slaughtering billions or hundreds of billions of mortals was no different from slaughtering billions or billions of pigs and dogs.

Chapter 1582
This Blood Curse Art was another method of using infernal energy.
Infernal energy was a type of curse power, but the Great Expansion Universe didn't have a systematic method of using infernal energy.
Chen Huangyi, who had taught Luo Zheng the Yi God Sword, had developed a method of using infernal energy to suppress one's emotions and cultivate the Severing Emotions Divine Dao.
In comparison, the saints were far more skilled in using infernal energy.
They could freely transfer the curses within the infernal energy …
A single life form's curse power before death was miniscule. But when a large number of curses were added together, it was terrifying.
This Blood Curse Art only targeted races of the same origin.
For instance, a Blood Curse Art created with human blood was only effective on humans. It had no effect on other races or other life forms.
Thus, once the Blood Curse Art was successfully released, the Blood Curse Art created with human blood would completely target humans. As for the saints, demons, demons, and even various vicious beasts, it wouldn't affect them …
Only after a human reached the Empyrean realm would they no longer fear the Blood Curse Art. Even World Lords would be affected. If they stayed in an area covered by the Blood Curse Art for too long, even World Lords would be corroded!
Now that the saints had conquered a great world, they would immediately create the Blood Curse Art. Like this, they wouldn't have to worry about the humans taking over, unless the other side used an Empyrean to remove the Blood Curse Art.
But in this situation, the Empyreans of the Great Expansion Universe were all hidden within the Empyrean Race. If they dared to come alone, they wouldn't be able to return. After all, the humans had already lost three Empyreans. With each death of an Empyrean, the scales of victory tilted in the saints' favor.
This was a step-by-step strategy.
"Shua!"
The whip suddenly lashed down like a venomous snake swooping down.
If the whip really hit, the old woman and the ponytailed girl would be turned into a bloody mist.
The surrounding mortals all had cold and tired expressions. Not a single one of them dared to help.
In the eyes of these humans, the saints were demonic existences. Anyone that dared to resist would be crushed to death like an ant.
Four hours ago, over a hundred people were shouting to rest. But, the saints didn't care at all and directly lashed out with the whip.
As the whip swept out, it was like a sharp knife, directly splitting over a hundred people in half, causing them to miserably fall into a pool of blood.
From then on, everyone understood that they shouldn't try to beg the saints for mercy. Doing so would only make them die faster.
But at this moment, a figure wearing a golden dragon robe suddenly flashed out, grabbed the little girl's arm, and suddenly rolled to the side!
Just as the little girl left her original spot, the whip landed heavily on the old woman's body.
The old woman's frail body was instantly pulverized by the whip. She turned into a cloud of blood mist and disappeared before everyone's eyes.
"King … King …"
When the little girl saw the person who had saved her, she was also stunned.
The middle-aged man in a yellow robe nodded at the little girl in his arms. He raised his head and said in a clear voice, "Lord Sacred Clan, I will lead my subjects and continue on our way. Please forgive me, Lord!"
The group of over 300,000 mortals came from the country of Xi, and this yellow-robed man was the king of the country of Xi. He was also the only Divine Reflection warrior in the country.
The country of Xi had been developing well for the past few years, and they had even established two second grade sects. The king of Xi was the Sect Chief of one of the second grade sects.
Facing the saints king of the saints, the king of a small country was completely helpless.
He knew that the saints wanted to send his people to the Seven Absolutes Immortal City for blood sacrifice. But, he was powerless against the saints, and could only cooperate with them as they migrated to the Seven Absolutes Immortal City.
In the process of migration alone, more than 100,000 of the 500,000 citizens of the State of Xi had perished. A small portion of them had been killed by the Saint Clan, while the majority had died from exhaustion.
"Forgive me? I want you to forgive me! What if you waste my time? "The Divine Core saint stared at the king of Xi and said with a cold voice.
The king of Xi was extremely angry, but he knew that these few hundred thousand mortals didn't even have the right to fight for their lives. These saints were driving these few hundred thousand mortals like they were herding a herd of sheep.
"I'll supervise my people and ask them to speed up." The king of Xi promised.
"Hmph! That's good! "
In the eyes of the Saint Race cultivators, the King of the State of Xi was also nothing more than an insignificant ant. However, these hundreds of thousands of people were only able to travel smoothly because of the King of Xi's constant deployment. Without this fellow, the speed of their journey would have been much slower! Many more people would die along the way.
In fact, the saints didn't want too many mortals to die. After all, the reason they were rushing these mortals to the Seven Absolutes Immortal City was to squeeze out their blood. If more than half of them died along the way, the losses would outweigh the gains.
The king of Xi sighed, then he said to a minister standing next to him helplessly. "Pass down my order, ask everyone to speed up."
That official was in his seventies. Although he was also a martial artist, his body was not as good as it used to be. Even a young man in his prime would find it unbearable to travel forty miles a day, let alone an old man like him.
"But my king … they can't hold on any longer!" The minister said with grief and indignation.
Along the way, many mortals had died from exhaustion. Wasn't this just pushing them to their deaths?
"We have no choice, just bear with it for a little longer." The king of Xi said with a deep voice.
"Bear with it? Until when? Send us to the Seven Absolutes Immortal City and let the saints suck our blood dry? "The man who spoke was a general from Xi.
This general was a peak Xiantian warrior, and he had a fiery temper. After enduring for so long, he couldn't bear it any longer!
"You're right! We're still going to die! "
"Since we're going to die anyway, why don't we just fight them!"
"So what if we die? I'd rather everyone die more comfortably!"
Humans were not lambs waiting to be slaughtered. Lambs didn't know their own fate, so they would be very obedient until the very last moment.
If they knew that they would die if they continued, then there would be no need to continue. Since they were going to die anyway, why should they contribute to the saints?
The rebellious emotions that had been suppressed for so long began to spread, and some low level warriors and mortals began to explode!
As for the little girl with the ponytail, she buried her head in the king's chest, not lifting it up at all. Her grandmother had died right in front of her, and to a child of her age, this world was filled with evil intentions.
"What's all the noise! You're courting death! "
Hearing the ruckus below, the saint race martial artists in the air suddenly raised the whips in their hands and lashed out at Lin Ming!
The long whip exploded with an explosive sound, and a vigorous force immediately spread out!
"Pa!"
Several people were instantly torn to pieces, and their broken limbs flew all over the sky. Screams rang out one after another.
The eyes of the Xiantian realm warriors turned red, and they could no longer bear it!
"Fight!"
"Kill him!"
"Even if we have to die, let's die a quick death …"
These Xiantian realm warriors couldn't fly, so they had to jump high into the air with the help of the mountain walls, then they threw themselves at the saint warriors.
A mocking expression emerged on the saint warriors' face. "With just you? You don't even have the qualifications to fight with your lives! "
If a rabbit fought with its life on the line against a human, it would be a ridiculous scene. This was what the saint warriors were thinking.
After saying that, the saint warriors raised the long whip in their hands. The long whip kept twisting and turning into numerous snake shadows, then they shot toward the Xiantian realm warriors.
The king of Xi closed his eyes. He couldn't bear to watch this scene, and the pain in his heart made his face twist.
Three months ago, he was still having a bunch of concubines, and the entire kingdom was living a leisurely and carefree life. Who would have thought that just a few months later, he would bring his Xi kingdom into the abyss?
He was powerless to stop this from happening, and this was the sorrow of a weakling.
If he was strong enough, strong enough to kill that Divine Core warrior, then perhaps he would be able to save his subjects …
"So, being weak is a sin?" The king lamented in his mind.
But in the next moment, the king didn't hear any screams. Instead, he heard a surprised cry from the saint warriors. "Who are you?!"

Chapter 1583
The Divine Core warrior from the Holy Clan stared at Luo Zheng with a surprised and doubtful expression …
The aura emanating from Luo Zheng's body made him realize that this man was a mighty warrior!
The gap between his cultivation base and Luo Zheng's was too great, and he couldn't see through Luo Zheng's true strength. But, he could guess that Luo Zheng was at least a Third Transformation God, and he might even be a Realm Lord!
Wasn't the Seven Extreme Worlds occupied by the Sacred Clan? A mighty warrior like this dared to step into the Seven Extreme Worlds? Wasn't he just courting death?
"You, who are you … what do you want? Don't tell me you're going to surrender to the Sacred Clan … "Although the Sacred Clan warrior's cultivation base wasn't high, his mind was still sharp, and he instantly came up with this answer.
If this man was really going to surrender to the Sacred Clan, he might still have a chance to survive. Humans would always think in a positive direction, and the Sacred Clan was no exception.
"You don't have the qualifications to know my purpose." Luo Zheng gazed at the man with an indifferent expression, then he loosened his grip. The whip dropped down and returned to the Sacred Clan warrior's hand.
Luo Zheng's action immediately made the Sacred Clan warrior happy. He felt like he had guessed Luo Zheng's purpose correctly. This man was really here to surrender to the Sacred Clan, but he didn't want to tell the Sacred Clan warrior …
But with Luo Zheng's next words, the hope that had just risen in the Sacred Race martial artists' hearts was crushed.
"Weaklings, die!" Luo Zheng closed his eyes.
Luo Zheng unleashed his soul pressure, and it spread out in all directions with him as the center.
There were a few Divine Core warriors hovering in midair. When they saw what was happening, they kept a certain distance from Luo Zheng and the others, and they were carefully observing Luo Zheng's movements.
When they heard Luo Zheng's words, these Sacred Clan warriors immediately had a strong sense of crisis, and when they realized that something bad was about to happen, they immediately turned around and fled!
But as soon as they thought of running away, they felt their heads sink, as if there was a million kilograms of weight pressing down on their heads. Then, their souls were like fragile bubbles, instantly bursting. Their heads exploded along with their souls.
A Divine Core warrior's soul had never been tempered before, so it was as weak as a newborn baby. There was no way for him to fight back.
The several Saint Race cultivators who were escorting the humans suddenly turned into headless corpses that plummeted downwards.
There was only silence in the vast valley …
All the mortals raised their heads to look at Luo Zheng, their eyes filled with disbelief. This person had only closed his eyes to meditate, but he had caused the heads of those arrogant Sacred Race people to explode. What kind of existence was this person?
At this moment, in their hearts, Luo Zheng had become a savior sent by the Heavens to save the people of the Country of Xi.
Luo Zheng glanced at these mortals indifferently and was about to leave when a voice suddenly sounded from below, "Benefactor, please wait!"
It was the monarch of the Country of Xi. He was in the Divine Illumination Realm and could fly in the air. Fly!
There were only a handful of people in the Country of Xi who had such a cultivation.
"Benefactor, I'm the monarch of these people. Since benefactor is willing to help us, can you please bring the people of the Country of Xi out of this Seven Extreme Worlds?!" The monarch asked with a begging expression. He knew there was a huge gap between his cultivation and Luo Zheng's. It was already a huge favor for this man to be willing to help them, and he shouldn't have any other extravagant hopes.
But, the monarch also knew that even if Luo Zheng saved the people of his country, they wouldn't be able to survive in the Seven Extreme Worlds. A few hundred thousand people were too big of a target, and they would be easily discovered by the Sacred Race cultivators.
Most importantly, once the Blood Curse Art was successfully cast, no one in this world would have a chance to survive. Even if they were hiding in a cave thousands of meters underground, they would still be affected by the Blood Curse Art!
The only way out was to leave this world …
The ponytailed girl poked her head out of the monarch's wide arm, her pair of black eyes staring at Luo Zheng. Her curiosity towards Luo Zheng seemed to have suppressed her fear and sadness, and she carefully sized up Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng obviously understood the monarch's words, but he shook his head slightly, "There are too many people, I can't bring them away."
The monarch's face turned gloomy. His request was a bit too much. It was already a huge favor for this person to help them, and his request was actually a bit too much.
But Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and he looked at the mountain wall on the side of the canyon, and pondered for a moment.
Then he approached the mountain wall, stretched out a finger, and lightly tapped on the mountain wall. The mountain wall instantly collapsed, and a large hole about ten feet high appeared.
Luo Zheng burrowed into the hole, and his finger was like a blade, constantly cutting and expanding the hole. In just ten breaths of time, Luo Zheng had dug out a cave that was ten thousand feet wide and a hundred thousand feet deep.
When Luo Zheng returned to the outside of the cave, he asked the monarch, "Do you have enough food?"
The monarch understood Luo Zheng's intention, and his face revealed a difficult expression, "There is indeed enough food, but hiding in this cave …"
The Country of Xi was forced to migrate by the Divine Clan, and before they left, the monarch had already mobilized everyone to bring all the food they could. Everyone's remaining rations could last them for half a month without any problem.
But was hiding in the cave useful? Once the Blood Curse Art took effect, the result would be the same no matter where they hid.
Luo Zheng of course understood the monarch's worries, "I will not let the Blood Curse Art take effect, you just need to hide in there for a few days."
Hearing this, the monarch's face was slightly shocked.
Perhaps the monarch's cultivation base was low, but that didn't mean his vision was low. After all, he was a warrior from the Upper Realm.
Judging from Luo Zheng's words, it seemed like he was going to stop the Divine Clan from using the Blood Curse Art?
"Trust me." Luo Zheng said with an indifferent tone.
Looking at Luo Zheng's firm and calm expression, the monarch suddenly felt a sense of trust in him. Perhaps this man could really do it …
"Thank you, benefactor, for saving us. If benefactor can help our Country of Xi survive this calamity, we, we …"
The monarch wanted to express his gratitude, but halfway through, he suddenly didn't know what to say next.
He was a Divine Reflection Realm martial artist. Only martial artists understood the difference in strength between the strong and the weak.
Mortals couldn't tell the difference, and in their eyes, the gap between a Divine Core warrior and a World Lord wasn't that big.
The monarch could vaguely understand Luo Zheng's strength. Since he dared to come to the Seven Extreme Realm alone, he was at least a World Lord, or even a Heavenly Lord. There was no way they could repay a man like this.
"We … we can't repay you, we can only kowtow to you to express our gratitude!"
After saying that, the monarch dropped to his knees in midair, kneeling down in front of Luo Zheng.
Since the monarch of the Xi Country had knelt down, there was no reason for the mortals to remain standing. Hundreds of thousands of mortals lined up in a long line, and at this moment, all of them knelt down in front of Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng shook his head and said, "You can all enter now. I'll set up an illusory formation at the entrance of the cave."
The monarch nodded his head, then gave out an order.
Hundreds of thousands of mortals filed into the cave created by Luo Zheng. Using the Divine Rune Art, Luo Zheng casually set up an illusory formation at the entrance of the cave. When he activated the formation, the entire cave disappeared from sight.
This illusory formation couldn't stop a World Lord, and even a Divine Transformation warrior could easily find the flaw in the formation, and discover the hundreds of thousands of mortals in the cave.
However, the task of escorting these mortals was done by Divine Core warriors. With Luo Zheng's current strength, let alone a Divine Core warrior, even a False Tribulation warrior or Life and Death warrior wouldn't be able to see through the formation.
Therefore, with this formation, these mortals should be safe.
After arranging everything, Luo Zheng finally left, continuing his journey towards the Seven Extreme Immortal City.
The monarch was standing at the entrance of the cave, holding the girl with ponytails in his arms, and staring blankly at Luo Zheng's back.
"Monarch, who is he?" The girl with ponytails asked with a clear and melodious voice.
The monarch responded with a smile, "He … is a great hero of our human race!"
"Great hero? A very, very great hero? "The girl with ponytails asked again.
"Yes, very, very great …" The monarch replied.

Chapter 1584
The canyon wasn't far from the Seven Absolutes Immortal City. It was only a hundred miles away. Luo Zheng only needed one step to teleport there.
Out of caution, Luo Zheng didn't do so.
After returning from the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Luo Zheng's strength had improved greatly, but it didn't mean that he could face the Sacred Race's unscrupulous rampage.
The closer he got to the Sacred Race, the more cautious Luo Zheng became. He even lowered his speed and moved stealthily.
Along the way, Luo Zheng also discovered several groups of mortals. The number of mortals ranged from 100,000 to 500,000, and they were all escorted by Saint Race martial artists. It was clear that they were being sent to the Seven Absolute Immortal City.
Luo Zheng didn't rescue them. They were too close to the Seven Absolutes Immortal City. The fluctuations caused by the battle could easily be detected by the experts in the city.
Moreover, the root of the problem was in the Seven Absolutes Immortal City. As long as he destroyed the Seven Absolutes Immortal City and stopped them from using the Blood Curse Art, he believed that the Sacred Race wouldn't wantonly slaughter mortals. After all, the strength of these mortals was too weak. Just like how humans had no interest in slaughtering ants, the Sacred Race wouldn't kill these mortals out of boredom.
Soon, a dazzling golden main city appeared in Luo Zheng's field of vision. The Seven Absolutes Immortal City was the largest immortal city in the Seven Absolutes Domain.
Looking from afar, he saw a huge blood-red disc floating above the Seven Absolutes Immortal City. The blood-red disc almost covered the entire main city. Countless tiny blood-red runes were constantly rolling on the disc.
There were four blood pillars that extended upwards from the Seven Ultimate Immortal City and were connected to the blood-red disc. The blood-red disc seemed to be continuously absorbing the crimson blood through the four blood pillars. Then, it scattered the blood into the sky, forming blood-red clouds that spread throughout the Seven Ultimate Immortal City.
The closer he got to the Seven Absolutes Immortal City, the stronger the smell of blood became. It was as if there was a mountain of corpses and a sea of blood in front of him. The once bustling immortal city had now turned into a genuine Asura purgatory.
After sizing them up for a moment, Luo Zheng silently floated towards the side, and then the killing intent in his eyes vanished without a trace. At the same time, his Dantian was completely sealed up, causing him to instantly change his appearance and transform into a mortal with a declining aura. He silently descended at the center of the road, and this was the path that one had to pass to enter the Seven Absolutes Immortal City.
Not long after that, a group of mortals slowly arrived at the Seven Ultimate Immortal City. There were a few Saint warriors escorting these mortals in midair. The closer they got to the Seven Ultimate Immortal City, the more impatient these Divine Core warriors became.
"Move faster!"
"It took us nine days to travel three hundred li! It's too slow! "
The saint martial artist roared in the air. He raised the whip in his hand and ruthlessly lashed out. The sound of the whip was ear-piercing, and some mortals couldn't help but cover their ears.
These mortals were already completely exhausted from their journey, and no matter how fast they traveled, they couldn't go any faster. However, they still squeezed out a sliver of energy from all over their bodies and walked forward mechanically.
At this time, the leading Saint Race martial artist discovered Luo Zheng lying on the ground.
"Why is there one that slipped through the net?!"
After saying that, the Saint Race martial artist abruptly lashed out with the whip in his hand toward Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered. He wasn't afraid of the whip, as even if he just lay there and let the martial artist whip him, he wouldn't be harmed at all. However, if the whip hit him, he would be exposed.
Slap!
In the end, the whip didn't hit Luo Zheng's body. Instead, it hit the side of Luo Zheng's body, causing a huge hole to appear on the ground.
"You, follow the group!" The Saint Race martial artist said to Luo Zheng with a cold voice.
The goal of the Saint Race was to obtain human blood. If the human race cooperated with them and rushed to the Seven Absolutes Immortal City at the fastest speed, they wouldn't be willing to kill indiscriminately. Now that they were already quite close to the Seven Absolutes Immortal City, Luo Zheng was just a stray sheep in the eyes of the Saint Race martial artist. He casually chased Luo Zheng into the crowd.
The aura on Luo Zheng's face was extremely weak. His entire body was covered in mud, and he had concealed his cultivation base and soul. At this moment, he naturally put on a good show, rolling and crawling as he blended into the group.
The surrounding mortals were stupefied. They didn't even have the interest to look at Luo Zheng. They were like walking corpses. They only knew that they had to move forward, but they didn't know where to go or what their fate would be.
This place was only 20 some miles away from the Seven Absolutes Immortal City. Even though it was right in front of their eyes, these exhausted mortals still spent half a day to arrive at the gates of the Seven Absolutes Immortal City.
On top of the grand walls of the Seven Absolutes Immortal City, there was a row of Saint Race martial artists. Most of them were Divine Sea realm martial artists, and a small number of them were Divine Reaching realm martial artists. They were all gathered at the top of the city walls, chatting.
"Did you hear? It seems that the Blue Squad was killed by someone?"
"The seven people in that squad were all Divine Transformation realm elites. Someone was actually able to kill them without them knowing. This person's strength is at least at the World Lord realm!"
"World Lord? Did you know that the Blue Squad killed a World Lord before? "
"If it isn't a World Lord, then could it be an Empyrean? Would an Empyrean dare to come to the Seven Absolutes World? "
"That's the strange part! There aren't many Empyreans in the Great Expansion Universe. They wouldn't dare to act rashly. If an Empyrean were to die, that would be a great loss to humanity. How could they possibly send an Empyrean to attack a small peripheral great world? "
"This matter is indeed strange …"
Luo Zheng's eyes and ears were sharp. Although these martial artists were far away from him, he still heard every word they said.
When he entered the city gates with the mortals, these martial artists released their sense and swept it over everyone's heads.
Although the Blood Curse Art could occupy a great world once and for all, it was extremely troublesome to use. Moreover, the array formations that the Blood Curse Art laid down were extremely fragile. If there was even the slightest bit of carelessness, it would be destroyed and all previous efforts would be wasted.
When the saints used the Blood Curse Art in other great worlds, they had encountered this sort of situation before. The saints had learned their lesson and in order to prevent this from happening, they had become much stricter in the selection of mortals.
If one wanted to be sent into the blood pond for sacrifice, they would have to pass through three layers of screening. They would be screened once before entering the city gates, once more within the city, and once more when they approached the blood pond.
In this first round, the saint martial artists didn't discover anything strange.
After smoothly entering the Seven Supreme Immortal City, Luo Zheng continued to follow the mortals as they walked along the wide roads of the city.
After the Seven Supreme Immortal City was occupied by the saints, all of the martial artists in the city had been slaughtered. The once bustling streets were now empty without a single person to be seen. As the smell of blood became increasingly thick, it was like a ghost city with a horrifying atmosphere. Even the saint martial artists felt uncomfortable here.
In order to use the Blood Curse Art, the saints had laid down four massive blood ponds within the Seven Supreme Immortal City. Each blood pond was able to hold hundreds of thousands of people, just like a small lake. In the south of the city, six Divine Transformation realm martial artists appeared on the main road leading to the blood ponds.
These Divine Transformation realm martial artists had cold and gloomy expressions. Their auras were powerful, but compared to the saint martial artists at the city gates, they were even more vigilant.
Powerful senses constantly swept over everyone's heads. One of these senses even stopped above Luo Zheng's head.
Luo Zheng swayed as he walked forwards, as if he would fall to the ground at any moment.
The Divine Transformation realm martial artist looked at Luo Zheng. As he saw Luo Zheng's filthy appearance, he looked away.
When a higher boundary martial artist faced a lower boundary martial artist, they had an advantage to begin with. The Divine Transformation realm martial artist didn't discover any flaws. He only subconsciously felt that there was something different about Luo Zheng, but he wasn't able to pinpoint what it was.
After passing through the screening of these martial artists, Luo Zheng drew closer and closer to the blood ponds.
When the blood ponds appeared in front of everyone's eyes, there was an uproar in the crowd …
Although these mortals had rushed all the way here and were already exhausted, as they saw this hellish scene in front of them, they immediately sobered up.
All sorts of broken limbs floated in the blood ponds, bobbing up and down on the surface. The thick smell of blood wafted out from the blood ponds.
And to the side of the blood ponds, groups of mortals were lined up in neat rows. Then, they were herded into the blood ponds.
The moment these mortals jumped into the blood ponds, an invisible strength appeared in the air and twisted these mortals. Blood splashed out from their twisted bodies, creating a horrifying scene.

Chapter 1586
Sending the most outstanding geniuses of the universe into the Forbidden Land of God Refinement to cultivate was a common practice. The saint race's Maha had once been to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, and it was an open world.
But in the end, the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was Saint Jiu's territory. At this time, how could Saint Jiu allow the martial artists of the Great Extreme Universe to enter?
Saint Jiu and Mu Haiji were members of the Saint Halls. On the surface, the two of them seemed to be on good terms, and under the rules, they didn't tear apart each other. But, the struggle between them had been going on for a long time.
The saints couldn't chase down a Dao Child in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, but Mu Haiji still thought of a method. Outside the Great Extreme Universe, he had laid down a Greater Space Technique. This technique was secretly used by a True Divinity of the saints.
When a Dao Child of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement returned, this Greater Space Technique would activate and distort their path of return.
So when Luo Zheng returned, he didn't directly return to the transmission array of the Demi-Immortal Race, but instead landed in the Seven Extremity World.
"Are you sure?" Maha cautiously asked.
If a Dao Child returned, under the influence of the Greater Space Technique, they would fall into any great world within the Great Extreme Universe. For the saints, this was a very important opportunity!
"Whether I'm sure or not, we'll know once we try!"
The wolf tooth birthmark on Wu 'er's face became increasingly red, representing her growing excitement. She stared at Luo Zheng on the other side of the blood pool, quietly gathering true essence within her body.
"Wu 'er, don't do anything. I will hurry over as fast as I can!" Maha warned.
This Maha was a Saint Son of the saints. Although he was incomparably arrogant, his thoughts were extremely meticulous!
These Dao Children were carefully molded by a Saint, and they were the final counterattack of a Saint before he died. They were the crystallization of a Saint's creation, and each Dao Child was not simple!
They had cultivated in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement for three years, and now that they were willing to return, their strength and cultivation would inevitably rise.
Maha, who had stayed in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, naturally had a deep understanding of this.
But, Wu 'er didn't intend to listen to Maha's advice.
… … …
As Luo Zheng slid towards the blood pool, the strength within his body constantly brewed.
His goal wasn't just the blood pool. If everything went smoothly, he would destroy the four blood pools in the Seven Extremity Immortal City in the shortest possible time.
But what Luo Zheng didn't know was that he had already been targeted by a woman of the Sacred Race …
1000 feet …
500 feet … 500 feet …
100 feet …
Luo Zheng Luo Zheng........ but Luo Zheng's Luo Zheng.....

Dao Luo Zheng Immortal God, Luo Luo Luo, Luo Luo Lin..... Luo Zheng, Luo God, Luo Zheng........ Luo Zheng Dao...... o God of Luo Zheng.... Luo Zheng, God............. it.........., he.......
