.. Luo Zheng had been preparing for a long time. Once he made his move, he didn't hesitate. He rushed towards the Blood Curse Immortal God above the blood pool.
"Hee hee, good disguise." At the same time, Wu 'er, who had been observing Luo Zheng, also made her move.
She stepped lightly in the air. Like a dancing butterfly, black beads appeared around her. In an instant, she moved and blocked Luo Zheng's way.
"What do you want to do? Handsome guy from the Human Clan, "Wu 'er said to Luo Zheng with a smile.
Previously, Luo Zheng hadn't noticed that this woman had discovered him, so he was slightly surprised. However, Luo Zheng's surprise only lasted for the blink of an eye before he coldly shouted, "Scram!" As soon as his voice fell, he sent out a palm strike!
"Oh, you're so angry," Wu 'er smiled and threw out a palm as well. She didn't show any fear.
There were Dao Children in the Great Evolution Realm. There were also Dao Children in the Great Extremity Realm.
This "Wu 'er" was one of them. She had already become a Realm Lord. Her next step was to condense a Godhead and break through to the True God Realm. The Sacred Clan's bloodline was noble. Everyone was a Dao Child. How could Wu' er put this Luo Zheng in her eyes?
But the moment Wu 'er's palm touched Luo Zheng, her expression changed!
She didn't think that Luo Zheng's palm would contain such a terrifying power. It was so strong that she couldn't resist it!
The power contained in this palm was probably enough to kill an ordinary Realm Lord!
Pa pa pa pa!
That massive power flowed through Wu 'er's arm, and the black beads around her began to explode one by one!
In an instant, four beads exploded!
Wu 'er was suppressed by that huge power and was directly slapped into the blood pool below. A huge wave of blood splashed out.
As Wu 'er sank into the blood pool, the shock in her heart was nearly indescribable.
The seven black beads around her were precious treasures bestowed to her by the Sacred Clan Empyreans. These black beads were called Life Sacrificing Beads. Each bead could withstand a single fatal wound, enough to die in her place once!
In other words, every time a black bead exploded, it was equal to her dying once.
These seven Life Sacrificing Beads had followed her for so many years and never once had a single one exploded. After all, her status in the Sacred Clan was revered and there were very few opportunities for her to face a life or death crisis.
But Wu 'er never imagined that this fellow would casually slap out with his palm and shatter four of her Life Sacrificing Beads! This was equal to Luo Zheng killing her four times with a single slap!
At this time, Wu 'er's mood could be imagined. She suddenly remembered Maha's warning not to act rashly!
In the blood pool, Wu 'er was shocked and frightened. At the same time, she crushed a light purple divine rune in her hand. The moment this divine rune was crushed, the entire Seven Absolute Immortal City emitted a piercing cry.
Hearing this sound, the complexions of all the saint martial artists changed. This cry was the highest level of alert!
Luo Zheng's eyes never paused on this saint woman; the only goal in his eyes was the blood curse array formation. After he slapped out with his palm, his body shook in the air and he continued to rush towards the array formation!
The moment Luo Zheng rushed forwards, he heard a humming sound. In front of Luo Zheng, a light yellow translucent curtain of light blocked his path.
"An enchantment?"
Luo Zheng had been looking at this blood curse array formation for a long time so he naturally knew that there was likely a protective enchantment around it. Following the momentum of his impact, Luo Zheng punched out at the enchantment!
Dong!
Contrary to Luo Zheng's expectations, the enchantment shook twice but didn't break.
Wu 'er slowly floated up from the blood pool. When she saw Luo Zheng's actions, a sneer appeared on her face.
Even if this Dao Child's strength was boundless, it was impossible for him to break this enchantment with his own brute force. This enchantment was built to deal with brute force.
Above the four blood pools in the Seven Absolute Immortal City, there were four identical enchantments. The four enchantments were connected to each other, and the four enchantments were mutually damaged. If one wanted to break them, they would have to break all four enchantments at the same time.
With the strength of a realm lord, this was simply impossible!
Wu 'er was optimistic by nature and wasn't afraid that Luo Zheng would turn around and kill her. Instead, she slowly took out a sound transmission token and said with a smile, "Maha, when will you arrive? This guy has already started attacking the blood curse array formation. However, this person actually intends to use brute force to break the enchantment. This is simply …"
Before Wu 'er could finish her words, her pupils had already widened at this moment.
She saw Luo Zheng suddenly raise his hand, and with his fist as the center, the space around him began to distort!
Distorting the power of spatial laws was just a piece of cake for a realm lord.
However, she didn't sense any spatial laws from Luo Zheng, which meant that this guy wasn't using spatial laws to distort space!
He was using a terrifying force that she couldn't imagine! He was relying on this force to distort space!
Hualala …
The blood in the blood pool, under the distortion of this gravitational force, rippled. In everyone's sight, the center of the blood pool caved in as if there was an invisible sphere pressing down on the blood pool. And this sphere was centered around Luo Zheng's fist!

Chapter 1588
There was no such cultivation method as Thunder Calamity Annihilation in the Great Evolution Universe. Mu Haiji had extremely strict control over the heavenly tribulations in the Great Evolution Universe.
Therefore, when the sacred race martial artists saw the sudden appearance of the heavenly tribulation, they looked at each other in dismay.
Could it be that the human world lord had broken through just now?
"Not good, quickly stop the heavenly tribulation!"
"Without the protection of the barrier, the blood curse array is too weak!"
Thunder Calamity Annihilation was an extremely special cultivation method. This method was to communicate with the heavenly dao and summon the heavenly tribulation.
Once the summoning was successful, the entire process was irreversible. Even Luo Zheng himself couldn't stop it. What methods did these world lords have to stop the heavenly tribulation?
Moreover, there wasn't enough time.
Crackle …
A burst of violent lightning surged like a torrential downpour, directly enveloping the entire blood curse array.
The hundred thousand divine runes in the Blood Curse Array were extremely fragile and intricate. Every single divine rune was tightly connected to each other, and the slightest bit of damage could cause the entire Blood Curse Array to collapse!
Under the chaotic barrage of lightning, the array was instantly charred. How could those divine runes withstand it?
In an instant, half of the divine runes were incinerated. The blood curse array above the blood pool could be considered destroyed.
Originally, the blood curse array was still extracting the blood from the blood pool, transforming it into a blood pillar and sending it to the blood disc in the sky. Ma Zan's expression was similarly gloomy and cold. Without saying a word, he also used greater teleportation and rushed towards the blood pool in the north of the city.
However, Luo Zheng used the Greater Teleportation Technique and his position was uncertain. The two sector lords could only try their luck and each chose a blood pool to investigate. Other than the two sector lords, the other sector lords naturally wouldn't stay idle. They rushed towards the blood pool in the west of the city.
Shi Kongwu climbed out of the blood pool. With a light shake of his body, all the blood turned into blood droplets and flowed away. Looking at the destroyed blood curse array, he coldly snorted. Chapter 1593
Ever since the saints invaded the universe, they had been unstoppable.

On one hand, they were strong enough. Combined with this propaganda, many martial artists had the misconception that the high and mighty saints were invincible. Compared to a perfect race like them, humanity was nothing but an inferior race.
In this sort of situation, many human martial artists feared the saints before they even fought. This pessimistic mood constantly spread through the human martial artists, ruthlessly striking at their morale!
Luo Zheng's current methods were simple. Not to mention that the saints in the Great Extreme Universe weren't the true saints, but even if these people really were the inheritances of the perfect race in the Realm of the Gods, Luo Zheng would still casually make them up!
First, he had to dampen the other side's spirit in order to save humanity's morale!
Now that the Roar Command had been activated, his voice spread throughout the universe. Countless lives were listening to him.

Encountering such a situation, he didn't know how to respond.

You can only fight with all your might, hoping to squeeze into my Great Extremity Universe and struggle at death's door like stray dogs!" Luo Zheng clutched the Roaring Command Token tightly. His voice was extremely infectious.

This was also Luo Zheng's declaration of war!

Halfway through, Heavenly Venerable Oracle had indignantly chosen to confront the saints head-on. However, he had suffered a crushing defeat, severely damaging the morale of the human race.
However, the old patriarch and the demi-immortal race couldn't be blamed for this. After all, the purpose of the demi-immortal race's existence was to wait for the Dao children to mature. This was also Luo Xiao's plan. However, there had been a flaw in his plan. He hadn't anticipated that the saints would invade the universe ahead of time …
Now that Luo Zheng had returned, the first thing he did was to represent the human race and declare war!
This' declaration of war 'was spoken with a heroic spirit that reached the heavens. It stirred up the pride in the hearts of countless living beings in the universe. Their fear of the saints also vanished like smoke in thin air!
"Good! This Luo Zheng is worthy of being called a first-rate genius! "
"As a member of the universe, we will contribute our last drop of blood!"
"Although we are mortals, we do not care about the lives of mortals. The saints are the barbarians. They may be strong, but it is wishful thinking to conquer us!"
"… …"
Throughout the universe, all the major races and martial artists of all levels, as well as all the intelligent beings hiding in underground caves and in the depths of the starry sky, were in an uproar at this moment!
"Hahahahaha … …"
Suddenly, a burst of heroic laughter rang out in the universe.
This voice belonged to Heavenly Venerable Origin Demon of the demons!
"Luo Zheng, boy, not bad, not bad. In the past, the demons wanted to get rid of you as soon as possible. I didn't expect that today, we would have to join forces with you to fight the enemy! This barbarian race is not worth mentioning. Once I wipe out these traitors of the Demon Night Clan, I will join forces with you! "
As soon as Heavenly Venerable Origin Demon finished speaking, another voice rang out. This time, it was the voice of the old patriarch of the demi-immortal race. Old man Feng said, "The demi-immortal race has received the secret order of the Lord of Dayan to support Luo Zheng in order to control the barbarians. Now that Luo Zheng has returned, we naturally declare war!"
"The Xuanyuan clan is the disciple of the Lord of Dayan. Today … …"
"The Ten Thousand Buddha Sacred Region is … …"
"The Human Alliance is … …"
Luo Zheng's' declaration of war 'was like a spark thrown into a pot of kerosene, instantly igniting the universe. At the same time, several great human influences used the Roar Command to declare war on the saints … …
Within the Human Alliance, at another great boundary.
The corpses of the saints had already piled up into mountains. The masters of these corpses had all been at the Third Divine Transformation.
Among them, 326 were at the Divine Sea realm, 67 were at the Extreme Divine realm, and 14 were at the Divine Transformation realm … …
A young human girl stood at the peak of this mountain of corpses.
Her eye-catching nine-colored dress fluttered in the wind. Under her pitch-black long hair, the right side of her face, which had some baby fat, was already badly mutilated. One of her eyeballs had even burst open. It was too horrible to look at. However, just by looking at the left side of her face, one could still feel the beauty of this girl.
In front of this young girl were two saint race World Lords.
These two saint race World Lords were also heavily wounded. They floated in the air, constantly gasping for breath as they vigilantly stared at this young human girl.
Because Luo Zheng and Maha were arguing with each other through the Roar Command, the battle between the two sides had come to a halt.
The young girl's eyes never fell on the two saint race World Lords. At this time, her wooden face was looking towards the distant skies.
Her remaining right eye was blinking …
The moment she heard Luo Zheng's voice, the sadness in her eyes vanished, replaced with joy and relief.
"Did you hear that?" The young girl's large eye suddenly curved into a crescent moon shape as she smiled at the two saint race World Lords.
The two saint race World Lords were stunned … being tricked by Luo Zheng like this was a great blow to their mental state.
"Hear my ass!"
"Kill this woman!"
The two World Lords felt a sense of irritation and irritation in their hearts.
This woman's methods were far too terrifying. In front of them, she had exterminated an entire saint race army!
What was even more hateful was that this woman came and went without a trace. She had even been able to safely escape the pursuit of a saint race Empyrean!
"If you didn't hear it, then you should listen carefully." The young girl's voice was crisp and clear, but it contained a trace of icy cold killing intent, as if there were no emotions contained within.
Seeing the two World Lords rush over, the young girl stepped on her wooden clogs and leapt backwards, maintaining a free landing position.
Her originally heavily wounded left face began to rapidly recover. The wounds on her face constantly wriggled and her ruptured eyeball returned to its original position. The wounds on her face began to rapidly heal, instantly restoring her beautiful face!
From behind her, beams of nine colored light shot out. These beams of light formed ribbons that were like the tail feathers of a phoenix, sweeping towards the two saint race World Lords …
"Listen carefully, that is the greatest pride of our human race!"
These were the last words the two saint race World Lords heard from the young girl before they died.

Chapter 1594
Within the Cinderclast World.
Giant rocks were burned red.
The two figures shuttled back and forth rapidly. The moment their feet touched the rocks, they shot forward. In an instant, they were dozens of miles away. Their figures were exceptionally agile.
And behind the two of them, there was a saint race sector lord following closely behind!
These two were Ji Luoxue and Xuanyuan Chenfeng!
Sage Jiu sent the Daoists back to the Great Expansion Universe, but due to the effects of the Greater Space Technique, the nine Daoists were scattered all over the universe …

In his eyes, the two of them were just rats scurrying through the cracks in the rocks!

The Sacred Race realm lord waved his hand and sent out a profound seal.
Rays of black light spread out from the distant sky. These rays of light seemed to cut off a corner of the sky and formed a giant handprint that fiercely crashed down!

Ji Luoxue nodded at the same time. White light spread out from their feet and they disappeared in the blink of an eye.
The Sacred Race realm lord sneered. He stepped out and also used Greater Space Teleportation to chase after them.
This saint race world lord's cultivation was much more stable than Xuanyuan Chenfeng and Ji Luoxue. His greater teleportation distance was tens of times greater than the two of them. Before this, he had already spread his senses out to a thousand miles radius. The instant the two fellows teleported, he was able to detect their location!

Ji Luoxue and Xuanyuan Chenfeng appeared on top of a flaming mountain a hundred li away!

The Blazing Mountain Range was red all year round. Streams of Earth Fire shot out from the cracks in the rocks, spewing out blazing flames.

This Great Saint Race World Lord had already killed 12 human world overlords after entering the Great Yan Universe. He didn't place these two human world overlords in his eyes at all.

Along the way, he had already asked this question three times, but Xuanyuan Chenfeng and Ji Luoxue never answered.
Fiery Rock World was the core of the Human Alliance. Because of this, the Saint Race didn't send too many experts to guard this place. They also didn't need to use the Blood Curse Technique. Just as the Saint Race Great World Lord was about to attack …
Maha and Luo Zheng's voices echoed in the universe.
The Saint Race Great World Lord revealed a puzzled look as he listened attentively.
Ji Luoxue and Xuanyuan Chenfeng heard Luo Zheng's voice and revealed a trace of a smile on their faces.

There was even a period of time when they ran back and forth around the borders of Fiery Rock World. They didn't seem to want to completely shake him off.

Recently, he had been stationed in Fiery Rock World and was indeed a bit bored, so he took the initiative to participate in this game of cat and mouse.

In these three days and nights, his subordinates had all been shaken off. He was the only one left!

Two Sector Lords wanted to kill a Great World Lord. This was playing with fire.

Why did these two fellows get lost! Because they didn't run over from Gods No Thoughts, but came from outside the universe!

No matter what, the strength of these Dao Children far exceeded those of the same level. The two of them really could kill him!

With his Great World Lord's mastery of the Great Shift Technique, it required half a breath of time to completely release it.

Chapter 1595
Maha had originally planned on using the Great Domination Art to force Luo Zheng to submit to him.

As Maha heard this roar from the universe, his complexion darkened to the point where it could drip water!
Maha had led the saint martial artists to slaughter their way through, completely suppressing the morale of humanity. Then, the rebellion of the Demon Night Clan had dealt a fatal blow to humanity. Although the so-called Demon Race had intervened, the morale of humanity's three great influences had already fallen to a freezing point.
Because of Luo Zheng's words, all of his previous efforts seemed to have been wasted …

He couldn't use the Great Domination Art to force Luo Zheng to submit, but that didn't mean he couldn't control Luo Zheng. There were many ways to enslave a martial artist.
In Maha's opinion, no matter how eloquent or sharp-tongued Luo Zheng was, he wouldn't be able to escape the palm of his hand. The two saint Empyreans behind Maha already had this intention. They were also silently cursing His Highness Daozi in their hearts.

Since Luo Zheng was trapped in the Seven Extreme Boundaries, he was nothing more than a piece of meat on a chopping board. They could cut him up however they wanted.

Empyrean Dim Darkness's complexion didn't change. "Do you think you can escape?"
He had already planted a mark on Luo Zheng's body, so no matter which direction Luo Zheng moved, he would be able to detect it in an instant.
In his eyes, Luo Zheng was like a little sheep running wildly on the plains. No matter how hard he tried to run, it was impossible for him to escape the hunt of a falcon. He could even let Luo Zheng run as much as he wanted and let him fall into despair before hunting him down.
Maha, Shi Kongwu, and Empyrean Myriad Poison also thought the same. Therefore, when they saw Luo Zheng escape, they did not show any expression on their faces.
Right when Heavenly Exalt Dim intended to carry out spatial teleportation in pursuit, his face suddenly froze, and his eyes suddenly revealed an expression of disbelief …
Maha held Shi Kongwu in his arms, calming his mind as he thought about how to deal with Luo Zheng. After completely enslaving him, he could use his status as a Dao Child to suppress the human race. In addition, this boy seemed to have many secrets on him …

An Empyrean's perception was extremely powerful. It was impossible for any living being within 10,000 miles to escape his detection.

On a small hill a hundred miles south of the Seven Supreme Immortal City, there was a small mini Immortal Abode.

If they really let Luo Zheng slip away, even Maha would probably face severe punishment.

At this moment, Heavenly Exalt Dim Dim Dim was serious. He focused on cultivating spatial nomological laws, his destructive power was extremely strong!

This brat went into that divine artifact Immortal Abode again …
From the start, these Heavenly Exalts weren't worried about Luo Zheng's safety.

Maha felt like he was going crazy.
Where did this brat find this spatial Dharma treasure!

"Yes, Your Highness Daozi!"
This Heavenly Exalt Myriad Poison was an expert in researching all kinds of poisons. The Mandate of Heaven he bore was the law of life!

"Ka ka ka ka …"

Chapter 1596
Maha narrowed his eyes and stared at the immortal mansion for a long time before saying coldly, "I'll let my father do it."
The Sacred Race was a totalitarian society, and there was only one force in the entire universe.
The status of Celestial Venerables was not equal, but arranged like a pyramid. Maha's father represented the strongest Celestial Venerables of the Sacred Race!
He didn't believe that he couldn't do anything to a mere spatial treasure.
But just as he finished speaking, the laws of space fluctuated, and the immortal mansion sank down.
In the blink of an eye, the immortal mansion disappeared from everyone's eyes.
Thus, Maha and the others were stunned and looked at each other again.
Next, they used all kinds of methods to find the immortal mansion.
But whether they used their perception or dug three feet into the ground, they couldn't find the shadow of the immortal mansion.
In the end, the angry roar of His Highness Daoist came from the hillside outside the Seven Absolutes Immortal City.
Inside the immortal mansion.
Luo Zheng held a teleportation token in his hand with a faint smile on his face.
With the "Gen" token and the "teleportation token", he could bring the entire immortal mansion to any place in the universe.
At this moment, Luo Zheng took the immortal mansion and escaped into the space created by the teleportation token. In this space, Luo Zheng could choose any large world to descend to.
Behind Luo Zheng, a group of people stood in the square of the immortal mansion, looking at Luo Zheng with eager eyes.
Since Luo Zheng returned to the Vast Thousand Realms, he had taken all his close relatives in the Cloud Hall into the immortal mansion.
The entire universe was in great danger, and the safest place was probably this immortal mansion.
This immortal mansion was completely under Luo Zheng's control. It could be said that even if the entire universe was overturned, this immortal mansion would still be intact. As long as he didn't connect the immortal mansion's projection to this place, others wouldn't be able to find this place.
In the crowd, Ning Yudie's beautiful eyes stared at Luo Zheng.
In the past ten years, she and Luo Zheng had been apart more than together. Every time they met, they would leave in a hurry. But she knew that her husband was entangled by the heavens, and she couldn't help him. She could only wait here quietly, but there was no one to talk about the pain in her heart.
"Husband …"
Ning Yudie tiptoed and her body swayed slightly. She rushed to Luo Zheng's side and touched Luo Zheng's face with her cold little hands. Her eyes were full of tenderness and sweetness.
When Granny Yu and the others saw this scene, they also felt gratified. At least Luo Zheng had returned safely.
After returning to his immortal mansion, Luo Zheng wasn't in a hurry to go to the Demi-Immortal Clan. Instead, he decided to stay for a while ….
After hugging Ning Yudie for a few breaths of time, Luo Zheng asked softly, "Where's Luo Nian?"
On the square, Luo Zheng didn't see Luo Nian.
Four years ago, when Luo Zheng saw Luo Nian, he was only seven years old. Now, almost four years later, Luo Nian was already eleven years old.
At that time, Luo Nian was still a little naughty child. She didn't know how he was now.
Hearing Luo Zheng's question, Ning Yudie showed a reproachful expression. As a father, he was indeed too unqualified, but she couldn't bear to blame her husband.
"Nian 'er, Nian' er is in the library," Ning Yudie smiled faintly.
"The library?" Luo Zheng was surprised. Before he left, he did give the library's token to Ning Yudie so that they could freely enter and leave.
After hiding in this immortal mansion for several years, they couldn't be confined to the square all the time. That would be too boring.
Ning Yudie's face showed a trace of pride. "Husband, you'll know when you see it."
Seeing Ning Yudie's reaction, Luo Zheng was even more confused. He immediately went straight to the library.
As soon as he entered the library, he saw Luo Nian sitting under a huge book with his eyes closed in thought. It seemed that he was reading the contents of the book.
Luo Zheng was just about to speak when the immortal in the book suddenly made a shushing motion. Ning Yudie hurriedly stretched out two slender fingers and pressed them against Luo Zheng's mouth.
"Husband, don't disturb Nian 'er," Ning Yudie said hurriedly.
Luo Zheng's face was full of astonishment. What did this kid do these past few years?
Luo Zheng had only been with Luo Nian for a very short time. Four years ago, they met for the first time for only a few months. However, this didn't mean that Luo Zheng and Luo Nian didn't have any father-son relationship.
Since everyone had such a reaction, Luo Zheng didn't say anything else. He also waited on the side with everyone.
After a long time, Luo Nian exhaled slightly. His eyes flashed slightly and a heroic spirit appeared on his face. His eyes turned and shot towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Nian smiled slightly and called out, "Father! You're back. "
Hearing Luo Nian call him father, Luo Zheng's heart swayed slightly. He felt that his son seemed to have grown up overnight. He was overjoyed.
"Hehe," the immortal in the book laughed mischievously. "Nian 'er, did you learn anything today?"
Luo Nian nodded. "I can write down the contents for you, Grandpa Immortal in the book."
Hearing this, the immortal in the book was overjoyed. He waved his hand and pieces of paper floated towards Luo Nian. "Quick, quick. Otherwise, you'll forget."
Luo Nian smiled faintly. "You know that Nian 'er has an extraordinary memory. How could I forget what I've seen? It's just that you're in a hurry to read it. "

If Luo Nian could really become a talent like her husband, Ning Yudie would naturally be willing.
In the past few years, apart from trying to figure out Gu Bei's various speculations, he also tried to decipher the golden Sanskrit characters himself. He didn't expect Luo Nian to succeed.

Chapter 1597

Chapter 1598

Seeing Old Man Feng's finger, Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed. He realized that Old Man Feng was testing him. He flipped his palm and blocked it, sending out a powerful force.
Old Man Feng faintly smiled. He changed his move again. This time, he used his index finger and thumb to snap at Luo Zheng's palm.
Luo Zheng didn't dodge at all. Instead, he used his palm to block. However, the power in his palm grew stronger and stronger. He didn't back down at all!
The two of them fought with one hand. In just a few breaths of time, they had exchanged several hundred moves!
The several Empyreans of the Demi-Immortal Clan watched Luo Zheng's performance with smiles on their faces. Obviously, they were very satisfied with Luo Zheng's performance.
In a mere three years, they were able to raise a Divine Realm Dao Child to such a level. The Forbidden Land of God Refinement was miraculous enough.
"It's a pity that the time was too short. If we had another hundred years, the fake Holy Tribe would have fled without a fight." Empyrean Enchanter of the Demi-Immortal Clan sighed.
It wasn't that Empyrean Enchanter was pessimistic. Although Luo Zheng's return was like a clear stream that injected vitality and confidence into the entire universe, in the eyes of those with discerning eyes, the situation was still not optimistic …
With the return of the ten Dao Children, there were only ten more Realm Lords.
Perhaps the Dao Children's strength far surpassed their peers, but these Dao Children could at most contend with Great Realm Lords. They weren't enough to contend with Empyreans.
And the people who would decide the outcome of the two universes were the Empyreans and True Divinities of the two universes …
The Dao Children had just broken through to Realm Lord. They were still far from condensing their godhood and becoming True Divinities.
Thus, the return of the Dao Children didn't reverse the situation.
Moreover, Luo Zheng's "declaration of war" would likely anger the fake Holy Tribe. As a result, the current state of tug-of-war would lead to an all-out war …
When the old Patriarch tested Luo Zheng, he only used his physical body. Moreover, the old Patriarch limited his strength to around 10%. With Luo Zheng's cultivation and his current cultivation, it wasn't strange that he could receive the old Patriarch's attack. It could only be considered very outstanding.
However, the several Celestial Venerables present didn't know that the power Luo Zheng used was even more pitiful …
At this moment, he was only using the power in his body to deal with the old patriarch, but he already had the upper hand!
"Bang!"
Luo Zheng threw out a punch and hit the edge of Old Man Feng's palm, forcing Old Man Feng back a foot.
"Not bad." Although Old Man Feng wanted to test Luo Zheng's strength, he was also a person who refused to admit defeat. Furthermore, he did not see any signs of Luo Zheng being exhausted. After retreating a foot, he said, "Be careful. This time, I will use 30% of my strength!"
Hearing old man Feng's words, the Heavenly Sovereigns' eyes flickered slightly as a look of surprise appeared on their faces.
Although Old Man Feng was not an expert in body refinement techniques, he was still one of the strongest Celestial Lords in the universe. His pure physical strength was at the peak of Celestial Lords!
From his words, it seemed that he had failed to test Luo Zheng's depth, and now he wanted to test Luo Zheng with only 30% of his strength?
This kid … could withstand it?
The Heavenly Sovereigns' eyes revealed faint traces of worry. In their eyes, although Luo Zheng's body was tyrannical, he was still a junior. In the end, he was still only a Realm Lord. And in the universe, Realm Lords were worthless …
Old Man Feng released 30% of his strength. His originally thin and shriveled body suddenly filled out.
From a skinny little old man, he transformed into a muscular man. Only his face was as old as ever! Changing one's appearance was only a matter of thought for martial artists of their level. However, most martial artists did not think it was necessary.
"My strength has increased by three times." Luo Zheng's expression was still indifferent.
He had increased his own strength by three times as well, which was just enough to suppress Old Man Feng. However, this time, Luo Zheng drew out a portion of the power from his eight-layered Dao Platforms!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
With their strength increased by three times, the two's movements were also twice as fast!
Their right hands constantly crisscrossed in the air. They changed moves, stopped moves, skillfully, covertly, and released power …
The more they fought, the more shocked Old Man Feng became!
Luo Zheng easily received 30% of his strength.
Heavenly Sovereign Huan Miao did not speak. She just stared blankly at Luo Zheng. Seeing him so easily receive 30% of Old Man Feng's strength, then Luo Zheng's actual strength was probably half of Old Man Feng's!
The other Heavenly Sovereigns, including Old Man Feng himself, thought so …
Old Man Feng's competitive spirit grew stronger and stronger. He laughed heartily. "Luo Zheng kid, not bad, not bad. Again! 50% of my strength! I'll add some astral force! Be careful! "
If it had been a simple test before, then Old Man Feng's attack was close to a real battle. He had taken Luo Zheng as his opponent!
One of the top Heavenly Sovereigns of the Great Yan Universe, taking a Realm Lord junior as his opponent. It sounded unbelievable.
Ning Yudie and Mu Mingxue stood behind Luo Zheng. The two beauties had different expressions on their faces …
"That old man … seems to be very powerful!" Ning Yudie asked carefully.
"Heavenly Sovereign, the people in this hall are all Heavenly Sovereigns of the Heavenly Immortal Race." Mu Mingxue explained softly. "It seems that Luo Zheng is even more powerful than we thought."
Mu Mingxue had traveled with Luo Zheng in the Upper Realm. Naturally, her insight was much better than Ning Yudie's. She knew that this Heavenly Sovereign was testing Luo Zheng. However, when she saw Luo Zheng's calm expression, Mu Mingxue suddenly had a crazy thought. Would Luo Zheng's strength far surpass all the Heavenly Sovereigns present?
Naturally, she didn't dare to say it out loud.
Sou sou sou!
Now that the old patriarch attacked, streaks of gray energy flowed in all directions. His every move became even more ferocious.
The forces were like Sword Qi as they scattered around the hall. Some of the forces flew toward the two women behind Luo Zheng. However, with a wave of the Heavenly Sovereign Huan Miao's hand, the forces were instantly annihilated.
After Luo Zheng took a few steps back, a serious look appeared on his face.
At this moment, the eight-layered dao platforms in his body began to vibrate, emitting traces of seven-colored light. This was the color of the origin of power!
Clap clap clap clap …
At this moment, Luo Zheng's right hand was moving like a bolt of lightning towards the Supreme Force of the old patriarch. Every time he made a move, he would be able to accurately crush a Supreme Force, creating a crisp and clear sound of an explosion!
The sound was so loud that it reverberated throughout the entire Soaring Sky Mountain.
Mu Mingxue and Ning Yudie could not bear the sound. They could only cover their ears with their hands …
"He crushed the old patriarch's streaks of energy with one hand …" Heavenly Sovereign Qian Lan murmured from nearby.
"The problem is that this kid … is still doing it with ease!" Heavenly Sovereign Huan Miao's eyes widened. For a moment, she could not find a better word to describe it.
Now that the old patriarch had used fifty percent of his strength and even added a streak of energy, if Luo Zheng could just barely block it, it would be beyond the expectations of many Heavenly Sovereigns. That would be quite outstanding.
However, Luo Zheng clearly had not used his full strength. For a moment, the Heavenly Sovereigns present, including Old Man Feng, felt somewhat embarrassed.
